Method: SkNeXt采用拓扑优先（topology-first）框架：1）不密集处理整个体数据，先将神经元形态转换为保持长程连通性的紧凑SWC骨架；2）将人工校对集中到稀疏的神经元树结构上，在进入高分辨率重建前修正分支、连续性与连通性错误；3）以校正后的骨架作为持久结构先验，在保持神经元身份与拓扑的前提下恢复细节形态；4）将骨架用作空间索引，实现选择性数据访问，仅沿重建轨迹检索高分辨率图像区域，跳过大部分背景与无信号体数据，从而大幅降低I/O与计算开销。

Result: 使用SkNeXt在单块GPU上、一周内完成了PB级小鼠脑超分辨荧光数据集的神经元重建，且无需对整个成像体积进行穷尽的密集推理；重建成本随神经元形态规模扩展，而非随数据集总大小扩展。

Conclusion: SkNeXt表明，以拓扑为先导、骨架驱动的高选择性重建可显著降低大规模体积显微数据神经元重建的计算与I/O开销，为PB级脑数据的高效可扩展重建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in high-resolution fluorescence and electron microscopy have enabled nanoscale imaging across increasingly large brain volumes, but the resulting terabyte- to petabyte-scale datasets make complete neuronal reconstruction prohibitively expensive in computation, data movement, and manual proofreading. Here, we present SkNeXt, a topology-first framework for scalable neuronal reconstruction from large volumetric microscopy datasets. Instead of densely processing entire image volumes, SkNeXt first converts neuronal morphology into compact SWC skeletons that preserve long-range connectivity. Proofreading is therefore focused on sparse neuronal trees, allowing branch, continuity, and connectivity errors to be corrected before high-resolution reconstruction. The corrected skeletons then serve as persistent structural priors for recovering detailed morphology while preserving neuronal identity and topology. Crucially, SkNeXt also uses neuronal skeletons as spatial indices for selective data access, retrieving high-resolution image regions only along reconstructed trajectories and bypassing most background and signal-free volumes. This substantially reduces I/O and computational overhead, allowing reconstruction cost to scale with neuronal morphology rather than total dataset size. Using SkNeXt, we reconstructed neurons from a petabyte-scale super-resolution fluorescence dataset of the mouse brain on a single GPU within one week, without requiring exhaustive dense inference across the complete imaging volume.

</details>


### [36] [Pretraining and Distillation Matter More Than Architecture Family for Label-Free Single-Cell Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09863)
*Philip Graemer,Giuseppe Di Caprio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在LIVECell相差显微数据上以严格防泄漏划分和匹配协议进行对照基准测试，发现CNN与Transformer的性能差异主要源于预训练而非架构，且知识蒸馏比架构选择更能提升单细胞分类的实用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向无标记单细胞分类的深度学习架构选择仍是开放问题，显微基准研究对CNN与Transformer的结论相互矛盾，且已有结论可能被预训练差异、数据泄漏和不一致的训练评估协议所混淆，因此需要一个严格控制变量的基准来厘清各因素的真实贡献。

Method: 在LIVECell相差显微数据上构建对照基准：采用源图像不相交的train/validation/test划分以防止父图像泄漏，对EfficientNet、ViT与EVA-02在优化、增强和评估协议上保持一致；据此分离架构、预训练、微调、tokenisation与蒸馏的影响，并进一步测试层级学习率衰减和知识蒸馏（紧凑EfficientNet-B0学生模型对教师委员会蒸馏）。

Result: 预训练提升macro-F1约3-4个百分点，最小预训练模型优于最强从头训练模型；最佳预训练CNN与Transformer差距小于0.5个百分点。ViT-S/8优于ViT-S/16，并以四分之一参数量匹配四倍大的ViT-B/16；EVA-02微调配方中的层级学习率衰减反而损害性能；蒸馏得到的EfficientNet-B0学生模型超越所有单独训练的骨干网络，包括EfficientNet-B5和EVA-02教师。

Conclusion: 生物医学架构基准必须严格控制预训练与评估条件才能得到可解释结论；对实用的单细胞分类而言，知识蒸馏可能是比单纯选择架构更有效的途径。

Abstract: Choosing a deep learning architecture for label-free single-cell classification remains an open question, with microscopy benchmarks reporting conflicting conclusions about CNNs versus transformers. We present a controlled benchmark on LIVECell phase-contrast microscopy data using source-image-disjoint train/validation/test splits to prevent parent-image leakage and matched optimisation, augmentation, and evaluation protocols across EfficientNet, Vision Transformer (ViT), and EVA-02 models. This allows the effects of architecture, pretraining, fine-tuning, tokenisation, and distillation to be disentangled. We find that the previously reported CNN advantage is largely explained by pretraining rather than architecture: the smallest pretrained model outperforms the strongest model trained from scratch despite far fewer parameters. Pretraining improves macro-F1 by 3-4 points, while the gap between the best pretrained CNN and transformer is below 0.5 points. Architectural choices nevertheless matter: ViT-S/8 outperforms ViT-S/16 and matches the four-times-larger ViT-B/16 at a quarter of the parameters, showing that finer tokenisation benefits small cell crops. Conversely, layer-wise learning-rate decay, central to the EVA-02 fine-tuning recipe, degrades performance, highlighting that transfer heuristics from natural-image recognition may not generalise to microscopy. Finally, knowledge distillation substantially improves the deployment frontier: compact EfficientNet-B0 students distilled from teacher councils outperform every individually trained backbone, including the EfficientNet-B5 and EVA-02 teachers. Overall, our results show that rigorous control of pretraining and evaluation is essential for interpreting biomedical architecture benchmarks, while distillation may be a more effective route to practical single-cell classification than architecture choice alone.

</details>


### [37] [From Pixels to Hierarchical Sequences: Quadtree Mask Encoding for Vision-Language Binary Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09876)
*Xiao An,Ruikang Zhang,Chen Zhong,Xuli Shen,Jiaxing Sun,Jiang Wu,Wei He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出QUAKE-CD框架，将遥感密集变化检测重构为语法可验证的结构化生成任务，用四叉树token序列表示变化掩码，并配套QUAKE-CoT数据与渐进式课程+语法门控双奖励RL训练，取得78.31%累计F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在遥感变化检测中多局限于变化描述，少数能输出像素级掩码的方法依赖外部解码器或扁平的"文本即掩码"序列化方式，对细小、碎片化变化效果不佳。

Method: 将二值变化掩码表示为语法约束的四叉树token序列，使掩码紧凑、语法可校验、可在自回归生成空间内确定性解码；构建QUAKE-CoT数据集，将序列与基于视觉证据的思维链结合；采用渐进式课程学习加语法门控双奖励强化学习，联合优化文本推理与空间密集预测。

Result: 在QUAKE-CoT上达到78.31%累计F1，优于基于解码器和扁平文本即掩码的VLM方法，并生成更忠实的双时相推理。

Conclusion: 将密集变化预测重构为语法可验证的结构化生成是可行且有效的，四叉树序列化与RL联合优化能提升掩码精度与推理忠实度。

Abstract: Dense change detection in remote sensing requires vision-language models (VLMs) to compare bi-temporal images and generate accurate pixel-level masks. Existing VLMs are largely confined to change captioning outputs, and the few that produce pixel-level masks still rely on external decoders or flat text-as-mask serialization, which are less effective for small and fragmented changes. We introduce QUAKE-CD, a framework that recasts dense change prediction as syntax-verifiable structured generation. QUAKE-CD represents binary change masks as grammar-constrained quadtree token sequences, making the masks compact, syntactically checkable, and deterministically decodable within an autoregressive generation space. We further construct QUAKE-CoT, which pairs these sequences with chain-of-thought traces grounded in visual evidence, and jointly optimizes textual reasoning and spatial dense prediction through a progressive curriculum followed by grammar-gated dual-reward RL. On QUAKE-CoT, QUAKE-CD achieves 78.31% accumulated F1, outperforming decoder-based and flat text-as-mask VLMs while producing more faithful bi-temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [38] [CLFTv2: Efficient Camera-LiDAR Fusion for Semantic Segmentation via Hierarchical Feature Pyramids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09881)
*Toomas Tahves,Mauro Bellone,Raivo Sell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLFTv2：一种面向自动驾驶的分层相机-激光雷达融合语义分割框架，用Swin多尺度编码器与轻量FPN残差解码器替代全局ViT注意力和查询式解码器，在三个数据集上提升弱势道路使用者召回率，并具备更高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶语义分割需在严重类别不平衡下可靠检测弱势道路使用者（VRU）。既有基于全局ViT注意力与查询匹配解码器的融合方案计算开销大，需寻求更高效、可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 提出CLFTv2分层相机-LiDAR融合框架：以Swin式多尺度编码器替换全局ViT注意力，配轻量FPN式残差解码器；在2D透视域操作，通过移位窗口注意力与逐尺度残差融合整合多尺度几何线索，规避查询匹配解码器的算力开销。并设模态隔离研究分析ViT全局感受野的融合增益条件。

Result: 在三个驾驶数据集上一致提升VRU召回率。ZOD上CLFTv2-Large达53.5% mIoU，行人IoU由35.5%提升至44.9%；Waymo上达61.7% mIoU。模态隔离研究表明ViT全局感受野仅在密集LiDAR回波下带来更强融合增益。相比Swin版Mask2Former，GFLOPs减少1.4倍、吞吐量提高2.2倍，总体精度相当。

Conclusion: 分层局部注意力融合是相比全局注意力与查询式解码器更高效、可扩展的方案，适用于智能交通系统中实时车载感知。源代码已公开。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation for autonomous driving requires reliable detection of vulnerable road users (VRUs) despite heavy class imbalance. We introduce CLFTv2, a hierarchical camera-LiDAR fusion framework replacing global ViT attention with a Swin-based multi-scale encoder and a lightweight FPN-style residual decoder. Operating in the 2D perspective domain, CLFTv2 integrates multi-scale geometric cues through shifted-window attention and per-scale residual fusion, avoiding the computational overhead of query-matching decoders. Across three driving datasets, CLFTv2 consistently improves VRU recall. On ZOD, CLFTv2-Large achieves 53.5\% mIoU, improving pedestrian IoU from 35.5\% to 44.9\% over the prior CLFT model. On Waymo, CLFTv2 reaches 61.7\% mIoU. Additionally, a modality-isolation study suggests ViT's global receptive field yields stronger fusion gains only under dense LiDAR returns. Compared to a Swin-based Mask2Former adaptation, CLFTv2 requires 1.4$\times$ fewer GFLOPs and delivers 2.2$\times$ higher throughput, while achieving comparable overall accuracy. These results demonstrate that hierarchical local-attention fusion offers an efficient, scalable alternative to global-attention and query-based decoders for real-time on-vehicle perception in intelligent transportation systems. Source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [39] [Albedo Estimation via Latent Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09884)
*Carme Corbi,David Serrano-Lozano,Javier Vazquez-Corral,Maria Vanrell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于潜在桥匹配（LBM）的反照率估计框架，通过像素重建损失保证物理一致性、利用LBM低推理成本、并以着色条件提升泛化性，扩展版进一步让着色估计器以预测反照率为条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成模型的本征图像分解（IID）面临三大瓶颈：物理一致性不足、推理计算开销高、跨数据集泛化能力有限。作者希望验证潜在桥匹配能否有效缓解这些问题，尤其是反照率估计任务。

Method: 提出一种新的LBM架构：以像素重建损失作为约束以强化物理一致性；依托LBM本身的低开销推理实现高效推断；在反照率估计中引入着色条件以增强泛化。扩展版本进一步对反照率—着色进行双向条件建模，即用预测反照率来条件化着色估计器。

Result: 在五个真实与合成数据集上将最优模型与当前最先进的IID方法进行基准对比，验证了所提方法在重建保真度与泛化性上的改进；着色估计器的反照率条件化进一步提升了重建保真度。

Conclusion: 潜在桥匹配为IID中的反照率估计提供了兼顾物理一致性、推理效率与泛化性的有效路径，反照率与着色之间的循环条件建模可带来额外收益。

Abstract: Recent advances in Intrinsic Image Decomposition (IID) have increasingly relied on generative models. However, progress remains limited by three key challenges: (a) insufficient physical consistency, (b) high computational cost at inference time, and (c) limited generalization capabilities. In this work, we show that latent bridge matching (LBM) effectively addresses these limitations for albedo estimation. We introduce a novel LBM-based architecture that enforces physical consistency through a pixel reconstruction loss, benefits from the inherent efficiency of LBM low-cost inference, and improves generalization across diverse datasets by incorporating a shading conditioning. In this extended version, we additionally show that conditioning the shading estimator itself on the predicted albedo further improves reconstruction fidelity, and we benchmark our best model against stateof-the-art IID methods across five real and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [40] [StreetDiff: Multi-view Street Scenes Generation via Cross-view Consistent Multi-view Stable Diffusion with Structure Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09890)
*Qi Zhang,Yanyifan Wang,Weiyuan Zhang,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreetDiff：通过全景-透视协同与球面投影注意力约束，显式强制跨视图对齐，解决复杂城市场景多视图扩散生成的跨视图一致性难题，并构建 Street360 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图扩散模型在几何先验强、语义稀疏的场景（如室内房间、田野、庭院等简单室外环境）表现良好，但在相机旋转条件下难以维持跨视图一致性，尤其在结构复杂的城市场景中。由于缺乏对跨视图球面对应关系的显式建模，现有方法易出现物体重复、结构畸变与布局不一致等问题。

Method: 提出 StreetDiff 多视图扩散框架，在去噪过程中显式强制跨视图对齐。其核心包括：(1) Panorama-Perspective Synergy 设计，将全局布局推理与局部细节合成解耦；(2) Panorama Alignment Module（PAM），基于球面投影建立跨视图注意力约束。该方法在不修改扩散骨干网络的条件下注入结构化对齐约束。此外构建 Street360，一个大规模 HDR 多视图城市全景数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，与先前的多视图扩散生成方法相比，StreetDiff 在结构一致性与视觉保真度上均有显著提升，在具有挑战性的城市街景生成任务中实现了稳健的跨视图连贯性。

Conclusion: 通过显式球面投影对齐约束与全景-透视协同设计，可在不改变扩散骨干的前提下有效提升复杂城市场景多视图生成的结构一致性，同时所构建的 Street360 数据集为该任务提供了数据支撑。

Abstract: Multi-view diffusion models have shown strong performance in scenes with strong geometric priors and sparse semantics, such as indoor rooms or simple outdoor environments (e.g., fields, courtyards). However, they often fail to maintain cross-view consistency under camera rotation, especially in structurally complex urban environments. Without explicit modeling of spherical correspondence across views, existing approaches tend to produce object duplication, structural distortion, and layout inconsistency. To address this limitation, we propose StreetDiff, a multi-view diffusion framework that explicitly enforces cross-view alignment during denoising. StreetDiff introduces a Panorama--Perspective Synergy design to decouple global layout reasoning from local detail synthesis, and incorporates a Panorama Alignment Module (PAM) that establishes spherical-projection-based attention constraints across views. By injecting structured alignment constraints without modifying the diffusion backbone, our framework achieves robust cross-view coherence in challenging urban street scene generation tasks. In addition, we construct Street360, a large-scale HDR multi-view urban panorama dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StreetDiff significantly improves structural consistency and visual fidelity compared to prior multi-view diffusion generation methods.

</details>


### [41] [Can We Trust Video Hallucination Detectors? VidHalLoc for Evaluating the Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09895)
*Xinyu Chen,Adnan Mahmood,Mark Dras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VidHalLoc 基准与 VideoHALO 多智能体构建流程，统一评测视频幻觉检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频-语言模型与视频智能体会产生与时空证据相冲突的幻觉；现有基准主要评测模型幻觉本身，且各检测机制异构，导致检测器可靠性难以横向比较。

Method: 构建 VidHalLoc 基准，采用统一诊断评测协议，包含 2,000 个对抗性幻觉样本，覆盖视频问答与视频描述两类任务，涉及本体（Ontology）与动态（Dynamic）幻觉类别；并提出 VideoHALO——一种基于 Harness Engineering 的多智能体工作流，将数据构建分解为四个可执行阶段，由记忆系统与通信协议支撑。

Result: 对十五种方法的评测显示，四种专用检测器的 Overall 准确率最高仅为 34.63%，表明其在各类视频幻觉类型上的可靠性有限。

Conclusion: VidHalLoc 提供了统一的视频幻觉检测评测协议，并揭示现有检测器可靠性不足，为后续研究提供基准与数据集支持。

Abstract: Video-language models and video agents can produce hallucinations that conflict with spatiotemporal evidence. Existing benchmarks mainly evaluate model hallucinations, and heterogeneous mechanisms make detector reliability difficult to compare. We introduce VidHalLoc, a benchmark that evaluates hallucination detection methods under a unified diagnostic evaluation protocol using 2,000 adversarial hallucination samples across Video Question Answering and Video Captioning tasks, spanning Ontology and Dynamic hallucination categories. To construct VidHalLoc efficiently, we introduce VideoHALO, a Harness Engineering-informed multi-agent workflow that decomposes data construction into four executable stages supported by a memory system and a communication protocol. Evaluation of fifteen methods reveals that the four dedicated detectors peak at an Overall accuracy of only 34.63%, indicating limited reliability across video hallucination types [Dataset Repository: https://huggingface.co/datasets/wesfggfd/VidHalLoc].

</details>


### [42] [Interpreting Object-Dependent Concept Brittleness in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09909)
*Yifan Yuan,Xiangyu Liu,Hongming Shan,Yu Han,Yu Jiang,Hao Tan,Junping Zhang,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 发现文生图扩散模型存在"对象依赖的概念脆弱性"失效模式,提出基于稀疏自编码器(SAE)去噪轨迹分析的可解释框架,并通过推理时特征插值实现轻量修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型虽具备较强的提示词跟随能力,但存在一类此前未被充分探索的失效模式:仅对象不同的少量提示词在同一生成设置下,始终无法实现相同目标概念。这类问题呈现系统性内部盲点特征,而非随机采样噪声。作者希望审计并最小化地修正此类失败。

Method: 提出面向可解释性的框架。核心思想是在逐步(step-wise)稀疏自编码器(SAE)空间中分析去噪轨迹,该空间中抽象的风格与属性概念比原始去噪表示更可分离。借此对比成功与失败生成,定位证据缺失、减弱或时间延迟的概念维度,并从可靠的类内一致样本构建类级概念原型。基于审计结果,提出轻量推理时修正策略,将去噪特征向SAE空间中对应原型插值。该干预并非任务特定重训练方法,而是对诊断出的概念缺陷的验证。

Result: 在多个扩散主干上对风格与属性失败案例进行评估,在概念一致性、文本保真度和修复成功率上均有显著提升。进一步分析表明:更深的去噪表示提供更清晰的概念结构,而早期阶段的干预提供最强的修正杠杆效应。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示并形式化了对象依赖的概念脆弱性,证明在SAE去噪空间中定位概念缺陷并进行原型插值可有效修复失败,同时为概念结构随去噪深度变化及干预时机选择提供了可解释性洞察。

Abstract: Although text-to-image diffusion models generally exhibit strong prompt-following ability, we identify a persistent and previously underexplored failure pattern in which a small subset of prompts differing only in the object consistently fails to realize the same target concept under identical generation settings. We term this phenomenon object-dependent concept brittleness. Such cases suggest systematic internal blind spots rather than random sampling noise. In this paper, we present an interpretability-oriented framework to audit and minimally correct these failures. Our key idea is to analyze denoising trajectories in a step-wise sparse autoencoder (SAE) space, where abstract style and attribute concepts become more separable than in the raw denoising representation. This sparse space enables us to compare successful and failed generations, identify concept dimensions whose evidence is missing, weakened, or temporally delayed, and construct class-level concept prototypes from reliable class-consistent samples. Based on this audit process, we introduce a lightweight inference-time correction strategy that interpolates denoising features toward the corresponding prototype in SAE space. Rather than serving as a task-specific retraining method, this intervention acts as a validation of the diagnosed concept deficiency. We evaluate the proposed framework on style and attribute failure cases across multiple diffusion backbones, with significant improvements in concept consistency, text fidelity, and repair success. Further analyses show that deeper denoising representations provide clearer concept structure, while early-stage intervention offers the strongest correction leverage. Code is available at https://github.com/Metecade/Object-Dependent-Concept-Brittleness.

</details>


### [43] [Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations via Class-Wise Adaptive Modality Fusion and Affective Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09924)
*Oriol Marín,Roger Marí,Gloria Haro,Rafael Redondo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在Self-Distillation Transformer基础上，通过引入几何视觉表征、类别自适应模态融合与效价-唤醒先验，提升多模态对话情感识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话情感识别（ERC）需整合文本、音频、视觉等异构线索，并同时建模对话上下文与情感动态变化，现有方法在视觉表征、模态融合权重分配及情感转移建模上仍有改进空间。

Method: 扩展Self-Distillation Transformer架构：1）采用外观+几何的视觉联合表征；2）提出类别自适应模态融合（class-wise adaptive modality fusion）；3）引入效价-唤醒（valence-arousal）先验以刻画情感转移。在MELD与IEMOCAP数据集上评估。

Result: 相比仅用外观特征，几何增强视觉表征使加权F1在MELD和IEMOCAP上分别提升0.27与4.36个百分点；类别自适应融合相较原始softmax门控再提升0.17与0.25个百分点；效价-唤醒先验在情感发生转移的语句上分别带来0.30与0.74的准确率提升，同时在情感稳定语句上性能保持不变。

Conclusion: 结构化面部线索、情感依赖的模态加权与情感几何信息对多模态ERC具有互补增益，三者结合可有效提升识别性能。

Abstract: Emotion Recognition in Conversations (ERC) requires integrating heterogeneous textual, audio, and visual cues while accounting for conversational context and emotional dynamics. We extend the Self-Distillation Transformer architecture for ERC with appearance+geometry visual representations, class-wise adaptive modality fusion, and a valence-arousal prior for affective transitions. On the MELD and IEMOCAP datasets, geometry-enhanced visual representations improve weighted F1 by 0.27 and 4.36 points over appearance-only features, respectively, while class-wise adaptive fusion provides further gains of 0.17 and 0.25 points over the original softmax gate. The valence-arousal prior yields targeted improvements of 0.30 and 0.74 accuracy points on emotionally shifted utterances while preserving performance on stable turns. These results indicate that structured facial cues, emotion-dependent modality weighting, and affective geometry provide complementary benefits for multimodal ERC.

</details>


### [44] [Vague2Detect: Handling Ambiguous Prompts in Knowledge-Based Open-World Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09949)
*Ibrohimjon Muminov,Jihie Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Vague2Detect 混合流水线，用微调 Sentence-BERT 从结构化家居知识库检索候选，再由 YOLO-World 验证图像中是否存在，并用 GPT-3.5-turbo 动态扩展知识库，将模糊提示成功率从 32% 提升至 61%，加 GPT 回退后达 85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实检测器常需理解功能性或模糊提示，但 YOLO 等传统模型仅支持固定类别列表；即便 YOLO-World 等开放词汇模型也常将模糊语言与目标物体错误对齐。作者在前期工作（Commonsense-Guided Open-World Object Detection Using LLMs and Visual-Semantic Matching）基础上，针对 YOLO-World 在任务驱动查询定位上的不足展开研究。

Method: 提出 Vague2Detect 混合流水线：使用微调的 Sentence-BERT 从结构化家居知识库（KB）中检索候选物体描述，再由 YOLO-World 在图像中验证其是否存在。对于知识库未覆盖的提示，调用大语言模型 GPT-3.5-turbo 生成候选描述，动态扩展知识库以覆盖新概念。

Result: 在家居场景基准（自建图像与 Open Images V7 子集）上，单独使用 YOLO-World 的模糊提示成功率（VPSR，即把模糊查询映射到正确检测的能力）仅为 32%；Vague2Detect 在高精度下将 VPSR 提升至 61%，加入 GPT 回退机制后最高可达 85%。

Conclusion: 将语义检索、结构化知识库与开放词汇检测器结合，并辅以 LLM 动态扩展知识库，可显著缓解开放词汇检测器对模糊、任务驱动提示的语义对齐问题，大幅提升模糊提示下的检测成功率。

Abstract: Real-world detectors must often interpret functional or ambiguous prompts, yet conventional models such as YOLO remain restricted to fixed class lists. Even open-vocabulary models like YOLO-World frequently misalign vague language with the intended objects. Building on our prior work Commonsense-Guided Open-World Object Detection Using LLMs and Visual-Semantic Matching, we address YOLO-World's limitations in grounding task-driven queries. We propose Vague2Detect, a hybrid pipeline in which a fine-tuned Sentence-BERT retrieves candidates from a structured household Knowledge Base (KB), and YOLO-World verifies their presence in the image. For prompts outside the KB, a large language model (GPT-3.5-turbo) generates candidate descriptions, dynamically expanding the KB to cover novel concepts. On a benchmark of household scenes using custom images and an Open Images V7 subset, YOLO-World alone achieves only 32% Vague Prompt Success Rate (VPSR), the ability to map ambiguous queries to correct detections. In contrast, Vague2Detect improves performance to 61% VPSR with high precision, and up to 85% when augmented with GPT fallback.

</details>


### [45] [Putting Captions to the Test: Evaluating Video Caption Quality through Multiple-Choice Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09973)
*Zizhen Wang,Bo Feng,Zhengfeng Lai,Shiyu Li,Yang Lu,Meng Cao,Ping Huang,Xiaoming Simon Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CapQuiz——一个无需参考的视频描述评估基准，通过"信息保真度"重新定义描述质量，用人工校验的细粒度多选题衡量描述对显著视觉信息的覆盖与事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频描述评估指标主要依赖与参考文本的匹配，受"一对多"特性困扰：高质量描述常因词汇不匹配或视觉焦点合理转移而被误判；同时评估维度单一，缺乏细粒度分析。

Method: 以信息保真度重新定义描述质量（最大化显著视觉信息覆盖并保证严格事实性），构建无需参考的基准 CapQuiz：基于视频生成人工校验的细粒度多选题，以描述回答这些问题的效用作为评价依据；并建立涵盖描述性与推理性两类、共10种问题类型的层次化分类体系，覆盖24个视频领域。

Result: 大量实验表明，CapQuiz 与人类判断的相关性显著优于现有指标，并能提供关于模型性能的可解释洞察。

Conclusion: CapQuiz 为视频描述评估提供了更贴近人类判断、更具可解释性的无需参考范式，可替代或补充传统基于参考的匹配式指标。

Abstract: Evaluating video captioning remains a critical challenge for Visual Large Language Models (VLLMs). Existing metrics primarily rely on matching generated text against ground-truth references. This paradigm suffers from the ``one-to-many'' nature of video description, where high-quality captions are often penalized for lexical mismatches or valid shifts in visual focus. Furthermore, such assessments are typically one-dimensional, failing to provide a fine-grained analysis of caption quality. To address this, we redefine caption quality through the lens of information fidelity: A caption must maximize the coverage of salient visual information while ensuring strict factuality. We introduce CapQuiz, a novel reference-free benchmark that assesses captions based on their utility in answering human-verified, fine-grained, multiple-choice questions derived from the video. CapQuiz features a hierarchical taxonomy of 10 question types (spanning Descriptive and Inferential categories) across 24 diverse video domains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CapQuiz correlates significantly better with human judgments than existing metrics and offers interpretable insights into model performance.

</details>


### [46] [From Few-Shot Segmentation to Clinician-in-the-Loop Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10001)
*Yazhou Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将小样本医学图像分割（FSMIS）重构为"临床医生—模型"序贯决策问题：在固定支持集预算K与独立交互预算B下，系统对每一步分割结果选择接受、请求反馈或转交专家全审，并以响应条件化的净期望信息价值选取查询。框架要求反馈经溯源、一致性与安全门控后方可用于有界自适应，并将"分布非典型性"与"预测的临床失败"区分开。作者综合了小样本/跨域分割向交互式与选择性自适应的演进，指出整合缺口，提出四个可证伪的研究方向与外部域校准、质量—人力权衡、阅片者研究及前瞻性工作流评估的评测体系。核心主张：交互本身不解决域偏移，稀缺专家注意力应仅在预期能降低临床相关风险时才被分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准FSMIS在推理前就固定了定义任务的支持证据，这一假设在查询病例出现采集偏移、非典型病理、边界模糊或图像质量差时非常脆弱。原型学习、跨域匹配、交互式分割、不确定性估计、测试时自适应和可提示基础模型各自解决了部分问题，但从未在一个统一的"专家注意力—临床风险"模型下被联合评估，因此缺乏可比性与整合视角。本文旨在填补这一评估与整合缺口。

Method: 这是一篇Perspective（观点/立场）论文，而非提出新算法。其方法为概念性重构与综合：(1) 将FSMIS形式化为序贯临床医生—模型决策问题，设定静态支持预算K与独立交互预算B；(2) 每步动作空间为接受当前分割、请求反馈、或转交专家全审，查询按响应条件化的净期望信息价值（net EVOI）选择，且查询在位置与模态上可变化；(3) 临床反馈仅在预设的溯源、一致性、安全门控通过后进入有界自适应，并把医生响应建模为有信息但可错的观测；(4) 明确区分分布非典型性与预测的临床失败；(5) 通过文献综合刻画从少样本/跨域分割到交互式与选择性自适应的转变，界定整合缺口，并给出四个含可证伪假设的研究方向与对应评测设计。

Result: 作为观点性文章，未报告实验数值结果。其"结果"为分析性产出：识别出整合缺口这一核心问题；给出统一决策论框架与动作空间/预算分离的设计；提出四类可证伪假设的研究方向；并界定评测维度——外部域校准、质量—人力权衡、阅片者研究、前瞻性工作流评估。

Conclusion: 核心结论具有辨析性：交互本身并不能消解域偏移。真正的设计原则是选择性求助——仅当预期能降低临床相关风险时才动用稀缺的专家注意力。该框架将FSMIS的评价标准从纯分割精度转向风险敏感的注意力分配与工作流中的临床效用，为后续研究提供了可操作、可证伪的议程。

Abstract: Few-shot medical image segmentation (FSMIS) seeks to delineate unseen structures from a small support set, but its standard formulation fixes task-defining evidence before inference. This assumption is fragile when query cases exhibit acquisition shift, atypical pathology, ambiguous boundaries, or poor image quality. Prototype learning, cross-domain matching, interactive segmentation, uncertainty estimation, test-time adaptation, and promptable foundation models address parts of this problem, yet have not been jointly evaluated under a common model of expert attention and clinical risk. This Perspective reframes FSMIS as a sequential clinician-model decision problem with a static support budget $K$ and a distinct interaction budget $B$. At each step, a system accepts the current segmentation, requests feedback, or defers to full expert review. Queries vary in location and modality and are selected by response-conditioned net expected value of information; clinician-provided feedback informs bounded adaptation only after prespecified provenance, consistency, and safety gates. The framework separates distributional atypicality from predicted clinical failure and treats clinician responses as informative but fallible observations. We synthesize the transition from few-shot and cross-domain segmentation to interactive and selective adaptation, delineate the integration gap, and define four research directions with falsifiable hypotheses. Evaluation spans external-domain calibration, quality-effort trade-offs, reader studies, and prospective workflow assessment. The central claim is not that interaction alone resolves domain shift, but that scarce expert attention should be allocated only when it is expected to reduce clinically relevant risk.

</details>


### [47] [What Makes Adversarial Examples Transfer Across Deepfake Detectors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10002)
*Rafael M. Mamede,Pedro C. Neto,Ana F. Sequeira*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对60个深度伪造检测器进行受控迁移攻击评测，揭示源-目标兼容性结构并指出源平均会低估目标脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移式黑盒攻击对深度伪造检测器构成威胁，但源模型与目标模型的兼容性如何影响攻击成功率尚不明确；既有研究检测器池有限，且难以解耦架构与训练因素。

Method: 在60个检测器（6种骨干、2种预训练范式、5种训练数据配置）上开展受控迁移性评测，采用AutoAttack（AA）与带期望变换的Carlini-Wagner攻击（CW-EOT），并做配对比较分析源-目标兼容性。

Result: 源与目标共享完全相同的骨干、架构族、预训练范式或训练数据时迁移显著更高；兼容性结构依攻击方式而异：AA下完全骨干匹配影响最大，CW-EOT下共享预训练与训练数据影响最大。跨非目标源平均时，平均ASR在AA下为7.21%，CW-EOT下为19.52%；而排除骨干与训练数据完全匹配后，结合两种攻击的多源oracle平均ASR达64.48%。

Conclusion: 源-目标兼容性与源模型选择是可信迁移式黑盒鲁棒性评测的核心维度，单一源平均会显著低估目标脆弱性；并公开24万张对抗扰动图像、完整配对迁移结果、检测器配置与评测代码。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors remain vulnerable to transfer-based black-box attacks, in which adversarial examples are generated on a source surrogate model and transferred to a target model, unknown to the attacker. Yet how source--target compatibility shapes attack success remains poorly understood. Prior studies evaluate limited detector pools and rarely disentangle architectural from training factors. We conduct a controlled evaluation of adversarial transferability across 60 detectors spanning six backbones, two pretraining regimes, and five training-data configurations, using two attack procedures: AutoAttack (AA) and the Carlini--Wagner attack with Expectation over Transformation (CW--EOT). Matched comparisons reveal significantly higher transfer when source and target share an exact backbone, architecture family, pretraining regime, or training data. This compatibility structure is attack-dependent: exact backbone compatibility has the largest effect under AA, whereas shared pretraining and training data have the largest effects under CW--EOT. When transfer is averaged across non-target sources, mean attack success rate (ASR) is $7.21\%$ under AA and $19.52\%$ under CW--EOT. By contrast, a multi-source oracle combining both attacks attains a \(64.48\%\) mean ASR after excluding exact backbone and training-data matches, showing that source averaging can substantially understate target vulnerability. We release 240,000 adversarially perturbed images, complete pairwise transfer results, detector configurations, and evaluation code. These findings establish source--target compatibility and source-model selection as central dimensions of credible transfer-based black-box robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond Similarity: Foundation Models as an Efficient Backbone for Training-Free Composed Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10008)
*Dmitry Demidov,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer,Omkar Thawakar,Abdelrahman Mohamed Shaker,Rao Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVRAGE，一个免训练的组合视频检索框架，通过让冻结基础模型承担互补角色并按查询难度自适应分配推理深度，在Dense-WebVid-CoVR和CoVR-R上分别达到89.55与93.43的R@1，刷新免训练方法的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合视频检索（CoVR）需要在视频库中检索出对源片段进行自然语言修改后的目标视频。在库规模下存在根本性矛盾：紧凑嵌入虽高效可复用，却会漏掉瞬态动作、状态变化和细微约束等细粒度视频推理所需信息；而统一使用大型多模态模型又会牺牲可扩展性。因此需要在效率与细粒度推理之间寻求更优的平衡。

Method: CoVRAGE让冻结的基础模型占据互补角色，并将推理深度与查询难度自适应匹配，实现免训练的组合视频检索。具体流程为：先用组合查询嵌入检索可复用的纯视频库表示；对不确定查询进行有界重排与候选扩展；对模糊编辑触发目标描述生成；仅让领先的候选进入多模态验证。为支撑各阶段角色，帧选择、空间分辨率和时间线索按阶段自适应调整。

Result: 在完整目标库评测中，该方法在免训练方案中达到最优性能：Dense-WebVid-CoVR上R@1为89.55，CoVR-R上为93.43，相较最接近的对比方法分别有超过+35%和+25%的绝对提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过自适应地编排基础模型能力，可以在无需任务特定训练的前提下，将可扩展检索与细粒度推理结合起来。源码与相关指南已发布于 https://github.com/demidovd98/CoVRAGE。

Abstract: Composed video retrieval (CoVR) searches a gallery for the target video that realizes a natural-language modification of a source clip. However, at gallery scale, this creates a fundamental tension: compact embeddings enable efficient, reusable search but can miss the transient actions, state changes, and subtle constraints that demand fine-grained video reasoning, whereas applying large multimodal models uniformly sacrifices scalability. To address these limitations, we propose that frozen foundation models should instead occupy complementary roles, with inference depth adapted to query difficulty. Based on this premise, we introduce \methodname{}, a framework for training-free \methodexpansion{}. Specifically, a composed-query embedding first searches reusable video-only gallery representations; uncertain queries undergo bounded reranking and candidate expansion; ambiguous edits trigger target-description generation; and only close leading candidates reach multimodal verification. To support these roles, frame selection, spatial resolution, and time cues are adapted to each stage. Across complete target-gallery evaluations, our method reaches state-of-the-art performance among training-free approaches, with 89.55 and 93.43 R@1 on Dense-WebVid-CoVR and CoVR-R, respectively (with more than +35\% and +25\% absolute margins to the closest counterpart). These results show that adaptively orchestrating foundation-model capabilities can combine scalable retrieval with fine-grained reasoning without task-specific training. The source code and all relevant guidelines are available on https://github.com/demidovd98/CoVRAGE.

</details>


### [49] [Elastoformer: Enabling Dynamic Adaptivity via Elastic Model Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10018)
*Sudaksh Kalra,Dolly Sapra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Elastoformer框架，将常规神经网络转化为可在运行时弹性推理的Elastic NN，用单一模块化模型替代多模型方案，在视觉Transformer和CNN上实现最高85% FLOPs、50%延迟和76%内存开销的降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EdgeAI视觉应用面临高度动态的运行条件（延迟、功耗、内存约束波动），而DNN采用固定计算执行流，缺乏适应性，导致边缘场景下性能低效次优，因此需要运行时动态可伸缩的架构。

Method: Elastoformer将常规神经网络转化为Elastic NN，支持实时弹性推理；区别于需为不同工况维护多个独立模型的bag-of-models方案，它提供单一模块化解决方案，可在运行时于多种工作模式间动态切换，适应边缘设备变化的计算预算，且无需管理多个模型的额外开销。

Result: 计算FLOPs最多降低85%，延迟降低50%，内存开销降低76%；并在Vision Transformer与CNN上验证了框架的架构无关性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Elastoformer以单一模块化弹性网络实现了对动态计算预算的高效适应，显著降低计算、延迟与内存开销，且具备架构无关的通用性，适用于边缘视觉推理场景。

Abstract: EdgeAI systems are increasingly employing computer vision applications to enable intelligent, on-device decision-making in real-time. However, these deployments face highly dynamic operational conditions, with fluctuating constraints on latency, power availability, and memory resources. Deep Neural Networks (DNN), which follow fixed computational execution flows, lack the flexibility to adapt to such variability, resulting in inefficient and suboptimal performance in edge scenarios. This underscores the need for architectures that are not only efficient but also dynamically scalable at runtime. In this paper, we propose Elastoformer: A framework that transforms conventional neural networks (NN) into Elastic NN capable of real-time elastic inference. Unlike the conventional bag-of-models approach, which requires maintaining multiple independent models for different operating conditions, Elastoformer offers a single, modular solution that dynamically switches between multiple modes of operation at runtime, adapting efficiently to the changing computational budgets of edge devices without the overhead of managing separate models. Experiments reveal that our framework achieves up to 85% reduction in computation FLOPs, 50% reduction in latency and 76% reduction in memory overhead, while showcasing the architecture agnostic nature of the framework across both Vision Transformers and CNNs. Our code is available at https://github.com/sudaksh14/Elastoformer.

</details>


### [50] [LinearMask-GS: Stable-Mask Importance Pruning for Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10095)
*Donghun Ryu,Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LinearMask-GS，用线性增量激活替代Gumbel-Sigmoid，解决LP-3DGS掩码训练中分布过早双峰化的问题，在保持或提升渲染质量的同时实现更高压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting通过自适应致密化产生数百万个基元，导致显著存储开销。学习掩码剪枝方法（如LP-3DGS）通过为每个高斯分配可学习掩码来识别并剪除冗余基元，但其Gumbel-Sigmoid激活的陡峭斜率会在较短的掩码训练窗口内将掩码值推向两极，此时重要性排序尚未稳定，导致掩码分布急剧双峰化，排序无法被可靠恢复。

Method: LinearMask-GS将Gumbel-Sigmoid替换为线性增量激活，使掩码值在掩码训练全程保持在中置信度区间，从而产生稳定、单峰的掩码分布，且其排序与重要性保持一致。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360数据集上，相比3DGS和LP-3DGS分别实现3.6倍和1.6倍的高斯数量压缩，同时保持或提升渲染质量。户外场景实现1.6倍压缩（从2.18M降至1.36M），PSNR提升+0.38 dB、SSIM提升+0.025、LPIPS降低-0.029。

Conclusion: LinearMask-GS通过改进掩码激活函数，克服了学习掩码剪枝范式中的排序不稳定问题，在压缩效率与渲染质量上均优于3DGS和LP-3DGS，是一种有效的高斯压缩方法。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis but produces millions of primitives through adaptive densification, leading to significant storage overhead. Learned-mask pruning methods such as LP-3DGS address this by assigning each Gaussian a learnable mask to identify and prune redundant primitives. However, we identify a limitation of this paradigm: the steep slope of the Gumbel-Sigmoid activation drives mask values to the extremes within the short mask-training window, before the importance ranking has stabilized, producing a sharply bimodal distribution from which that ranking can no longer be reliably recovered. We propose LinearMask-GS, which replaces Gumbel-Sigmoid with a linear increment activation that keeps mask values in a mid-confidence regime throughout mask training, producing a stable, unimodal mask distribution whose ranking tracks importance. On Mip-NeRF 360, our method achieves 3.6x and 1.6x Gaussian reductions over 3DGS and LP-3DGS, respectively, while maintaining or improving rendering quality. For outdoor scenes, it yields a 1.6x reduction (from 2.18M to 1.36M) with notable gains in PSNR (+0.38 dB), SSIM (+0.025), and LPIPS (-0.029).

</details>


### [51] [SA-Profile: Automated Sulcus Angle Profiling from Super-Resolution MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10125)
*Michael Wehrli,Leo Widmer,Edwin Li,Noel Fiechter,Lorenzo Pettinari,Sidaty El Hadramy,Carol C. Hasler,Philippe C. Cattin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于隐式神经表示超分辨重建与双U-Net地标检测的自动框架，实现股骨滑车沟角（SA）沿滑车区域的连续剖面刻画，免于依赖单层轴位手动测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑车发育不良（TD）与前膝痛及髌骨不稳相关，滑车沟角（SA）是评估滑车形态的关键指标，但传统测量仅基于单层轴位MR图像，缺乏明确的选层规范，导致结果对层位选择与地标定位高度敏感、重复性差。

Method: 将临床采集的轴位、冠状位、矢状位MR扫描通过隐式神经表示（INR）融合重建为高分辨率体数据；在超分辨体数据上，使用两个地标检测U-Net分别定位滑车沟内外侧顶点，于滑车区域内连续计算SA，形成剖面曲线。在公开fastMRI数据集与小型院内TD患者队列上进行评估。

Result: 与常规手动单层SA测量相比，所提自动方法平均绝对误差为11.6°；同时提供滑车形态的连续刻画。群体层面分析显示，公开队列与院内TD队列的平均SA剖面存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 该框架在无需额外成像的前提下，将传统SA评估扩展为基于连续剖面的滑车形态描述，且概念上与现行临床评估保持一致，有望用于TD的刻画；但尚需进一步验证。代码已开源。

Abstract: Trochlear dysplasia (TD) is an abnormality of the femoral trochlea associated with anterior knee pain and patellar instability. The sulcus angle (SA) is used to assess trochlear morphology, but it is typically measured on a single axial MR slice with no clear guidance on which to select, making it sensitive to slice selection and landmark placement. We propose an automatic framework for continuous SA profiling from super-resolved MR volumes. Clinically acquired axial, coronal, and sagittal MR scans are combined using implicit neural representations to reconstruct a high-resolution volume. SA measurements are computed across the trochlear region using two landmark detection U-Net models. The approach was evaluated on the public fastMRI dataset and a small in-house cohort of patients with TD. Compared with conventional manual single-slice SA measurements, the proposed automated method yielded a mean absolute error of 11.6$^\circ$ while providing continuous characterization of trochlear morphology. Population-level analysis demonstrated distinct mean SA profiles between the public cohort and the in-house TD cohort, highlighting the potential of profile-based assessment to characterize TD. By reducing reliance on a single manually selected axial slice, the proposed framework extends conventional SA assessment to a continuous profile-based description of trochlear morphology without additional imaging, while remaining conceptually linked to current clinical assessment. Further validation is required. The code is available: https://github.com/wehrlimi/SA_Profile.

</details>


### [52] [TransGaze-Object: Transformer Based Driver Gaze Object Prediction Framework in Real Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10139)
*Pavan Kumar Sharma,Ayush Pande,Pranamesh Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端驾驶员注视目标预测框架TransGaze-Object，利用Transformer跨注意力融合面部与交通场景特征直接预测注视对象，并构建UD-FSG基准数据集，注视目标预测准确率达60%，优于基于注视点关联方法的51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶员注视估计研究多以注视区域或注视向量/注视点（PoG）表示注视，缺乏语义信息；而对象级注视信息能通过识别被注视对象（如车辆、行人、交通信号）提供更具语义的视觉注意力表征。

Method: 提出端到端框架TransGaze-Object：先提取面部特征（含人脸与虹膜加权的眼部特征）及交通对象空间特征，再通过基于Transformer的跨注意力机制计算相似度分数与注意力权重以预测注视对象；同时构建基准数据集UD-FSG，包含同步的驾驶员面部图像、交通场景图像、场景目标边界框及以2D注视坐标和注视对象表示的注视标签。

Result: TransGaze-Object注视目标预测总体准确率为60%，而将估计的注视点关联到交通对象的方法为51%；误差分析显示本方法将交通对象与背景之间的混淆错误率降至11.68%，相比基于PoG关联的23.21%实现49.7%的相对降低。

Conclusion: 结果证明，直接从驾驶员面部与交通场景信息预测注视对象，比先估计中间注视点再与交通对象关联的方式更有效。

Abstract: Driver gaze provides information regarding driver visual attention and situational awareness to the surrounding traffic. Existing driver gaze estimation studies represent gaze in terms of gaze zone or gaze vector/point-of-gaze (PoG). However, object-level gaze information provides a more semantically meaningful representation of visual attention by identifying attended objects, such as vehicles, pedestrians, or traffic signals. In this study, we propose an end-to-end driver gaze object prediction framework, TransGaze-Object, Transformer-based Gaze Object prediction model. The proposed framework first extracts facial features, including face and iris-weighted eye features, along with trafficobject spatial features. A transformer based cross-attention mechanism is then used to compute similarity scores and attention weights for predicting the drivers gaze object. To train this model, we propose a benchmark driver gaze dataset, Urban Driving-Face Scene Gaze (UD-FSG), comprising synchronized driver-face and traffic-scene images, scene objects bounding boxes, and gaze labels in terms of 2D gaze coordinate and gaze object. The TransGaze-Object model achieves an overall accuracy of 60% for gaze-object prediction, compared to 51% accuracy obtained from associating the estimated Point-of-Gaze to traffic objects. The error analysis reveals that TransGaze-Object reduces confusion between traffic objects (predicted) and the background (ground-truth), achieving an error rate of 11.68%, a 49.7% relative reduction compared with 23.21% error obtained from PoG-based gaze-object association. Overall, the results demonstrate the effectiveness of directly predicting gaze objects from driver-face and traffic-scene information, rather than estimating an intermediate Point-of-Gaze and subsequently associating it with traffic objects.

</details>


### [53] [ScopeMamba-YOLO: Widening the Perceptual Scope Inward and Outward for Small Object Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10156)
*Junjie Fan,Yijun Mai,Linduo Wei,Jiayu Rao,Junmin Bao,Qiushi Jin,Guijia Li,Yong Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScopeMamba-YOLO，以"离路零门控选择性扫描"原则解耦上下文建模与卷积主干，在UAV/遥感小目标检测上以极少参数取得显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV与遥感影像中的小目标检测需同时保留高分辨率细节并建模长距离上下文。增加 stride-4 检测层并移除 stride-32 阶段虽有利于微小目标，却削弱了外围空间支撑；而将选择性扫描直接插入主特征路径又会干扰微弱的局部线索。因此需要一种既能引入全局上下文又不破坏局部卷积特征的机制。

Method: 提出离路、零门控的选择性扫描原则，将上下文建模与卷积流解耦。该原则由主干中的级联全局上下文模块（CGCM）和颈部中的选择性扫描PAN（SS-PAN）实例化；设计自适应多尺度条带（AMS）块以降低高分辨率特征提取开销；提出尺度自适应DFL（SA-DFL）检测头，仅增加0.008M参数即在各尺度间重新分配分布支撑与回归能力。通过消融（主路径扫描对照、无CGCM三种子基线、算子控制）与ERF分析验证。

Result: 对照实验显示主路径同配置选择性扫描使mAP50下降0.98个百分点，而离路CGCM相较无CGCM三种子均值提升0.67个百分点，且增益不能仅由辅助分支容量解释。ERF分析表明完整上下文通路将stride 8处外围能量比从0.008提升到0.090。VisDrone-2019上，ScopeMamba-S以3.57M参数达50.8% mAP50，较YOLOv8s高10.8个百分点且仅用其32%参数；ScopeMamba-M以6.48M参数达52.6% mAP50。在AI-TOD上亦有稳定提升，尤其对very-tiny与tiny目标。

Conclusion: 离路零门控选择性扫描可在不干扰局部卷积线索的前提下有效补充长距离上下文，配合AMS与SA-DFL，使小目标检测在极低参数成本下获得一致且可解释的精度提升。

Abstract: Small object detection in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) and remote sensing imagery requires preserving high-resolution detail while modeling long-range context. Adding a stride-4 detection level and removing the stride-32 stage benefits tiny targets but weakens peripheral spatial support, whereas directly inserting selective scanning into the main feature path can interfere with weak local cues. We propose ScopeMamba-YOLO, built around an off-path, zero-gated selective-scanning principle that decouples contextual modeling from the convolutional stream. The principle is instantiated by a Cascaded Global-Context Module (CGCM) in the backbone and a Selective-Scan PAN (SS-PAN) in the neck. An Adaptive Multi-scale Strip (AMS) Block reduces the cost of high-resolution feature extraction, while a Scale-Adaptive DFL (SA-DFL) head reallocates distributional support and regression capacity across scales with only 0.008M additional parameters. Controlled experiments show that matched main-path selective scanning reduces mAP50 by 0.98 pp, whereas off-path CGCM improves the final configuration by 0.67 pp over the three-seed no-CGCM mean; operator controls indicate that this gain is not explained by auxiliary branch capacity alone. ERF analysis further shows that the complete context pathway increases the peripheral energy ratio from 0.008 to 0.090 at stride 8. On VisDrone-2019, ScopeMamba-S achieves 50.8% mAP50 with 3.57M parameters, exceeding YOLOv8s by 10.8 pp while using 32% of its parameters; ScopeMamba-M reaches 52.6% mAP50 with 6.48M parameters. Consistent improvements are also observed on AI-TOD, especially for very-tiny and tiny objects.

</details>


### [54] [PACE: Perceived-Latency-Aware Cascading Service Routing and Filler Control for QoE-Efficient Retrieval-Augmented Dialogue Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10372)
*Lin Huang,Yujuan Tan,Weisheng Li,Lixiang Zeng,Kun Yang,Suihan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE是一个检索增强对话服务框架，将“感知首响应时间”（PTFR）形式化为QoE目标，在质量与成本约束下最小化该指标，并在人形机器人销售服务中验证：P95时延显著降低，卡片填充器减少94%调用且零冲突，易变性感知缓存准入将陈旧回答从86%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多聚焦级联路由、语义缓存或自适应检索，但未联合控制“由哪个答案源组构响应”与“等待窗口填充什么内容”；同时缺乏对填充答案冲突风险的量化，服务体验（等待时延）与质量/成本之间的权衡未被统一优化。

Method: 提出PACE框架，将PTFR作为QoE目标进行形式化，并在质量/成本约束下最小化。采用三项机制联合控制：(1) 负载自适应级联路由器，决定答案源；(2) 联合路径填充控制器，控制等待窗口内容；(3) 易变性感知缓存准入。核心在于对答案源与等待窗口填充的联合优化。

Result: 在75k CarQA请求上，级联将纯LLM的P95 PTFR减半（0.29s vs 0.53s，c16）；自适应控制器达0.41s P95，高负载下比RAG快2.4倍且质量相当；填充控制器减少94%调用且零冲突；易变性感知准入将陈旧答案从86%降至0%；门控规则保证控制器不劣于基线，暴露上界为一个保持周期。

Conclusion: PACE首次量化了已部署服务中填充答案的冲突风险，证明联合控制答案源与等待窗口可在质量/成本约束下大幅改善PTFR，并通过门控规则提供性能下界保障，具有良好的部署可行性与安全性。

Abstract: We present the PACE, a framework for retrieval-augmented dialogue serving that formalizes Perceived Time-to-First-Response (PTFR) as a QoE objective and minimizes it under quality/cost constraints. Unlike prior work on cascaded routing, semantic caching, or adaptive retrieval, PACE jointly controls which answer source composes the response and what fills the waiting window. Deployed on a humanoid-robot sales service, it combines three mechanisms: a load-adaptive cascading router, a joint path-filler controller, and volatility-aware cache admission. On 75k CarQA requests, the cascade halves pure-LLM PTFR at P95 (0.29 vs 0.53s at c16). The adaptive controller reaches 0.41s P95, outperforming RAG by 2.4 times at high load with equal quality. The filler controller cuts calls by 94% with zero conflict. Volatility-aware admission reduces stale answers from 86% to 0%. A gating rule ensures the controller never worse than the baseline, with exposure bounded by one hold period. This is the first quantification of filler-answer conflict risk in deployed services.

</details>


### [55] [3rd Place Solution to Human Motion Challenges in Real-World and Clinical Settings (MoCha) @ECCV2026: Language-Aligned Motion Representations for Domain-Generalizable UPDRS-Gait Severity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10187)
*Soojie Kim,Muhammad Munsif,Minkyung Kim,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语言对齐的运动表示方法，用于跨临床域泛化的UPDRS-Gait严重程度估计，在MoCha 2026挑战赛中取得macro-F1 0.57、排名第3的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UPDRS-Gait严重程度估计面临异构临床域之间的域偏移问题，且训练数据存在类别不平衡，需要学习具有语义结构且能跨域泛化的运动特征。

Method: 以Bi-GRU为骨干网络捕获SMPL序列的时序动态；训练前用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct离线生成运动文本描述；骨干网络联合分类目标与文本对齐目标进行训练以学习判别性且语义结构化的表示，并处理类别不平衡；随后将学习到的骨干网络独立适配到各源域以捕获域特有运动特征，再将各源域模型在参数层面合并为单一域泛化模型；进一步使用GPT-5.5进行伪标注以缓解类别不平衡，最终各站点合并模型在推理时不使用类别先验校正。

Result: 在MoCha Challenge的unseen-site设定下，以Macro F1为主要指标评估，方法在隐藏测试集上取得macro-F1 0.57，推理时仅需637K活跃参数，在58个榜单条目中排名第3。该挑战赛共收到来自112位参赛者的1,669次提交。

Conclusion: 语言对齐的运动表示结合源域独立适配与参数级模型合并，可在低参数量下实现跨域泛化的UPDRS-Gait严重程度估计，验证了语义结构化表示与域泛化合并策略的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce language-aligned motion representations for domain-generalizable UPDRS-Gait severity estimation, aiming to learn semantically structured motion features that generalize across heterogeneous clinical domains. We first learn motion representations using a Bi-GRU backbone that captures the temporal dynamics of SMPL sequences. Prior to model training, motion captions are generated offline using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. The backbone is then trained with both classification and text-alignment objectives to learn discriminative and semantically structured motion representations while accounting for the class imbalance present in the training data. We subsequently adapt the learned backbone independently to each source domain so that the model can capture domain-specific motion characteristics. The resulting source-specific models are then merged at the parameter level to consolidate complementary knowledge across source domains into a single domain-generalized model. To further mitigate class imbalance, we perform GPT-5.5-based pseudo labeling, and our final merged models for each site do not use any class-prior correction during inference. The resulting model is evaluated under the unseen-site setting of the MoCha Challenge, using Macro F1 as the primary evaluation metric. Our method achieves a macro-F1 of 0.57 on the hidden test set with only 637K active parameters at inference, ranking 3rd among 58 leaderboard entries in the MoCha 2026 Challenge. The challenge attracted 1,669 submissions from 112 participants and offered monetary prizes sponsored by Machine Medicine Technologies.

</details>


### [56] [UOT-Gap: A Variational Principle for the Modality Gap in Vision-Language Models via Unbalanced Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10224)
*Zonglin Yang,Huilan Ma,Xudan Zheng,Yuejun Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练变分诊断框架UOT-Gap，用非平衡熵最优传输刻画CLIP类模型模态差距，其配对残差能高精度追踪检索性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）的图像与文本嵌入存在模态差距，已有研究将其归因于初始化、对比学习动态和信息不平衡，但该差距的分布性来源及其对检索性能的成对贡献尚不明确。

Method: 提出UOT-Gap，一种免训练变分诊断方法：以非平衡熵最优传输（UOT）对冻结的图像与文本嵌入建模，UOT最优解分解为传输成本、耦合复杂度和边缘质量变化；并设计配对的残差项，将观测到的图文对与UOT软匹配结果进行比较。在Flickr8K和COCO-1K上，使用冻结的CLIP、OpenCLIP和SigLIP编码器进行评测。

Result: 字幕退化使Flickr8K Recall@1从0.559降至0.003；在六种数据集-模型条件下，配对残差追踪检索退化的平均绝对Spearman相关系数为0.973，而平均模态差距仅为0.392；在五个COCO-1K随机子集上关联稳定（0.954±0.026，最低0.943）；UOT重心更新降低传输目标却使检索性能变差，区分了几何目标下降与任务提升。

Conclusion: UOT-Gap可作为诊断字幕质量、模态对齐与检索鲁棒性的有效工具，且配对残差比整体模态差距更能反映检索性能变化。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP embed images and text in a shared space, where modality-specific distributions often remain separated. Existing accounts connect this modality gap to initialization, contrastive dynamics, and information imbalance, while its distributional and pairwise contributions to retrieval remain unresolved. We introduce UOT-Gap, a training-free variational diagnostic that models frozen image and text embeddings with unbalanced entropic optimal transport (UOT). The UOT optimum separates transport, coupling complexity, and marginal mass variation; a complementary pair-aware residual compares observed image-caption pairs with the UOT soft matching. On Flickr8K and COCO-1K with frozen CLIP, OpenCLIP, and SigLIP encoders, caption degradation reduces Flickr8K Recall@1 from 0.559 to 0.003. Across six dataset-model conditions, the pair-aware residual tracks retrieval degradation with mean absolute Spearman 0.973, compared with 0.392 for the mean gap. The association remains stable across five random COCO-1K subsets at $0.954\pm0.026$, with a minimum of 0.943. UOT barycentric updates reduce the transport objective while degrading retrieval, distinguishing geometric objective descent from task improvement. These results establish UOT-Gap as a diagnostic for caption quality, modality alignment, and retrieval robustness.

</details>


### [57] [When Fusion Fails: Corruption-Aware Rebalanced Fusion for Multi-Modal Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10261)
*Yuchen Pei,Xiaoyu Hu,Yixiong Zou,Dingwen Hu,Hui Chu,Yutao Ma,Shijun Qiu,Gang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态医学图像分割中空间对齐但质量不一致（主要为分辨率退化）导致的融合性能下降问题，提出腐化感知重平衡融合框架 CoReFuse-Med。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态医学图像分割应利用互补诊断信息，但当空间对齐的输入存在质量差异时，融合模型反而可能不如单模态基线。作者关注到"训练-推理不一致"这一关键优化问题：退化模态在训练中获得的更新较弱，却在推理时显著影响预测，形成对融合的主动干扰。

Method: 作者将失败归因于重采样引发的特征腐化与优化偏置：噪声特征通过跳跃连接传播，并导致不可靠的模态选择。据此提出 CoReFuse-Med（Corruption-aware Rebalanced Fusion）框架，在特征传递阶段抑制腐化，并在高层融合阶段重平衡各模态贡献。文中"腐化"主要指分辨率引发的退化，而非错位或模态完全缺失，合成噪声仅作为辅助设置评估。

Result: 在 EPVS、BraTS 和 WMH 三个数据集上开展实验，涵盖多个 Z 轴切片保留比例以及辅助噪声测试，结果表明该方法在模态质量差异条件下具有更高的分割精度和更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CoReFuse-Med 通过抑制腐化传播与重平衡模态贡献，缓解了质量不一致输入下的融合失效问题，验证了融合稳定性的重要性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-modal medical image segmentation leverages complementary diagnostic information, yet fusion can underperform single-modality baselines when spatially aligned inputs differ in quality. Here, "corruption" primarily denotes resolution-induced degradation rather than misalignment or complete modality absence, while synthetic noise is evaluated only as an auxiliary setting. We identify a critical optimization-inference inconsistency: degraded modalities can receive weak training updates yet substantially affect predictions, indicating active interference with fusion. We attribute this failure to resampling-induced feature corruption and optimization bias, where noisy features propagate through skip connections and encourage unreliable modality selection. We therefore propose CoReFuse-Med, a Corruption-aware Rebalanced Fusion framework that suppresses corruption during feature transmission and rebalances modality contributions during high-level fusion. Experiments on EPVS, BraTS, and WMH, including multiple Z-axis slice-retention ratios and an auxiliary noise test, demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness under modality-quality discrepancies. Our code is available at https://github.com/lrever/CoReFuse.

</details>


### [58] [FreqFLD: Towards All-in-One Facial Landmark Detection via Frequency Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10278)
*Shun Ren,Kaijie Jin,Shengkai Hu,Beihang Song,Hang Sun,Wenwen Min,Youfa Liu,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreqFLD：一种频域调制的人脸关键点检测框架，通过频率先验解耦、混合专家与路由损失，实现All-in-One跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸关键点检测方法多在空间域、以数据集特定范式训练，忽略了该任务本质上是几何驱动且对频率变化敏感的特性，导致复杂场景下跨数据集泛化能力受限，难以形成统一的All-in-One模型。

Method: 提出FreqFLD框架，包含三部分：(1) Frequency Modulation Module (FreqMoM)，通过解耦并调制低频与高频分量显式引入频率先验，注入后续特征建模以平衡全局人脸结构与局部关键点细节；(2) Frequency-Modulated Mixture-of-Experts (FreqMoE)，专家选择以频率调制先验为条件自适应进行，灵活建模异构关键点模式；(3) Frequency-Consistent Routing (FreqCR) 损失，约束频率感知专家的路由与分配，促进跨场景专家利用率均衡，实现稳定的专家专业化。

Result: 大量实验表明FreqFLD在主流数据集上取得了可比性能。

Conclusion: 频域调制与混合专家结合可有效提升All-in-One人脸关键点检测的鲁棒性与跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent progress in deep learning has significantly advanced facial landmark detection. However, most existing methods process features in a spatial-domain manner under a dataset-specific training paradigm, which overlooks the fact that facial landmark detection is inherently geometry-driven and sensitive to frequency variations, thereby limiting cross-dataset generalization under complex scenarios and hindering the development of a facial landmark detection model. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{FreqFLD}, a \textbf{freq}uency-modulated framework towards All-in-One \textbf{f}acial \textbf{l}andmark \textbf{d}etection. Specifically, FreqFLD introduces a Frequency Modulation Module (FreqMoM) to explicitly induce the frequency prior by decoupling and modulating low- and high-frequency components, which is then injected into subsequent feature modeling to enable balanced modeling of global facial structure and local landmark details. Furthermore, FreqFLD employs a Frequency-Modulated Mixture-of-Experts (FreqMoE), with expert selection adaptively conditioned on frequency-modulated priors, enabling flexible modeling of heterogeneous facial landmark patterns under diverse and challenging scenarios. To regularize frequency-consistent modeling under the All-in-One paradigm, we further introduce a Frequency-Consistent Routing (FreqCR) loss, which constrains the routing and assignment of frequency-aware experts to promote balanced expert utilization across diverse facial scenarios, thereby enabling stable expert specialization and achieving robust facial landmark detection. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed FreqFLD achieves comparable performance on popular datasets. The code is available at: https://github.com/jkj1059657014/FreqFLD.

</details>


### [59] [Isotropic Embedding Perturbations for Robust Vision Language Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10292)
*Hyesong Choi,Daeun Kim,Song Park,Taekyung Kim,Byeongho Heo,Sangdoo Yun,Dongbo Min,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Aether：一种在嵌入空间通过受控alpha混合施加扩散式随机扰动的即插即用增强方法，提供各向同性且语义一致的温和正则，在多种架构与识别任务上稳定超越CutMix、Mixup、DropPath与RandAug的组合配方，并在多模态对齐上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增强方法（RandAug、CutMix、Mixup、RandErase、DropPath）单独使用正则效果强，但组合使用时因功能重叠而性能饱和；激进的像素级操作还会破坏跨模态对齐。因此需要跳出输入空间，在嵌入空间寻找新的增强维度，以获得语义一致、各向同性的正则信号。

Method: Aether是一种即插即用的嵌入空间增强方法：通过受控的alpha混合在embedding空间施加扩散风格的随机扰动，产生温和却有效的扰动以平滑表示。其思想受语言模型特征空间扰动与生成式预训练中图像退化启发，设计目标是各向同性正则，同时保留强视觉-语言编码器所需的细粒度结构信息，不损害多模态对齐。

Result: 在多种架构和多个识别任务上，Aether相较CutMix+Mixup+DropPath+RandAug的先进组合配方取得一致提升，这种增益幅度在现代增强替代方案中较为罕见；在多模态对齐方面效果尤为显著，传统像素空间增强失效的场景下Aether仍能成功。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间增强是超越输入空间增强饱和的有效新方向。Aether通过提供稳定、各向同性的正则信号并尊重高维特征空间完整性，实现了优于传统像素空间增强的多模态对齐与一致的性能增益，可作为现有增强配方的简单插件替代或补充。

Abstract: Data augmentation is fundamental to training modern deep vision and multimodal models. While individual methods, such as RandAug, CutMix, Mixup, RandErase, and DropPath, offer strong regularization effects, their combined use has saturated in performance due to overlapping functionalities, and aggressive pixel-level manipulations may disrupt delicate cross-modal alignment. This saturation motivates the search for a new augmentation axis within the embedding space rather than the input space. We introduce Aether, a simple plug-in method that applies diffusion-style random perturbations in the embedding space via controlled alpha-mixing, specifically designed to provide isotropic regularization that remains semantically consistent. Inspired by feature-space perturbations in language models and image degradation in generative pretraining, Aether induces mild yet effective perturbations that smooth the representations without compromising the fine-grained structural information required for strong vision-language encoders. Across diverse architectures and across multiple recognition tasks, Aether delivers consistent gains over the advanced recipe combining CutMix, Mixup, DropPath, and RandAug---a level of improvement rarely observed with modern augmentation alternatives. Notably, Aether demonstrates superior effectiveness in multi-modal alignment, succeeding where traditional pixel-space augmentations fail by providing a stable, isotropic regularization signal that respects the integrity of the high-dimensional feature space.

</details>


### [60] [Characterizing Text Branch Sensitivity in Medical Vision-Language Segmentation via Evidence Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02663)
*Ziquan Liu,Zhewei Zhu,Xuyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了文本在多模态医学图像分割中的作用，发现分割性能对融合模块选择不敏感，且文本贡献因数据集而异：在 BUSI 和 BTMRI 上文本缺失会导致性能骤降，而在 ISIC 和 Kvasir-SEG 上影响较小。作者提出证据解耦解码器（EDD）分解图像证据与文本调制证据，揭示文本主要通过全局语义调制而非独立空间定位影响预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）在医学图像分割中已展现良好性能，但文本信息究竟对像素级预测贡献多少仍不明确，缺乏对多模态交互机制的系统性理解。

Method: 1) 系统分析常用融合策略；2) 基于证据深度学习与深监督提出证据解耦解码器（EDD），在解码过程中将图像证据与文本调制证据解耦，作为内部表示分析工具，同时保持有竞争力的分割性能；3) 通过文本扰动敏感性实验跨数据集对比分析。

Result: 分割性能对融合模块的选择基本不敏感；对文本扰动的敏感性因数据集差异显著——BUSI 和 BTMRI 上移除文本导致灾难性性能下降（模型强依赖文本），ISIC 和 Kvasir-SEG 上文本影响相对边缘；文本主要通过全局语义调制而非独立空间定位影响预测，且驱动文本敏感性的具体语义成分因数据集而异。

Conclusion: 研究深化了对多模态医学图像分割中模态交互的理解，表明文本的贡献高度依赖数据集与语义成分，为未来模型设计提供了实用启示，如不应过度依赖融合模块设计，而需针对数据特性审慎评估文本的实际作用。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance in medical image segmentation by incorporating clinical text. However, it remains unclear how much textual information actually contributes to pixel-level predictions. In this work, we systematically investigate the role of text in multimodal medical image segmentation. We first analyze several commonly used fusion strategies and find that segmentation performance is largely insensitive to the choice of fusion module. To further understand modality interactions, we propose an Evidence Decoupling Decoder (EDD) based on evidential deep learning and deep supervision. EDD serves as an internal representation analysis tool that decomposes image evidence and text-modulated evidence throughout the decoding process while maintaining competitive segmentation performance. Experimental results show that the sensitivity to text perturbation varies substantially across datasets. On BUSI and BTMRI, removing text causes catastrophic performance drops, indicating strong model reliance on textual input. On ISIC and Kvasir-SEG, text exerts relatively marginal influence. We further find that text affects predictions mainly through global semantic modulation rather than independent spatial localization, and that the specific semantic components driving text sensitivity differ across datasets. These findings provide a deeper understanding of modality interaction in multimodal medical image segmentation and offer practical insights for future model design.

</details>


### [61] [TRACE: Trajectory-robust Admission with Evidence Ordering for Efficient GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10297)
*Yuhao Wang,Mu Qiao,Xindong Zhang,Yunzhi Zhuge,Lei Zhang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的可视化token剪枝框架，通过轨迹鲁棒的准入与覆盖感知证据排序，在紧凑预算下保持GUI智能体的空间覆盖与缓存可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体随轨迹累积高分辨率截图，导致推理延迟与内存开销增大。免训练的可视化token剪枝可降低成本，但缓存复用带来根本约束：token一经丢弃便无法恢复，剪枝成为不可逆的准入决策，必须在未知未来目标下仍保持有用性，并在紧张预算下维持可操作区域的覆盖。

Method: 提出无需训练框架，实现轨迹鲁棒准入与覆盖感知证据排序。将查询无关的布局交互先验与指令相关性、特征新颖度结合，按潜在未来效用与多样性对可视证据排序；预留部分预算给分布于屏幕的原生可视token，修复空间覆盖且不破坏排序；由此形成嵌套token序，使保留证据在预算变化下单调收缩且全轨迹可复用；最后用单调KV收缩将退役帧增量压缩为紧凑会话状态，避免重复编码或剪枝。

Result: 在六个GUI基准和多种模型上开展大量实验，验证方法在紧张预算下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该免训练剪枝框架通过嵌套token序与单调KV收缩，解决了缓存复用下不可逆剪枝的覆盖与效用矛盾，在紧凑预算下表现有效，源代码将开源。

Abstract: GUI agents accumulate high-resolution screenshots as the trajectory unfolds, increasing inference latency and memory usage. Training-free visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but cache reuse introduces a fundamental constraint. Once tokens are discarded, the corresponding visual evidence cannot be recovered without re-encoding. Pruning therefore becomes an \textit{irreversible admission decision} that must remain useful for unknown future targets while preserving coverage of operable regions under tight budgets. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{\method{}}, a training-free framework for \emph{\textbf{T}rajectory-\textbf{r}obust \textbf{A}dmission and \textbf{C}overage-aware \textbf{E}vidence ordering}. Specifically, we combine a query-independent layout-derived interaction prior with instruction relevance and feature novelty to rank visual evidence according to both potential future utility and diversity. Then, we reserve part of the budget for native visual tokens distributed across the screen, repairing missing spatial coverage without breaking the ordering. Together, these mechanisms produce a nested token order, allowing retained visual evidence to shrink monotonically across budgets while remaining reusable throughout the trajectory. Finally, our monotone KV contraction incrementally contracts retired frames into compact session state, avoiding repeated visual encoding or pruning. Extensive experiments across six GUI benchmarks and diverse models verify the effectiveness of our proposed \method{} under tight budgets. The source code will be released.

</details>


### [62] [SynThermFace: Amplifying Limited Paired Data for Visible-Thermal Face Recognition via Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10303)
*Anjith George,Adam Unal,Sebastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SynThermFace 框架：用少量真实可见光-热红外配对数据适配扩散模型，生成大规模可见光-合成热红外配对数据，用于将可见光人脸识别模型适配为跨光谱人脸识别模型，推理时仅需单次前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨光谱人脸识别可缓解可见光模型在低照度、夜间等受限条件下性能退化的问题，但配对可见光-热红外数据稀缺且大规模采集成本高，制约了研究进展。

Method: 先用有限真实配对可见光-热红外图像适配扩散模型，再从现有真实或合成可见光人脸数据生成大规模配对可见光-合成热红外数据；用这些生成配对将预训练可见光人脸识别模型适配为 CFR 模型（PACT 适配目标），推理阶段无需图像翻译，单次前向即可完成识别。

Result: 在 MCXFace 真实配对协议下，PACT 优于所评估的 CFR 适配基线，验证了所提适配目标的效果；在更大规模生成配对数据上训练 PACT 相比未适配模型和真实配对 PACT 配置均有进一步提升；在 Tufts 数据集上的跨库评估表明所学表征可迁移到未见数据库。

Conclusion: 将生成过程从测试阶段转移到训练阶段可有效利用生成配对数据增强跨光谱人脸识别，缓解真实配对数据稀缺问题，且具备跨库泛化能力；源代码与训练模型将公开。

Abstract: Face recognition (FR) is a widely used modality for biometric authentication, but conventional models rely on visible-spectrum imagery and degrade when high-quality RGB images cannot be captured. Cross-spectral face recognition addresses this limitation by matching visible images with other modalities such as thermal imagery, enabling more reliable performance in low-light, nighttime, and unconstrained conditions. However, progress is limited by the scarcity of paired visible-thermal data, which is difficult and costly to collect at scale. We propose SynThermFace, a framework that amplifies limited real visible-thermal supervision into larger paired adaptation datasets for cross-spectral face recognition. A diffusion model is first adapted using a limited set of paired visible--thermal images and then used to generate large-scale paired visible--synthetic thermal data from existing real or synthetic visible face datasets. The generated pairs are used to adapt a pretrained visible-spectrum face recognition model into a CFR model. Unlike synthesis-based approaches that require image translation at test time, the proposed method shifts generation to the training stage and performs inference with a single forward pass through the adapted recognition model. Under the same MCXFace real-pair protocol, PACT improves over the evaluated CFR adaptation baselines, isolating the effect of the proposed adaptation objective. Training PACT on larger generated paired datasets provides additional improvements over both the unadapted model and the real-pair PACT configuration. Cross-database evaluation on the Tufts dataset provides evidence that the learned representation transfers to an unseen database. The source code and trained models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [63] [Decoupled Self-Forcing Distillation for Streaming Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10317)
*Yanru An,Ruiyan Wang,Wenwu Wei,Rui Bu,Qi Wang,Hongwei Hu,Zhengxue Cheng,Rong Xie,Li Song,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在低维、身份解耦的运动空间中融合音频与运动描述条件，用小规模因果自回归Transformer生成运动隐变量，再由预训练扩散渲染器转成视频；并提出解耦自强制蒸馏，在单一冻结教师下同时蒸馏出块因果渲染器与学生运动生成器，实现15.4 FPS、1.3 s延迟的流式说话人头像生成且无质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式说话人头像生成中保真度与效率长期相互制约：端到端方法直接把音频条件注入视频扩散模型，质量高但依赖大规模模型；两阶段方法先生成中间运动表示，成本低但保真度落后。作者认为前者的代价源于融合目标选择不当——视频隐变量由身份、外观与背景主导，与音频无关，把音频耦合到每个像素会模糊细节并浪费容量。

Method: 将条件融合改在低维、身份解耦的运动空间中进行，按时间粒度分别路由音频与运动描述；用小型因果自回归Transformer生成运动隐变量，再由预训练扩散渲染器生成视频，使条件以传递方式控制视频，从而无需大骨干即可获得高保真。为支持流式，两模型均需因果化，并借助双向教师通过自强制缓解曝光偏差。然运动空间缺乏此类教师，故提出解耦自强制蒸馏：在冻结教师下，以运动为条件将渲染器蒸馏为块因果学生；同时无条件地对渲染出的 rollout 与真实视频打分，用所生成视频监督运动。该机制把保真度上限从运动生成器转移到更强的渲染器上。

Result: 两模型以并行因果流运行，达到15.4 FPS、1.3 s延迟，且无质量退化。

Conclusion: 通过在身份解耦的低维运动空间中融合条件并以解耦自强制蒸馏同时因果化两模型，可将保真度上限转移至更强的渲染器，从而在不增大骨干的前提下实现高效、高保真的流式说话人头像生成。

Abstract: Streaming talking-head generation produces each frame as its driving audio arrives, yet fidelity and efficiency have so far pulled in opposite directions: end-to-end methods condition a video diffusion model on audio directly and achieve high quality but only at large scale, while cheaper two-stage methods generate an intermediate motion representation and trail in fidelity. We argue the cost of the former lies in the target of fusion: the video latent is dominated by identity, appearance and background, none of which audio bears on, so coupling audio to every pixel blurs detail and wastes capacity. We instead fuse conditions in a low-dimensional identity-disentangled motion space, routing audio and motion captions by their temporal granularity, and generate motion latents with a small causal autoregressive transformer that a pretrained diffusion renderer turns into video. Conditions thus control video transitively, and high fidelity no longer requires a large backbone. Streaming this decomposition needs both models to be causal, and the exposure-bias problem could be solved by self-forcing given a bidirectional teacher. But there is no such teacher in motion space. Our decoupled self-forcing distillation resolves both models under one frozen teacher: conditioned on motion, it distills the renderer into a block-causal student; unconditionally, it scores rendered rollouts against real videos, supervising motion by the video it produces. This lifts the fidelity ceiling from the motion generator onto the stronger renderer. The two models run as parallel causal streams, reaching 15.4 FPS at 1.3 s latency with no quality degradation.

</details>


### [64] [Geometry Without Coordinates: LiDAR Diffusion as a 3D Feature Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10322)
*Samed Doğan,Nico Leuze,Alfred Schöttl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以LiDAR为条件的扩散模型，利用现成2D基础模型的伪标签训练，支持深度、语义分割和实例预测等多任务输出，并可直接在点云空间分析其学到的3D表征，发现该表征具有分层、模态相关的流形结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大规模2D基础模型的丰富先验迁移到稀疏3D LiDAR存在困难：原生3D基础模型因数据与标注稀缺，难以在可比规模上训练。作者希望探索能否仅依赖2D监督，通过LiDAR条件扩散模型诱导出结构化的3D表征。

Method: 训练一个以LiDAR点云为条件的扩散模型，监督信号来自现成2D基础模型的伪标签；通过文本任务提示切换多个输出模态（深度、语义分割、实例预测）。由于模型以LiDAR为条件，其输出及中间UNet特征可投影回输入点云，从而在点云空间直接分析3D表征，并显式排除原始空间坐标以隔离特征内容与投影几何。用线性探针评估特征，并以匹配的高斯噪声作对照；同时用跨模态特征流的成对余弦相似度分析组织方式。

Result: 线性探针在3D语义类别上取得约23% MIoU，而匹配的高斯噪声对照仅约3.5%，说明特征包含显著的非平凡结构。跨模态相似度显示分层组织：编码器浅层各模态对齐弱但可单独解码，中间层收敛到共享表征，解码器层重新特化到各自任务输出。

Conclusion: 以LiDAR为条件的扩散模型可仅凭2D监督诱导出结构化的3D表征，其模态相关的流形在共享瓶颈附近局部统一。这表明扩散是向稀疏3D域迁移大规模2D先验的可行机制。

Abstract: Transferring the rich priors of large 2D foundation models to sparse 3D LiDAR remains challenging, as training native 3D foundation models at comparable scale is limited by data and annotation scarcity. We introduce a LiDAR-conditioned diffusion model trained on pseudo-labels from off-the-shelf 2D foundation models. The model supports multiple output modalities, including depth, semantic segmentation and instance prediction, selectable via a textual task prompt. Because the model is conditioned on LiDAR, both its outputs and its intermediate UNet features can be projected back onto the input point cloud, enabling analysis of a 3D representation learned entirely under 2D supervision. We study this representation directly in point-cloud space, explicitly excluding raw spatial coordinates to isolate feature content from projection geometry. Linear probes recover up to ~23% Mean Intersection over Union (MIoU) on 3D semantic classes, compared to ~3.5% for a matched Gaussian-noise control, indicating substantial non-trivial structure. Pairwise cosine similarity across modality-specific feature streams reveals a layered organization. Early encoder layers remain weakly aligned across modalities while individually decodable, intermediate layers converge toward a shared representation, and decoder layers re-specialize toward task-specific outputs. These findings indicate that LiDAR-conditioned diffusion models can induce structured 3D representations from 2D supervision alone, with a modality-dependent manifold that locally unifies near a shared bottleneck. This positions diffusion as a viable mechanism for transferring large-scale 2D priors into sparse 3D domains.

</details>


### [65] [Learning to Adapt and Calibrate: Score Distribution Alignment for Few-Shot Uncertainty Prediction in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10333)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlignCP：在少样本迁移下通过重加权校准分布来对齐支持集与查询集的非一致性分数分布，从而在监督微调破坏可交换性时恢复可靠的保形覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言模型的保形预测依赖校准集与测试集的可交换性及足够大的校准集，这在少样本迁移中难以满足：仅有少量标注支持集用于适配预训练VLM，无标注查询集用于评估。监督微调改变模型参数、移动非一致性分数分布，破坏可交换性并导致覆盖不可靠。现有直推式保形适配方法靠回避监督更新来保持有效性，但浪费了稀缺标注数据、限制任务适配。

Method: 提出AlignCP框架，在非可交换条件下调和监督式少样本适配与保形不确定性估计：学习一个重加权的校准分布，以缩减标注支持集与无标注查询集之间在分数层面的差异；通过对一维非一致性分数分布进行对齐，在不需要查询标签的情况下缩小由适配引起的覆盖差距。

Result: 摘要中未报告具体实验结果（数值、数据集、覆盖率指标等）。

Conclusion: 保形预测在少样本迁移中应作为支撑已适配模型的不确定性估计层，而非阻碍适配本身；AlignCP通过分数分布对齐在监督适配下恢复保形有效性，兼顾任务适配与可靠覆盖。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation for medical vision--language models (VLMs) using conformal prediction has gained increasing attention due to its distribution-free coverage guarantees. However, standard conformal prediction relies on exchangeability between calibration and test data and typically requires a sufficiently large calibration set to obtain reliable coverage. These assumptions are difficult to satisfy in few-shot transfer settings, where only a small labeled support set is available to adapt a pretrained VLM to a new medical task, while an unlabeled query set is used for evaluation. Supervised fine-tuning on the support set changes the model parameters and consequently shifts the nonconformity score distribution, breaking exchangeability between calibration and query samples and leading to unreliable coverage under distribution shift. Existing transductive conformal adaptation methods often preserve validity by avoiding supervised updates. While this helps maintain conformal assumptions, it underutilizes the scarce labeled support data and limits task adaptation, which is the primary objective in few-shot learning. In this setting, conformal prediction should serve as an uncertainty estimation layer that supports the adapted model, rather than preventing adaptation itself. To this end, we propose AlignCP, a framework that reconciles supervised few-shot adaptation with conformal uncertainty estimation under non-exchangeability. AlignCP learns a reweighted calibration distribution that reduces the score-level discrepancy between the labeled support set and the unlabeled query set. By aligning the one-dimensional nonconformity score distributions, AlignCP aims to close the coverage gap induced by adaptation without requiring query labels.

</details>


### [66] [Dimensionality Reduction for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10334)
*Mohamed Cherifi,Ammar Mesloub,Mohammed Nabil El Korso,Tayeb Touhami,Abdennour Hacine Gharbi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对高光谱卫星影像的监督分类问题，研究降维与分类器选择两个环节，比较PCA、LDA两种降维方法与KNN、SVM、RF三种监督分类算法，结果表明PCA结合RF取得最高总体精度与Kappa系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱数据波段数量多、维度高，存在内存占用大、计算耗时高的问题，且不同降维方法与分类器的组合效果差异明显，因此需要系统评估降维方法与监督分类技术的适用性。

Method: 采用两种常用降维方法——主成分分析（PCA）与线性判别分析（LDA）；并基于真实高光谱数据比较三种监督分类算法：K近邻（KNN）、支持向量机（SVM）与随机森林（RF）。

Result: 实验结果显示，PCA与RF的组合在总体精度（Overall Accuracy）和Kappa系数上均表现最优。

Conclusion: 在高光谱卫星影像监督分类中，采用PCA进行降维并配合随机森林分类器可获得最佳分类性能。

Abstract: This paper addresses the issue of supervised classification in the context of hyperspectral satellite images. It deals with two fundamental aspects: dimensionality reduction of data and the selection of appropriate supervised classification techniques.
  Firstly, we delve into dimensionality reduction, a critical step in simplifying the management of hyperspectral data. The reduction aims to decrease complexity in terms of memory and computing time. We examine two commonly used methods: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) and Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA).
  Subsequently, we explore the selection of the most suitable supervised classification algorithms for hyperspectral images. We compare the performance of three methods: K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), Support Vector Machines (SVM), and Random Forest (RF) using real hyperspectral data. The results highlight that the combination of PCA and RF yields the highest overall accuracy and Kappa coefficient.

</details>


### [67] [Beyond One-Size-Fits-All: Sample-Adaptive Strategy Routing for Vision Token Pruning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10346)
*Haiji Liang,Pengfei Zhou,Zhenglin Wan,Wei Wang,Yang You,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VIP-Router，一种轻量级视觉 token 剪枝路由器，针对每个输入自适应选择最优剪枝策略，在不修改原剪枝算法与模型权重的前提下显著提升剪枝敏感基准上的平均精度与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型每张图像需处理数百至数千个视觉 token，推理开销巨大。现有视觉 token 剪枝方法默认对所有输入统一采用单一固定剪枝策略，但作者分析发现：按平均基准精度对策略排序会掩盖样本级的互补性——平均最优策略虽然在整体上最好，却在相当比例的单个样本上被其他策略超越。这说明剪枝策略存在显著的输入依赖性，固定策略并非最优。

Method: 提出 VIP-Router（VIsion Pruning Router），一个轻量级路由器：以低成本的视觉与文本特征为条件，在给定剪枝率下预测并对每个输入自适应选择最合适的候选剪枝策略，同时在预测剪枝不利时保留全 token 推理选项。该方法为即插即用式，不修改底层剪枝算法与模型权重，可训练参数仅为骨干网络的约 0.017%。

Result: 在专门构建的剪枝敏感视觉感知基准 VTC-Bench Group A 上，VIP-Router 在所有压缩比下均优于最佳固定策略基线：平均精度相对提升 26.9%，计入实际 token 成本后的平均效用相对提升 22.0%。此外，该方法在多种 MLLM 骨架上均有效，并在未见基准上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 结果表明样本自适应路由在视觉 token 剪枝中具有巨大潜力，可在极低参数开销下以即插即用方式突破固定策略的局限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) process hundreds or thousands of visual tokens per image, incurring prohibitive inference costs. While existing vision token pruning methods mitigate this overhead, they implicitly assume that a single fixed pruning strategy can be applied uniformly across all inputs. Our analysis further reveals that ranking pruning methods by average benchmark accuracy conceals substantial sample-wise complementarity: although the average-best strategy excels overall, alternative strategies prove superior on a significant fraction of individual samples. To harness this diversity, we propose VIP-Router, a lightweight VIsion Pruning Router that adaptively selects the pruning strategy predicted to be best suited to each input at a specified pruning level. Conditioned on low-cost visual and textual features, VIP-Router identifies the most suitable candidate strategy while retaining full-token inference as an option when pruning is predicted to be unfavorable. Evaluated on a curated suite of pruning-sensitive visual perception benchmarks, VTC-Bench Group A, VIP-Router consistently outperforms the best fixed strategy baseline across all reduction ratios, achieving a 26.9% relative improvement in average accuracy, and a 22.0% relative increase in average utility after accounting for realized token cost. Crucially, VIP-Router operates in a plug-and-play manner without modifying underlying pruning algorithms or model weights, introducing trainable parameters equivalent to merely 0.017\% of the backbone. Furthermore, VIP-Router proves effective across various MLLM backbones and yields consistent gains on unseen benchmarks, highlighting the potential of sample adaptive routing for visual token pruning.

</details>


### [68] [Why Is Video Still So Expensive? A Survey of Inference-Efficiency Mechanisms in Video and Audiovisual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10355)
*Killian Steunou,Yannis Tevissen,Mounîm A. El Yacoubi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理面向视觉与视听VideoLLM的推理效率优化机制，按流水线阶段组织方法，并汇总可比条件下的精度-成本证据，指出视听效率与标准化评估的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VideoLLM在视频描述、问答、检索与时序定位等任务上表现强劲，但计算与内存开销随帧数和上下文长度增长，难以部署于实时、移动端及资源受限场景，因此需要系统梳理推理效率机制。

Method: 综述仅纳入报告了参数量、每输入FLOPs、时延、内存或视觉/音频token数具体降低的方法；分析帧采样、模态编码、连接器级token缩减、LLM预填充与解码各环节瓶颈；按方法作用的流水线阶段分类，覆盖2022年末以来的VideoLLM及更早的帧采样与视觉编码器机制；在共享宿主模型与输入协议下汇总文献报告的精度-成本对比，并与跨论文异构证据区分。

Result: 在统一宿主模型与输入协议下整合了可比的精度-成本数据，同时区分出异构的跨论文证据，并识别出视听效率与标准化评估方面的研究空白。

Conclusion: 该综述为VideoLLM推理效率研究提供了按流水线阶段组织的框架与可比证据基础，维护开源仓库以持续跟踪，并指明视听效率与标准化评测为未来方向。

Abstract: Video understanding has rapidly evolved toward video large language models (VideoLLMs): systems that couple video representations with pretrained large language models and condition generation on a textual prompt. Their strong performance on captioning, question answering, retrieval and temporal grounding comes at a computation and memory cost that grows with frame count and context length, limiting deployment in real-time, mobile and resource-constrained settings. This survey covers inference-efficiency mechanisms for visual and audiovisual VideoLLMs that report concrete reductions in parameter count, FLOPs per input, latency, memory, or visual and audio token count. We analyze bottlenecks across frame sampling, modality encoding, connector-level token reduction, and LLM prefilling and decoding. We organize methods by the pipeline stage at which they act, covering VideoLLMs developed since late 2022 together with earlier frame-sampling and vision-encoder mechanisms that remain components of current pipelines.
