Motivation: 非侵入式语音解码受限于神经记录的信噪比偏低，难以对音素或单个词进行细粒度重建。受神经科学证据启发——高层语义表征分布于大脑皮层且演化于较慢的时间尺度——作者假设语义内容比低层声学或词汇特征更适合作为非侵入式解码的目标。

Method: 提出Brain2Semantics2Text：先构建中间语义嵌入空间作为语义瓶颈，将句子级MEG响应映射到语义流形，再将其反演为自然语言，从而在无需词级对齐的情况下恢复高层语义。文中阐述了该方法的核心理念、实现方式，以及用于缓解学习可靠神经-语义映射困难的策略。

Result: 与先前非侵入式Brain2Text方法相比，该方法在句子级重建结果上取得提升。

Conclusion: 以语义内容为解码目标、借助中间语义嵌入瓶颈，可绕开低层声学/词汇特征的信噪比瓶颈，为非侵入式语音解码提供更可行的路径，并在句子级任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Non-invasive speech decoding remains constrained by the low signal-to-noise ratio of neural recordings, which makes fine-grained reconstruction of phonemes or individual words difficult. Motivated by neuroscientific evidence that high-level semantic representations are distributed across cortical regions and evolve over slower temporal scales, we hypothesize that semantic content may provide a more suitable target for non-invasive decoding than low-level acoustic or lexical features. We introduce Brain2Semantics2Text, a method that reconstructs text through an intermediate semantic embedding space. Our model maps sentence-level MEG responses into a semantic manifold and then inverts the predicted embeddings into natural language. This semantic bottleneck enables recovery of high-level meaning without word-level alignment. We describe the core principles of the approach, its implementation, and the strategies used to mitigate the challenges of learning a reliable neural-to-semantic mapping. Finally, we compare against prior non-invasive Brain2Text methods and show improved sentence-level results.

</details>


### [140] [RiLM: Parameter-Efficient Language Modeling via Geodesic Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10305)
*Fang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RiLM 框架，用黎曼流形上的测地线距离解码替代独立输出矩阵，在百万参数以下的小模型中显著降低困惑度，HypRiLM 在 WT-2 上达到 54.2 PPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万参数以下的语言模型对边缘部署、领域适配与可复现研究很重要，但两层 LSTM/Transformer 在嵌入宽度 d=128 时，输出矩阵 W_out ∈ R^(d×|V|) 仍占用约三分之一容量。作者希望完全移除该层，以释放参数预算。

Method: 提出 RiLM：上下文作为黎曼流形上的轨迹展开，下一词概率由当前状态与词表嵌入之间的测地线距离平方给出，输入与输出共享同一嵌入映射，解码即几何。在平坦 R^d（Flat RiLM）与 Poincaré 球 H^d（HypRiLM）上实例化，共享 MLP 组合映射 φ（约 290k 参数，d=128，|V|=2000）。针对朴素双曲循环中的边界塌缩问题，引入 Möbius 稳定化以恢复可训练性。

Result: 在 WikiText-2 上五个随机种子下，HypRiLM 验证困惑度为 54.2±0.2，Flat RiLM 为 87.6±0.6；绑定权重且参数量匹配的 LSTM、Transformer、SSM 基线维持在 113–147 PPL，HypRiLM 相对最强绑定循环基线（SSM，113.0±3.8）约领先 2 倍。Penn Treebank 与 10k 词表压力测试验证了测地线解码可跨语料与更大 |V| 迁移，双曲曲率带来选择性增益。

Conclusion: 测地线解码是移除输出矩阵的可行替代方案，双曲几何在受控小模型设定下带来明显收益，但结论仅限于受控小模型比较，而非全词表 SOTA。

Abstract: Language models under one million parameters matter for edge deployment, domain adaptation, and reproducible research, yet a two-layer LSTM or Transformer at embedding width d = 128 still spends roughly one third of its capacity on the output matrix W_out in R^(d x |V|). We propose Riemannian Language Models (RiLM), which remove that layer entirely: context unfolds as a trajectory on a Riemannian manifold, and next-token probabilities arise from squared geodesic distance between the current state and vocabulary embeddings. The same embedding map serves input and output -- decoding is geometry. We instantiate the framework on flat R^d (Flat RiLM) and the Poincare ball H^d (HypRiLM) with a shared MLP composition map phi (~290k parameters, d = 128, |V| = 2000). Across five seeds on WikiText-2, HypRiLM reaches 54.2 +/- 0.2 validation perplexity versus 87.6 +/- 0.6 for Flat RiLM; tied and matched LSTM, Transformer, and SSM controls remain at 113-147 PPL on WT-2 -- HypRiLM leads by roughly 2x over the strongest tied recurrent baseline (SSM, 113.0 +/- 3.8). Penn Treebank and a 10k-vocabulary stress test confirm that geodesic decoding transfers across corpora and larger |V|, while hyperbolic curvature helps selectively. We also characterize boundary collapse in naive hyperbolic recurrence and show how Mobius stabilization restores trainability. Claims are scoped to controlled small-model comparisons, not full-vocabulary state of the art.

</details>


### [141] [On-Policy Distillation for Vision-Language Model Adaptation, an Effective Paradigm on Low-Quality Multimodal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10321)
*Hongyuan Zhang,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Qianlong Yang,Yubin Guo,Pinhan Fu,Mulin Chen,Xiaozhen Qiao,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 OnPoKD，一种面向视觉语言模型适配的 on-policy 蒸馏框架，将蒸馏目标构建视为动态策略决策，通过轻量控制器生成样本自适应目标，在多个基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言蒸馏方法的训练目标通常由教师预测固定构建，并统一应用于所有样本，在类别偏移和域偏移下不可靠，缺乏对样本可靠性的自适应能力。

Method: OnPoKD 学习一个轻量控制器，利用教师模型、学生模型和零样本先验提供的可靠性与分歧线索，构建样本自适应的蒸馏目标；控制器通过有界策略动作动态权衡教师监督、零样本先验引导和硬标签锚定，并利用验证反馈更新，使目标构建优化可迁移性而非仅拟合训练分布；控制器仅在训练阶段使用。

Result: 在 Base-to-novel 泛化与跨数据集迁移基准上，OnPoKD 一致优于强视觉语言蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 蒸馏目标构建应被视为动态训练决策而非固定配方；OnPoKD 可无缝集成现有蒸馏流程，保持原推理架构与测试开销。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation offers an efficient route to transfer a task-adapted vision-language teacher to a compact student. The training target in current vision-language distillation methods is typically constructed from the teacher prediction and applied uniformly to all training samples, making it unreliable under class and domain shifts. In this paper, we argue that distillation target construction should be treated as a dynamic training decision rather than a fixed recipe. To this end, we propose OnPoKD, an on-policy distillation framework for vision-language model adaptation. To the best of our knowledge, OnPoKD is the first framework that applies on-policy distillation to vision-language model adaptation by learning target construction as a policy decision. OnPoKD learns a lightweight controller that constructs sample-wise adaptive targets using reliability and disagreement cues from the teacher model, student model, and zero-shot prior. Instead of relying on a fixed teacher prediction, the controller dynamically balances teacher supervision, zero-shot prior guidance, and hard-label anchoring through bounded policy actions, allowing the distillation target to adapt to varying sample reliability and training stages. The policy controller is updated with validation feedback, encouraging target construction to optimize transferability rather than merely fitting the training distribution. Since the controller is only used during training, OnPoKD can be seamlessly integrated into existing vision-language distillation pipelines while preserving the original inference architecture and test-time cost. Extensive experiments on Base-to-novel generalization and Cross-dataset transfer benchmarks show that OnPoKD consistently improves over strong vision-language distillation baselines.

</details>


### [142] [Rosetta at AlexandriaX-2026: LoRA-Adapted NileChat for Context-Aware Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10395)
*Nada Esmaeil,Fathima Rena,Sibi Subhash,Osama Elgendy,Mina Naguib,Salma Omar,Muhammad Arif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 AlexandriaX 共享任务子任务 1（上下文感知的英语到方言阿拉伯语对话翻译）的 Rosetta 系统，参加受限与不受限两个赛道。方法是在 NileChat-3B 上微调 LoRA 适配器，并以结构化系统/用户提示词将方言与对话上下文作为生成条件；不受限赛道额外在 MADAR 和 PADIC 上预训练该适配器。Rosetta 在受限赛道排名第 4（spBLEU 26.10），不受限赛道排名第 5（spBLEU 25.09）。实验表明外部预训练仅在 13 种方言中的 2 种上带来增益，整体性能略有下降，提示存在负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向方言阿拉伯语对话翻译任务，需在有限资源下提升上下文感知的英语到方言阿拉伯语翻译质量，并探究额外方言数据预训练是否能带来收益。

Method: 基于 NileChat-3B 微调 LoRA 适配器，使用结构化系统/用户提示词对方言与对话上下文进行条件建模；不受限赛道在 MADAR 与 PADIC 上进一步预训练该适配器。

Result: 受限赛道排名第 4，spBLEU 26.10；不受限赛道排名第 5，spBLEU 25.09。外部预训练仅在 13 种方言中的 2 种上有效，整体性能略有下降。

Conclusion: 结构化提示与 LoRA 微调可有效支撑方言对话翻译；额外外部数据预训练存在负迁移风险，整体收益有限。

Abstract: This paper describes the Rosetta system for Subtask 1 (Context-Aware English-to-Dialectal Arabic Dialogue Translation) of the AlexandriaX shared task, participating in both constrained and unconstrained tracks. The approach fine-tunes a LoRA adapter on NileChat-3B using structured system/user prompts that condition generation on dialect and dialogue context. For the unconstrained track, the adapter is additionally pretrained on MADAR and PADIC. Rosetta ranked 4th in the constrained track (spBLEU 26.10) and 5th in the unconstrained track (spBLEU 25.09). The experimental results demonstrate that external pretraining helps only two of thirteen dialects while slightly hurting overall performance, suggesting negative transfer.

</details>


### [143] [Can Foundation Models Moderate Online Content? Evaluating Instruction- vs. Example-Driven Policy Operationalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10410)
*Ayan Majumdar,Shounak Paul,Pushpdeep Singh,Ines Abdelaziz,Sayeh Jarollahi,Seungeon Lee,Krishna P. Gummadi,Ingmar Weber,Abhisek Dash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了指令驱动与示例驱动两种VLM内容审核范式，并构建ModerationBench基准（4,000条Bluesky真实帖子），发现基础模型显著优于平台现用审核系统（F1 0.60 vs 0.22），两种范式峰值效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容审核政策日益复杂，其一致性落地执行面临关键挑战。基础模型具备应对该挑战的基本能力，但能否可靠地用于在线内容审核尚无定论，因此需系统评估不同指导范式的实际效果。

Method: 构建ModerationBench基准，包含来自Bluesky平台的4,000条人工标注真实帖子；在此基准上系统比较两种VLM指导范式：一是指令驱动（模型依据政策准则进行推理），二是示例驱动（模型从既有判例中泛化）；并与Bluesky已部署的审核系统进行对比。

Result: 基础模型在基准的Random Posts上大幅超越Bluesky现用审核系统，F1分数接近其三倍（0.60 vs. 0.22）；指令驱动与示例驱动两种范式达到的峰值效果相当。

Conclusion: 基础模型可有效且可靠地支撑内容审核政策的规模化落地，指令驱动与示例驱动范式均具备实用潜力，为可扩展、可适应的政策运营化指明了路径。

Abstract: The growing complexity of content moderation policies presents a critical challenge for their consistent operationalization. While foundation models possess the basic capabilities needed to confront this challenge, whether they can reliably moderate online content remains an unanswered question. In this paper, we systematically compare two competing paradigms for Vision-Language Model (VLM) guidance: an instruction-driven approach where models reason from policy precepts, and an example-driven approach where they generalize from prior precedents. We ground this investigation in ModerationBench, a new benchmark of 4,000 manually annotated, in-the-wild posts from the Bluesky platform. Our experiments reveal that foundation models can substantially outperform Bluesky's deployed moderation system, nearly tripling its $F_1$ score (0.60 vs. 0.22) on Random Posts in the benchmark, with both instruction- and example-driven paradigms achieving comparable peak effectiveness. Our findings thus chart a path toward reliable and adaptable policy operationalization at scale.

</details>


### [144] [Do speech foundation models really learn words?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10434)
*Robin Huo,Ewan Dunbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过残差化剔除音素信息后发现，HuBERT 与 wav2vec 2.0 的较深层表示确实能以独立于局部语音内容的方式编码单词，且该解耦方法可增强词发现任务中的高阶语言信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音基础模型广泛用于下游任务，但对其有用性的理解多聚焦于表示对音素和单词的判别能力。然而单词判别能力好未必意味着存在专门的单词表示：可能只是对词形（音素）编码良好，而非编码单词身份或句法/语义等与形式无关的表示。

Method: 采用残差化（residualization）方法，将音素信息从表示中偏回归（partial out）剔除，以检验模型表示在去除局部语音内容后是否仍能编码单词。

Result: 在 HuBERT 和 wav2vec 2.0 的较深层中，模型总体上学习到了以合理保真度编码单词的表示，且这种编码独立于局部语音内容。

Conclusion: 这种简单的解耦方法能够增强词发现任务中的高阶语言信息。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech foundation models are now used in a wide array of downstream applications, including traditional speech recognition and as the basis for tokens in speech-aware language models. Attempts to understand their usefulness have largely focused on probing their representations' ability to discriminate phonemes and words. However, discriminative ability for words need not imply specialized representation of words per se. Good discrimination of words may be explained by good encoding of word form (phonemes) rather than form-independent word representations encoding identity or syntactic/semantic properties. By partialling out phoneme information using residualization, we show that, in later layers, HuBERT and wav2vec 2.0 do in general learn representations which encode words with reasonable fidelity independently of local phonetic content. We show that this simple approach to disentanglement can enhance higher-order linguistic information in word discovery tasks.

</details>


### [145] [Building Multilingual Bridges: Data Mixing as the Pillar of Generalization for In-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10445)
*Mehrnaz Mofakhami,Ananya Sahu,Alejandro R. Salamanca,Daniel D'souza,Alexandre Berard,Thomas Euyang,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出L2推理（在用户提示语言中一致推理）的目标，从数据角度优化SFT的数据组成与调度，训练3.35B的Tiny Aya L2-Thinker，在60种语言、6类基准上实现超过93%的L2推理率，并公开模型权重与多语言推理数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型虽在复杂任务上表现优异，但能力高度英语中心化：无论提示语言为何，模型主要以英语推理。这导致非英语用户难以使用、可能丢失原始问题意图，并无法利用目标语言中更易表达的知识。因此需要让模型在用户提示语言中进行一致推理，在提示与答案之间建立语言内的桥梁。

Method: 从数据中心化角度出发，研究SFT中数据组成与调度如何优化推理泛化；训练3.35B规模的Tiny Aya L2-Thinker模型，涉及多语言数据配比、调度策略以及英语推理骨干与多语言非推理数据的结合。

Result: 在60种语言、覆盖数学、常识推理、指令遵循、开放式生成与文化推理的6个基准上，L2推理率超过93%，同时保持较强性能。将L2推理泛化到未见语言的关键在于：更广的语言覆盖、现成可用的多语言非推理数据，以及足够的英语推理骨干。

Conclusion: 推理是一种语言无关的行为，可通过精心的数据混合在类型学上多样的语言间迁移，而无需为每种目标语言提供推理监督。研究公开了模型权重与多语言推理数据，以支持可及的语言内推理研究。

Abstract: Reasoning language models have made substantial advances on a variety of complex tasks, yet their capabilities remain overwhelmingly English-centric: models primarily reason in English regardless of the language they are prompted in. This is inaccessible for non-English-speaking users, risks losing the intent of the original question, and forgoes knowledge more readily expressed in the target language. In this work, we advance L2 reasoning, the ability of a model to reason consistently in the language of the user's prompt, thus building an in-language bridge between the prompt and the answer. We approach this problem from a data-centric angle, investigating how to optimize data composition and scheduling in SFT for reasoning generalization. Building Tiny Aya L2-Thinker at 3.35B scale, we achieve an L2 reasoning rate above 93% across 60 languages on 6 benchmarks spanning math, commonsense reasoning, instruction following, open-ended generation, and cultural reasoning while keeping performance strong. We show the path to generalizing L2 reasoning to held-out languages goes through broader language coverage, readily available multilingual non-reasoning data, and a sufficient English reasoning backbone. These findings indicate that reasoning is a language-agnostic behavior that can be transferred across typologically diverse languages through careful data mixing and without requiring reasoning supervision in every target language. We release our model weights and multilingual reasoning data to support further research on accessible, in-language reasoning.

</details>


### [146] [IBIB: A Protocol for Measuring Enterprise AI Systems by Serving Route, Not Model Identifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10494)
*Blake Stenstrom,Charangan Vasantharajan,Brian Sathianathan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文将"以模型标识符为单位的基准评测"视为测量误差，提出 IB2 协议（gold-blind 能力绑定预检、含可靠性的首轮评分、结构性盲评裁决），并用 128 个锁定任务、987 条断言、11 个系统的参考实例给出四项结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署的是系统而非检查点，可用能力同时取决于权重、服务路由、精度、输出契约与评测夹（harness），但现有 18 个受审基准都只对宣传的模型标识符打分，导致测量不可报告、结论可能被误导。

Method: 提出 IB2 协议，包含三部分：(1) gold-blind 能力绑定预检，在任务到达前验证某条路由能否执行评测契约；(2) 可靠性包含式首轮评分规则，既把失败保留在分数中，又把不支持的能力排除在外；(3) 结构上分数盲的裁决。公开算法、分类表、请求契约与清单模式；参考实例保持封存，程序即产物而非语料。

Result: 四项结果：(1) 能力可用性可测量——相同权重的两次完整单路由运行随后未通过最终绑定门的不同谓词，第三次在新一轮前通过，而宣传标识符未暴露任一限制；(2) 区分度不均衡——七个套件中四个在六系统带宽下饱和，差异几乎全部来自受控数据库工作与多表连接，故报告带区间的分辨率分组而非排名，名义五标签输出的四个切点中有两个未通过多重性校正；(3) 服务臂选择使某一声明修订与精度从 77.38 变为 82.54，配对区间 [0.11,10.60]，但各臂在访问模式、harness 代数与服务工具调用解析器上不同，且 harness 代数属评测方属性而非端点属性；(4) 将失败响应排除出分母会改变点排序，即可靠性包含改变的是结论而非措辞。

Conclusion: 以模型标识符为中心的评测存在系统性测量误差，IB2 使能力绑定、可靠性与裁决过程可报告；评测应报告区间支撑的分辨率分组而非排名，并显式纳入失败响应与服务臂差异。

Abstract: Enterprises deploy systems, not checkpoints. Usable capability depends jointly on weights, serving route, precision, output contract, and harness, yet all 18 audited benchmarks score advertised model identifiers. We treat this as measurement error and give a protocol that makes it reportable. It has three parts. A gold-blind capability-binding preflight verifies that a route can execute the evaluation contract before any task reaches it; a reliability-inclusive first-pass scoring rule keeps failure in the score while keeping unsupported capability out; and adjudication is structurally score-blind. We call the protocol IB2 and release its algorithms, classification tables, request contract, and manifest schemas. Its reference instantiation, 128 locked tasks and 987 assertions over document, spreadsheet, chart, tool and database work, stays sealed: the procedure is the artifact, not the corpus. Across eleven systems, four results. Capability availability is measurable: two complete single-route runs on identical weights later failed distinct predicates of the finalized binding gate, while a third passed that gate before a fresh run. The advertised identifier exposed neither limit. Discrimination is not uniform: four of seven suites saturate under a six-system band, with the spread almost entirely from governed database work and multi-tab joins, so we report interval-backed resolution groups, not ranks; two of the nominal five-label output's four cuts fail multiplicity adjustment. Serving-arm choice moved one declared revision and precision from 77.38 to 82.54, paired interval [0.11,10.60], though the arms differ in access mode, harness generation, and the serving tool-call parser, and harness generation is a property of our evaluator, not any endpoint. Excluding failed responses from denominators changes the point ordering, so reliability inclusion changes a conclusion, not its wording.

</details>


### [147] [IdeaAMBIG: Benchmarking Implementation-Critical Gaps in Research-Idea Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10539)
*Yiling Ma,Yilun Zhao,Sihong Wu,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出“实现级研究方法规格的可编码就绪性”概念，并构建IdeaAMBIG基准（660实例），评估LLM在缺陷定位与澄清动作生成上的能力，发现缺陷定位是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究想法即使新颖、连贯且科学上可行，其方法规格仍可能不足以被忠实实现；现有研究缺乏对“方法规格是否足以支撑实现”的系统评估与基准。

Method: 从论文、代码库、issue讨论和复现工件中构建有证据支撑的规格及其支持性解决方案；提出IdeaAMBIG基准，含163个来自复现报告与GitHub issues的真实缺口，以及497个注入到“可编码就绪”参考文献中的受控合成缺口；评估三项能力：可编码就绪性评估、缺陷定位、澄清动作生成。

Result: 在13个LLM上，最佳模型在真实实例上的宏缺陷恢复率仅9.6%；但在给定缺陷后，宏澄清动作成功率达80.6%。Oracle研究中，提供金标准解决方案可将下游可编码就绪率从14%提升至98%。

Conclusion: 缺陷定位是主要瓶颈，而给定缺陷后模型澄清能力较强；未来工作应重点提升对方法规格中实现缺口的自动识别与定位能力。

Abstract: A research idea may be novel, coherent, and scientifically plausible, yet its proposed method may remain insufficiently specified for faithful implementation. We study the codification readiness of implementation-facing research-method specifications, defined by whether they provide sufficient methodological information for a competent implementer or coding agent to construct the intended method without unsupported assumptions. We construct evidence-grounded specifications and their supported resolutions from papers, codebases, issue threads, and reproduction artifacts. We introduce IdeaAMBIG, a benchmark of 660 evidence-grounded instances: 163 real-world gaps from reproducibility reports and GitHub issues, and 497 controlled synthetic gaps injected into codification-ready references. IdeaAMBIG evaluates three capabilities: codification-readiness assessment, defect localization, and clarification action generation. Defect localization receives only the specification, whereas clarification additionally receives the annotated defect. Across 13 LLMs, the best model achieves 9.6% Macro Defect Recovery Rate on real-world instances but 80.6% Macro Clarification Action Success Rate when given the defect. In an oracle study, supplying the gold resolution raises the downstream codification-ready rate from 14% to 98%. Across all evaluated models, defect localization is the main bottleneck, with stronger clarification given the defect.

</details>


### [148] [Every Activation Boosted: Scaling General Reasoner to 1 Trillion Open Language Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22115)
*Ling Team,Ang Li,Ben Liu,Binbin Hu,Bing Li,Bingwei Zeng,Borui Ye,Caizhi Tang,Changxin Tian,Chao Huang,Chao Zhang,Chen Qian,Chenchen Ju,Chenchen Li,Chengfu Tang,Chilin Fu,Chunshao Ren,Chunwei Wu,Cong Zhang,Cunyin Peng,Dafeng Xu,Daixin Wang,Dalong Zhang,Dingnan Jin,Dingyuan Zhu,Dongke Hu,Fangzheng Zhao,Feifan Wu,Feng Zhu,Gangshan Wang,Haitao Zhang,Hailin Zhao,Hanxiao Zhang,Hanzi Wang,Hao Qian,Haoyi Yu,Heng Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Hongzhi Luan,Huirong Dong,Huizhong Li,Jia Li,Jia Liu,Jialong Zhu,Jian Sha,Jianping Wei,Jiaolong Yang,Jieyue Ma,Jiewei Wu,Jinjing Huang,Jingyun Tian,Jingyuan Zhang,Jinquan Sun,Juanhui Tu,Jun Liu,Jun Xu,Jun Zhou,Junjie Ou,Junpeng Fang,Kaihong Zhang,Kaiqin Hu,Ke Shi,Kun Tang,Kunlong Chen,Lanyin Mei,Lei Liang,Lei Xu,Libo Zhang,Lin Ju,Lin Yuan,Ling Zhong,Lintao Ma,Lu Liu,Lu Yu,Lun Cai,Meiqi Zhu,Mengying Li,Min Chen,Minghao Xue,Minghong Cai,Mingming Yin,Peijie Jiang,Peilong Zhao,Pingping Liu,Qian Zhao,Qing Cui,Qingxiang Huang,Qingyuan Yang,Quankun Yu,Shaowei Wei,Shijie Lian,Shoujian Zheng,Shun Song,Shungen Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Siyuan Li,Song Liu,Ting Guo,Tong Zhao,Wanli Gu,Weichang Wu,Weiguang Han,Wenjing Fang,Wubin Wang,Xiang Shu,Xiao Shi,Xiaoshun Lan,Xiaolu Zhang,Xiaqing Sun,Xin Zhao,Xingyu Lu,Xiong Xu,Xudong Wang,Xudong Wang,Xuemin Yang,Yajie Yang,Yang Xiang,Yanzhe Li,Yi Zhang,Yilong Wang,Yingxue Li,Yongzhen Guo,Yuzhuo Fu,Yuanyuan Wang,Yue Yang,Yue Yu,Yufeng Deng,Yun Zhang,Yunfei Yu,Yuqi Zhang,Yuxiao He,Zengke Gui,Zhaoxin Huan,Zhaoyang Wang,Zhibo Zhu,Zhihao Wang,Zhiqiang Zhang,Zhoufei Wang,Zihang Zeng,Ziqi Liu,Zitao Xuan,Zuoli Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ling 2.0 是一系列面向推理的语言基础模型，覆盖 16B 至 1T 总参数规模，采用高稀疏 MoE 架构与统一训练范式，在推理精度与计算效率之间建立新的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型在扩展参数规模时面临计算效率瓶颈，稠密架构的激活计算开销难以支撑从数十亿到万亿参数的规模化推理需求。本文旨在通过高稀疏 MoE 与推理目标对齐，实现可扩展且高效的智能。

Method: 基于"每次激活都提升推理能力"原则构建统一 MoE 范式，强调高稀疏性、跨规模一致性与经验缩放律指导的效率；系列包含 Ling-mini-2.0、Ling-flash-2.0、Ling-1T 三个非思考（instruct）模型；在架构、预训练、后训练与基础设施层面协同创新，包括带 MTP 的高稀疏 MoE、推理导向数据与中训练 CoT 激活、基于强化的微调（DFT、Evo-CoT），以及全规模 FP8 训练与细粒度异构流水线。

Result: 模型总参数范围 16B 至 1T，相比稠密对应模型实现最高约 7 倍的激活计算效率；万亿规模的 Ling-1T 在推理精度与计算效率之间建立了新的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: Ling 2.0 表明，当稀疏激活与推理目标正确对齐时，可实现可扩展且高效的智能，为后续推理与思考模型（包括基于同一基座的 Ring 系列）提供了连贯、开放且高效的基础。

Abstract: We introduce Ling 2.0, a series reasoning-oriented language foundation built upon the principle that every activation boosts reasoning capability. Designed to scale from tens of billions to one trillion parameters under a unified Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) paradigm, Ling 2.0 emphasizes high sparsity, cross-scale consistency, and efficiency guided by empirical scaling laws. The series includes three non-thinking (instruct) models - Ling-mini-2.0, Ling-flash-2.0, and Ling-1T - ranging from 16B to 1T total parameters and achieving up to 7-fold active-compute efficiency compared with dense counterparts. Ling 2.0 integrates coordinated innovations across model architecture, pre-training, post-training, and infrastructure: a high-sparsity MoE with MTP for efficient reasoning, reasoning-oriented data and mid-training CoT activation, reinforcement-based fine-tuning (DFT, Evo-CoT), and full-scale FP8 training with fine-grained heterogeneous pipelines. At the trillion scale, Ling-1T establishes a new Pareto frontier of reasoning accuracy versus computational efficiency, demonstrating that sparse activation, when properly aligned with reasoning objectives, enables scalable and efficient intelligence. Collectively, Ling 2.0 provides a coherent, open, and efficient foundation for advancing future reasoning and thinking models, including the Ring series built upon the same base.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Multi-Task Bacterial Colony Detection and Classification Using YOLOv8 with Edge Optimization for Resource-Constrained Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09818)
*Belaguppa Manjunath Ashwin Desai,Rohan Rajesh,Shreyas Murthy,Pronama Biswas,Revathi Vaithiyanathan*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 该研究基于AGAR数据集，采用YOLOv8高分辨率检测框架实现菌落检测与分类，分类准确率98.13%、计数准确率98.27%；并通过剪枝与量化优化，在树莓派4B上平衡速度与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工菌落计数与分类工作量大、易出错，尤其在密集平板上。研究旨在实现菌落形成单位（CFU）计数与菌种分类的自动化，并解决模型在实际资源受限设备上的部署问题。

Method: 以AGAR数据集（18,000张图像；9,202训练/3,067测试）为基础。先构建多任务CNN全局回归基线，但因缺乏空间定位在密集菌落场景表现受限；改采用YOLOv8目标检测架构，输入分辨率为1024×1024，实现实例级菌落检测与标签分配。随后通过非结构化与结构化剪枝、ONNX转换及FP32/FP16/INT8降精度推理进行优化。

Result: YOLOv8模型分类准确率98.13%，计数准确率98.27%（10菌落误差范围内）。树莓派4B上，ONNX FP32与FP16在速度（约6.4s）与精度（MAE约2.20）间取得最佳平衡；非结构化剪枝保持精度（MAE约2.01）但无运行时收益；结构化剪枝精度显著下降（MAE约6.3），表明实例级菌落检测对结构压缩较敏感。

Conclusion: 实例级检测优于全局回归基线，高分辨率YOLOv8可获得高精度计数与分类。资源受限部署中，ONNX FP32/FP16是较优选择；非结构化剪枝可保精度但难提速，结构化剪枝会明显损害精度，为实验室边缘部署的优化策略选择提供实践指导。

Abstract: Manual counting and classification of bacterial colonies are critical yet labor-intensive tasks in microbiology, prone to human error particularly on densely populated plates. This work proposes a multi-task deep learning framework trained on the Annotated Germs for Automated Recognition (AGAR) dataset (18,000 images; 9,202 training / 3,067 testing) to automate Colony Forming Unit (CFU) enumeration and species classification. A custom multi-task CNN employing global regression served as the baseline, but demonstrated limited performance in clustered colony environments due to the absence of spatial localization. To address this, a YOLOv8 object detection architecture was adopted with high-resolution 1024x1024 inputs, enabling instance-level colony detection and label assignment. The model achieved a classification accuracy of 98.13% and a counting accuracy of 98.27% (within a 10-colony margin), demonstrating strong predictive capability. To bridge the gap between model performance and practical deployability, the trained model was optimized through unstructured and structured pruning, ONNX conversion, and reduced-precision inference (FP32, FP16, INT8). On a Raspberry Pi 4B, ONNX FP32 and FP16 variants offered the best balance between inference speed (~6.4s) and accuracy (MAE ~2.20). Unstructured pruning preserved predictive accuracy (MAE ~2.01) without runtime gains, while structured pruning resulted in significant accuracy degradation (MAE ~6.3), revealing the sensitivity of instance-level colony detection to architectural compression. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting optimization strategies in resource-constrained laboratory deployments.

</details>


### [150] [ADMET-EvO: a self-evolving scientific agent for sustained research across heterogeneous tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10121)
*Yiling Zhou,Yilin Wang,Jianmin Wang,Heqin Zhu,Zirui Wang,Chang-yu Hiesh,Kejun Ying,Jiaqi Wang,Yuzhi Xu,Tingjun Hou,Odin Zhang*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: ADMET-EvO 是一个"证据门控"科学智能体，用累积证据修正问题与实验策略，在 22 项 TDC ADMET 基准上取得最高任务归一化分数 96.77，并将累积拟合时间减少 72.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学智能体若仅做自动化建模，难以适应异构任务；核心挑战在于跨任务持续适应，同时避免将决策过拟合到内部验证集。ADMET 预测涉及多样化的检测方法、数据集与化学域，是检验该能力的严苛场景。

Method: 提出 ADMET-EvO：将终点形式化、生成可证伪假设，并在数据、特征与模型三个轴上测试干预；把"支持/否定/不确定"三类结果携带到下一轮循环，以证据门控机制筛选策略而非依赖内部验证分数。

Result: 在 22 项 TDC ADMET 基准上取得最高任务归一化分数 96.77；证据引导的选择在预设非劣性边界内将累积拟合时间降低 72.2%；另有 43 项毒性相关任务被形式化并构建了终点专属预测器。

Conclusion: 结果表明 ADMET-EvO 能够累积证据、修正策略并随时间扩展研究范围，为超越自动化建模的自适应科学智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scientific agents can move beyond automated model building by using accumulated evidence to revise both their questions and experimental strategies. The challenge is sustaining this adaptation across heterogeneous tasks without overfitting decisions to internal validation. Absorption, distribution, metabolism, excretion and toxicity (ADMET) prediction provides a demanding setting across diverse assays, datasets and chemical domains. We therefore developed ADMET-EvO, an evidence-gated agent that formalizes endpoints, generates falsifiable hypotheses and tests interventions across data, feature and model axes. It carries supported, rejected and inconclusive outcomes forward to guide each new cycle. Across the 22-task Therapeutics Data Commons (TDC) ADMET benchmark, ADMET-EvO achieved the highest task-normalized score of 96.77. Evidence-guided selection reduced cumulative fitting time by 72.2% within a predefined non-inferiority margin. It also formalized 43 toxicity-related tasks and constructed endpoint-specific predictors. Together, these results show how ADMET-EvO can accumulate evidence, revise its strategy and expand its research scope over time.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Shift-Accumulate Attention: Multiplier-Free Query--Key Products for Transformer Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09208)
*Khubaib Ahmed,Amna Noor,Ahsan Ul haq*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文将键缓存量化为带符号的2的幂（PoT）定点编码，使QK^T中每次标量乘法变为符号翻转、位移和整数累加，并配合亚幂尾数扩展、位移精确的在线softmax及对数量化AV乘积，在1.1B Llama上实现了显著的加速与KV缓存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Power-of-two量化仅用于post-softmax的attention-value乘积，而更早且计算量更大的QK^T乘积尚未被同样重构。作者希望消除乘法器，用位移-累加替代标量乘法，从而提升注意力的效率并压缩KV缓存。

Method: 将键缓存量化为带符号PoT定点码；用定点head-room F>=e_max使每个移位指数r=F-e非负，从而在整数运算中精确累加；引入亚幂尾数扩展以一半的score误差换取一次额外移位-加；设计位移精确的在线softmax，其运行最大值位于整数log2域以保证重缩放为精确移位；并对AV乘积采用对数量化。实现为融合CUDA内核。

Result: 在RTX 4090上的1.1B Llama解码器：B=8、T=32k时4-bit内核比FP16缩放点积注意力快4.60倍，KV缓存小2.5倍；批解码在B=64时达到FP16吞吐的1.22倍，峰值内存11.02GB对13.77GB。匹配的INT8乘累加内核达5.29倍，说明在有硬件4路INT8点积的GPU上，位移-累加并不比其替代的MAC更快。等存储控制实验（每键1字节的8-bit位移码）将算术替代本身的收益定价为2.02倍且精度相当。指数宽度、尾数水平、粒度和舍入的扫描表明：最近PoT相对误差与尺度无关，因此额外指数位无益（3、4、5位时eps_S=0.1302），精度必须来自尾数项。

Conclusion: PoT表示的优势在于其密度和消除乘法器，而非原始GPU吞吐；精度提升需依赖尾数项而非指数位。

Abstract: Power-of-two (PoT) quantisation turns a multiplication into a bit shift, so far only for the post-softmax attention--value product. The earlier and larger product, S=QK^T, has not been reformulated the same way. We quantise the key cache to a signed power-of-two fixed-point code, so that every scalar multiplication in QK^T becomes a sign flip, a bit shift and an integer accumulation. A fixed-point head-room F>= e_max turns every shift index r=F-e into a non-negative left shift, making the accumulation exact in integer arithmetic. We add a sub-power mantissa extension that halves the score error for one extra shift-add, a shift-exact online softmax whose running maximum lives in an integer log2 domain so that every rescaling is itself an exact shift, and a log-quantised AV product.
  Fused CUDA kernels in a 1.1B Llama decoder on an RTX 4090: at B=8, T=32k the 4-bit kernel is 4.60x faster than FP16 scaled dot-product attention with a 2.5x smaller KV cache, and batched decoding crosses over at B=64 to 1.22x FP16 throughput at 11.02 against 13.77 GB peak memory. A matched INT8 multiply--accumulate kernel on the same tiling reaches 5.29x: on a GPU with a hardware 4-way INT8 dot product, shift-accumulate is not faster than the MAC it replaces. An iso-storage control--an 8-bit shift code of exactly one byte per key, run through the identical kernel--prices the arithmetic substitution alone at 2.02x at comparable accuracy. The advantage of the PoT representation is therefore its density and the removal of the multiplier, not raw GPU throughput. A sweep over exponent width, mantissa levels, granularity and rounding gives the design lesson: nearest-PoT relative error is scale-free, so extra exponent bits buy nothing (eps_S=0.1302 at 3, 4 and 5 bits) and accuracy must come from mantissa terms.

</details>


### [152] [Academia x Industry: The Role of Fundamentals for Silicon in an AI Native Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09344)
*Vincent T. Lee,Armin Alaghi,Carole-Jean Wu,Sai Zhang,Brandon Reagen,Thierry Tambe,Jean Boufarhat,Matheus Trevisan Moreira*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文从学术界与工业界硅工程从业者的联合视角，阐述了Agentic AI对硅设计与工程带来的变革性影响，强调在迈向AI原生硅设计时代的过程中，核心硅设计基础知识的重新重要性，并呼吁社区汇聚多元观点以推动转型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI正成为一代人中最具变革性的技术之一，正在实时改变硅设计与工程的方式，但快速变化的格局使学术界与工业界从业者难以适应AI原生硅设计时代。作者希望为这一转型提供结构化视角，梳理挑战、机遇与考量，帮助社区顺利过渡。

Method: 本文采用学术界与工业界硅从业者的联合观点综述形式，而非具体技术实验。作者基于双方实践经验，分析AI原生硅设计转型中的挑战、机会与关键考量，并着重论证核心硅设计基础知识在学术培养、科研与工业实践中的重新重要性。

Result: 论文提出的核心论点是：在AI原生硅设计时代，硅设计基础原理必须被重新强调，其在学术训练、研究和工业实践中的重要性不降反升。作者还指出，不同利益相关者的具体技术细节会有所差异，因此社区汇聚多样化观点具有重要价值。

Conclusion: 本文通过提供来自学术界与工业界的互补视角，旨在帮助从业者解读、启发并推动向AI原生时代的转型。作者预期会出现许多相似或重叠的观点，但强调具体技术细节因主体而异，呼吁社区积累多元视角以促进共同进步。

Abstract: Agentic AI is set to become one of the most transformational technologies in generations and materially change how we approach silicon design and engineering. The impact is being felt in real time amid a rapidly changing landscape, which can make it overwhelming for both silicon practitioners and academics to adapt to the AI native silicon design era. To add structure to how we navigate this transition, we provide a joint view from academia and industry silicon practitioners of the challenges, opportunities, and considerations we expect will catalyze how the community transitions into an AI native silicon future. In particular, we reemphasize the importance of core silicon design fundamentals in academic training and why they have renewed importance in research and industry practice for AI native silicon design. It is our hope that the views provided here will offer valuable and complementary perspectives to those in academia and industry to interpret, inform, and catalyze the transition to the AI native era. We expect that many similar and overlapping views will emerge, but the precise technical details will differ across stakeholders, so it is valuable for the community to amass a diversity of viewpoints.

</details>


### [153] [HLSFactory-Agent: Large-Scale Agentic HLS Dataset Construction from Academic and Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09519)
*Kaushik Chandana,Jay Imperatori,Tanmay Shukla,Justin Zhou,Stefan Abi-Karam,Callie Hao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出 HLSFactory-Agent，用 LLM 智能体自动化大规模 HLS 数据集策展，从大型代码库中抽取可独立综合的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向硬件设计的深度学习与 LLM 需要大规模、多样化的 HLS 设计数据集，用于训练 QoR 模型及评测 LLM 在 HLS 任务上的表现。然而现有数据集来源仍局限于社区基准，策展依赖人工：需在学术论文与开源资源中定位 HLS 设计，再从大型代码库中剥离出独立设计，过程易出错且依赖专家知识、反复测试与大量按仓库定制工程。

Method: HLSFactory-Agent 是一个 LLM 智能体，在 Docker 容器内运行开源 Pi agent 框架，对每个抽取出的设计进行构建与评估。用户只需提供 GitHub 链接或代码目录，即可获得可直接并入 HLSFactory 数据集框架的抽取后 HLS 设计文件夹。此外提供开源脚本，抓取并索引计算机体系结构、EDA、FPGA 会议中可能实现或使用 HLS 设计的论文，加速人工发现与策展。

Result: 在已索引仓库的一小部分子集上运行 HLSFactory-Agent，初步结果表明可从结构化代码库中成功抽取可综合的设计。

Conclusion: HLSFactory-Agent 与索引脚本已开源（https://github.com/sharc-lab/hlsfactory-agent），为大规模 HLS 数据集策展提供了自动化、开箱即用的方案。

Abstract: Building large, diverse datasets of high-level synthesis (HLS) designs beyond common community benchmarks remains an open challenge. This challenge is made urgent by the rise of deep learning and LLMs for hardware design, which demand such datasets to train QoR models and benchmark LLMs on HLS tasks. Despite ongoing efforts to broaden sources, dataset curation still depends on manual work: locating HLS designs across academic publications and open source, then extracting standalone designs from larger codebases. The process is error-prone and demands expert knowledge, iterative testing, and substantial per-repository engineering.
  To address this, we present HLSFactory-Agent, an LLM agent that automates large-scale HLS dataset curation by extracting standalone designs from larger codebases. HLSFactory-Agent runs the open-source Pi agent framework inside Docker containers to build and evaluate each extracted design. This turnkey automation allows users to pass a GitHub link or code directory to HLSFactory-Agent and receive a folder of extracted HLS designs ready to be integrated into the HLSFactory dataset framework. Additionally, we provide open-source scripts to scrape and index papers from computer architecture, EDA, and FPGA conferences that possibly implement or use HLS designs, allowing for faster human discovery and curation of HLS designs for HLSFactory-Agent.
  We report initial results from running HLSFactory-Agent across a small subset of our indexed repositories, demonstrating successful extraction of synthesizable designs from structured codebases.
  We open source HLSFactory-Agent and indexing scripts at https://github.com/sharc-lab/hlsfactory-agent.

</details>


### [154] [Benchmarking Agentic HLS Design Tasks With HLS-Eval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09526)
*Stefan Abi-Karam,Callie Hao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 将 HLS-Eval 扩展为基于 mini-swe-agent 的智能体评测流程，初步结果显示开源 LLM 配合智能体框架可解决所有简单 HLS 代码生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 硬件设计评测（HLS-Eval）仅关注零样本生成与编辑，未考察智能体（agent）如何完成 HLS 设计任务，因此需要引入智能体评测流程以填补这一空白。

Method: 在 HLS-Eval 基础上构建智能体评测流程，基于开源 mini-swe-agent 框架，允许设计智能体使用文件编辑工具、调用 C++ 编译器自验证并在推理中迭代优化，同时记录智能体轨迹以分析成本、token 用量和迭代次数。

Result: 在现有 HLS-Eval 基准上，开源 LLM 配合智能体框架可解决所有简单 HLS 代码生成任务；通过分析成功与失败轨迹，揭示了模型规模、token 用量和轨迹长度与设计通过率之间的关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为智能体化 HLS 设计奠定基础，说明随着模型能力提升需扩展基准难度并开发新的智能体工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and AI agents are increasingly explored for hardware design, including high-level digital design. While most work targets code generation and editing for hardware description languages (HDLs), our prior work introduced HLS-Eval, an open-source benchmark for evaluating LLMs on high-level synthesis (HLS) design tasks. Those evaluations, however, focused on zero-shot generation and editing, leaving open how agents achieve HLS design tasks.
  We therefore extend HLS-Eval with an agentic evaluation flow built on the open-source mini-swe-agent framework. The flow lets HLS design agents use file-editing tools, invoke a C++ compiler for self-verification, and iteratively refine designs during inference, while logging agent traces for analysis of cost, token usage, and iteration count. We present initial results on the existing HLS-Eval benchmarks.
  In our initial evaluation, we find open-source LLMs paired with an agentic harness solve every simple HLS code generation task in our evaluation, underscoring the need to expand benchmark difficulty as model capabilities advance. Analyzing traces from passing and failing runs, we show how model size, token usage, and trajectory length relate to design pass rates. These results establish a foundation for agentic HLS design and motivate harder benchmarks and new agentic tooling as model capabilities progress.

</details>


### [155] [Differential Stochastic Simulated Annealing Processor for Fully Connected 2048-Spin Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09559)
*Naoya Onizawa,Md Mohaimenul Alam,Sean Smithson,Duckgyu Shin,Takahiro Hanyu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于差分随机模拟退火（DSSA）的2048自旋全连接退火处理器架构，采用TSMC 28 nm CMOS工艺，后版图面积3 mm x 4 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向大规模组合优化问题，全连接退火硬件在保持全连接性的同时面临功耗与求解能量开销大的问题，需要一种高密度、低功耗的架构设计方案。

Method: 采用DSSA架构：串行化数据通路以提高密度，对发生翻转的自旋重新计算相互作用，将有效工作负载收缩至活跃前沿；结合自旋选择调度、优先级权重读取以及跳过空闲步骤的温度控制器加速稀疏更新；集成16 Mb SRAM权重存储器，通过面积高效的随机数发生器将随机噪声在16个自旋间分摊。

Result: 后版图仿真显示，在2000自旋问题上，500 MHz时序收敛，求解时间（TTS）为2.7 ms，求解能量为0.86 mJ，功耗316 mW（0.15 mW/自旋）；相比此前的全连接退火器，功耗降低1.5倍，TTS能量降低3.5倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明所提出的DSSA架构在后版图评估下对大规模组合优化硬件具有潜力。

Abstract: A 2,048-spin fully connected annealing processor based on differential stochastic simulated annealing (DSSA) is presented as an architectural design in TSMC 28 nm CMOS with a 3 mm x 4 mm post-layout area. The processor closes timing at 500 MHz, integrates a 16 Mb SRAM weight memory, and amortizes stochastic noise across 16 spins with area-efficient random number generators. DSSA keeps a serialized datapath for density but recomputes interactions only for spins that flip, shrinking the effective workload to the active frontier during each annealing run. Spin-select scheduling, priority-based weight reads, and a temperature controller that skips idle steps accelerate sparse updates without sacrificing full connectivity. Post-layout simulation results show 2.7 ms time-to-solution (TTS) and 0.86 mJ energy-to- solution on 2,000-spin problems at 316 mW (0.15 mW/spin), achieving 1.5x lower power and 3.5x lower TTS energy than projected prior fully connected annealers. These results demonstrate the potential of the proposed DSSA architecture for large-scale combinatorial optimization hardware under post-layout evaluation.

</details>


### [156] [UNISON: A Co-Designed Near-Memory Scheduler of Session KV Residency for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09643)
*Fan He,Yan Li,Xiaoyang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出 UNISON——一种事件驱动的近存调度器，通过 SPEAR 淘汰策略与 TIDE 分层策略共享同一在线排序，针对智能体循环中的 KV 缓存会话级效率问题，在 1,415 会话、33,596 轮次上显著提升命中率并降低 AMAT 与 TTFT，硬件开销仅 0.169 mm²、13.6 mW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型正日益被组织为"规划—调用工具—恢复任务"的智能体循环，这类循环比传统多轮对话对共享存储层次施加更大压力：工具等待期间需保留不断增长的 KV 前缀，且多会话共享同一 SRAM/HBM 池，使淘汰与分层放置成为与计算模式优化正交的会话级效率问题。现有基于最近使用、超时或身份的代理策略缺失循环的机制信息，会把"活跃等待"误判为可丢弃的冷单元。

Method: 提出 UNISON，一种事件驱动的近存调度器。其中 SPEAR（面向智能体返回间隔的存活惩罚淘汰）依据间隔均值与轮次索引风险选择淘汰对象；TIDE（空闲窗口 DMA 事件分层）将观测到的等待时间作为 DMA 预算，决定哪些单元驻留于快速层。二者共享同一在线排序，形成统一的近存策略。

Result: 在编码与通用任务基准、三种模型家族上，共 1,415 个会话与 33,596 轮次，联合策略在每条 trace 上均为最优非预言机方案：命中率提升 0.3%–23.1%，AMAT 降低 22%–51%，长时程 trace 上 TTFT 降低 58%–89%。结构必要性分析表明该统一近存设计无法拆分为独立 IP 或用软件实现而不重新引入已知失效模式。28nm CMOS 调度核心面积 0.169 mm²，功耗 13.6 mW，频率 150 MHz，相对所管理的 KV 层次开销可忽略，并以 Kendall tau > 0.998 复现浮点排序结果。

Conclusion: 面向智能体循环的 KV 缓存管理本质上是一个会话级的统一调度问题：只有将淘汰与分层在近存处共享同一在线排序，才能充分利用循环的机制信息并获得稳定收益，且该统一性具有结构必要性，硬件代价极低。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly composed into agent loops that plan, call tools, and resume the same task after each action. These loops press a shared memory hierarchy harder than conventional multi-turn chat, because they hold a growing key-value (KV) prefix across tool waits and place many sessions on one SRAM/HBM pool, so that eviction and hierarchical placement become a session-level efficiency problem orthogonal to compute-mode optimization. Existing proxies based on recency, timeout, or identity miss the mechanism information of the loop and therefore treat a live wait as a cold, discardable unit. We present Unified Native Inter-turn Session Orchestration Nexus (UNISON), an event-driven near-memory scheduler in which Survival-Penalty Eviction for Agent Return-gap (SPEAR) and Tiering in Idle-window DMA Events (TIDE) share one live ranking. SPEAR selects who leaves from a gap average and a turn-indexed hazard, while TIDE spends the observed wait as a DMA budget for who sits in the fast tier. On coding and general-mission benchmarks with three model families, totaling 1,415 sessions and 33,596 turns, the joint policy is the best non-oracle entry on every trace, raising hit rate by 0.3% to 23.1%, reducing AMAT by 22% to 51%, and lowering TTFT by 58% to 89% on long-horizon traces. A structural necessity analysis shows that the unified near-memory design cannot be decomposed into independent IPs or realized in software without re-introducing documented failure modes. The 28-nm CMOS scheduling core occupies 0.169 mm^2 at 13.6 mW and 150 MHz, a negligible overhead relative to the KV hierarchy it manages, reproducing the floating-point ranking at Kendall tau exceeding 0.998.

</details>


### [157] [Minimal Deadlock-Free Routing for Degree-Six Triangular-Lattice Meshes and Tori with Two Forbidden Turns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09746)
*Zibo Diao,Rongxi Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该文研究六度三角格互连网络（有限六边形网格及其环面商）在虫洞流控下的无死锁路由，构造了有限网格的单虚通道部分自适应最小路由（仅禁两个转向），并为环面提出双虚通道加哈密顿坐标与分组日期线的方案，证明可达性与资源CDG无环，并扩展到单链路故障场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六度三角格网络具有丰富的极小路径多样性，但六个方向使虫洞流控下的无死锁路由设计更困难；环面周期结构还会引入残余的周期性环依赖，需要消除。

Method: 在统一六方向坐标系下研究有限六边形网格与周期环面；为网格构造仅禁两个有向转向、使用一个虚通道的最小部分自适应路由关系；为环面证明每个源-目的对存在唯一最近格点提升，指出同一两转向物理路由关系在n≥3时存在带环的一VC资源CDG，进而结合双虚通道、哈密顿坐标与分组特定日期线，使各同组段至多穿越其日期线一次，从而在VC标记的信道资源上建立全局秩；对单条静态双向链路故障，旋转转向规则朝向故障方向并以同组两跳三角旁路替换故障跳。

Result: 证明了两种物理路由关系的最小全对可达性；证明一VC网格与两VC环面构造的完整资源CDG无环；故障扩展保持全对可达性与原虚通道数，且相对健康最短路径至多多一跳。

Conclusion: 所提方法以最少虚通道和受限转向实现六度三角格网格与环面的无死锁最小自适应路由，并可容忍单链路故障而基本不牺牲跳数与虚通道开销。

Abstract: Degree-six triangular-lattice interconnection networks offer substantial minimal-path diversity, but their additional directions complicate deadlock-free routing under wormhole flow control. We study a finite hexagon-shaped mesh and its periodic torus quotient in a common six-direction coordinate system. For the finite mesh, we construct a minimal partially adaptive routing relation that uses one virtual channel and forbids only two directed turns. For the torus, we prove that every source-destination pair has a unique closest lattice lift, but that the same two-turn physical routing relation still has a cyclic one-VC resource CDG for every n >= 3. We eliminate this residual periodic dependency by combining two virtual channels with Hamiltonian coordinates and group-specific datelines. Each same-group segment crosses its dateline at most once, which permits a global rank on VC-labelled channel resources. We prove minimal all-pairs connectivity for both physical routing relations and acyclicity of the complete resource CDG for the proposed one-VC mesh and two-VC torus constructions. For a single static bidirectional link failure known before a routing epoch, we further rotate the turn rule toward the failed orientation and replace a failed hop by a same-group two-hop triangle bypass. This restricted extension preserves all-pairs connectivity and the original VC counts, with at most one additional hop relative to the healthy shortest-path distance.

</details>


### [158] [HBFSim: Fast and Faithful Simulation of High-Bandwidth Flash Under Real GPU Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09800)
*Yanpeng Hu,Yiwei Yang,Yuanwu Zhu,Yusheng Zheng,Wei Zhang,Andi Quinn*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HBFSim 是首个在真实 GPU 上运行真实推理负载、同时施加 HBF 时序/容量/热效应的评测平台，通过 PTX 重写与内核发射门控实现，结果与实测器件完全吻合且加速比达 20.8x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务受限于内存容量。HBF 将 NAND 闪存堆叠在加速器封装内（比 HBM 低一档），但规范发布于 2026 年 8 月、首批推理器件预计 2027 年初才送样，容量与数据放置决策却无法等待硅片。现有方法均无法胜任：存储模拟器只重放记录访问序列、不真正执行负载；GPU 模拟器不运行真实计算内核；周期精确模拟器跑不完一次 LLM 推理。因此需要一个能在真实工作负载下提前评估 HBF 决策的平台。

Method: HBFSim 在真实推理负载于真实 GPU 上执行的同时，施加 HBF 的时序、容量与热效应。其核心是重写 NVIDIA 编译器输出的中间代码 PTX 并对内核发射进行门控；将「发射」与「消费」分离，使真实硬件提供的计算能够隐藏访问延迟。时序参数来自真实器件的实测而非参数表；结温同时决定 HBF 可持续的速率以及强制刷新写入的保持期限。

Result: HBFSim 在全部六个校准断点上与实测器件精确吻合，零不安全发射；未经修改的 vLLM 0.15.1 服务 Qwen3-30B-A3B 时返回与无插桩基线一致的 token 标识。器件快速路径服务同一 Qwen3-30B-A3B 用例仅需 2s，而详细参考路径需 44s，加速 20.8 倍。

Conclusion: 在 HBF 器件送样之前，HBFSim 使设计者能够在真实工作负载下实测容量或放置决策，而不再只能依赖假设。

Abstract: Serving a large language model (LLM) is limited by memory capacity. High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) stacks NAND flash inside the accelerator package, one tier below high-bandwidth memory (HBM); the specification was published on August 3, 2026, and the first inference devices are expected to sample in early 2027. Decisions about capacity and data placement cannot wait for silicon. No existing method settles those decisions: a storage simulator replaying a recorded access sequence never executes the workload, a GPU simulator does not run the real compute kernels, and a cycle-accurate simulator cannot finish one LLM inference run. We present HBFSim, the first evaluation platform that applies HBF timing, capacity, and thermal effects to a real inference workload while that workload executes on a real GPU. HBFSim rewrites PTX, the intermediate code NVIDIA's compiler emits, and gates kernel launches; issue is separated from consumption, so real hardware supplies the computation that hides an access. Timing comes from measurements of a real device rather than a parameter sheet, and junction temperature sets both the rate HBF sustains and the retention deadline that forces refresh writes. HBFSim matches the measured device exactly at all six calibration breakpoints, with zero unsafe launches, and an unmodified vLLM 0.15.1 serving Qwen3-30B-A3B returns the token identifiers of the uninstrumented baseline. The device fast path serves the same Qwen3-30B-A3B case in 2s against 44s on the detailed reference path, 20.8x faster. Before HBF parts sample, HBFSim lets a designer measure a capacity or placement decision under a real workload instead of assuming one.

</details>


### [159] [AMEND: Audited Margins Enable Nonblocking Drops in GPU-PIM LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09823)
*Zuxiong Tan,Will Wei-Jen Wang,Wei Shao,Ali Karkehabadi,Houman Homayoun,Avesta Sasan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AMEND是一种GPU-PIM协同注意力设计，通过跨步预测BLASST判据并在HBM近存单元中并行补算被省略的KV块，在不阻塞当前token的前提下实现长上下文解码加速与节能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LLM解码每步都要重读不断增长的KV cache，长上下文注意力受GPU显存带宽限制。块稀疏注意力可跳过低贡献KV块，但两类现有选择器存在问题：一是基于当前QK乘积后再决策的选择器（如BLASST的最大相对块阈值）仍需读取全部K块；二是基于当前query决策的PIM滤波器会在关键路径上引入串行PIM阶段。因此需要一种同时消除这两项依赖的方案。

Method: AMEND采用GPU-PIM协同设计：利用先前步骤审计得到的margin预测每个块的BLASST判据，GPU仅取回预测为保留的块，同时HBM内的近存PIM单元并行对被省略的补集打分；栈级控制器合并两路观测、更新预测器，并提前生成下一步掩码，使每个被预测丢弃的块都能被重新观测而不阻塞当前token。工作点由带false-drop预算约束的离线贝叶斯优化选取。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER上，AMEND保持接近基线的任务质量；batch size为8的仿真中，在8K–64K上下文范围内相比dense attention实现1.40–3.63倍端到端解码加速，动态解码能耗降低28–66%。

Conclusion: 通过将稀疏判据的决策从关键路径上解耦——GPU侧预测取块、PIM侧并行复核，AMEND在不牺牲任务质量的情况下同时缓解了块稀疏注意力的带宽瓶颈与串行PIM关键路径问题，验证了GPU-PIM协同在长上下文LLM解码中的加速与节能潜力。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model (LLM) decoding re-reads a growing key-value (KV) cache at every step, so long-context attention is bound by graphics processing unit (GPU) memory bandwidth. Block-sparse attention skips low-contribution KV blocks, but a selector that decides after the current query-key (QK) product, such as max-relative block thresholding (BLASST), still reads every K block, and a processing-in-memory (PIM) filter that decides from the current query places a serial PIM stage on the critical path. We present AMEND, a GPU-PIM attention design that removes both dependencies. AMEND predicts each block's BLASST verdict from margins audited at earlier steps, so the GPU fetches only predicted survivors while near-bank PIM units in high-bandwidth memory (HBM) concurrently score the omitted complement. A stack-level controller merges both observations, updates the predictor, and eagerly generates the next step's mask, so every predicted drop is re-observed without blocking the current token. Operating points are selected offline by constrained Bayesian optimization under a false-drop budget. Across LongBench and RULER runs, AMEND preserves near-baseline task quality; in simulation at batch size 8, it achieves $1.40$-$3.63\times$ end-to-end decode speedup and 28-66% lower dynamic decode energy than dense attention across 8K-64K contexts.

</details>


### [160] [SAGE: Semantic-Aware Geographic Error Recovery for AI Data Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10126)
*Patrick S. Y. Hung,Zitong Wang,Zekai Zhang,Yu Hin Chan,Shengzhe Lyu,Ray C. C. Cheung*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAGE——一种语义感知的地理化错误恢复架构，将"故障是否值得重放"与"重放从何处重启"解耦，在 BF16 类数据上按故障严重性分级处理，并显著降低质量归一化终端时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 互连通常对数据包统一保护与重放，但数值比特故障后果差异巨大：低位尾数翻转近似量化噪声，而高位指数翻转可能产生灾难性离群值或非有限值。统一重放策略无法区分这类语义差异，造成带宽与延迟浪费或严重质量损失。

Method: 针对 BF16 类数据，采用工作负载校准的契约将故障分为灾难性 Class-H、有界 Class-M 与精度类 Class-L；先施加 Class-H 静默投递约束，再以质量归一化终端时延 Ψ_del 对可行策略排序。独立的源本地区域表根据故障地理自适应调整检查点间隔，缩短噪声区域的恢复段。检测到的 Class-H 失败触发受保护负确认与整 flit 重放，而 Class-M/L 不触发默认网络重放。端点与重放协议在 gem5 Garnet 中实现，全流水检查器在 ASAP7 中综合。

Result: 在稳定合成工作点、十种子争用忠实直连 Garnet 实验中，SAGE 相对固定 34 跳恢复将 Ψ_del 降低 30.1%，平均时延降低 28.0%，并改善投递语义质量。在相同负载的高 BER 压力下，SAGE 保持队列有界，而固定基线出现积压。基于 DeiT-S 通信轨迹在非零 BER 下也表现出更低的均值与 p99.5 时延。在合格工作包络内，CRC32 解码试验给出同时 95% 的每原始 Class-H 静默投递上界 3.18×10⁻⁷。

Conclusion: SAGE 通过语义分级与地理自适应检查点实现差异化的错误恢复，在延迟、拥塞控制与语义质量上均优于固定重放基线，并满足严格的 Class-H 静默投递可靠性上界。

Abstract: AI interconnects typically protect and replay packets uniformly, yet numerical bit faults differ sharply in consequence: a low-order mantissa flip may resemble quantization noise, while a high-significance exponent flip can produce a catastrophic outlier or non-finite value. We present SAGE, a semantic-aware geographic error-recovery architecture that decouples whether a detected fault merits replay from where replay restarts. For BF16-like data, a workload-calibrated contract separates catastrophic Class-H faults from bounded Class-M and precision Class-L damage. It first applies a Class-H silent-delivery constraint, then ranks admissible policies by quality-normalized terminal latency, $Ψ_{\rm del}$. Independently, a source-local region table adapts checkpoint intervals to fault geography, shortening recovery segments in noisy regions. Detected Class-H failures may trigger protected negative acknowledgments and full-flit replay; Class-M and Class-L outcomes do not trigger default network replay.
  We implement SAGE's endpoint and replay protocol in gem5 Garnet and synthesize its fully pipelined checker in ASAP7. At a stable synthetic operating point, a ten-seed contention-faithful direct-Garnet campaign shows that SAGE reduces $Ψ_{\rm del}$ by 30.1% relative to fixed 34-hop recovery, combining 28.0% lower mean latency with improved delivered semantic quality. Under higher-BER synthetic stress at the same offered load, SAGE maintains bounded queues while the fixed baseline accumulates backlog. Application-derived DeiT-S communication traces also show lower mean and p99.5 latency at the evaluated nonzero BERs. Within the qualified operating envelope, CRC32 decoder trials yield a simultaneous 95% per-original Class-H silent-delivery upper bound of $3.18\times10^{-7}$.

</details>


### [161] [CertiFlash: A Formal Verification Framework for Flash Translation Layers in Computational Solid State Drives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10347)
*Harshita Gupta,Mayank Kabra,Rakesh Nadig,Nika Mansouri Ghiasi,Sahand Divsalar,F. Nisa Bostanci,Ataberk Olgun,Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,Jisung Park,Haiyu Mao,Abdullah Giray Yaglikci,Mohammad Sadrosadati,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CertiFlash——一个在 Rocq 中机械化的 FTL 形式化验证框架，通过对通用 FTL 模型一次性证明安全性不变量保持与块设备精化，使新设计只需证明 5 个局部假设，显著降低验证负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以存储为中心的计算需修改 SSD 固件（FTL）以将计算下沉至 SSD，但 FTL 直接访问安全关键部件，有缺陷的 FTL 会破坏隔离性、完整性与归属权。已有工作仅验证功能正确性，且与单一 FTL 设计绑定，每次修改都需重做全部证明。

Method: 将 FTL 建模为确定性状态机，建立覆盖映射、隔离、完整性、归属权与分配的单一全局不变量；在通用 FTL 模型上一次性证明 (i) 所有 FTL 操作保持不变量、(ii) 模型精化理想块设备。新设计只需证明关于自身操作的 5 个假设，而非重做两项证明。框架在 Rocq 证明助手中机械化。

Result: 在 DaisyPlus OpenSSD 上展示了有缺陷 FTL 可在 SSD 内部五个表面破坏设备状态。四个案例研究中，设计者仅需在 16,489 行框架上新增 27 至 3,231 行代码，验证工作量大幅降低。

Conclusion: CertiFlash 提供了可复用的、机器检查的 FTL 安全性与正确性验证框架，兼顾通用性与低增量成本，且已开源。

Abstract: Data-intensive applications move large amounts of data from storage to the compute unit, incurring significant data movement overhead. Storage-centric computing reduces this overhead by moving computation near or inside solid-state drives (SSDs). Enabling it requires modifying SSD policies, e.g., address translation and garbage collection, which are part of the Flash Translation Layer (FTL), the SSD's firmware. Modifying the FTL is error-prone. Because FTL logic has direct access to security-critical device components, even a functionally correct FTL can leak data between tenants, drop integrity tags, or assign a flash block to the wrong tenant. We show that a faulty FTL can corrupt the device state at five surfaces inside the SSD, and demonstrate them on a DaisyPlus OpenSSD. Prior work verifies individual FTL designs, but has two limitations. (1) It establishes only functional correctness, so a modified FTL can violate isolation, integrity, and ownership and still pass verification. (2) It is tied to a single FTL design, so every modification requires redoing every proof.
  We propose CertiFlash, a formal verification framework for FTLs, mechanized in the Rocq proof assistant, that gives designers a machine-checked proof of security and correctness. CertiFlash models an FTL as a deterministic state machine with a single global invariant over mapping, isolation, integrity, ownership, and allocation. We prove once, over a general FTL model, that (i) every FTL operation preserves the invariant and (ii) the model refines an idealized block device. For a new design, a designer discharges five hypotheses about its own operations instead of redoing either proof. Across four case studies, a designer adds 27 to 3,231 lines against a 16,489-line framework, significantly reducing the verification effort. CertiFlash is open source.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Spectral origin of the topological gap exponent d + η: mechanism, kernel, decomposition, and scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09159)
*Matthew Loftus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过谱积分 $I(\alpha)$ 的标度分析，解析推导了临界点云拓扑间隙 $\Delta \sim L^{d+\eta}$，将体积因子与反常维度因子分离，并用数值实验验证其 $d=2$ 的有效性与 $d=3$ 的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑间隙 $\Delta$（临界点云相对密度匹配零模型的 $H_1$ 总持续性的超出量）经验上呈 $\Delta\sim L^{d+\eta}$ 标度，但其解析来源、维度限制及与磁化强度的关系不明确，且密度归一化在 $d=3$ 的必要性缺乏解释。

Method: 引入谱积分 $I(\alpha)=\sum_{k\neq 0} S_{\mathrm{conn}}(k)|k|^\alpha$，利用 IR 主导条件下 $I(\alpha)\sim L^{2-\alpha-\eta}$ 推导 $\Delta$ 标度；将 $I(-2\eta)$ 分解为体积因子 $I_0\propto N(1-m^2)\sim L^d$ 与形状因子 $I_{\mathrm{shape}}\sim L^\eta$；对 Potts $q=4$ 在 $L=32$--$256$ 做 $\alpha$ 扫描；用偏相关/协方差分析检验 $\langle m^2\cdot I(-2\eta)\rangle$ 与单构型 $R^2$；并将 Divol--Polonik 启发式论证推广到非均匀 Poisson 强度。

Result: IR 主导要求 $d<2+\eta$，物理上仅 $d=2$ 成立；$d=3$ 时谱积分为 UV 主导，解释了密度归一化的必要性。$\alpha$ 扫描得到 $\alpha_{\mathrm{opt}}\in[-0.75,-0.5]$，与 $-2\eta_{\mathrm{Ising}}$ 一致而与 $-2\eta_{q=4}=-1$ 不符（暂定，待 $L\geq1024$ 验证）。$\langle m^2\cdot I(-2\eta)\rangle$ 由共享 $I_0$ 幅度的相关性 $r(m^2,I)\approx-0.98$ 主导，故作为协方差修正报告。裸 PH 核平坦，有效核仅在临界时获得 $k$ 依赖。单构型 $\Delta$ 与 $I(-0.5)$ 的 $R^2=0.91$（$L=256$）在剔除 $|M|$ 后塌缩至 $\approx 0$，说明该一致性主要是磁化相关性。

Conclusion: 单构型证据支持 $I_0$ 的 Parseval 恒等式，但不支持 $|k|^{-2\eta}$ 形状因子；后者由系综 $L$-标度确立。$q=4$ 的 $\alpha_{\mathrm{opt}}$ 偏差提示需更大尺度确认。

Abstract: The topological gap $Δ$ -- the excess $H_1$ total persistence of a critical point cloud over a density-matched null -- scales as $Δ\sim L^{d+η}$. We derive this analytically: the spectral integral $I(α) = \sum_{k\neq 0} S_{\mathrm{conn}}(k)\,|k|^α$ scales as $L^{2-α-η}$ when IR-dominated, giving $I(-2η) \sim L^{d+η}$. The decomposition $I(-2η) = I_0 \cdot I_{\mathrm{shape}}$ separates volume ($I_0 \propto N(1-m^2) \sim L^d$) from anomalous dimension ($I_{\mathrm{shape}} \sim L^η$); the volume factor accounts for the magnetization-driven per-configuration variance of $Δ$. We prove the mechanism requires $d < 2 + η$ (IR dominance), confining it to $d = 2$ for physical systems; in $d = 3$ the spectral integral is UV-dominated, explaining why density normalization is needed. An $α$-sweep for Potts $q = 4$ at $L = 32$--$256$ finds $α_{\mathrm{opt}}$ in $[-0.75, -0.5]$, consistent with $-2η_{\mathrm{Ising}}$ and inconsistent with $-2η_{q=4} = -1$; we flag this as tentative pending $L \geq 1024$ confirmation. The $\langle m^2 \cdot I(-2η)\rangle$ hyperscaling product is dominated by the correlation $r(m^2, I) \approx -0.98$ via the shared $I_0$ amplitude, so we report it as a covariance-correction analysis. Under a heuristic argument extending Divol--Polonik to inhomogeneous Poisson intensities, the bare PH kernel is flat; the effective kernel acquires $k$-dependence only at criticality. The per-configuration agreement between $Δ$ and $I(-0.5)$ is primarily a magnetization correlation: $R^2 = 0.91$ at $L = 256$ collapses to $R^2 \approx 0$ once $|M|$ is partialed out. Per-configuration evidence corroborates the $I_0$ Parseval identity but not the $|k|^{-2η}$ shape factor; the latter is established by ensemble $L$-scaling.

</details>


### [163] [Physics-informed neural networks by Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling (3GAS-PINNs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09162)
*Yousen Wang,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出梯度引导高斯自适应采样物理信息神经网络(3GAS-PINNs)，通过融合均匀分布与基于解空间梯度的高斯平滑概率分布实现配点自适应，在Burgers、KdV、非线性薛定谔方程上将精度提升至多14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs采用固定损失权重与均匀配点分布，难以适应训练中解场的演化复杂性，导致非线性问题中收敛慢、梯度失衡、分辨率不足，无法捕捉激波、孤立子等局部间歇结构。

Method: 3GAS-PINNs在配点采样上结合均匀概率分布与由解空间梯度导出的高斯平滑概率分布：前者维持全局约束满足，后者将配点集中到高梯度区域，从而自适应捕捉间歇性结构。

Result: 在一维强迫Burgers方程、Korteweg-de Vries方程与非线性薛定谔方程三个含陡梯度/强非线性的基准问题上评估，相较基线PINNs，3GAS-PINNs提升间歇区域的物理一致性，数值模拟精度最高提升约14倍。

Conclusion: 梯度引导的高斯自适应采样能有效缓解PINNs在非线性问题中的采样与收敛瓶颈，显著提升对激波、孤立子等局部结构的求解精度，为强非线性偏微分方程求解提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) provide a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations, yet their performance in nonlinear problems is often limited by slow convergence, gradient imbalance, and insufficient resolution to capture localized intermittent structures such as shock waves[1]. These issues arise primarily from the use of fixed weights of loss and uniform collocation point distributions, which cannot adapt to the evolving complexity of the solution field during training. To address these challenges, Gradient-Guided Gaussian Adaptive Sampling Physics-Informed Neural Networks (3GAS-PINNs) is proposed in this paper, which combines uniform probability distribution and Gaussian-smoothed probability distribution derived from the spatial gradients of solution, to maintain global constraint satisfaction as well as concentrating collocation points in regions of high gradient. Thus, intermittency structures like shock wave and solitons can be accurately captured. The method is evaluated on three benchmark nonlinear problems, including one-dimensional forced Burgers equation, Korteweg-de Vries (KdV) equation and nonlinear Schrodinger equation, all of which exhibit steep gradients or strong nonlinearity. In comparison with baseline PINNs, 3GAS-PINNs can effectively promote the physical consistency in intermittent regions. The accuracy of the numerical simulation can be improved by a factor of up to 14.

</details>


### [164] [World-Time Compute with Verified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09163)
*James Schwoebel,Ingrida Semenec,Jenia Rousseva,Marcos Ortiz,Collin Overbay,Christopher Klaus,Anderson Edmond,Manish Bhatt,Rome Thorstenson,Jessica Tsai,Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"world-time compute"：当领域动态可写成代码时，用模板生成大量可验证的世界模型，为LLM提供精确标注的轨迹，从而在少样本/零样本领域泛化上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的跨领域泛化依赖大量真实标注样本，而多数领域恰恰缺乏这类数据，因此需要一种低成本"制造"训练数据的方法。

Method: 将领域动态表示为代码模板，实例化为许多可执行、可验证的符号状态世界模型，每个世界提供无穷多精确标注的轨迹；用这些轨迹微调LLM（world-time compute，测试时计算的训练期类比），并配合 OpenWorld 零依赖框架生成与托管世界。

Result: 在未训练过的合成世界家族上泛化提升，0.5B模型+29分，最大模型提升在噪声内（饱和）；合成后校验的动态在20步rollout上精确，OOD探针100%正确，而逐步LLM/MLP预测误差累积崩溃；真实基准（ARC-AGI网格、List Functions、CLRS）同样有效，List Functions跨世界形式达40%对6%（+34分，CI [29,39]）；标签损坏对照表明增益源于标签精确性而非任务多样性。

Conclusion: 该增益是饱和的规律而非普遍定律：对少步推理、小/弱模型增益最大，长链、感知类及已饱和任务增益衰减，无共享技能时跨任务迁移弱；适用范围限于符号状态，像素原生领域仍依赖学习模型；代码、配方与稿件均可从单一仓库复现。

Abstract: LLMs generalize across a domain only after seeing many real, labeled examples, which most domains lack. We study a way to manufacture it cheaply. When a domain's dynamics can be written as code, one template instantiates into many world models: executable, verifiable programs over symbolic state, each an inexhaustible source of exactly-labeled trajectories. Fine-tuning an LLM on trajectories through many such worlds, which we call world-time compute, a training-time analogue of test-time compute, lifts generalization to held-out worlds it never trained on (synthesized world families). Gains are largest where capability is scarcest: +29 points at 0.5B; the largest model's lift is within noise, consistent with saturation. Labels can be trusted because the worlds are verified code: synthesized-then-checked dynamics are exact over 20-step rollouts and answer 10x out-of-distribution probes exactly (100%), whereas per-step LLM and MLP predictors compound error and collapse. Unlike domain randomization, each world is independently authored and verified; a corrupted-label control shows label exactness, not task variety, drives the gains. On real benchmarks (ARC-AGI grids, List Functions, CLRS) the same lever holds as per-world test-time training. On List Functions the harder cross-world form holds: one adapter trained on 128 disjoint worlds reaches 40% on held-out worlds versus 6% for a corrupted-label control (+34 points, CI [29, 39]). The gain is a saturating regularity, not a law: largest for few-step reasoning and small/weak models, fading for long chains, perception-induced tasks, and saturated tasks; cross-task transfer is weak without shared skill. Worlds are authored and served by OpenWorld, a zero-dependency framework (companion paper). Scope: symbolic state; pixel-native domains remain territory of learned models. All code, recipes, and this manuscript regenerate from one repository.

</details>


### [165] [ProMeta: Few-shot PROTAC-targeted degradation prediction across E3 ligases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09891)
*Yuansheng Liu,Yufei Ye,Tao Tang,Jiawei Luo,Wen Tao,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将跨E3连接酶的PROTAC降解活性预测重构为小样本元学习问题，提出原型图神经网络ProMeta，通过情景式元学习与支持样本条件推断实现跨连接酶泛化，在CRBN↔VHL迁移基准上显著优于监督GNN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PROTAC可选择性降解传统"不可成药"靶点，但现有计算预测方法受限于标注数据稀缺且在不同E3连接酶间严重不平衡：数据集中于CRBN、VHL等少数连接酶，多数连接酶研究不足却对拓展降解剂设计空间至关重要。因此需在极少标注样本下实现稳健的跨连接酶泛化。

Method: 将跨E3连接酶的降解活性预测建模为小样本元学习任务；提出ProMeta——基于原型的图神经网络，在源E3任务上进行情景式（episodic）元学习训练，在留出的目标E3任务上以支持样本条件推断进行评估；推断时不更新编码器，而是由目标连接酶的少量支持样本动态估计类原型。

Result: 在CRBN→VHL基准上，K=2,Q=3时AUROC为0.796，K=2,Q=5时为0.883，较对应监督GNN基线分别提升19.9%与6.8%；反向VHL→CRBN迁移在同协议下AUROC为0.702（K=2,Q=3）与0.821（K=2,Q=5），验证了双向适用性，同时揭示迁移效果依赖方向与数据量区间。

Conclusion: ProMeta在所评估的支持/查询协议下可作为跨连接酶小样本预测的实用框架；其无需更新编码器的原型式推断为数据稀缺条件下的PROTAC计算发现提供了可行路径，但跨连接酶迁移性能仍受迁移方向与数据规模影响。

Abstract: Proteolysis-targeting chimeras (PROTACs) have emerged as a transformative therapeutic strategy that selectively degrades historically ''undruggable'' targets via the ubiquitin-proteasome system. Despite growing efforts to develop computational predictors of PROTAC degradation activity, existing supervised approaches remain severely challenged by data scarcity and imbalance across E3 ligases, limiting their ability to generalize beyond well-studied ligase contexts. In practice, labeled data are heavily concentrated on a few ligases (e.g., CRBN and VHL), while the majority of E3 ligases remain underexplored yet are critical for expanding the design space of targeted degraders. Developing methods that enable robust cross-ligase generalization with minimal labeled data is therefore essential for improving the practical utility of computational PROTAC discovery. We reformulate PROTAC degradation activity prediction across E3 ligases as a few-shot meta-learning problem and present ProMeta, a prototype-based graph neural network trained through episodic meta-learning on source-E3 tasks and evaluated on held-out target-E3 tasks through support-conditioned inference. ProMeta performs inference without updating the encoder by dynamically estimating class prototypes from minimal target-ligase support samples. On the CRBN-to-VHL benchmark, ProMeta achieves AUROC values of 0.796 under K=2, Q=3 and 0.883 under K=2, Q=5, improving by 19.9% and 6.8%, respectively, over the corresponding supervised GNN baseline. Reverse VHL-to-CRBN transfer under the same protocol yielded AUROC values of 0.702 (K=2, Q=3) and 0.821 (K=2, Q=5), confirming bidirectional applicability while revealing direction and data-regime dependence. Together, these results support ProMeta as a practical framework for cross-ligase few-shot prediction under the evaluated support/query protocols.

</details>


### [166] [Scaling Post-Training Ternarisation to Qwen3-8B Capability Retention, Reproduction, Lossless Packing, and Packed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09240)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将Qwen3-4B的激进训练后“1.58-bit”转换流程放大到Qwen3-8B，进行端到端表征：外部复现门控、4B/8B能力匹配对比、跨语料困惑度、有效比特核算、无损格感知打包与直接打包执行。8B三语料困惑度比为1.361x，八项零样本均值64.6%对FP16的72.4%（机会校正保留78.5%）；匹配4B仅保留69.6%，8B领先8.9点；打包检查点8.24 GiB且保持记录的困惑度，直接执行达15.52 tokens/s、占7.35 GiB，但打包GEMV仍慢于FP16 cuBLAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低比特语言模型宣称可降低存储与内存流量，但“1.58-bit”这类名义标签并不说明实际部署表示或执行开销，缺乏对激进训练后离散化在更大规模模型上的端到端验证与可复现基线。

Method: 采用仅权重的A16配置，使用KOTMS旋转、E2M-ATQ自适应三值化与GPTQ风格误差补偿，将Qwen3-4B转换流程放大到Qwen3-8B；不宣称算法创新，贡献为端到端规模放大表征，包括外部复现门控、匹配的4B/8B能力分析、跨语料困惑度评估、有效比特核算、无损格感知打包以及直接打包执行测量。

Result: 8B模型三语料困惑度比为1.361x（WikiText-2 1.318x、C4 1.393x、PTB 1.371x）；八项零样本任务在n=500时平均准确率64.6%，FP16为72.4%，机会校正保留率78.5%，绝对代价7.8个百分点；匹配4B保留69.6%，8B领先8.9点。打包检查点在测量精度内保持记录困惑度，体积8.24 GiB；直接打包执行达15.52 tokens/s、内存7.35 GiB，而初步打包GEMV仍慢于FP16 cuBLAS。

Conclusion: 结果构成一个经验证的规模放大基线：模型增大可提升对激进训练后离散化的鲁棒性，针对所测工件解决了实际序列化问题，直接执行可行；更广的随机种子、校准分布与内核优化仍待研究。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models promise reductions in storage and memory traffic, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not specify the deployed representation or its execution cost. We study a scale-up of an aggressive post-training conversion pipeline from Qwen3-4B to Qwen3-8B.
  The conversion uses KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ adaptive ternarisation, and GPTQ-style error compensation in a weight-only A16 configuration. We do not claim these algorithms as new. Our contribution is the end-to-end scale-up characterisation: an external reproduction gate, matched 4B/8B capability analysis, cross-corpus perplexity, effective-bit accounting, lossless lattice-aware packing, and direct packed execution.
  The 8B model reaches a three-corpus perplexity ratio of 1.361x, with WikiText-2, C4, and PTB ratios of 1.318x, 1.393x, and 1.371x. On eight zero-shot tasks at n = 500, mean accuracy is 64.6% versus 72.4% for FP16, corresponding to 78.5% chance-corrected retention and a 7.8-point absolute cost. The matched 4B run retains 69.6%, yielding an 8.9-point 8B advantage.
  The packed checkpoint is 8.24 GiB and preserves the recorded perplexity to measurement precision. Direct packed execution reaches 15.52 tokens/s in 7.35 GiB, while a preliminary packed GEMV remains slower than FP16 cuBLAS. The result is a validated scale-up baseline: model size improves robustness to aggressive post-training discretisation, actual serialisation is solved for the measured artefact, and direct execution is feasible, while broader seeds, calibration distributions, and kernel optimisation remain open.

</details>


### [167] [XAI-Refine: An Automated Explanation-Knowledge Loop for Brain-Age Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09388)
*Yang Qiao,Junjie Wu,Deqiang Qiu,James J. Lah,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XAI-Refine，一个用于静息态功能连接脑龄预测的自动化"解释-知识"迭代闭环框架，将可靠的事后解释转化为神经生物学问题，检索并验证文献证据，构造可采纳解释集，并通过可微约束引导模型向证据支持的方向修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑龄预测模型通常仅以预测精度评价，但高精度并不能保证模型依赖可复现或具神经生物学依据的机制。现有事后解释工作流要么止步于诊断，要么需在模型分析前预先指定修正目标，缺乏自动化的证据驱动修正路径。

Method: XAI-Refine在每个迭代中：1）整合多次训练运行的互补事后分析，形成可靠的结构化模型解释；2）将每个可靠解释转为中立的神经生物学问题，检索并验证相关文献，将验证证据编译为同一类型化解释空间中的可采纳集；3）将修正目标定义为当前解释在可采纳集上的最小投影；4）把修正后的解释翻译为可微约束，同时保留原模型变量、测量算子与适用域；5）仅当多种子验证确认解释朝目标移动、预测性能在预设护栏内、且非目标解释漂移有界时，才接纳候选更新。

Result: 在基于功能连接的脑龄预测任务上，实验评估了预测性能、解释可靠性、文献一致性以及目标特定的模型修正效果，展示了从事后分析到证据引导模型修正的结构化路径。

Conclusion: XAI-Refine提供了一套将事后解释与外部文献证据自动耦合、并受多重约束保护的模型精修流程，使脑龄预测模型的修正更具可复现性与神经生物学依据。

Abstract: Brain-age prediction models are commonly evaluated by predictive accuracy, yet accurate predictions alone do not establish that a model relies on reproducible or neurobiologically supported mechanisms. Post-hoc explanation methods can expose these mechanisms, but existing workflows typically stop at diagnosis or require correction targets to be specified before model analysis. We propose XAI-Refine, an automated explanation-knowledge loop for brain-age prediction from resting-state functional connectivity. At each iteration, XAI-Refine consolidates complementary post-hoc analyses across repeated training runs into reliable, structured model explanations. It converts each reliable explanation into a neutral neurobiological question, retrieves and verifies relevant literature, and compiles the verified evidence into an admissible set in the same typed explanation space. The target for refinement is defined as the minimal projection of the current model explanation onto the admissible set induced by applicable verified knowledge. This revised explanation is then translated into a differentiable constraint while preserving the originating model variable, measurement operator, and applicable scope. Candidate updates are promoted only when multi-seed validation confirms target-directed explanatory movement, predictive performance remains within a prespecified guardrail, and non-target explanatory drift remains bounded. Experiments on functional-connectivity-based brain-age prediction evaluate predictive performance, explanation reliability, literature alignment, and target-specific model revision, illustrating a structured route from post-hoc analysis to evidence-guided model refinement.

</details>


### [168] [Distribution-Consistent Inference for Dynamic Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09241)
*Dohyeon Kim,Bedionita Soro,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层间分布对齐（LDA），一种轻量级推理时校正方法，用于解决动态top-k路由中因偏离训练配置而导致的表示分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数MoE模型采用固定top-k专家选择策略，为每个token分配相同的专家预算，即使更少专家已足够。推理时动态top-k路由可减少计算且无需重训练，但现有方法忽视了偏离训练时路由配置所造成的分布偏移。

Method: 作者发现减少激活专家数会持续增大SMoE输出的RMS尺度与方差，导致表示失配。为此提出Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA)，利用逐层校准统计量，将减少路由下的表示与默认配置对齐。LDA为轻量级推理时校正，开销可忽略。

Result: 在多个SMoE大语言模型、基准测试和路由策略上，LDA在减少路由下恢复了因分布偏移而损失的大部分性能，同时保持稀疏推理效率。

Conclusion: 减少专家激活导致的性能下降含有一个可校正的分布偏移成分，LDA能有效对齐推理时表示，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a powerful paradigm for scaling model capacity while preserving efficient inference in large foundation models. However, most MoE models use a fixed top-$k$ expert selection policy, assigning the same expert budget to every token even when fewer experts may be sufficient. Inference-time dynamic top-$k$ routing can reduce computation without retraining, but existing methods often overlook the distributional shift caused by deviating from the training-time routing configuration. We show that reducing the number of activated experts consistently increases the RMS scale and variance of SMoE outputs, inducing a representation mismatch that contributes to downstream performance degradation in addition to the loss of expert capacity. To address this correctable component, we propose Layer-wise Distribution Alignment (LDA), a lightweight inference-time correction that uses layer-wise calibration statistics to align reduced-routing representations with the default configuration. Across multiple SMoE LLMs, benchmarks, and routing strategies, LDA recovers much of the performance lost induced by the distributional shift under reduced routing while preserving sparse-inference efficiency with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [169] [EEGBind: Detecting Source-Level Interictal Epileptiform Discharges via EEG-Centric Multimodal Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09728)
*Muchen Li,Anglin Liu,Xuetian Gao,Ruijian Xu,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EEGBind，一种以EEG为核心的多模态绑定框架，用于五类发作间期癫痫样放电（IED）源级分类，在NMM-Source-IED基准上加权F1达0.8395，优于强竞争者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源级IED分析对术前评估与治疗规划具有临床意义，需将IED阳性活动归入有临床意义的脑区类别。该任务困难在于：短时EEG窗口中的源区证据微弱、不完整，且受被试差异、类别不平衡及不完善多模态上下文影响。

Method: EEGBind将EEG作为主模态，在EEG为核心的表示周围绑定同步的视频上下文特征；不采用早期或过强的多模态融合（以免扰动对源敏感的EEG表示），而是将视频上下文作为辅助证据；并引入视图一致性修复阶段以提升隐藏集鲁棒性，同时保持已学得的源类边界。

Result: 在NeuroMM 2026 Grand Challenge Track 3 NMM-Source-IED基准上，加权F1为0.8395，优于强竞争者。

Conclusion: 结果支持以EEG为核心的多模态绑定是源级IED分类的实用策略。开源代码已公开。

Abstract: Source-level analysis of interictal epileptiform discharges (IEDs) is relevant to presurgical evaluation and treatment planning because it helps characterize where epileptiform activity is likely to arise. Beyond detecting whether an IED is present, this setting requires assigning IED-positive activity to clinically meaningful brain-region categories. This setting is challenging because source-region evidence in short electroencephalography (EEG) windows can be subtle, partial, and affected by subject variability, class imbalance, and imperfect multimodal context. We present EEGBind, an EEG-centric multimodal binding framework for five-class source-level IED classification. EEGBind treats EEG as the primary modality and binds synchronized video-context features around an EEG-centric representation. Instead of relying on early or overly strong multimodal fusion, which may perturb the source-sensitive EEG representation, EEGBind uses video context as auxiliary evidence for robust classification. A view-consistent repair stage is further used to improve hidden-set robustness while preserving the learned source-class boundary. On the NeuroMM 2026 Grand Challenge Track 3 NMM-Source-IED benchmark, EEGBind achieves 0.8395 on weighted-F1 and outperforms strong competitors. These results support EEG-centric multimodal binding as a practical strategy for source-level IED classification. The open-source code is available at https://github.com/HKUSTGZ-ML4Health-Lab/NeuroMM2026_IED_Detection.

</details>


### [170] [DiffLUT-Net: Differentiable Training of FPGA LUT Networks with Learnable Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09254)
*Jiaqi Ye,Xinrui Gong,Jingcun Wang,Olga Kondrateva,Bing Li,Grace Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffLUT-Net 是一种FPGA原生网络，其由六输入查找表（LUT）连接而成，并在训练时端到端联合学习LUT真值表与输入端口连接关系，训练后离散化并导出为可综合Verilog。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPGA神经网络部署流程主要分两类：一是加速乘累加运算，二是将预训练量化模型转换为查找表（LUT）。两者都未充分发挥FPGA的LUT原生特性，因此作者希望构建一种直接从零训练的FPGA原生网络，以更好地利用LUT结构并提升资源效率。

Method: 提出DiffLUT-Net：采用可微LUT函数松弛和硬件源选择机制，在训练中联合学习每个六输入LUT的64个真值表条目以及其六个输入端口的来源连接。训练完成后对真值表和连接进行离散化，剪除未使用逻辑，并直接导出为可综合的Verilog。

Result: 在五个基准任务上，DiffLUT-Net 取得了较好的精度-资源权衡表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合学习LUT函数与稀疏连接关系对于构建紧凑的FPGA原生推理网络是有效的。代码已开源。

Abstract: Field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) enable efficient neural-network inference, but most deployment flows either accelerate multiply-accumulate operations or convert pretrained quantized models into lookup tables (LUTs). We present DiffLUT-Net, an FPGA-native network connected by six-input LUTs that are trained from scratch. We jointly learn the 64 truth-table entries of a LUT and the source to each of its six input ports using a differentiable LUT function relaxation and hardware source selection. After training, the truth tables and connections are discretized, unused logic can be pruned, and the network is exported directly as synthesizable Verilog. Across five benchmarks, DiffLUT-Net achieves favorable accuracy-resource trade-offs. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly learning LUT functions and sparse connectivity for compact FPGA-native inference. The code is available at https://github.com/TUDa-HWAI/DiffLUT-Network.

</details>


### [171] [Accountable and uncertainty-aware evaluation of sensor-based AI under distribution shift: devices, subjects, and nearly three years underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09257)
*Benny Platte,Rico Thomanek,Christian Roschke,Marc Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分阶段、可问责的评估协议，将部署模型的评估视为带声明参考水平与量化不确定性的测量；在四个累积泛化阶段（留出设备、受试者、时间）上以重复训练的5%分位数对照机会基线判断，并引入"超出当前范围率"揭示向已不存在类别的隐性误判。在两座真实地下矿井的智能手机地磁定位实验中验证，训练34个月后、未知设备代际与留出测量员条件下，当前条件化精度的5%分位数为0.39（299次重复训练，为机会水平16.5倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于传感器的AI系统很少在训练条件下运行：设备、人员与记录时段都会变化，而随机划分训练-测试集无法揭示这些变化带来的性能退化。因此需要一个能暴露部署条件下真实泛化能力、并对发布决策负责的评估协议。

Method: 提出分阶段、可问责的评估协议：评估被视为带声明参考水平与量化不确定性的测量。四个累积泛化阶段分别留出设备、受试者与时间。每阶段基于重复训练的分位数与具有正确类别数的机会参考进行比较；引入"超出当前范围率"（out-of-present-scope rate）以暴露模型向部署中已不存在类别的隐性误判；并用显式决策规则将发布决策绑定到5%分位数而非均值。以无基础设施的地磁定位、智能手机递归分类器在两座真实地下矿井中演示，并在第二处复现训练阶段方案。

Result: 未更改的模型在训练活动34个月后记录的数据、训练时未知的设备代际以及留出的测量员条件下重新评估。当前条件化精度的5%分位数为0.39（299次重复训练），是机会水平的16.5倍；在42个可达位置类别的构成上，该数值波动±0.08，为数倍于重复运行间的差异。单一配置的重复训练显示均值的误导性：双峰配置在基于均值的检验中被决定性通过，而其5%分位数却低于机会水平一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 评估应被构建为带参考水平与不确定性的测量，并以分位数（尤其5%分位数）而非均值驱动发布决策；所提协议能揭示随机划分无法暴露的跨设备、跨人员、跨时间的泛化退化与隐性误判，防止均值掩盖双峰性能带来的部署风险。

Abstract: Sensor-based AI systems are rarely operated under the conditions under which they were trained: devices, personnel and recording epochs change, and each change degrades performance in ways a random train-test split cannot reveal.
