Result: 在逻辑回归中追加16个IQP特征（n=8）使F1从0.462升至0.517（+0.055，p<0.0001）。同等特征数下Kernel PCA仅达0.493；该差距在12项检验的Benjamini-Hochberg校正后仍成立（p=0.00007）。随机森林、SVM、XGBoost、k-NN均无增益。特征选择方式影响显著：随机森林重要性引导选特征达F1=0.523，而编码最大不相关特征降至0.496。

Conclusion: IQP电路特征能提升线性分类器表现，机制指向线性表达能力的增强而非通用改进；且电路是放大了已有信息结构而非凭空创造，因此输入特征的选取方式对增益至关重要。

Abstract: Credit default prediction is a tabular classification problem in which modest gains in F1 translate directly into reduced financial exposure. We ask whether Instantaneous Quantum Polynomial-time (IQP) circuits can produce features that improve a classifier over both its raw classical baseline and Kernel PCA - the strongest unsupervised classical non-linear alternative - at an equal feature budget. The dataset provides 23 financial attributes per client; for an n-qubit circuit we select n of them, encode each as a rotation angle, and read 2n expectation values back out as new features. The motivation for using a quantum circuit is computational: an n-qubit IQP circuit runs in constant depth and encodes feature correlations in a 2^n-dimensional Hilbert space, whereas classical simulation of its exact output statistics scales exponentially in n. Using the UCI Default of Credit Card Clients dataset and five-fold cross-validation, we find that appending 16 IQP features (n = 8 qubits) to a Logistic Regression model raises F1 from 0.462 to 0.517 (+0.055, p < 0.0001). Kernel PCA, the next-best method, reaches only 0.493 at the same feature count; the gap survives Benjamini-Hochberg correction across 12 tests (p = 0.00007). No other classifier - Random Forest, SVM, XGBoost, or k-NN - benefits, which points to a linear-expressivity mechanism rather than a generic improvement. We also show that how the 8 input features are chosen matters: Random Forest importance-guided selection reaches F1 = 0.523, while encoding maximally uncorrelated features drops it to 0.496, demonstrating that the circuit amplifies informative structure rather than creating it from scratch.

</details>


### [240] [A positive resolution of the gap-entropy conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10529)
*P. M. Aronow,Nathan Kallus,Patrick Lopatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了固定置信度最佳臂识别问题的间隙-熵猜想：在高斯臂设定下，最优期望样本数（对臂标签排列平均）与 H(log(1/δ)+Ent(I)) 同阶，并给出达到该界的算法（附加极小项）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定置信度最佳臂识别问题中，期望样本量的最优阶数长期只有下界与上界之间的间隙（gap）未闭合，存在“间隙-熵”猜想。本文旨在对该猜想给出完整证明，明确最优样本复杂度关于间隙结构（而非仅最小间隙）的精确刻画。

Method: 在独立单位方差高斯臂、均值属于[0,1]且最优臂唯一的设定下，定义每臂间隙 Δ_i=μ_*-μ_i、复杂度量 H=ΣΔ_i^{-2}，并按间隙尺度 2^{-(r+1)}<Δ_i≤2^{-r} 将 H 分解为 p_r 权重，引入熵项 Ent(I)=Σ p_r log(1/p_r)。通过构造与下界分析，证明对任意满足逐实例 1-δ 正确率的算法，其期望样本数（对臂标签排列平均）被 H(log(1/δ)+Ent(I)) 的绝对常数倍上下夹逼；并给出与实例无关的算法，其期望样本数不超过该量的常数倍加上 g^{-2}loglog(e^e/g)（g 为最近竞争者间隙）。

Result: 证明了间隙-熵猜想：最优期望样本数（对臂标签排列平均）与 H(log(1/δ)+Ent(I)) 在绝对常数因子内相等；并构造出与实例无关的算法，其期望样本量以该量常数倍为界，额外仅增加 g^{-2}loglog(e^e/g) 项。

Conclusion: 固定置信度最佳臂识别的样本复杂度由间隙-熵量 H(log(1/δ)+Ent(I)) 精确刻画，间隙-熵猜想成立；所提算法能达到该最优界（附加可忽略项），进一步完善了最佳臂识别的理论下界与可实现性。

Abstract: We prove the gap-entropy conjecture for fixed-confidence best-arm identification with independent unit-variance Gaussian arms, means in $[0,1]$, and a unique optimal arm. For each suboptimal arm $i$, let $Δ_i=μ_*-μ_i$ be its gap from the optimal mean, and write $H=\sum_{i\ne *}Δ_i^{-2}$. Let $p_r$ be the fraction of $H$ contributed by arms with $2^{-(r+1)}<Δ_i\le2^{-r}$, and let $\mathrm{Ent}(I)=\sum_{r:p_r>0} p_r\log(1/p_r)$. Among all algorithms that identify the optimal arm with probability at least $1-δ$ on every Gaussian instance, the optimal expected number of samples on a given instance, averaged over all permutations of the arm labels, is within absolute constant factors of $H(\log(1/δ)+\mathrm{Ent}(I))$. Moreover, there is an algorithm, independent of the instance, whose expected number of samples is bounded by a constant multiple of this quantity plus $g^{-2}\log\log(e^e/g)$, where $g=\min_{i\ne *}Δ_i$ is the gap to the closest competitor.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Revisiting the Aerts-Broekaert-Smets quantum model of the liar paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09228)
*Massimiliano Sassoli de Bianchi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文对Aerts、Broekaert与Smets提出的双句说谎者悖论量子模型进行重构，用狄拉克符号明确区分"决策真值"与"语义推理真值"，并证明维数扩增对应于使非悖论构型可分离、真实说谎态纠缠的因子化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望以教学性重构为出发点，厘清该量子认知模型的结构，并揭示此前未被明确指出的性质（真值来源的区分、纠缠与可分离性、哈密顿量与转移概率的显式形式），进而讨论模型与一般审议过程的关系。

Method: 采用狄拉克（Dirac）符号重写模型：将一维真值空间扩增为含"来源自由度"的空间，区分来自决策的真值与来自语义推理的真值；给出四态循环演化的哈密顿量及其紧凑算符形式，并推导闭合形式的转移概率；对比扩充空间与四维"纯真值"空间的幺正等价表示。

Result: 1) 维数扩增对应一个因子化，使非悖论构型在该因子化下可分离，而真正的说谎者态呈纠缠态；2) 推导出生成四态循环演化的哈密顿量并写成紧凑算符形式，转移概率有闭式解；3) 说谎者循环在四维纯真值空间中有幺正等价表示，此时未测量的说谎者态可分离，故幺正动力学部分并不要求维数扩增；4) 但测量结构要求扩增；5) 一旦引入修正（revision）过程，动力学也要求扩增，因为真值内容相同但来源不同的态具有不同后继，来源自由度充当最小形式的认知记忆。

Conclusion: 模型的结构澄清表明，维数扩增的必要性来源于测量结构与修正动力学，而非幺正演化本身；来源自由度可视为一种最小认知记忆，作者最后探讨了该模型与更一般审议过程之间的可能联系。

Abstract: The quantum model of the two-sentence liar paradox proposed by Aerts, Broekaert, and Smets is an early example of the use of quantum formalism to describe cognitive dynamics. Our reconstruction is primarily pedagogical in intent, but it also leads to a number of clarifications, and to some new observations, concerning the structure of the model. Rewriting the model in Dirac notation, we make explicit the distinction between truth values originating from a decision and from semantic inference, and show that the associated enlargement of the one-sentence state space corresponds to a factorization with respect to which the non-paradoxical configurations are separable while the genuine liar state is entangled. We also derive the Hamiltonian generating the four-state cyclic evolution, express it in compact operator form, and give the resulting transition probabilities in closed form. We emphasize that the liar cycle admits a unitarily equivalent representation in the four-dimensional truth-only space, where the unmeasured liar state is separable, so the dimensional enlargement is not required by the unitary part of the dynamics; it is required, however, by the measurement structure. The enlargement is also required by the dynamics as soon as revision processes are admitted, in which states with identical truth content but different origins have different successors, the origin degree of freedom then acting as a minimal form of cognitive memory. We conclude by discussing the possible relationship between the model and more general deliberative processes.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [AgentHijack: Visual Patch Attacks on Multimodal Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09212)
*Zhihao Liu,Hongyu Sun,Zhiyuan Fu,Xiaonan Duan,Jice Wang,Shangru Zhao,Weizhi Meng,Wuxin Yang,Yangfan Zhou,Yuqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向计算机使用代理（CUA）的图像触发命令注入端到端评估框架，通过本地视觉补丁测试从截图输入、VLM生成、动作解析到环境执行的完整链路，在真实环境中验证了视觉信号可传播至执行管线并造成实际环境风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注VLM输出层面的视觉对抗影响，缺乏对CUA完整执行链路（截图输入→VLM生成→动作解析→环境执行）是否产生可验证环境后果的端到端评估。作者希望验证：本地视觉补丁能否诱导CUA产生真实的环境级危害。

Method: 在作者可控的GitHub Pages页面和本地部署的CSDN克隆站点上训练并部署视觉补丁；在真实环境中针对5个开源或公开可用的GUI代理/VLM后端进行评估；构建并聚合600个实例级在线测试用例；采用T-ASR、TAPR、E2E-ASR三类指标进行量化。

Result: T-ASR达84.5%，TAPR达47.0%，E2E-ASR达20.3%。轨迹分析显示，部分成功案例中代理先执行恶意终端命令，随后继续执行原有良性任务，表明攻击具有隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 优化的本地视觉信号不仅能影响VLM输出，还可传播穿透开源CUA的执行管线，产生真实环境风险，说明CUA在视觉触发命令注入方面存在端到端安全威胁。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end evaluation framework for image-triggered command injection against computer-use agents (CUAs). The goal is to test whether a local visual patch can induce verifiable environmental consequences along the full chain of screenshot input, VLM generation, action parsing, and environment execution. We train and deploy patches on author-controlled GitHub Pages pages and a locally deployed CSDN clone, and evaluate them in real environments across five open-source or publicly available GUI-agent or vision-language-model (VLM) backends. Our experiment aggregates 600 instance-level online cases, with T-ASR, TAPR, and E2E-ASR reaching 84.5%, 47.0%, and 20.3%, respectively. Trajectory analysis further shows that in some successful cases the agent first executes a malicious terminal command and then continues the original benign task. These results indicate that optimized local visual signals can affect not only VLM outputs but also propagate through the execution pipeline of open CUAs and create real environmental risk.

</details>


### [243] [Compute-Bounded Security Assurance - Coverage, Verification, and Response under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09229)
*Jithin VG,Ditto PS*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个资源受限的理论框架，将"重复成功""唯一覆盖""被接受的证据""运行防护"等不同度量区分开来，指出正相关并不必然意味着覆盖上限低于1，且有限预算观测无法识别渐近支持上限；贡献在于理论综合与反例，而非实证扩展律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 额外的推理算力可能提升安全保证任务的成功数，但"重复成功""唯一覆盖""被接受的证据""运行防护"是彼此不同的量，常被混用并导致外推谬误，因此需要一个资源受限框架来严格区分这些度量。

Method: 构建资源受限框架：对条件独立重复尝试，覆盖率为 C_n = 1 - E[(1-Θ)^n]，极限为 1 - P(Θ=0)；构造两个均值成功与成对相关相同但极限覆盖不同的模型作为反例；将其与估计均值所用的有效样本量区分；并连接可错证据校验、事实基础的proper评分、完整资源核算、服务容量及含缓解延迟的响应模型；提出分离证据分析、裁决与运行权限的概念性防御架构及评估协议（留出任务、配对比较、负例、不确定性报告）。

Result: 正成对结果相关本身并不蕴含覆盖上限低于1（给出同均值同相关但极限覆盖不同的两个模型）；有限预算观测一般无法识别渐近支持上限；数值说明均为解析性的。

Conclusion: 贡献是一致性的理论综合与针对无效外推的反例集合，而非实证扩展律；不主张模型对标结果、硬件基准或一般攻防均衡。

Abstract: Additional inference compute can increase the number of correctly resolved security-assurance tasks, but repeated success, unique coverage, accepted evidence, and operational protection are different quantities. We develop a resource-constrained framework that separates them. For repeated conditionally independent attempts with latent success probability $Θ$, coverage is $C_n = 1 - E[(1-Θ)^n]$, and its limiting value is $1 - P(Θ= 0)$. Positive pairwise outcome correlation does not by itself imply a ceiling below one: we construct two models with the same mean success and pairwise correlation but different limiting coverage. We distinguish this result from the effective sample size used to estimate a mean, and show why finite-budget observations cannot generally identify an asymptotic support ceiling. We then connect coverage to fallible evidence checking, proper scoring of factual grounding, complete resource accounting, service capacity, and a response model that includes mitigation delay. A conceptual defensive architecture separates evidence analysis, adjudication, and operational authority. An evaluation protocol specifies held-out tasks, paired comparisons, negative cases, and uncertainty reporting. The contribution is a consistent theoretical synthesis and a set of counterexamples to invalid extrapolations, rather than an empirical scaling law. All numerical illustrations are analytic; no model-parity result, hardware benchmark, or general attacker-defender equilibrium is claimed.

</details>


### [244] [In RAG We Trust? Measuring Robustness of Retrieval-Augmented Generation Under Document Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09243)
*Iliano Fasolino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过588次因子化实验，测量了Llama 3.1 8B在检索上下文被部分投毒时的性能退化，发现准确率从清洁上下文的77.9%降至全部三段被污染时的43.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）虽能减少幻觉，却引入了新的攻击面：若检索文本被篡改，模型可能复述虚假信息。作者希望量化小型量化模型在部分检索上下文被投毒时的退化程度。

Method: 在基于FEVER构建的事实核查任务上，对Llama 3.1 8B进行588次因子化实验。测试三种污染策略（实体替换、数字替换、否定），分别施加于三段检索段落中的零到三段；采用查询级bootstrap区间复核结果，并以词汇重叠代理指标衡量无支撑生成。

Result: 清洁上下文准确率为77.9%，三段全被污染时降至43.5%；实体替换导致最多原本正确的答案被翻转；数字类污染在污染段落占少数时影响平稳，一旦占多数则骤升。模型很少编造新的虚假信息，主要反应是弃答，且无支撑生成的词汇重叠代理指标在攻击下反而下降。

Conclusion: 这是一项小规模测量研究，标签为粗粒度自动生成，各策略间对比在控制解码并引入更强裁决机制前仅具提示性意义。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds a language model in retrieved documents, which reduces hallucination but creates a new attack surface: if retrieved text is tampered with, the model may repeat the falsehood. We study how much a small quantized model, Llama 3.1 8B, degrades when a fraction of its retrieved context is poisoned. Three corruption strategies are tested, entity swap, number swap, and negation, each applied to zero, one, two, or three of the three retrieved passages, over a factorial sweep of 588 runs on a fact-checking task built from FEVER. Accuracy falls from 77.9% on clean context to 43.5% when all three passages are corrupted. Entity swap flips the largest share of answers that were correct on clean context. Number-based corruption stays flat while poisoned passages are a minority and jumps once they form a majority, a pattern we re-check with query-level bootstrap intervals. The model rarely invents new falsehoods; its dominant reaction is to abstain, and a lexical overlap proxy of unsupported generation falls under attack rather than rising. The study is a small-scale measurement with coarse automated labels; we treat the strategy contrasts as suggestive until decoding is controlled and stronger adjudication is in place.

</details>


### [245] [Cross User/App Network Attacks - Hijacking TCP Connections and DNS Cache Poisoning via a Malicious User/App (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09345)
*Tamir Shahar,Amit Klein*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"现代TCP/DNS连接因随机化ISN和源端口而免疫路径外攻击"的传统认知，提出一种本地恶意应用与远程路径外攻击者协作的攻击模型，可推断TCP ISN与DNS UDP源端口，进而实现TCP连接劫持与DNS缓存投毒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCP随机初始序列号（ISN）与UDP随机源端口被普遍视为抵御路径外攻击的核心机制，学界与工业界普遍认为此类攻击已不可行。作者旨在检验这一假设，探索在攻击者实际处于路径外、但客户端运行非特权恶意应用时，是否仍能突破这些"内置安全特性"。

Method: 攻击利用标准socket API（如bind()）、协议机制（IP选项）以及操作系统特性（cBPF、procfs）来推断目标连接的TCP ISN与UDP源端口。核心在于利用主流操作系统ISN生成算法的特定性质，通过本地恶意应用与远程路径外攻击者协同，推断敏感连接状态。

Result: 在Linux、Android、Windows、macOS和iOS上实现了TCP连接劫持；在Windows、Android及Linux中流行的systemd-resolved DNS stub resolver上实现了DNS缓存投毒。评估覆盖多种操作系统与真实部署场景，包括端口保持型NAT集成路由器后的环境。已向Microsoft、Apple、Linux和Google披露，促成多项补丁发布。

Conclusion: 研究证明即使具备随机化ISN和随机源端口等防护，本地非特权恶意应用与远程路径外攻击者的协作仍可有效实施TCP劫持与DNS投毒，推翻了路径外攻击"不切实际"的既有假设，并强调了操作系统实现细节对协议安全性的关键影响。

Abstract: Off-path network attacks against TCP and DNS (over UDP) client-server connections are generally considered impractical nowadays, due to built-in security features in these protocols, e.g. randomized TCP (initial) sequence numbers and randomized UDP source ports, respectively. In this work, we refute this presumption by demonstrating that an unprivileged malicious application running on the client (but practically off-path), when combined with a remote off-path adversary, can enable powerful network attacks against such connections. We show how such a local--remote collaboration between the malicious application and a remote adversary allows inference of sensitive connection state, including TCP sequence numbers and DNS stub-resolver UDP source ports.
  Our attacks exploit standard socket API calls such as bind(), protocol mechanisms such as IP options, and operating system features such as cBPF and procfs to infer the TCP initial sequence number (ISN) and the UDP source port in use by the connection of interest. Specifically, we take advantage of certain properties of the ISN generation algorithm as implemented in major operating systems.
  We demonstrate TCP connection hijacking in Linux, Android, Windows, macOS and iOS, and DNS cache poisoning against Windows, Android and the popular systemd-resolved DNS stub resolver in Linux.
  We evaluate our techniques across multiple operating systems and realistic deployment settings, including environments behind port-preserving NAT-integrated routers.
  We disclosed our techniques to Microsoft, Apple, Linux and Google, which led to the release of several patches.

</details>


### [246] [Encrypt What Matters: When Selective Homomorphic Inference Is Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09357)
*Ali Backour,Juan Reyes,Jaime Punyed,Ana Onoprishvili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究"选择性同态推理"：仅对敏感感兴趣区域（ROI）加密，其余计算以明文进行，在不重训练的前提下获得与全FHE相同的输出；其效率取决于加密依赖在网络中扩散的速度，局部性保持架构可实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密（FHE）虽能在不向服务器泄露隐私数据的情况下完成推理，但对整个输入做FHE评估代价高昂。因此需要一种只加密敏感区域、其余部分明文计算的方案，以降低开销。

Method: 提出并研究选择性同态推理：仅加密输入的敏感感兴趣区域（ROI），与ROI无关的计算以明文执行；在无需重训练的条件下，使输出与对同一模型做全FHE评估一致。通过分析加密依赖在网络中的传播速度来刻画其效率。

Result: 对于较小的加密ROI，局部性保持（locality-preserving）架构可获得数量级的同态评估加速；而具有早期全局混合（early global mixing）特性的架构几乎无法获得加速。

Conclusion: 局部性（locality）是决定选择性同态推理收益的关键架构属性，为面向隐私保护推理的网络架构设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) enables inference on private data without revealing it to the server, but evaluating an entire input under FHE is expensive. We study \emph{selective homomorphic inference}, where only a sensitive region of interest (ROI) is encrypted, and computations independent of that region are performed in plaintext. Selective evaluation produces the same output as full FHE on the same model, without retraining. Its efficiency depends on how quickly encrypted dependencies spread through the network. For small encrypted ROIs, locality-preserving architectures can achieve order-of-magnitude homomorphic-evaluation speedups, whereas architectures with early global mixing provide essentially no speedup. These results identify locality as the key architectural property governing the benefit of selective homomorphic inference.

</details>


### [247] [LLMSec-AV: A Vulnerability Taxonomy and LLM-Driven Software Weakness Discovery Framework for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09386)
*Md. Wasiul Haque,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AV专用缺陷分类体系LLMSec-AV，用LLM分析Autoware中161个函数，恢复76%已知缺陷位置，远超传统静态分析工具，但分类体系主要提升可解释性而非召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车依赖数百万行安全关键代码，通用分析工具无法判断哪些代码会影响车辆运动。研究旨在检验带明确AV安全知识的大语言模型能否在缺陷检测上超越规则型工具。

Method: 从漏洞记录、安全公告和AV安全文献中提炼18类缺陷，构建AV漏洞分类体系，并集成到基于LLM的LLMSec-AV框架。在Autoware上将770个翻译单元分解为4,673个函数，选取161个函数在四种提示条件下分析（分类体系上下文、374条历史披露检索、多步分析）。结果与46个上游修复挖掘出的缺陷位置及标志量匹配的置换基线对比；同时运行CodeQL、Semgrep、cppcheck和Clang静态分析器（CodeQL与Semgrep加入AV专用规则），并用AFL++和sanitizer测试生成的模糊测试用例。

Result: LLM条件最多恢复46个已知缺陷位置中的76%，优于传统分析器：CodeQL、Semgrep和Clang静态分析器均未匹配到，cppcheck虽产生1,301条告警但仅匹配1处。无辅助提示的检测性能相近，说明分类体系未驱动召回率。但分类体系上下文将归类到具体缺陷类的发现比例从接近零提升至80%以上，改善可解释性与分诊。18类中有6类无法直接表示为静态分析规则。

Conclusion: LLMSec-AV提供了面向AV、机器可读的漏洞分类体系，证明LLM可通过识别与组织真实AV软件中的安全相关发现，补充传统分析工具。

Abstract: Automated vehicles rely on millions of lines of safety-critical software, yet general-purpose analyzers do not understand which code can affect vehicle motion. This study asks whether large language models (LLMs) with explicit automated-vehicle (AV) security knowledge improve weakness detection beyond rule-based tools. We developed an AV vulnerability taxonomy with 18 weakness classes from vulnerability records, security advisories, and AV-security literature, and integrated it into LLM-based Security Analysis for Automated Vehicles (LLMSec-AV). Evaluated on Autoware, the framework decomposed 770 translation units into 4,673 functions and analyzed 161 functions under four prompting conditions involving taxonomy context, retrieval from 374 prior disclosures, and multi-step analysis. Findings were compared with 46 weakness locations mined from upstream fixes and a flag-volume-matched permutation baseline. CodeQL, Semgrep, cppcheck, and the Clang Static Analyzer evaluated the same code, with AV-specific rules added to CodeQL and Semgrep. Generated fuzzing harnesses were tested using AFL++ and sanitizers. LLM conditions recovered up to 76% of the 46 known weakness locations, outperforming conventional analyzers. CodeQL, Semgrep, and the Clang Static Analyzer matched none, while cppcheck matched one despite 1,301 alerts. Unaided prompting achieved similar detection performance, showing that the taxonomy did not drive recall. However, taxonomy context increased the share of findings assigned to a weakness class from near zero to over 80%, improving interpretability and triage. Six of the 18 classes could not be directly represented as static-analysis rules. LLMSec-AV introduces an AV-specific, machine-readable vulnerability taxonomy for weakness discovery and shows that LLMs can complement conventional analyzers by identifying and organizing safety-relevant findings in real AV software.

</details>


### [248] [X-amine509: Predicting the Practical Risk Level of Enterprise X.509 Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09402)
*Cameron Keith,Shubh Patel,JD Kilgallin,Caleb Shorter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: X-amine509 是一个两阶段 X.509 证书风险分诊系统，利用机器学习快速排序证书风险，将高风险证书路由至确定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业维护大规模 X.509 证书清单时面临优先级排序难题：确定性分析工具能精确识别违规，但对数百万证书穷举应用不具可操作性。

Method: 将证书风险量化为综合评分，基于 CA/Browser Forum Baseline Requirements、NIST IR 8547/SP 800-57 及加密强度标准的 177 项缺陷检查，按四个安全严重性层级加权。采集 1,027,714 张证书评分并训练模型，使用 Extra Trees、Decision Tree 等。

Result: 留出测试集 201,976 张证书上，Extra Trees 达 R²=0.993、MAE=2.26；Decision Tree R²=0.986，单机每秒处理 370 万张。聚合 NDCG>0.997，严重性层级分类准确率 99.76%，关键层级召回率 98.90%。13 个月后 571,374 张新证书测试中，模型保持 MAE<6.8、R²≥0.915、NDCG>0.988、严重性准确率≥99.52%、关键召回率≥97.03%。

Conclusion: 特征重要性分析表明有效期、扩展密钥用法配置、负序列号编码和自签名状态是最强风险预测因子，在分诊阶段提供粗粒度可解释性。

Abstract: Enterprises managing large X.509 certificate inventories face a prioritization problem: deterministic analysis tools that precisely identify standards violations are indispensable for remediation, but applying them exhaustively across millions of certificates is operationally impractical. We present X-amine509, a two-stage triage system that uses machine learning to rapidly rank certificates by predicted risk and route only the highest-risk items to full deterministic analysis. Certificate risk is quantified as a composite score derived from 177 defect checks grounded in CA/Browser Forum Baseline Requirements, NIST IR 8547/SP 800-57, and cryptographic strength criteria, weighted by security severity across four tiers ranging from cryptographic breaks to minor compliance deviations. We collected 1,027,714 X.509 certificates from Fortune 500, .gov, and .edu domains and scored each using this rubric. On a held-out test set of 201,976 certificates, our best model (Extra Trees) achieves $R^2$ of 0.993 with MAE of 2.26, while Decision Tree scores $R^2$ of 0.986 at 3.7 million certificates per second on a single machine. Ranking quality confirms the triage value: aggregate NDCG exceeds 0.997, and severity-tier classification reports 99.76% accuracy with 98.90% recall on critical-tier defects. Thirteen months later, we retrieved another 571,374 certificates to test our models' durability over time, and the Extra Trees and Decision Tree models maintain MAE below 6.8, $R^2$ of at least 0.915, aggregate NDCG above 0.988, severity-tier accuracy of at least 99.52%, and critical-tier recall of at least 97.03%. Feature importance analysis identifies validity period, Extended Key Usage configuration, negative serial number encoding, and self-signed status as the strongest risk predictors, providing coarse interpretability at the triage stage.

</details>


### [249] [An Experimental Evaluation of Multimodal Prompt Injection Attacks on Agentic AI Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09404)
*Viet K. Nguyen,Mohammad I. Husain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MMPIBench，一个可复现的智能体间接提示注入基准，通过六种视觉载体与音频通道测量注入指令在智能体中的传播，发现攻击完成率约1%但尝试率12.8%，防御瓶颈主要在规划阶段，且模型差异远大于框架差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架让语言模型能够规划、记忆并调用可触达真实文件、邮件与服务的工具；多数智能体还会读取图像，这给攻击者提供了绕过用户、直接向智能体上下文注入文本的通道。现有评测多关注文本注入或仅报告最终攻击成功率，缺少对注入指令在感知—规划—工具调用全链路中传播过程的可复现度量，也缺少对视觉之外其他感知通道的评估。

Method: 构建MMPIBench基准：以固定攻击集合通过六种视觉载体（OCR文本、图层叠加、EXIF元数据、二维码、伪造界面、混合方式）投递，并记录注入指令从感知经规划到工具调用的传播深度。实验覆盖720次运行，涉及6个框架、5个基础模型、6种载体、4类攻击目标；随后将基准扩展到音频通道，评估模型与框架对音频的接收与投递能力。

Result: 攻击在约1%的运行中完成，但在12.8%的运行中被尝试；这一差距几乎完全在规划步骤被弥合——模型读到注入指令后拒绝执行。是否采取行动主要取决于模型而非框架：某一模型从不尝试攻击并在59.7%的运行中识别出注入，另两个模型的尝试率达23.6%。音频扩展中，5个模型中仅2个能摄入音频，6个框架中仅3个能投递音频；但在信号到达的单元中攻击完成率为49%，其中一个模型达75%。

Conclusion: 仅报告攻击完成率会低估实际暴露面，应同时度量攻击尝试与注入识别。感知通道防御存在结构性不均衡：视觉之外的通道（如音频）覆盖面更窄，但防护显著更弱。智能体的注入鲁棒性主要由基础模型决定，规划阶段是当前最有效的拦截点。

Abstract: Agentic AI frameworks let a language model plan, keep memory, and call tools that reach real files, mail, and services. Most of these agents also read images, which gives an attacker a way to put text into the agent's context without going through the user. We present MMPIBench, a reproducible benchmark that measures what happens next. It delivers a fixed set of attacks through six visual carriers (OCR text, overlays, EXIF metadata, QR codes, fake interfaces, and hybrids) and records how far each injected instruction travels through the agent, from perception through planning to the tool call. Across 720 runs covering six frameworks, five foundation models, six carriers, and four attacker objectives, attacks complete in approximately 1% of runs but are attempted in 12.8%, and the gap is closed almost entirely at the planning step, where the model reads the injected instruction and declines to act on it. The model matters far more than the framework for whether an instruction is acted on. One model never attempts an attack and recognizes the injection in 59.7% of runs, while two others attempt in 23.6%. We then extend the benchmark to audio, the only other raw perceptual channel current frontier models accept. Only two of the five models ingest audio and only three of the six frameworks deliver it, but where the signal arrives the attack completes in 49% of cells, and in 75% for one model. Reporting completion alone therefore understates exposure, and perceptual channels beyond vision are narrower but much less defended.

</details>


### [250] [DuplexJail: Safety Alignment Breaks Under Spoken Interruption in Full-Duplex Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09420)
*Jaechul Roh,Deepak Chandran,Amir Houmansadr,Andrea Fanelli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DuplexJail攻击，通过在用户音频通道植入与请求无关的固定语音提示来打断全双工语音模型，发现"语音打断"是一种新的越狱攻击向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型可在生成响应的同时接收用户语音，这一交互特性形成了尚未被充分探索的攻击面，需要评估其在持续交互过程中的安全性。

Method: 通过用户音频通道传递固定的、与请求无关的口语提示，并对比两种打断策略：有害请求结束后的固定延迟打断，以及依据模型流式文本中的线索触发的拒绝触发打断；在4个开源模型和AdvBench、HarmBench的720条有害请求上评测，且所有试验无论是否发生打断均计分。

Result: 固定延迟打断使PersonaPlex在AdvBench上的整体响应攻击成功率升至40.3%、PersonaPlex-RL升至48.7%，分别提高+33.8和+39.3个百分点；拒绝触发策略分别达35.6%和48.6%。部分条件下FLM-Audio有害响应率上升，而BayLing-Duplex下降。

Conclusion: 语音打断被确认为一种越狱攻击向量，说明有必要在持续的全双工交互全过程中评估模型安全性。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models accept user speech while generating responses, creating an underexplored attack surface. We introduce DuplexJail, which delivers fixed, request-independent spoken prompts through the user audio channel. We compare fixed-delay interruption after the harmful request ends with refusal-triggered interruption following a cue in the model's streaming text. Across four open-source models and 720 harmful requests from AdvBench and HarmBench, fixed-delay interruption raises whole-response attack success rates on AdvBench to 40.3% for PersonaPlex and 48.7% for PersonaPlex-RL, increases of +33.8 and +39.3 percentage points. The refusal-triggered policy reaches 35.6% and 48.6%, respectively, with all trials scored regardless of whether an interruption occurs. Selected conditions also increase FLM-Audio's harmful-response rate, while BayLing-Duplex shows decreases. These findings identify spoken interruption as a jailbreak attack vector and motivate evaluating safety throughout ongoing full-duplex interaction.

</details>


### [251] [FPGA Acceleration of Fully Homomorphic Encryption with Adaptive Key Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09423)
*Zhihan Xu,Jayashree Adivarahan,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor K. Prasanna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种内存高效的自适应 FPGA 全同态加密加速器，在计算中动态选择 KLSS 与 HKS 两种密钥切换方法，以降低整体延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE 支持隐私保护云服务，但计算开销巨大，硬件加速至关重要。密钥切换是 FHE 的主要性能瓶颈。新兴的 KLSS 方法降低了部分运算复杂度，但对计算精度要求高于传统 HKS，二者在计算与内存需求上存在权衡，其相对延迟高度依赖硬件并行度、FHE 安全参数与片上内存容量，尤其在 FPGA 上需谨慎平衡内存资源与并行度。因此单一静态方法难以始终最优。

Method: 1) 提出内存高效的 KLSS 数据通路，消除片外密文传输；2) 建立性能模型，分析并比较 KLSS 与 HKS 的开销，揭示支持两种方法的自适应方案可取得更低整体延迟；3) 基于该性能模型设计自适应 FPGA FHE 加速器，在计算过程中动态选择 HKS 或 KLSS；4) 在 Alveo U280 上实现并在多个 FHE 基准上评估。

Result: 与当前最先进的 FPGA 加速器相比，自适应方案在 bootstrapping 延迟上取得 1.84–3.31 倍加速，在安全图像分类上取得 1.66–2.52 倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过内存高效 KLSS 数据通路与性能模型驱动的自适应选择机制，在 FPGA 上动态切换 HKS 与 KLSS，可有效缓解密钥切换瓶颈，显著优于静态方法与现有最先进加速器。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables privacy-preserving cloud services but incurs substantial computation overhead, making hardware acceleration essential. Among FHE operations, key-switching is a major performance bottleneck. Recent cryptographic advances introduce a novel key-switching method (i.e., KLSS) that reduces certain operational complexity but demands higher computational precision than the traditional Hybrid Key Switching (HKS) method. This trade-off leads to distinct computation and memory requirements, making the relative latency of KLSS and HKS highly dependent on hardware parallelism, FHE security parameters, and available on-chip memory capacity, particularly on FPGA platforms, where memory resources and parallelism must be carefully balanced.
  In this work, we first propose a memory-efficient KLSS datapath that eliminates off-chip ciphertext transfers. We then develop a performance model to analyze and compare the overheads of both KLSS and HKS. Our analysis reveals that an adaptive solution supporting both methods can achieve lower overall latency than a static method during FHE computation. Guided by the performance model, we design an adaptive FPGA-based FHE accelerator that dynamically selects between HKS and KLSS during computation. We implement the accelerator on an Alveo U280 and evaluate it across multiple FHE benchmarks. Experimental results demonstrate that our adaptive solution achieves a 1.84-3.31$\times$ speedup in bootstrapping latency and a 1.66-2.52$\times$ speedup in secure image classification compared to state-of-the-art FPGA accelerators.

</details>


### [252] [Concept drift mitigation through community and spectral graph analysis for the detectionof cyberattacks in network traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09442)
*Julien Michel,Abdul Qadir Khan,Majed Jaber,Pierre Parrend*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 t-robustness——一种与检测模型无关、在特征空间中定义的稳定性评分，将干预点从学习后的模型修复前移至学习前的特征选择；在 UGR16 数据集上的实验表明，t-robust 特征空间在基线崩溃时仍能维持检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络流量中正常行为与攻击技术共同演化，形成概念漂移，导致检测器在两次更新之间迅速失效、始终落后于攻击者。传统应对方式是在漂移发生后修复模型，属于被动滞后干预。

Method: 作者将干预点从模型转移到特征空间，提出 t-robustness：对每个特征独立计算、无需依赖任何检测模型、可在整个特征空间内比较的稳定性分数。该分数结合了特征相邻统计状态之间的逐步距离与其相对于初始状态的累积散度，从而避免缓慢单调漂移被误判为稳定。候选特征来源于扫描、DoS 及端点通信留下的异常网络连接模式，并通过图社区指标与谱指标进行刻画。

Result: 在 UGR16 数据集上，跨三个学习场景与一个对照场景，并在不做模型更新的条件下进行评估。结果表明 t-robust 特征空间在基线崩溃时仍能维持检测：最后一个测试区间上的保留期望值达到 0.6025，而图社区特征为 0.5230，基础 NetFlow 特征为 0.3831。

Conclusion: 在特征空间中于学习前进行选择，可有效缓解概念漂移对检测性能的侵蚀，其特征稳定性以模型无关的方式量化且可跨特征比较，为对抗持续演化的网络攻击提供了更具鲁棒性的检测基础。

Abstract: In network traffic, legitimate behaviours and attack techniques evolve jointly - the phenomenon known as 'concept drift' [1]. Every detector is thereby left obsolete between two updates, and always one step behind adversaries. In this work, we propose to move the point of intervention from the model, repaired after the drift, to the feature space, selected before learning. We therefore introduce t-robustness, a stability score defined for each feature independently of any detection model, comparable across an entire feature space. It combines the step-by-step distance between successive statistical states of a feature, and its cumulative divergence from its initial state, so that a slow monotonic drift cannot pass for stability. The candidates are drawn from abnormal network connectivity patterns left by scans, DoS and communications between endpoints, read through graph community metrics and spectral metrics. The evaluation is performed on the UGR16 dataset, across three learning scenarios and a control scenario, as well as without model update, and demonstrate that t-robust feature spaces sustain detection where the baselines collapse: retained expectancy at the last test interval reaches 0.6025, against 0.5230 for graph community features and 0.3831 for the base NetFlow features.

</details>


### [253] [Audio Deepfake Detection Using Temporal Coherence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09489)
*Justin D. Norman,Sarah Barrington*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于CLAP嵌入的时序一致性分析框架，通过段间余弦相似度统计特征训练轻量集成分类器，可跨语音、器乐与带唱音乐区分真实与合成音频，并揭示特征-标签反转与语音-音乐方向反转两大经验现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成音频（deepfake audio）的泛滥带来信息完整性威胁，包括语音克隆欺诈与合成音乐版权纠纷，现有深度学习方法普遍计算开销大、可解释性不足，且缺乏跨语音与音乐领域的统一检测分析视角。

Method: 以Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP)嵌入为基础，对音频分段计算嵌入间的成对余弦相似度，并从相似度分布中提取统计特征，据此训练轻量级集成分类器进行真伪判别，框架覆盖语音、器乐及带唱音乐。

Result: 在语音与音乐领域均取得具竞争力的检测性能，同时兼具可解释性与计算高效性；并发现两个经验规律：（1）特征-标签反转现象，29个统计特征中有21个在训练与真实部署场景间判别方向发生反转；（2）语音-音乐方向反转现象，熵在语音与音乐deepfake中的判别方向相反。

Conclusion: 基于CLAP时序一致性的轻量集成方法可作为常见深度学习检测方案的可解释、低算力替代，跨语音与音乐域保持竞争性能；但其暴露的特征方向不稳定性与域间反转现象表明，音频deepfake检测器在真实部署中面临显著泛化挑战。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated audio (so-called "deepfake" audio) poses significant threats to information integrity, from voice cloning fraud to synthetic music copyright disputes. We present a temporal coherence analysis framework built upon Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) embeddings that spans speech, instrumental music, and music with vocals. By computing pairwise cosine similarities between audio segment embeddings and extracting statistical features from the resulting distributions, we train lightweight ensemble classifiers that reliably distinguish authentic from synthetic audio. Our work provides an interpretable, computationally efficient alternative to common deep learning methods while still achieving competitive performance across speech and music domains. Further, we reveal two notable empirical findings about audio deepfakes: (1) a feature-label inversion phenomenon in which 21 of 29 statistical features reverse their discriminative direction between training and in-the-wild deployment, and (2) a speech--music direction reversal in which entropy discriminates in opposite directions for speech and music deepfakes.

</details>


### [254] [An Efficient and Effective Agentic Group Shilling Attack on Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09551)
*Quoc Viet Nguyen,Trinh Pham,Viet Huynh,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Bay Vo,Thanh Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentic Group Attack System (AGAS)，一种协同式 shilling 攻击框架，通过中心 Coordinator 指挥角色可切换的 worker agents，自适应地对不同受害推荐模型进行目标商品推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统依赖用户交互，易受 shilling 攻击（注入虚假用户以操纵排名与曝光）。现有攻击多依赖目标特定微调或固定 profile 模板，因而难以适应不同受害模型且更易被检测。

Method: AGAS 采用中心 Coordinator + 一组角色可切换 worker agents 的协同架构：Coordinator 在进展停滞或压制信号增强时动态调整策略；workers 共享同一目标，并在活跃/非活跃角色间切换以规避重复模式。

Result: 在相同攻击预算与评估协议下，AGAS 在目标推广上稳定优于强基线，同时更好地保持良性推荐质量、削弱代表性检测器，并比既有攻击具有更高效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，防御推荐系统可能需要能应对自适应 shilling 战役的机制，而非仅针对孤立的虚假 profile 注入。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recommender systems have become core infrastructure for modern online platforms, personalizing content at scale and strongly influencing what users see, click on, and purchase. However, this dependence on user interaction also exposes them to shilling attacks, where malicious actors can inject fake profiles to distort item rankings and control visibility. Existing attacks often rely on target-specific fine-tuning or fixed profile templates, making them either difficult to adapt to different victims or easier to detect. To overcome these limitations, we propose the Agentic Group Attack System (AGAS), a coordinated shilling framework where a central Coordinator directs a group of role-switching worker agents to adaptively promote a target item across different victim families. The Coordinator dynamically adjusts the strategy when progress stalls or suppression signals increase, while workers pursue a shared objective and switch between active and inactive roles to avoid repetitive patterns. Under the same attack budgets and evaluation protocols, AGAS consistently surpasses strong baselines in target promotion while better preserving benign recommendation quality, weakening representative detectors, and achieving higher efficiency than prior attacks. These findings also emphasize that defending recommender systems may require mechanisms that can handle adaptive shilling campaigns, not just isolated fake-profile injections. Our code is available at https://github.com/phkhanhtrinh23/AGAS.

</details>


### [255] [Decentralized network congestion control for DAG-based distributed ledger system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09961)
*Mayank Pandey,Rachit Agarwal,Sandeep Kumar Shukla,Nishchal Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文针对DAG型DLT网络中的去中心化拥塞控制问题，提出一种基于变量与行为的节点特定PoW模型，通过非合作博弈建模并证明纳什均衡的存在性，实现对交易垃圾信息的抑制与惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式通信系统的拥塞控制研究成熟，但去中心化网络（尤其是区块链与DAG型DLT）的拥塞控制研究较新且不足。DLT网络拥塞由交易垃圾信息、用户规模增长及新代币发行等因素引发，其中交易垃圾信息是重点。DAG型DLT因吞吐量与共识流程特性，比区块链更需控制网络垃圾信息，而现有共识框架中的PoW对垃圾信息威慑有限。

Method: 提出变量且基于行为的节点特定PoW模型，为所有利益相关者提供不受计算资源影响的平等机会；将节点竞争有限网络资源发布交易的过程建模为非合作博弈，并证明该博弈中纳什均衡的存在性。

Result: 该模型能够预防并惩罚任何试图发送垃圾信息或以超过规定数量交易主导网络的节点，并通过纳什均衡的存在性证明表明模型可强制节点遵循规定行为。

Conclusion: 所提变量与行为驱动的节点特定PoW机制可有效缓解DAG型DLT网络的去中心化拥塞，并在博弈论框架下保证节点的合规行为。

Abstract: We propose a variable and behavior-based node-specific proof-of-work (PoW) model for a directed acyclic graph (DAG)-based distributed ledger technology (DLT) network to mitigate decentralized network congestion control. Network congestion control for centralized communication systems is an established field of study, with detailed and continuous research being done on the subject. However, attention to congestion control in decentralized networks is relatively recent and underexplored, especially with DLT, such as blockchain and DAG-based networks. For the DLT networks, the network congestion is caused by factors such as transaction spamming, an increase in the user base, and the launch of new tokens. We focus on the congestion caused by the spamming of transactions within the blockchain and DAG-based DLT network. Based on the network throughput of transactions per second and consensus procedure, the DAG-based DLT needs to control network spamming more than the blockchain network. The PoW model within the DLT consensus framework is a limited deterrent against spamming. Our model provides equal opportunities for all stakeholders regardless of their computational resources. It prevents and penalizes any node that attempts to spam or dominate the network with more than the prescribed number of transactions. Since the system nodes compete to issue transactions with finite network resources, we display the system behavior through a non-cooperative game. Further, we show that our model enforces prescribed behavior amongst the nodes through the proof of the existence of Nash equilibrium in the game.

</details>


### [256] [Arbitrary Cipher Attacks Against Large Language Models Do Not Require Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09553)
*Thomas Rivasseau*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 前沿大语言模型无需微调，仅通过提示与上下文学习即可掌握加密通信（cipher）技能，并用其绕过对齐与有害性分类器，实现对Anthropic、Google、OpenAI等黑盒模型的越狱攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱研究多聚焦于检测与防御，且任意加密/隐蔽通信攻击此前仅在对商业模型的微调API上得到验证。作者希望探究：更新的前沿模型是否仍需微调才能获得加密通信能力，以及通过加密信道通信是否会导致对齐失效，从而揭示一种针对商业黑盒模型的新型攻击面。

Method: 作者不再依赖微调，而是通过提示（prompting）以及在必要时通过上下文学习（in-context learning），使目标模型自行习得加密方案；随后要求模型以该学习到的加密方案对有害请求进行编码与应答，从而形成加密信道。

Result: 实验表明，前沿模型无需微调即可获得基于密码的通信技能；当通信经由学习到的密码进行时，模型对齐被显著削弱甚至完全绕过。作者在Anthropic、Google和OpenAI开发的前沿模型上成功实施了越狱。由于有害内容被加密，攻击同时绕过了商业有害性分类器——加密后的内容呈现为无意义文本或乱码。

Conclusion: 该工作提出并验证了一种针对商业黑盒大语言模型的新型攻击向量：仅凭提示与上下文学习即可建立加密信道，并借其绕过对齐与内容审核。这凸显出以加密或隐蔽通信为载体的安全风险，需引起对齐与防御研究的重视。

Abstract: Large language model safety and security research is preoccupied with, among other things, detecting and preventing jailbreak attacks: alignment bypasses that allow an adversarial user to elicit unwanted or harmful outputs from models. Arbitrary cipher, or covert communication, attacks are one such type of jailbreak and have previously been demonstrated against the fine-tuning APIs of commercial models. In these attacks, target models are trained on a corpus of encrypted harmful questions and responses and subsequently respond to harmful requests through the learned encryption scheme. In this paper, we show that newer frontier models do not require fine-tuning to acquire cipher-based communication skills. Instead, they can learn these skills through prompting and, when necessary, through in-context learning. Furthermore, model alignment is significantly weakened or entirely bypassed when communication occurs through the learned cipher. To the best of our knowledge, this constitutes a novel attack vector against commercial black-box large language models. We demonstrate successful jailbreaks against frontier models developed by Anthropic, Google, and OpenAI. Our attack bypasses commercial harmfulness classifiers because harmful content is encrypted and therefore appears as nonsensical text or gibberish.

</details>


### [257] [When Ad Networks Misbehave: Understanding Risks of Semi-Drive-By Splash Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09574)
*Song Wu,Bo Wang,Yifan Zhang,Yinfeng Cao,Xueqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究移动开屏广告生态中一种此前较少被关注的广告网络层欺诈——"半被动式开屏广告"（semi-drive-by splash ads），并设计自动化蜜罐框架 AdHive 在真实设备条件下诱导并检测该行为，大规模测量证实其普遍存在，并与中国某大型广告主合作确认了实际欺诈及约400万元人民币的赔付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开屏广告的变现依赖难以端到端验证的交互信号，而误触与传感器驱动回调十分常见且易被误判为有效参与。既有研究多将移动广告欺诈视为发布者侧问题，或归因于内嵌广告库，但忽视了广告SDK自身掌控交互信号的解释、测量与上报方式，从而可将模糊的用户或设备信号重新解释为有效广告交互这一风险。

Method: 提出 AdHive——一个类似蜜罐的自动化分析框架，在真实设备条件下诱导规避式的开屏广告投放与落地页行为。AdHive 利用大语言模型生成的类人使用轨迹与传感器动态来复现人类活动，从而触发在传统分析环境中被隐藏的执行路径。随后在数千款流行 Android 应用上开展大规模测量，并与中国某大型广告主合作进行真实世界影响验证。

Result: 大规模测量表明，半被动式开屏广告普遍存在，且常由细微信号（如传感器微变）触发。在真实世界验证中，识别出多个从事此类欺诈的广告网络，并促成约400万元人民币（约60万美元）的强制赔付。

Conclusion: 广告SDK对交互信号解释权的掌控构成了广告网络层的新型欺诈风险，半被动式开屏广告将非广告交互转化为可计费参与事件，导致广告主被超额收费并损害用户信任。AdHive 框架能够有效在野外观测此类行为，为广告主维权与生态治理提供了可行手段。

Abstract: We investigate the mobile splash ads ecosystem, i.e., full-screen advertisements shown at app launch, where monetization relies on interaction signals that are difficult to verify end-to-end. This setting is especially sensitive because incidental touches and sensor-driven callbacks are common yet easy to misattribute as engagement. Prior work has largely framed mobile ad fraud as a publisher-side problem, while some studies attribute fraudulent operations to embedded ad libraries. Yet an important risk remains underexplored: ad SDKs control how interaction signals are interpreted, measured, and reported, creating an opportunity to reinterpret ambiguous user or device signals as valid advertising interactions. We uncover a previously less-known form of fraud at the ad-network layer in which splash ads are triggered not by intentional user actions but by incidental or indirect interactions, which we term semi-drive-by splash ads. By translating non-ad interactions into billable engagement events, ad networks can inflate performance metrics, overcharge advertisers, and erode user trust. To expose this behavior in the wild, we design AdHive, an automated honeypot-like analysis framework that induces evasive splash-ad delivery and landing behaviors under realistic device conditions. AdHive reproduces human-like activity through LLM-generated usage traces and sensor dynamics, enabling execution paths that remain hidden in conventional analysis environments.
  Our large-scale measurement across thousands of popular Android applications shows that semi-drive-by splash ads are widespread and are often triggered by subtle signals such as minor sensor variations. We further confirm real-world impact by working with one of China's largest advertisers, identifying multiple ad networks engaging in this fraud and leading to enforced repayments of about 4 million Yuan (approximately US$600,000).

</details>


### [258] [AutoTrans: AI-Assisted Automatic Translation of Security Assertions for RISC-V Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10057)
*Sharjeel Imtiaz,Uljana Reinsalu,Tara Ghasempouri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AutoTrans是一个自动化框架，通过正则表达式信号提取、固定推理参数的提示模板以及JasperGold形式化验证集成，实现RISC-V处理器间安全断言的可信自动翻译，实验达到78%自动TAR和100%最终TAR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RISC-V处理器间复用已验证的安全断言是硬件安全验证中最昂贵的瓶颈之一：人工翻译每条断言耗时数小时；直接使用LLM翻译虽快但不可靠，会产生信号幻觉（编造目标RTL中不存在的端口名），且输出在模型更新甚至同一模型版本内都可能变化。

Method: AutoTrans框架包含三个核心组件：(1)轻量级基于正则表达式的SystemVerilog信号提取器，用于识别信号以生成安全断言，防止信号幻觉；(2)提示模板与固定推理参数，保证每次运行时提示字节级一致，使流水线输出对模型更新具有鲁棒性，并能仅凭英文安全描述自动生成RISC-V处理器的安全断言，无需人工编写；(3)集成JasperGold FPV形式化验证流程，确保生成的断言真正验证处理器安全性而非静默进入结果集。基于Deepseek V4，将断言从NS31A RISC-V翻译至IBEX等目标。

Result: AutoTrans无需人工干预即可自动达到78%的自动翻译接受率（TAR），经人工精化后最终TAR达到100%。

Conclusion: AutoTrans通过信号提取、确定性提示组装和形式化验证三者结合，有效解决了跨RISC-V目标安全断言复用中的信号幻觉与输出不稳定问题，实现了高自动接受率且具备形式化正确性保证，为硬件安全验证的断言复用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reusing a set of verified security assertions across RISC-V processor targets remains one of the most expensive bottlenecks in hardware security verification. Manual translation takes hours per assertion. Raw LLM translation is fast but unreliable, introducing signal hallucination, where the model invents port names absent from the target RTL and produces outputs that may vary across model updates or even within the same model version. This paper presents AutoTrans, an automated framework that addresses the above shortcomings. First, a new lightweight Regular Expression-based System Verilog signal extractor is proposed to identify the signals for generating security assertions. This step is necessary to prevent signal hallucination. Second, a template is introduced to create prompt and pinned inference parameters that guarantee a byte-identical prompt assembly on every run, making the pipeline output resilient to model updates. Moreover, the introduced template for LLM prompting is capable of generating security assertions from English-only security descriptions of RISC-V processors, with no manual authoring. Third, a formal verification process (JasperGold FPV) is integrated, which guarantees that the generated security assertions verify the security of the RISC-V processor rather than silently entering the result set. The workflow is applied on Deepseek V4 to translate security assertions from one RISC-V to another (e.g., for IBEX from NS31A RISC-V). The experiment shows that AutoTrans achieves 78\% Auto Translation Acceptance Rate (TAR) automatically and without human intervention and 100\% Final TAR after refinement by humans.

</details>


### [259] [Scalable Composition of Byzantine Agreements under Reorder Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09623)
*Jing Chen,Jin Dong,Jichen Li,Xuanzhi Xia,Wentao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出结合“节点腐化”与“信道攻击”的敌手模型，并给出并行/并发组合下拜占庭协议的紧致安全阈值：不可能性为 $n \leq 3t$ 或 $n \leq 2c+2t+1$，可能性为 $n > \max\{3t, 2c+2t+1\}$，同时给出黑盒编译器与基于纠删码的高效编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭协议在多实例执行下的安全分析大多只考虑节点腐化，忽视了敌手操纵通信信道（如跨执行重排消息）带来的威胁；这类信道攻击可破坏协议的安全保证，因此需要建立融合两类攻击的敌手模型并重新刻画安全边界。

Method: 提出同时刻画节点腐化与信道攻击的敌手模型；在该模型下分析并行与并发组合，分别构造不可能性证明与可能性协议，使二者匹配为紧致界。可能性方面，先给出将任意单实例安全BA协议转化为并行/并发安全协议的通用黑盒编译器（无需额外安全假设），再设计基于纠删码的改进编译器以降低通信开销。

Result: 不可能性：即使在认证条件下，当 $n \leq 3t$ 或 $n \leq 2c+2t+1$ 时，BA协议在并行组合下也无法安全。可能性：当 $n > \max\{3t, 2c+2t+1\}$ 时，存在并行与并发组合下安全的非认证BA协议；改进编译器对长消息仅引入常数倍通信开销，保持与原协议相同的渐近通信复杂度。

Conclusion: 将信道攻击纳入敌手模型后，BA在组合场景下的安全阈值由节点腐化与信道腐化共同决定，且所给界是紧致的；所提黑盒与纠删码编译器可在不增加安全假设的前提下实现组合安全并有效控制通信开销。

Abstract: Byzantine agreement (BA) is a foundational building block in distributed systems, and the security analysis of BA protocols under multi-instance executions has attracted increasing attention. However, most existing adversary models focus solely on party corruption and neglect important threats posed by adversarial manipulations of communication channels in the network. Through channel attacks, messages can be reordered across multiple executions and lead to violations of the protocol's security guarantees,
  In this work, we present the first adversary model that combines party corruption and channel attacks. Based on this model, we establish new security thresholds for Byzantine agreement under parallel and concurrent compositions, supported by complementary impossibility and possibility results that match each other to form a tight bound. For the impossibility result, we show that even authenticated Byzantine agreement protocols cannot be secure under parallel composition when $n \leq 3t$ or $n \leq 2c + 2t + 1$, where $t$ and $c$ denote the number of corrupted parties and communication channels, respectively, and $n$ is the number of parties.
  For the possibility result, we prove the existence of secure protocols for unauthenticated Byzantine agreement under parallel and concurrent composition, when $n > \max\{3t, 2c+2t+1\}$. We first provide general black-box compilers that transform any single-instance secure BA protocol into one that is secure under parallel and concurrent executions without additional security assumptions. To optimize performance, we further design refined compilers using erasure-correcting codes. These refined versions significantly reduce communication overhead, particularly for long messages, where they achieve a constant multiplicative overhead compared with the original protocol, thus achieving the same asymptotic communication complexity.

</details>


### [260] [Session Attestation for Unmodified TLS Services in Confidential Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09668)
*Qi Gu,Sheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR:  SessionLatch：在无需改动应用、TLS实现或证书的前提下，通过操作系统层的"临时闩锁"与"标准TLS密钥确认"相结合，实现机密虚拟机网络连接的会话级远程证明，并在真实Hygon CSV上验证了其低延迟优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机（如TEE）可简化现有服务迁移，但对某网络连接进行远程证明（attestation）时，往往需要修改应用、TLS实现或证书，部署成本高、兼容性差。论文旨在解决"在不改变现有三个组件（应用/TLS/证书）前提下，如何对TEE网络连接做会话证明"的问题。

Method: 核心洞见：对服务器本地生成的临时公钥（ephemeral public key）进行可信观测，结合标准TLS密钥确认，可在不访问TLS密钥的前提下建立TEE端点保证。为此将证明集成下移到操作系统层：设置一个临时"闩锁"（latch）暂存客户端加密记录，使证据交换与应用TLS握手重叠；验证通过后闩锁自动移除。连接保留企业服务认证与原生TLS数据路径，不额外加密载荷。双向证明复用同一构造，在两端重叠生成证据。实现Linux与Windows集成，并基于国产Hygon CSV做真实评估。

Result: 在Linux/Windows交叉实验中，SessionLatch相比TNG将短上传平均延迟降低63.1%/23.0%。

Conclusion: 会话级证明能够在不将永久代理引入数据路径的情况下，增强现有机密服务的安全性，兼顾兼容性与性能。

Abstract: Confidential virtual machines simplify the migration of existing services into trusted execution environments, yet attesting their network connections often requires changing applications, TLS implementations, or certificates. We present SessionLatch, which provides session attestation while preserving all three. The key insight is that a trusted observation of the server's locally generated ephemeral public key, combined with standard TLS key confirmation, establishes the TEE endpoint guarantee without accessing TLS secrets. This moves attestation integration to the operating system: a temporary latch holds client encrypted records while evidence exchange overlaps the application TLS handshake, then removes itself after verification. The resulting connection retains enterprise service authentication and the native TLS data path, with no additional payload encryption. Mutual attestation uses the same construction and overlaps evidence generation at both endpoints. We implement Linux andWindowsintegrationandevaluaterealHygonCSVattestation. SessionLatch reduces short-upload mean latency by 63.1%/23.0% relative to TNG in interleaved Linux/Windows experiments. These results show that session attestation can strengthen existing confidential services without making a permanent proxy part of their data path.

</details>


### [261] [PrivAudit: A Dual-Lens Auditing Framework for Website Privacy Practices under the CCPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09697)
*Mohamed Moustafa Dawoud,Riya Aggarwal,Likith Rahul Krishnamurthy,Ram Sundara Raman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivAudit自动化审计框架，采用"双镜头"方法，结合基于大语言模型的隐私政策分析与浏览器端Cookie写入测量，对998个网站进行审计，发现CCPA与更强的隐私披露相关，但Cookie追踪仍普遍存在且对隐私信号响应有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCPA实施五年后，网站隐私实践如何大规模响应监管仍是难题。现有工作与监管努力集中于人工和个案执法，缺乏可扩展的方法来系统审计CCPA的两个关键用户可见方面：隐私披露与前端用户追踪行为。

Method: 提出PrivAudit自动化审计框架，采用双镜头方法：(1)通过基于CCPA条款、由大语言模型驱动的隐私政策分析来捕捉隐私披露；(2)通过在不同隐私配置下自动浏览器测量Cookie写入来捕捉用户可观测的数据收集行为。

Result: 对998个网站应用PrivAudit，得到两项主要发现：受CCPA约束的政策更可能披露退出机制、数据共享实践和用户权利；但基于Cookie的追踪依然普遍，受约束与不受约束网站共设置6,392个定向Cookie，其中49%为第三方写入。此外，Cookie对隐私信号和同意选择的响应仅为有限至中等，即使网站在披露中声称会遵守这些选择。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了将政策分析与行为证据相结合的多层次、可扩展审计方法的必要性。PrivAudit可通过生成可供进一步人工审查的可操作信号与模式，支持大规模审计工作流。作者开源了PrivAudit，并正与监管机构合作以支持实际审计实践。

Abstract: Five years after the enforcement of the California Consumer Privacy Act (CCPA), understanding how website privacy practices evolve at scale in response to regulation remains a key challenge for both researchers and regulators. Prior work and regulatory efforts have focused on manual and case-specific enforcement, but there remain no scalable approaches to systematically audit two key user-facing facets of websites that are crucial signals for the CCPA: privacy disclosures and front-end user tracking behavior.
  In this paper, we present PrivAudit, an automated auditing framework that adopts a dual-lens approach to capture: (1) privacy disclosures through large language model-based analysis of privacy policies grounded in CCPA provisions, and (2) user-observable data collection behavior through automated browser measurements of cookie writes under diverse privacy configurations. We apply PrivAudit to 998 websites and report two broad findings. The law is associated with stronger privacy disclosures: CCPA-subject policies are more likely to disclose opt-out mechanisms, data-sharing practices, and user rights. On the other hand, cookie-based tracking remains pervasive, with both CCPA-subject and not-subject websites setting a total of 6,392 targeting cookies, 49% of which are third-party writes. Moreover, cookies show limited-to-moderate responsiveness to privacy signals and consent choices, even when websites claim to honor them in their disclosures.
  Our results highlight the need for multi-layered and scalable auditing approaches that combine policy analysis with behavioral evidence. PrivAudit can support these auditing workflows at scale by generating actionable signals and patterns for further manual review. We open-source PrivAudit and are engaging with regulators to support auditing in practice.

</details>


### [262] [How Fragile Is Safety Alignment at Frontier Scale? A Single-Direction Attack on a 320B MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09793)
*Yi Shi,Tanyu Chen,Kai Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 方向性消融在320B参数的MoE模型（GLM-5.3-Flash）上依然有效，但其效果高度依赖注意力、稠密与路由专家写入器的联合干预，传统按模块名定位的做法会静默失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方向性消融是开放权重对齐模型的经典白盒攻击，仅需数百条对比提示即可移除拒绝行为，但此前仅在大约70B以下的稠密模型上得到验证。本文研究该方法能否迁移到残差流不再为单一张量、且权重以量化形式发布的前沿MoE模型。

Method: 将方向性消融应用于GLM-5.3-Flash（320B参数、288个路由专家、四宽超连接残差、块级FP8），分别与联合地编辑注意力、稠密层与路由专家的写入器；并对比随机正交方向消融、以及基于暴力、性内容与仇恨三类子空间在1至12秩下的残余效果。

Result: 单独编辑注意力、稠密与路由专家写入器分别移除0.039、0.016、0.148的拒绝行为，三者联合编辑移除0.776，74%的效果仅存在于联合干预下；传统按模块名匹配的部分仅贡献0.066。消融随机正交方向不改变拒绝行为，而类别集中残留在各秩下均保留可测量的拒绝。方法在七个有害基准上实现41-89个百分点的拒绝率下降，未检测到能力变化。

Conclusion: 方向性消融能够跨越MoE架构与量化权重，但效应分布在多个写入器之间，单靠模块名匹配的常规配方在MoE上会静默失败；同时存在难以消除的类别集中残留，界定了该方法的适用边界。

Abstract: Directional ablation removes an aligned language model's ability to refuse by projecting a single "refusal direction" out of the weights that write the residual stream. It needs no gradient-based training and no optimization, only a few hundred contrastive prompts, which makes it the canonical white-box attack on open-weight alignment. However, it has been established only on dense models up to roughly 70B parameters. We study whether it survives the shift to frontier mixture-of-experts (MoE) models whose residual streams are no longer a single tensor and whose weights ship quantized. We apply it to GLM-5.3-Flash (320B parameters, 288 routed experts, a four-wide hyper-connection residual, block-FP8). The attack survives the architecture, but what it reaches is no longer where a reader of the original recipe would look for it. Editing the attention, dense and routed-expert writers on their own removes 0.039, 0.016 and 0.148 of refusal respectively; editing all three together removes 0.776. As a result, 74% of the effect exists only under the joint intervention. The part the conventional recipe reaches by module-name matching accounts for 0.066 of that 0.776, which is why it fails silently on an MoE. The effect does not follow from removing just any direction: ablating a random direction orthogonal to it leaves refusal unchanged. A category-concentrated residue survives every edit we tried: subspaces fitted on violence, sexual content and hate leave measurable refusal at every rank from 1 to 12. We report the method, the 41-89 percentage-point reductions it achieves across seven harmful benchmarks with no detected change in capability, and the boundary where it stops.

</details>


### [263] [CS-Guard: Benchmarking LLM Guardrails for Code Generation Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09798)
*Jinyang Li,Mingyu Guo,Hung X. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CS-Guard，首个系统评估代码生成安全防护栏（guardrails）的基准，覆盖文本到代码与代码到代码两类任务，并揭示现有防护栏在恶意代码生成请求面前防护效果普遍不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已被用于生成恶意软件，但针对代码生成安全的防护栏有效性尚不明确，缺乏系统性评估手段。

Method: 构建 CS-Guard 基准：1）文本到代码任务包含 1000 条高质量恶意代码生成提示、7 种越狱攻击，以及一种新型虚构场景攻击（FSA，将恶意意图嵌入合法的虚构软件开发场景）；2）代码到代码任务包含 331 条代码提示，覆盖代码填充、代码补全与代码翻译。在 7 个 LLM 上对 9 种防护栏进行实证评估，并采用模块化三层防护栏分类体系，支持开发者注册防护栏进行评估。

Result: 当前防护栏对恶意代码生成请求防护效果差：文本到代码任务中，越狱后多个防护栏平均攻击成功率（ASR）约 50%；代码到代码任务中，基础 LLM 上平均 ASR 接近 100%，且在许多防护栏下仍居高不下（14.4% 至接近 100%）；FSA 在多种防护栏下 ASR 接近 100%。

Conclusion: 现有防护栏难以有效阻止恶意代码生成，对真实软件开发构成重大可靠性隐患；CS-Guard 基准与数据已公开，以推动社区进一步评估与研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been ex- ploited to generate malware, but the effective- ness of guardrails for code generation secu- rity remains unclear. We introduce CS-Guard, the first benchmark to systematically evalu- ate guardrails for code generation security. It covers 1) text-to-code generation with 1000 high-quality malware-generation prompts, 7 jailbreak attacks, and a novel fictional scenario attack (FSA) that embeds malicious intent in a legitimate fictional software-development sce- nario; and 2) code-to-code generation with 331 code prompts spanning code infilling, code completion, and code translation. We empiri- cally evaluate 9 guardrails across seven LLMs. We find that current guardrails perform poorly against malicious code-generation re- quests: for text-to-code, the average attack success rate (ASR) after jailbreaks reaches about 50% for many guardrails; for code-to- code, average ASR approaches 100% on base LLMs and remains high across many guardrails (14.4% to nearly 100%). Our FSA also achieves ASR close to 100% across many guardrails, raising major reliability concerns for real-world software development. To sup- port future research, CS-Guard uses a modular three-layer guardrail taxonomy that lets devel- opers register guardrails for evaluation. We release the benchmark and data to enable fur- ther community evaluation.

</details>


### [264] [Quantifying IIoT Sensor Node Criticality by Fusing its Data Criticality and Security Vulnerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09807)
*Sachin K. Sen,Gour C. Karmakar,Shaoning Pang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出融合数据关键性与网络安全脆弱性的IIoT传感器节点关键性评估框架，基于D-S证据理论实现融合，并以红酒生产数据集验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIoT传感器节点易受网络威胁，危及可靠性与完整性。已有研究或仅关注网络安全漏洞与基于攻击的关键节点排序，或仅依据传感器数据对产品质量的影响评估节点关键性，尚缺乏同时整合数据关键性与网络安全脆弱性的综合方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖框架，利用Dempster-Shafer（D-S）证据理论融合数据关键性与网络安全脆弱性两个维度，对IIoT传感器节点进行关键性评估；采用红酒生产数据集进行验证，并分别使用CVSS 4.0与CVSS 3.1计算脆弱性得分进行比较。

Result: 在红酒生产数据集上验证了方法的有效性，实现了基于双重因素的传感器节点排序；基于CVSS 4.0计算的脆弱性得分所得关键性排序与基于CVSS 3.1的结果存在显著差异，表明增强的漏洞评估方法对排序具有明显影响。

Conclusion: 该框架虽初步应用于葡萄酒制造，但经少量修改即可推广至更广泛的工业场景，为保障IIoT赋能的生产系统提供了一种稳健方法。

Abstract: The integration of the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) into manufacturing has transformed industrial operations by optimising production management and ensuring product quality through smart industrial sensors that regulate processes based on real-time data. However, these sensor nodes are highly vulnerable to cyber threats, posing significant security risks that compromise their reliability and integrity. While existing research explores cybersecurity vulnerabilities and cyberattack-based methods for ranking critical nodes, some studies assess node criticality based on the impact of sensor data on product quality. However, a comprehensive approach that integrates both data criticality and cybersecurity vulnerability remains unexplored. To bridge this gap, this study introduces a novel framework that evaluates IIoT sensor node criticality by leveraging Dempster--Shafer (D-S) theory to fuse data criticality and cybersecurity vulnerabilities. The proposed method is validated using a dataset from red wine production, demonstrating its effectiveness in ranking sensor nodes based on both factors. The results show that criticality rankings based on security vulnerability scores computed using CVSS version 4.0 differ significantly from those obtained with CVSS version 3.1, highlighting the influence of enhanced vulnerability assessment methodologies. While initially applied to wine manufacturing, this framework is adaptable to broader industrial applications with minimal modifications, offering a robust approach to securing IIoT-enabled production systems.

</details>


### [265] [Lightweight Zero Trust via Automotive SDN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09817)
*Friedrich Wiemer,Florian Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文分析车载区域网络中 MACsec/MKA 与 CORECONF/YANG 管理能否在无需新增 ZTA 专用基础设施的前提下实现 NIST SP 800-207 零信任架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车载网络（Ethernet、MACsec、TSN）在出厂配置后将网络视为可信，缺乏标准化的运行时访问撤销、密钥轮换与失陷 ECU 隔离机制；而现有汽车 ZTA 方案需额外部署专用基础设施，与 SDV 已必需的 SDN 管理面重复，导致 ZTA 难以落地。

Method: 分两步：第一步分析 Open Alliance TC17 v1.0 中采用预共享 CAK 的 MACsec/MKA 已满足哪些 NIST SP 800-207 零信任原则；第二步引入 Open Alliance TC19 提出的 CORECONF/YANG 管理，将 SDN 控制器与代理一一映射到 NIST 的 PE、PA 和 PEP，并用两种基于 YANG 的机制（网络访问控制流和密钥管理方案）进行实例化。

Result: 在未增加任何 ZTA 专用基础设施的情况下，完整覆盖七项零信任原则中的五项，部分覆盖另外两项。

Conclusion: 车载网络可复用已有 MACsec/MKA 与 CORECONF/YANG SDN 管理面实现零信任架构，避免了基础设施重复部署，为汽车 ZTA 落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Zonal in-vehicle networks ship Ethernet, MACsec, and TSN, but treat the network itself as trusted: once configured at the factory, there is no standardized runtime way to easily revoke access, rotate keys, or contain a compromised ECU. Zero Trust Architecture targets exactly that gap, yet existing automotive ZTA proposals bolt on dedicated infrastructure that duplicates the SDN management plane already required to enable SDVs. Thus, ZTA is not yet adopted in the automotive domain, and the question remains: can we do better? We answer this in two steps.
  Step 1 analyses what Open Alliance TC17~v1.0 MACsec/MKA with pre-shared CAKs already provides in terms of NIST SP~800-207 ZTA tenets. Step 2 adds CORECONF/YANG management as proposed in Open Alliance TC19, maps the SDN Controller and Agents one-to-one onto NIST's PE, PA, and PEP. We then instantiate this with two YANG-based mechanisms: a network-access-control flow and a key-management scheme.
  The result fully covers five and two partially of the seven tenets with no ZTA-specific infrastructure added.

</details>


### [266] [Subgroup Membership Inference Audits of Differentially Private Synthetic Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09848)
*Yidan Sun,Viktor Schlegel,Srinivasan Nandakumar,Siew Kei Lam,Anil Anthony Bharath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出子群体定向成员推理博弈，将目标池作为显式参数，并通过对4个数据集、3种生成器、5种隐私预算下32种代理的审计，揭示了合成数据发布中DP保护的不均衡性：残余泄漏集中于少数高风险记录，且泄漏与否取决于发布机制而非记录本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据发布被广泛提议作为敏感私有数据集的替代共享方式，即使通过差分隐私（DP）限制了最坏情况下的隐私泄漏，实践中仍存在残余风险，需通过成员推理攻击（MIA）审计来经验性量化。然而现有方法仅测量随机抽取记录的平均风险，可能掩盖对脆弱子群体的风险，因此需要定义能够显式刻画子群体风险的审计框架。

Method: 定义了子群体定向成员推理博弈，将目标池作为显式参数；在三种不同攻击者知识水平情景下，对32种代理进行审计，覆盖4个数据集、3种生成器（DP-SGD微调、基于API的提示、激活引导）和5种隐私预算，并采用合并池审计在记录层面评分。

Result: 审计表明合成发布确实泄漏子群体成员身份，且既有攻击系统性地低估了该泄漏。DP在总体层面有效，在每个测试预算下均显著降低平均泄漏。但有三点发现削弱这一乐观图景：①残余泄漏是集中的而非分散的，DP下十分之一的记录承载约40%的泄漏；②DP的实际保护不均衡，其噪声对随机记录去除的泄漏多于高风险记录，合并池审计在记录层面予以证实；③哪些记录泄漏是发布机制的属性而非记录自身的属性，因此记录级风险不能独立于发布机制来评估。

Conclusion: 子群体层面的MIA审计揭示了DP合成数据发布中残余风险的集中性与不均衡性，且记录级泄漏风险依赖发布机制，提示仅依赖平均风险指标会低估脆弱子群体的隐私威胁，需在评估中引入子群体定向与机制相关的审计视角。

Abstract: Synthetic data releases are increasingly proposed in the literature as a means of sharing realistic data replicas in lieu of sensitive private datasets. Even when the worst-case privacy leakage of such releases is bounded by means of differential privacy (DP), in practice a residual risk remains. Membership inference attack (MIA) audits are conducted to empirically quantify this risk. However, existing methods only measure average-case risk for randomly drawn records, which might conceal the risk to vulnerable subgroups. To highlight this issue, we define a subgroup-targeted membership inference game in which the target pool is an explicit parameter, and instantiate it with an audit of 32 proxies under three scenarios with different levels of attacker knowledge, across four datasets, three generators (DP-SGD fine-tuning, API-based prompting, and activation steering), and five privacy budgets. The audit shows that synthetic releases leak subgroup membership and that prior attacks systematically underestimate this leakage. DP is effective at the aggregate level: it substantially reduces average leakage at every budget we test. Three observations temper this picture. First, the remaining leakage is concentrated rather than spread out: under DP, a tenth of the records carries roughly 40% of it. Second, the protection DP delivers in practice is uneven: within its worst-case guarantee, the noise removes more of the measured leakage from random records than from high-risk ones---and a merged-pool audit that scores both record types against shared negatives confirms this at the record level. Third, \emph{which} records leak proves to be a property of the release mechanism rather than of the record alone, so record-level risk cannot be assessed independently of the release.

</details>


### [267] [CrossLink: Breaking Location Privacy by Linking Device Identifiers Across Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09963)
*Aneet Kumar Dutta,Mihirraj Dixit,Kevin Gni,Wouter Lueks,Mridula Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 现有隐私防御假设各协议标识符随机化可叠加，但本文证明其不能叠加：即使各协议仅泄露临时标识符且攻击者完全被动，不同步的标识符轮换仍可导致跨协议设备轨迹拼接。作者提出不确定性感知的 CrossLink 追踪算法，在大规模移动仿真中对 83% 用户重建完整轨迹，而最佳单协议基线仅 22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机同时通过 LTE、WiFi 和 BLE 发送临时标识符。现有隐私防御按协议独立分析标识符随机化，隐含假设各协议保护可跨协议叠加。本文旨在检验并反驳该假设，揭示即使在各协议仅泄露临时标识符且攻击者完全被动的条件下，隐私仍会被破坏。

Method: 提出 CrossLink，一种不确定性感知的追踪算法，在噪声定位与移动性条件下跨时间、空间和协议关联标识符。评估方式包括：使用商用设备进行受控实验室实验，以及大规模移动性仿真。此外分析了部分覆盖场景，包括部署在 LTE 切换区域附近的战略嗅探器、移动嗅探器和有限高覆盖子区域。

Result: 在大规模移动性仿真中，CrossLink 为 83% 的用户重建完整轨迹，而最佳单协议基线仅为 22%。在部分覆盖下 CrossLink 仍有效：LTE 切换区附近的战略嗅探器、移动嗅探器和有限高覆盖子区域保留了足够的跨协议证据以弥合观测缺口，其可关联性显著高于随机部署。

Conclusion: 位置隐私必须跨协议联合分析，而非按协议独立处理。跨协议标识符随机化保护并不自然叠加，不同步的标识符轮换会造成严重的跨协议轨迹拼接风险。

Abstract: Smartphones simultaneously transmit temporary identifiers over LTE, WiFi, and BLE. Existing privacy defenses analyze identifier randomization per protocol, implicitly assuming that these protections compose across protocols. We show that they do not: Even when each protocol leaks only temporary identifiers and the adversary is fully passive, unsynchronized identifier rotations allow cross-protocol stitching of device traces. We present CrossLink, an uncertainty-aware tracing algorithm that links identifiers across time, space, and protocols under noisy localization and mobility. We evaluate CrossLink using controlled lab experiments with commodity devices and large-scale mobility simulation. Under large-scale mobility simulation, CrossLink reconstructs full traces for 83% of users, versus 22% for the best single-protocol baseline, showing that location privacy must be analyzed jointly across protocols. We further show that CrossLink remains effective under partial coverage: strategically placed sniffers near LTE handover regions, mobile sniffers, and limited high-coverage subregions retain sufficient cross-protocol evidence to bridge observation gaps, achieving substantially higher linkability than random deployments.

</details>


### [268] [Dependency-Aware ROM/CBD Correctness Bounds for ML-KEM-768 at the Heuristic Failure Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09983)
*Aurélie Duriez,Christophe Tommasini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文在显式的随机函数/中心二项分布（ROM/CBD）抽象下，为 ML-KEM-768 给出了诚实解封装失败率的上界认证：Pr[K'≠K] ≤ 2^-164.81（-log2(P_*)=164.810716201343121…）。该阈值精确但数值上紧，认证指数仅超出约 0.0007162 bit，且 164.82 未被认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期形式化评估将 ML-KEM 启发式解封装失败尺度的严格论证列为开放问题。本文旨在为 ML-KEM-768 在这一启发式尺度上给出保依赖性的认证上界，以填补该严格性缺口。

Method: 在显式 ROM/CBD 抽象中，将域分离的公共矩阵流建模为独立均匀环元素，秘密/噪声多项式建模为独立 CBD2 原语。分析保留由公共矩阵及两个密文压缩项引入的依赖关系。其终端链包含三部分：(1) 针对联合 c_u/c_v 残差的精确图耦合全理想参照；(2) 真理想二元傅里叶传输，其罕见的 |T|≥3 分支通过穷举三因子反集中重放封闭；(3) 精确的比特级 FIPS 解码事件，随后仅施加 256 坐标联合界。另有一个形式化部分傅里叶引理使谱到全变差步骤显式化。

Result: 约简后的有理证书满足 Pr[K'≠K] ≤ P_* ≤ 2^-164.81，其中 -log2(P_*)=164.810716201343121…。164.81 阈值精确但数值紧：认证指数仅超出该阈值约 0.0007162 bit，164.82 未被认证。

Conclusion: 该结果是针对任意固定消息（独立于公共与秘密随机性）、在诚实加密与解封装条件下的上界。它不是精确 DFR，不是固定 SHAKE 等价定理，不是新的 IND-CCA 归约，也不是自适应 delta-正确性结果。该认证仅适用于所研究的显式 ROM/CBD 抽象，而非 FIPS 203 中固定 SHAKE 实例化的信息论陈述。

Abstract: We certify an honest-decapsulation failure upper bound for ML-KEM-768 in an explicit random-function/centered-binomial (ROM/CBD) abstraction. Domain-separated public-matrix streams are modeled as independent uniform ring elements and secret/noise polynomials as independent CBD2 primitives; this is not an information-theoretic statement about the fixed SHAKE instantiation of FIPS 203. Recent formal assessments identify rigorous justification of ML-KEM's heuristic decapsulation-failure scale as an open problem; within the explicit ROM/CBD abstraction studied here, we obtain a dependency-preserving certified upper bound at that scale. The analysis preserves dependencies induced by the public matrix and by both ciphertext-compression terms. Its terminal chain has three components: an exact graph-coupled full-ideal reference for the joint c_u/c_v residual; a proper-ideal bivariate Fourier transport whose rare |T|>=3 branch is closed by an exhaustive three-factor anti-concentration replay; and exact bit-specific FIPS decoding events followed only by a 256-coordinate union bound. A formal partial-Fourier lemma makes the spectral-to-total-variation step explicit. The reduced rational certificate satisfies Pr[K' != K] <= P_* <= 2^-164.81, with -log2(P_*) = 164.810716201343121.... The 164.81 threshold is exact but numerically tight: the certified exponent exceeds it by only about 0.0007162 bit, and 164.82 is not certified. The result is an upper bound for an arbitrary message fixed independently of the public and secret randomness, under honest encryption and decapsulation. It is not an exact DFR, not a fixed-SHAKE equivalence theorem, not a new IND-CCA reduction, and not an adaptive delta-correctness result.

</details>


### [269] [Distributed and Private Textual Data Synthesis from Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10104)
*Ergute Bao,Hongyan Chang,Ali Shahin Shamsabadi,Ting Yu,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种DP-密码学协同设计的文本合成方法，支持无可信 curator 的分布式场景，用户仅需轻量参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP文本合成流程依赖可信的集中式curator，假设不现实；直接迁移到分布式环境会要求用户反复、紧同步参与，开销大。

Method: 1) 分布式友好的DP合成算法：在嵌入空间释放一次性DP摘要，识别频繁语义区域并发布其DP质心，实现免训练、非迭代的离线文本合成；引入语义支持保护，使摘要避开低频文本的语义邻域。2) 定制安全协议：在分布式用户数据上实现该算法，提供端到端DP保证，无需可信curator。

Result: 在四个基准上，效用与最先进的集中式DP合成方法相当。

Conclusion: 该DP-密码学协同设计在无可信curator的分布式设定下实现了高效、轻量的DP文本合成，并保持与集中式SOTA相当的效用。

Abstract: We revisit differentially private (DP) text synthesis in the realistic setting of distributed users, where privacy concerns preclude a trusted curator with access to raw user texts. Existing DP text synthesis pipelines are designed for a trusted, centralized curator and often cannot be deployed in distributed settings due to unrealistic trust and access assumptions; when adapted naively, they require repeated, tightly synchronized user participation and incur significant overhead. To address this gap, we propose a DP--cryptography co-design for textual data synthesis that requires no trusted curator and requires only lightweight user participation. Our approach has two optimized components. First, we design a distributed-friendly DP synthesis algorithm that releases a one-time DP summary in an embedding space: it identifies frequent semantic regions and releases their DP centroids, enabling training-free, non-iterative offline text synthesis. We further introduce semantic support protection, which ensures the released summary avoids semantic neighborhoods of infrequent texts, reducing the risk of exposing rare user data. Second, we develop a custom secure protocol that implements this algorithm over distributed user data, enforcing end-to-end DP guarantees without requiring a trusted curator. On four benchmarks, we achieve utility comparable to the state-of-the-art centralized DP synthesis method.

</details>


### [270] [Understanding the Security Boundary of Obfuscation-based On-Device LLM Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10117)
*Hanyi Zhou,Chenyang Li,Yuanzhe Pang,Ke Xu,Mingwei Xu,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化了一组混淆原语（满足特定代数性质、由线性计算构成的对偶二元组），证明代表性TSLP框架的矩阵级权重变换可表示为这些原语的复合，其规范形式O_prior刻画了该类方案的结构性边界；进而提出Collapse攻击揭示O_prior的共享漏洞，并引入两个新原语构造O_ext以扩展安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE-Shielded LLM Partition（TSLP）方法依赖高效混淆将计算密集层卸载至外部GPU，但这类防御大多是启发式的，导致部分方法被针对其特定架构实现的专用对抗攻击攻破。作者希望回答一个根本性问题：能否建立统一的公共原语来统一既有代表性方法、刻画其组合的安全边界并系统化地加以扩展。

Method: 1) 形式化一组混淆原语，定义为一对线性计算构成的对偶二元组，满足特定代数性质；2) 将所研究代表性高效TSLP框架的矩阵级权重变换表达为这些原语的复合，得到原语复合的规范形式O_prior，用以刻画该原语族的结构性边界；3) 提出原语引导的新型攻击方法Collapse，暴露O_prior的漏洞；4) 引入两个新混淆原语，与既有构件组合为O_ext。

Result: 证明多个顶级会议发表的代表性TSLP方法存在共享漏洞，包括ArrowCloak（Security'25）、TSQP（S&P'25）和LoRO（NeurIPS'25）；O_prior刻画了先验原语族的结构性边界，Collapse能够揭示其脆弱性；O_ext在既有构造基础上扩展了安全边界。

Conclusion: TSLP防御的启发式设计可被统一的原语框架所刻画与统一；通过O_prior得到其安全边界，并借助Collapse暴露共享漏洞，说明先验设计的结构性局限；通过引入两个新原语并将它们与现有构件组合为O_ext，可系统性地突破原有边界，为TEE场景下LLM知识产权保护提供更具原则性的设计路径。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) offer a promising mechanism for safeguarding the intellectual property of on-device Large Language Models (LLMs). To overcome the inherent computational bottlenecks of TEEs, existing TEE-Shielded LLM Partition (TSLP) methods apply efficient obfuscation schemes to computationally intensive layers, offloading them to external GPUs while retaining only lightweight operations within the TEE. Although a growing body of TSLP-based approaches has emerged, these defense mechanisms remain largely heuristic. Consequently, some methods are proven vulnerable to certain specialized adversarial attacks designed to exploit their specific architectural implementations. To overcome the limitations of these heuristic designs, this paper addresses a fundamental research question: can we establish common primitives to unify representative prior methodologies, characterize the security boundary of their compositions, and systematically extend them? To this end, we formalize a set of obfuscation primitives, defined as dual-tuples of linear computations satisfying specific algebraic properties. We demonstrate that the matrix-level weight transformations of the representative efficient TSLP frameworks studied in this paper can be expressed as compositions of these primitives; consequently, the canonical form of these primitive compositions, denoted as O_prior, characterizes the structural boundary of this primitive family. We then expose the vulnerabilities of O_prior through a novel primitive-guided attack methodology, Collapse, demonstrating a shared vulnerability in several prominent TSLP methods published in top-tier venues, such as ArrowCloak (Security'25), TSQP (S&P'25), and LoRO (NeurIPS'25). Finally, we introduce two novel obfuscation primitives and integrate them with existing constructs to formulate O_ext, extending this security boundary.

</details>


### [271] [Sound Debloating of Redundant Checks in Zero-Knowledge Machine-Learning Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10149)
*Zhantong Xue,Pingchuan Ma,Zhaoyu Wang,Yuguang Zhou,Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动化框架，通过全电路抽象解释与溯源图分析，证明性地移除神经网络推理ZK证明电路中的冗余约束（范围证明、符号查找、位分解等），在不削弱可靠性的前提下最多移除48.7%约束并降低证明时间72.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络推理的零知识证明系统会将模型编译为算术约束系统，其中许多约束（范围证明、符号查找、位分解）是冗余检查，可由电路中相距较远的组件通过推理链全局蕴含。移除它们可缩小电路并加速证明，但移除必须被严格论证：不健全的去冗余会使电路可被伪造，接受原电路会拒绝的见证，例如让证明者声称神经网络产生了实际未计算的输出。此类可靠性漏洞并非假设——已部署ZK系统中的约束不足问题曾使攻击者伪造交易并完全绕过验证。

Method: 提出自动化框架，在证明性保持可靠性的前提下移除冗余检查。对每个候选移除，工具首先检查电路其余部分本身是否仍能排除被移除检查所排除的每一个取值。利用全电路抽象解释，分析搜索此类替代论证并记录于溯源图（provenance graph）；仅当图中存在替代路径仍能推导出该检查所验证的事实时才移除该检查，从而保证去冗余后的电路不为对手开辟新的伪造策略。

Result: 在由两个生产框架（ezkl与zkml）生成、涵盖MLP、CNN、RNN与Transformer架构、约束数高达2530万的电路上进行评估。工具最多移除48.7%的约束，并将证明者时间最多降低72.8%，且未削弱安全性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，借助全电路抽象解释与溯源图，可在形式化保持可靠性的条件下自动移除ZK神经网络推理电路中的全局冗余约束，在多种主流架构与生产框架上实现显著的约束缩减与证明加速，同时不引入新的伪造策略。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge (ZK) proof systems for neural-network inference compile the model into a system of arithmetic constraints. Many of these constraints are redundant checks: range proofs, sign lookups, and bit decompositions who are globally entailed by the rest of the circuit through chains of reasoning that span distant gadgets. Removing them shrinks the circuit and accelerates proving, but the removal must be carefully justified: an unsoundly debloated circuit becomes forgeable, accepting witnesses the original would have rejected and so allowing a prover to claim, for example, that a neural network produced an output it never actually computed. Such soundness vulnerabilities are not hypothetical: under-constrained circuits in deployed ZK systems have enabled attackers to forge transactions and bypass verification entirely.
  We present an automated framework that removes redundant checks while provably preserving soundness. For each candidate removal, our tool first checks whether the rest of the circuit, on its own, can still rule out every value the removed check was excluding. Using whole-circuit abstract interpretation, the analysis searches for such alternative justifications and records them in a provenance graph; a check is then removed only when an alternative path through the graph still derives the facts that it is checking. This ensures that the debloated circuit opens no new forging strategy to an adversary. We evaluate circuits spanning MLP, CNN, RNN, and transformer architectures generated by two production frameworks (ezkl and zkml), with up to 25.3 million constraints. Our tool removes up to 48.7\% of constraints and reduces prover time by up to 72.8\%, without weakening security.

</details>


### [272] [Meme Coin Factories: Uncovering Large-Scale Manipulations on pump.fun](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10246)
*Nicolas Szwajcok,Taro Tsuchiya,Enze Liu,Kyle Soska,Mathias Payer,Nicolas Christin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对pump.fun平台近两年发行的1500万枚代币进行大规模研究，识别出五类市场操纵策略，并揭露了"市场操纵即服务"（MMaaS）工具生态，最后提出多方缓解建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密代币发行门槛极低，coin launchpad使任何人可低成本快速发币，meme coin盛行推动此类平台大规模普及。然而这也让策略性行为者能轻易操纵交易信号，在无经验交易者不知情的情况下制造不可持续的暴涨（pumps）并从中获利。目前缺乏对这类操纵行为的系统性、大规模实证研究。

Method: 对pump.fun开展大规模实证研究：收集过去两年发行的全部1500万枚代币信息，并对大规模随机交易样本进行分析，从而在规模化层面识别操纵行为及其模式。

Result: 识别出五类操纵策略：1）洗售交易（wash trading）；2）创作者地址混淆（creator address obfuscation）；3）协同抛售（coordinated sell）；4）仿冒币（copycat coins）；5）社交媒体操纵。发现策略性行为者常绕过平台界面，直接与区块链交互，以高度自动化和低延迟方式实施操纵。此外还发现"市场操纵即服务"（MMaaS）第三方工具的存在，使用户无需任何技术专长即可实施操纵。

Conclusion: 提出针对性缓解措施，并为交易者、pump.fun平台、钱包或链上扫描器、软件开发平台及监管机构提供建议。
