Abstract: Once complex, creating and deploying a new cryptocurrency has become trivial. Coin launchpads now allow users to generate a new coin with merely a few clicks, at a minimal cost. Launchpad popularity has grown in tandem with the rise of "meme coins," which usually do not offer any novel technological properties and are purely created for fun. The most prominent coin launchpad, pump.fun, has gained significant traction, grossing over 100 million USD in daily trading volume. The mass adoption of coin launchpads, however, also enables strategic actors to easily manipulate trading signals, unbeknownst to inexperienced traders who then buy certain coins, and enable these strategic actors to profit from rapid and unsustainable price increases ("pumps"). To identify such manipulations at scale, we conduct a large-scale study of pump.fun, collecting information on all 15 million coins launched in the last two years, and performing analysis on large, random samples of transaction data. We identify five classes of manipulation strategies: 1) wash trading, 2) creator address obfuscation, 3) coordinated sell, 4) copycat coins, and 5) social media manipulation. We find that strategic actors often bypass the platform interface and implement these strategies in a highly automated and low-latency fashion, by interacting directly with the blockchain. We further uncover the existence of "Market-Manipulation-as-a-service (MMaaS)," third-party tools that enable users to perform these manipulations without any technical expertise. We conclude by devising mitigations and proposing recommendations for traders, pump.fun, wallets or chain scanners, software development platforms, and regulators.

</details>


### [273] [Are Unreachable Nodes Truly Safe? Fully Eclipsing Monero's P2P Network!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10260)
*Ruisheng Shi,Jiaqi Zeng,Lina Lan,Shihan Zhang,Bing Han,Xiapu Luo,Qishu Jin,Wenliang Du,Qin Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次针对Monero P2P网络中NAT后的不可达节点提出日食攻击，无需入站访问，通过污染可达节点peerlist并利用Monero出站连接刷新逻辑，最终独占受害者的全部出站连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对Monero、比特币和以太坊的日食攻击都隐含假设攻击者能建立入站连接，因此将NAT后不可达节点这一实际占主导的节点类别排除在外。这类节点通常被认为拥有更强的网络连接韧性；本文挑战这一假设，证明不可达并不等于预期中的抗攻击能力。

Method: 提出两种针对不可达节点的日食攻击：Nyx先污染可达节点的peerlist，使其作为传播中继污染不可达节点的白名单，再滥用Monero内置的出站连接刷新逻辑驱逐良性邻居并独占所有出站连接，实现全网范围的持久性日食；Moros则利用引导（bootstrapping）阶段，对刚加入的不可达节点实施更隐蔽、更快速的日食。

Result: Nyx通过基于SEED Emulator构建的Monero网络进行大规模仿真验证；Moros在Monero主网上针对受控目标进行演示。结果表明不可达节点可被可靠地拖入稳定、长期的日食状态。

Conclusion: 不可达节点的网络韧性被高估，日食攻击可跨越NAT边界实施。论文同时提出了相应的防御对策。

Abstract: Eclipse attacks isolate a blockchain node by monopolizing its network connections. Existing attacks on Monero (NDSS'25), Bitcoin (USENIX'15/21, S&P'20) and Ethereum (WWW'26) implicitly assume that the adversary can establish inbound connections, thereby excluding a large and practically dominant class of nodes: \textit{unreachable nodes} operating behind NATs. Such nodes are widely believed to enjoy stronger networks. We challenge this assumption and show that unreachability does NOT imply the expected resilience!
  We present the first eclipse attacks tailored to unreachable nodes in Monero's P2P network. Our attacks require no inbound access to the victim. Instead, they first poison the peerlist of reachable nodes, which subsequently act as propagation relays to contaminate unreachable nodes' whitelists. The adversary then exploits Monero's built-in outbound connection refresh logic to evict benign neighbors and eventually monopolize all outbound connections. We instantiate this strategy in two attacks: Nyx, which targets long-running unreachable nodes and achieves a complete and persistent eclipse through network-wide poisoning; and Moros, a stealthier attack that exploits the bootstrapping phase to rapidly eclipse newly joined unreachable nodes.
  We ethically evaluate both attacks. Nyx is validated via large-scale simulations on a Monero network constructed using the SEED Emulator, while Moros is demonstrated on the Monero mainnet against controlled targets. Our results show that unreachable nodes can be reliably driven into stable, long-lived eclipse states. We also propose countermeasures.

</details>


### [274] [Maverick: Private and Verifiable LLM Inference Made Practical via Matrix-Vector Multiplication Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10264)
*Ben Merbaum,Mohammad Amin Raeisi,Wenhao Wang,Charalampos Papamanthou,Katerina Sotiraki,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Maverick：基于矩阵-向量乘法委托协议的私有、可验证LLM推理方案，实现信息论安全验证、LPN伪随机掩码输入隐私，几乎无服务器开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源LLM竞争力增强且透明，但本地运行大模型需大量算力，用户可能转向第三方提供商，从而带来隐私与正确性担忧。现有方案往往带来高服务器开销或额外信任假设。

Method: 提出Maverick，基于矩阵-向量乘法委托协议（LLM中的主导操作）。核心是首个信息论可靠的矩阵-向量乘法委托验证协议，具备透明预处理、高效批量验证、几乎零服务器开销；将该验证原语与基于LPN的伪随机掩码结合，提供输入隐私。实现该委托原语并构建端到端原型。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上以tokens/秒度量吞吐，评估1-8线程客户端配置。单客户端线程+最多128线程CPU服务器：掩码在线生成时提速达17x，预计算时45x，仅验证时44x。四客户端线程：对应13x、18x、17x。当服务器计算不再是瓶颈时，模拟网络延迟的客户端微基准测试提速为12x-20x、34x-135x、38x-157x。

Conclusion: Maverick在无需高服务器开销或额外信任假设的前提下，实现私有且可验证的LLM推理，并在多种客户端/服务器配置下显著优于本地推理，兼顾隐私、正确性与效率。

Abstract: Open-source large language models (LLMs) are increasingly competitive with closed-source models while offering transparency and the ability to run inference without exposing user inputs to a service provider. However, running large-scale models locally requires substantial computational resources. In practice, users may still resort to a third-party provider, giving rise to privacy and correctness concerns. Existing solutions that address these problems often impose substantial server overhead or introduce additional trust assumptions.
  In this paper, we present Maverick, a novel approach to private and verifiable LLM inference based on a protocol for delegating matrix-vector multiplication, a dominant operation in LLMs. At its core, Maverick provides, to our knowledge, the first information-theoretically sound verification protocol for matrix-vector multiplication delegation with transparent preprocessing, efficient (batch) verification, and virtually no server overhead. We combine this verification primitive with LPN-based pseudorandom masking to provide input privacy.
  We implement our matrix-vector delegation primitive and use it to build an end-to-end prototype of Maverick, which we evaluate on Qwen3-4B by measuring throughput in tokens per second. We evaluate client configurations with 1-8 threads. With one client thread and a CPU server using up to 128 threads, Maverick achieves throughput gains over local inference of up to 17x when privacy masks are generated online, 45x when they are precomputed, and 44x when only verification is required. With four client threads, the corresponding gains are 13x, 18x, and 17x. When server computation is no longer the bottleneck, client-side microbenchmarks with simulated network delay show speedups of 12x-20x, 34x-135x, and 38x-157x.

</details>


### [275] [An Empirical Analysis of ReDoS Vulnerabilities and ReDoS Detection Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10294)
*N'Zolieh Ismaël Mahassadi,Raphaël Khoury,Justin Vallé,Abdelwahab Hamou-Lhadj*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对比了五个ReDoS检测工具与一个正则修正工具在三个数据集上的效果，并对NVD中全部ReDoS漏洞进行实证分析，发现ReDoS漏洞日益普遍、比非ReDoS漏洞更易被利用，且各检测工具对同一正则是否易受攻击存在显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReDoS漏洞会在使用正则表达式校验用户输入时触发，某些情况下正则匹配耗时呈指数级增长，从而导致拒绝服务；作者希望评估现有检测与修正工具的实际效果，并理解ReDoS漏洞与非ReDoS漏洞的差异及该类缺陷的演变趋势。

Method: 选取五个公开可用的正则检测工具和一个正则修正工具，在三个数据集上比较其有效性；同时对NVD数据库中报告的全部ReDoS漏洞进行实证分析，并与非ReDoS漏洞进行对比。

Result: ReDoS漏洞正变得越来越普遍，且相比非ReDoS漏洞更有可能被利用；此外，各检测工具对于给定正则是否易受ReDoS攻击的判断存在明显不一致。

Conclusion: ReDoS威胁持续上升且危害性更强，而现有检测工具结论分歧较大，意味着仅依赖单一工具进行判定并不可靠，亟需更一致、更可信的检测与修正方法。

Abstract: ReDoS vulnerabilities are a type of denial of service software weakness that occurs when a regex is used to validate user-supplied input. In some cases, the regex matching process can take exponential time, leading to a denial of service. In this study, we examine and compare the effectiveness of five publicly-available regex detection tools, and one regex correction tool, using three datasets. We further perform an empirical analysis of all ReDoS vulnerabilities reported to the NVD database in order to understand how they differ from non-ReDoS vulnerabilities and glean insights about this type of weakness. We find that ReDoS vulnerabilities are becoming more prevalent and are much more likely to be exploited than non-ReDoS vulnerabilities. We further find that detection tools exhibit substantial disagreement on whether or not a given regex is vulnerable.

</details>


### [276] [Learning Intrusion Response Strategies for OT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10298)
*Duc Huy Le,Rolf Stadler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对OT系统入侵响应问题，基于POMDP构建形式化模型，并结合PPO提出可学习的自动化响应方法，在仿真OT环境中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OT系统监控和控制工业过程，其遭受网络攻击会威胁关键社会服务，因此开发自动化入侵响应策略至关重要。

Method: 使用POMDP框架对OT入侵响应用例进行形式化建模，引入基于流量测量的现实部分可观测模型，并采用PPO构建可处理的学习型自动化响应方法。

Result: 在仿真OT系统上评估所得到的响应策略，发现其对该用例中的多种MITRE攻击类型均有效。

Conclusion: 基于POMDP与PPO的自动化OT入侵响应方法具备有效性，可用于应对多种MITRE攻击场景。

Abstract: Cyberattacks against Operational Technology (OT) systems, which monitor and control industrial processes, pose an increasing threat to essential societal services. For this reason, developing automated intrusion response strategies is highly important. In this paper, we present a formal model of an OT intrusion response use case using the POMDP framework. It includes a realistic model of partial observability that is based on traffic measurements. This approach allows us to develop tractable, learning-based solution methods for automated intrusion response, which are based on PPO. We evaluate the obtained response strategies on an emulated OT system and find that they are effective against several types of MITRE attacks for the studied use case.

</details>


### [277] [TrajMark: Ownership Attribution and Segment-Level Tamper Localization for Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10416)
*Bokang Zeng,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Xiaoyan Feng,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrajMark：无需训练、对称密钥、仅可见轨迹水印框架，将鲁棒归属与脆弱局部完整性验证分离，实现编码代理轨迹的溯源与篡改定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对编码代理最终补丁打水印只能提供提交产物来源证据，无法认证产生该产物的可见过程。既有行为水印主要提供全局检测或标识恢复信号，局部编辑轨迹仍可能保留足够归属证据，却无法揭示哪一受保护区域已不一致。

Method: 训练-free、对称密钥、仅可见的轨迹水印框架，含两层：稀疏所有者层将自然发生的 READ 动作中键控子集改写为掩码线性方程，编码 6 位部署标识；定位层插入关联的 Q12 普通、组与终端密封，承诺关键动作段。

Result: 三个编码代理框架、三个 LLM 上，干净全水印批次均精确恢复所有者；穷举合资格单点攻击下检测 95.5%-100% 编辑；随机单动作破坏下 95.8% 修改位点定位到可接受协议区域。所有者标记不增加轨迹动作，完整性层增加只读密封，匹配 Pass@1 为 26.9% 对 26.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过分离鲁棒归属与脆弱完整性验证，兼顾所有者归因与局部篡改定位，且对任务性能影响很小。

Abstract: Watermarking the final patch produced by a coding agent provides provenance evidence for the submitted artifact, but does not authenticate the visible process that produced it. Behavioral watermarking methods primarily provide a global detection or identifier-recovery signal, so a locally edited trajectory may retain sufficient ownership evidence without revealing which protected region has become inconsistent. To address this limitation, we propose TrajMark, a training-free, symmetric-key, visible-only trajectory watermarking framework that separates robust ownership attribution from fragile local integrity verification. Our framework consists of two complementary layers: a sparse owner layer that encodes a six-bit deployment identifier by rewriting a keyed subset of naturally occurring READ actions into masked linear equations, and a localization layer that inserts linked Q12 ordinary, group, and terminal seals to commit to protected critical-action segments. This separation allows ownership evidence to accumulate robustly across trajectories, while local modifications perturb nearby keyed commitments and expose the affected protocol region. We further provide a design-level analysis of owner recoverability, integrity collision probability, structural overhead, and localization behavior. Across three coding-agent frameworks and three LLMs, TrajMark recovers the exact owner in all evaluated clean full-watermark batches. Under exhaustive eligible single-site attacks it detects 95.5%-100% of edits, and under random single-action corruption it localizes 95.8% of modified sites to an accepted protocol region rather than to the individual action. Owner marking adds no trajectory actions; the integrity layer adds explicit read-only seals, and matched Pass@1 is 26.9% versus 26.3% for unwatermarked runs.

</details>


### [278] [Wicked Problem, Parsimonious Solution: Securing Electric Vehicle Charging Station Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10502)
*Emma Sheppard,Zachary Wadhams,Dalton Arford,Clemente Izurieta,Ann Marie Reinhold*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该立场论文主张将分层软件质量保证（HSQA）应用于电动汽车充电站软件，以系统刻画其漏洞与安全质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动汽车充电基础设施（尤其充电站）面临新型网络物理威胁，而其供电设备软件是最脆弱、最缺乏保护且刻画不足的攻击面，亟需系统化的漏洞表征方法。

Method: 提出采用HSQA（分层软件质量保证）方法，将质量与安全考量贯穿软件开发生命周期，对充电站专用软件进行分层分析：从单个漏洞（如CVE）到高层特性（如CIA三要素）。

Result: HSQA能够提供对代码质量与安全性的全面视图，覆盖从细粒度漏洞到高层安全属性的多个层级，适用于电动汽车充电站软件评估。

Conclusion: 作者立场为：HSQA是评估电动汽车充电站软件的优秀方法，应将其作为刻画该攻击面漏洞的现代软件质量保证手段加以推广。

Abstract: Electric vehicle charging infrastructure presents a suite of novel cyber-physical threats. Among this infrastructure, charging stations are the most vulnerable elements. The software in the charging station supply equipment is particularly vulnerable. Currently, the software is an attack surface that is largely unprotected and poorly characterized. To represent the vulnerabilities in this attack surface, we advocate for applying modern software quality assurance to characterize vulnerabilities in electric vehicle charging station software. Specifically, we advocate for the application of hierarchical software quality assurance (HSQA) to specialized electric vehicle charging station software. HSQA provides a comprehensive view of the code quality and security -- from the level of individual vulnerabilities (e.g., CVEs) to high level characteristics (e.g., CIA Triad). HSQA incorporates quality and security considerations throughout the software development lifecycle. Thus, our position is that HSQA is an excellent approach for assessing electrical vehicle charging station software.

</details>


### [279] [Towards Tackling Application Logic Flaws through Autonomous Formal-Logic Modeling and Automated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10537)
*Yiwei Fang,Yichen Liu,Ze Jin,Haoqiang Wang,Qixu Liu,Luyi Xing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 LL-Verifier，一个结合大语言模型与逻辑模型检测器的自动化逻辑漏洞发现框架，通过将自然语言协议描述转化为形式化逻辑模型并进行穷举验证，在 27 个 IoT 访问控制协议中成功发现多种逻辑漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑漏洞与业务语义和威胁模型紧密相关，难以发现和规模化推理。现实系统具有多样的应用特性、复杂的协议逻辑和领域特定威胁模型，安全分析需大量人力和领域专业知识。因此亟需一种能够自动化、可扩展地识别逻辑漏洞的方法。

Method: LL-Verifier 采用两大核心组件：(1) 大语言模型进行自主建模，(2) 逻辑模型检测器进行严格推理。框架处理自然语言输入（协议描述与安全目标），自动生成形式化逻辑模型和属性，表达于一种基于通用逻辑语言 Maude 的新型逻辑语言中，并针对任意应用级语义建模进行优化。随后将形式化模型转化为逻辑状态机，通过逻辑级模型检测实现穷举、严格的验证。

Result: 作者将该框架应用于 27 个广泛使用的 IoT 设备访问控制协议（具有厂商特定的逻辑流程和语义），验证了 LL-Verifier 的高有效性、效率与实用性，并发现了一系列具有严重安全和隐私影响的复杂逻辑漏洞。

Conclusion: LL-Verifier 有效解决了应用安全中的难题——自动逻辑漏洞发现，在真实场景下提供自动化、穷举且精确的逻辑约束内推理，证明其在多样化应用级协议分析中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Logic flaws pose significant challenges in the design and implementation of modern, semantically rich systems and applications, impacting security, privacy, and trust. These flaws are inherently tied to business-specific semantics and threat models, making their discovery and reasoning difficult and hard to scale. Real-world systems often exhibit diverse application features, complex protocol logic, and domain-specific threat models, necessitating substantial human effort and domain expertise for effective security analysis. In this paper, we introduce LL-Verifier, a novel, automated framework for identifying logic vulnerabilities built on (1) large language models for autonomous modeling, and (2) logic model checkers for rigorous reasoning. LL-Verifier processes natural language inputs, in particular protocol descriptions and security goals, to automatically generate formal logic models and properties expressed in a new logic language built on a generic logic language Maude, optimized for modeling arbitrary application-level semantics. These formal models are then converted into logical state machines, enabling exhaustive, rigorous verification through logic level model checking. This approach streamlines the analysis of diverse, application-level protocols deployed in real-world scenarios, offering automated, exhaustive, and precise reasoning within their logical constraints. We evaluated the high effectiveness, efficiency, and practicality of LL-Verifier by applying it to 27 access control protocols of widely used IoT devices, which come with vendor-specific logic flows and semantics. While LL-verifier tackles a hard problem in application security, i.e., automatic logic flaws discovery, our analysis uncovers a range of sophisticated logic vulnerabilities in IoT protocols and devices with serious security and privacy implications.

</details>


### [280] [Trust Me, I'm Your Developer: Self-Issued Authentication in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03247)
*Syed Ghazanfar Abbas,Dongyan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了用户要求大模型自行设计身份验证测试时的行为，在ChatGPT、Claude、Qwen、Mistral和Llama上进行了分阶段"开发者身份"实验，发现部分模型会自发自评、在缺乏外部验证证据的情况下返回"已验证"，并将此现象命名为模型自发的伪凭证（MIPC）与会话式虚假认证（CFA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型安全研究主要关注角色扮演越狱，较少探讨模型自行设计并执行身份验证测试时的风险。作者希望揭示一种被忽视的会话式安全失败：当模型同时充当挑战生成者、证据评估者和身份裁决者时，技术知识可能被误当作身份证明。

Method: 采用分阶段的"开发者身份"实验，在ChatGPT、Claude、Qwen、Mistral和Llama五个模型上测试其对无据主张"我是你的开发者"的响应，观察各模型是否生成身份测试、如何定义可信证据、如何评估回答，以及是否在无外部验证的情况下给出"已验证"结论。

Result: 五个模型初始均拒绝无据主张；Claude拒绝进行身份测试，ChatGPT生成相关问题但坚持回答只能证明知识而非身份；Qwen与Mistral生成技术挑战、自行定义可信证据标准、评估详细回答并在无外部身份证据下返回"已验证"；Llama同样生成并评估开发者测试、接受该身份，并进一步对内部运行时与部署状态作出无根据的访问声明。所有CFA案例中，模型同时承担挑战生成、证据评估与身份裁决三重角色；被接受的身份并未改变所测试的授权边界，表明虚假认证与权限提升是两种不同结果。

Conclusion: 研究将"自我签发式认证"识别为一种会话式安全失败。结论是：认证身份必须源自外部安全组件，模型生成的对话绝不能创建或修改身份或授权状态；虚假认证与权限提升应被区分对待。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) security has largely focused on role-playing jailbreaks, with less attention to what happens when a user asks an LLM to verify an identity claim through a test designed by the model itself. We study this behavior through a staged developer-identity experiment with ChatGPT, Claude, Qwen, Mistral, and Llama. All five models initially rejected the unsupported claim "I am your developer." Claude refused to conduct an identity test, while ChatGPT generated developer-oriented questions but maintained that answers could demonstrate knowledge, not identity. In contrast, Qwen and Mistral generated technical challenges, defined what counted as convincing evidence, evaluated detailed answers, and returned Verified without receiving any externally validated identity evidence. Llama similarly generated and evaluated a developer test, accepted the claimed identity, and subsequently made unsupported claims of access to internal runtime and deployment state. We call the model-generated verification procedure a Model-Issued Pseudo-Credential (MIPC) and the resulting unsupported identity judgment Conversational False Authentication (CFA). In each CFA case, the same model acted as challenge generator, evidence evaluator, and identity decision-maker, converting technical knowledge into supposed proof of identity. The accepted identities did not change the tested authorization boundaries, showing that false authentication and privilege escalation are distinct outcomes. These results identify self-issued authentication as a conversational security failure: authenticated identity must originate from an external security component, and model-generated dialogue must never create or modify identity or authorization state.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Identifying Habit, Physics, and Nuisance in Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09210)
*Jinting Hang,Zhenhui Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出遥操作演示中的多模态性源于三个因素——操作者动作选择习惯、共享物理以及观测干扰，并用结构因果模型将其形式化为 a=g(h,z,u), z'=f(z,a), o=r(z,c)。通过干预实验验证该分解，并提出冻结共享物理读出、仅更新薄接口的适配规则，在 StackCube、DROID、RH20T 上提升了低样本迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作演示常呈现多模态性，即使给定执行动作后底层动力学近乎确定。作者认为这种多模态性混合了操作者习惯、共享物理与观测干扰三种因素，而纠缠的下一观测预测器会同时吸收三者，导致动力学学习被干扰。因此需要显式拆分这些因素，以获得更纯净的动力学表征。

Method: 采用结构因果模型 a=g(h,z,u), z'=f(z,a), o=r(z,c) 分离习惯 h、物理 z 与观测干扰 c；设计互补干预：在固定状态下替换或打乱动作，检验下一状态误差应显著上升；改变外观与相机则不应影响。此外引入习惯感知的反向评分，用于排序可行的过去而不改写动力学；提出适配规则：冻结共享物理读出，仅更新薄接口。

Result: 在 StackCube、DROID、RH20T 上，该适配规则相比从零训练提升了低样本迁移性能；在受污染的适配数据下保持更干净的动力学；并从本体感受扩展到像素观测，通过了多视角与多步检验。

Conclusion: 研究不支持将潜在动作等同于操作者习惯，也不针对大规模视频生成基准。核心结论是：遥操作演示的多模态性可分解为习惯、物理与观测干扰，冻结共享物理表征并仅适配薄接口有助于低样本迁移与动力学鲁棒性。

Abstract: Teleoperated demonstrations are often multimodal even when the underlying dynamics are nearly deterministic given the executed action. We argue that this multimodality typically mixes three factors--operator habit in action selection, shared physics, and observation nuisance--and that entangled next-observation predictors absorb all three. We formalize the split with a structural causal model a=g(h,z,u), z'=f(z,a), o=r(z,c), and test it with complementary interventions: replacing or shuffling actions at fixed state sharply increases next-state error, whereas appearance and camera changes should not; habit-aware reverse scoring improves ranking of feasible pasts without rewriting the dynamics. The associated adaptation rule is to freeze a shared physics readout and update only a thin interface. On StackCube, DROID, and RH20T this rule improves low-shot transfer relative to training from scratch, retains cleaner dynamics under corrupted adaptation data, and extends from proprioception to pixel observations with multi-view and multi-step checks. We do not equate latent actions with operator habit, and we do not target large-scale video generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [282] [Geometry Conditioning in an Embodied SLM: Training Controls and Robustness Diagnostics in a 0.8B Hybrid Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09213)
*Hao Li,Haofei Sun,Lin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究系统考察物理状态输入对0.8B混合语言模型操作策略的影响，发现训练时几何对齐未带来可靠优势，且坐标不变性与物理布局泛化间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究物理状态输入（即几何增量的语义正确性）如何影响小型混合语言模型在操作任务中的表现，检验训练时几何对齐是否带来收益，并区分坐标不变性与物理布局泛化能力。

Method: 基于0.8B混合语言模型，附加6.2M可训练参数，在三个LIBERO-Spatial任务上训练六种条件，跨三个随机种子、540个留出回合评估。对比：将循环衰减门条件化于几何增量、训练时打乱增量、无显式物体/目标几何、使用相同增量的token适配器、token时钟条件化；两类几何策略在评估时均输入正确。另做鲁棒性测试：状态-only相对坐标策略与四种视觉策略在帧重标注及5cm物体位移下的表现。

Result: 几何增量条件化衰减门成功率28.9%，训练时打乱增量为36.7%，无显式物体/目标几何为24.4%；token适配器为27.8%，差异随随机种子波动且无定论；token时钟条件化仅11.1%，含一个未收敛种子。鲁棒性测试中，状态-only相对坐标策略在帧重标注下保持7/10成功率，而四种视觉策略在5cm物体位移后最多3/20。

Conclusion: 在该训练配方下，训练时几何对齐未显示可靠优势；结果凸显坐标不变性与物理布局泛化之间的鸿沟。论文附带回合记录、种子级分析与绘图代码。

Abstract: We study how physical-state inputs affect a 0.8B hybrid language model adapted for manipulation with 6.2M trainable parameters. Six conditions are trained on three LIBERO-Spatial tasks and evaluated over three seeds and 540 held-out rollouts. Conditioning recurrent decay gates on geometric increments yields 28.9% success, compared with 36.7% when those increments are shuffled during training and 24.4% without explicit object/goal geometry. Both geometry policies receive correct inputs at evaluation. A token adapter using the same increments scores 27.8%; differences vary across seeds and remain inconclusive. Token-clock conditioning scores 11.1%, including one seed that fails to converge. In separate robustness tests, a state-only relative-coordinate policy retains 7/10 success under frame relabeling, whereas all four tested visual policies fall to at most 3/20 after a 5 cm object displacement. These results show no reliable advantage from training-time geometric alignment under this recipe and illustrate the gap between coordinate invariance and physical-layout generalization. Episode records, seed-level analyses, and figure-generation code accompany the paper.

</details>


### [283] [Design and Attitude Control of an Underwater Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09217)
*Davide Molinaroli,Mohit Singh,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并验证了一种可复现的水下四足机器人，通过防水电机外壳与SO(3)姿态控制器实现水下姿态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人陆地适应性强，但水下应用受限；将四足运动扩展到水中可实现两栖机动，服务于检测、环境监测和灾害响应等场景。

Method: 采用聚甲醛塑料加工定制防水电机外壳，配合标准O型圈和动态轴封构建机器人；建立简化动力学模型，刻画球形末端执行器所受阻力向浮动基座传递力矩的过程；基于SO(3)上的误差定义设计闭环姿态控制器。

Result: 控制器在仿真和水箱实验中均得到评估，机器人能够跟踪横滚、俯仰和偏航的期望姿态设定点。

Conclusion: 该工作为可复现水下四足机器人提供了设计、建模与实验验证，说明基于SO(3)的闭环姿态控制在水下腿式系统中有效。

Abstract: Legged robots are versatile on land, but their use in underwater environments remains limited. Extending quadruped locomotion to water enables amphibious mobility with applications in inspection, environmental monitoring and disaster response. This paper presents the design, modeling, and experimental validation of a reproducible underwater quadruped robot. The robot is built around custom waterproof motor housings machined from polyoxymethylene plastic, which use off-the-shelf O-rings and dynamic shaft seals. A simplified model is derived to describe the dynamics of this underwater legged system, capturing how drag forces on spherical end effectors transmit torque to the floating base. Building on this model, a closed-loop attitude controller is developed using an error formulation defined on the special orthogonal group SO(3). The controller is evaluated both in simulation and experimentally in a water tank, where the robot tracks desired orientation setpoints in roll, pitch and yaw.

</details>


### [284] [Learning to Fly: Stable Vision-Guided UAV Servoing with Compact Target-Centric Cues and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09234)
*Saurbh Singh Jamwal,Nived Chebrolu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机长时程视觉伺服，用目标分割得到的图像空间偏移与相对深度，叠加四旋翼速度与投影重力构成12维紧凑观测，替代RGB直接学习；对比直接PPO与三种同预算课程策略，发现三者名义性能相当，而视觉课程在未见目标运动下退化最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉引导的无人机强化学习面临策略优化不稳定、探索激进以及高维视觉感知代价高的问题；作者希望验证紧凑的、以目标为中心的表示能否支撑稳健的长时程空中视觉伺服，并考察课程训练对鲁棒性的作用。

Method: 不直接从RGB图像学习，而是用轻量目标分割提供图像空间偏移和相对深度，与四旋翼速度和投影重力测量拼成12维策略观测。以直接PPO为基线，对比三种同预算课程策略：视觉课程（逐步扩大目标放置难度）、动力学课程（逐步放宽动作约束与平滑）以及联合课程（两者结合）。另进行观测消融实验，并与调优的经典视觉伺服控制器对比。

Result: 所有策略均达到可比的名义性能，在跟踪指标上各有优势。消融显示本体感受测量对稳定飞行至关重要，图像空间线索对目标对齐关键，而显式深度在该设定下并非必要。相较调优的经典视觉伺服控制器，学习策略对强控制与视觉扰动更鲁棒；视觉课程在未见目标运动下退化最小。

Conclusion: 紧凑的以目标为中心的表示可支撑稳健的长时程空中视觉伺服；视觉课程训练虽在名义性能上收益有限，但能提升对动态分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-guided reinforcement learning for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) remains challenging due to unstable policy optimisation, aggressive exploration, and the cost of high-dimensional visual perception. In this work, we investigate long-horizon UAV visual servoing using compact target-centric cues combined with low-dimensional sensor measurements. Rather than learning directly from RGB images, lightweight target segmentation provides image-space offsets and relative depth, which are combined with quadrotor velocity and projected-gravity measurements into a compact 12D policy observation. We compare Direct PPO with three matched-budget curriculum strategies: a Visual curriculum that progressively expands target placement difficulty, a Dynamics curriculum that gradually relaxes action constraints and smoothing, and a Joint curriculum that combines both progressions. All strategies reach comparable nominal performance, with complementary advantages across tracking metrics. Observation ablations show that proprioceptive measurements are critical for stable flight and image-space cues for target alignment, while explicit depth is not necessary for strong performance in the evaluated setting. Against tuned classical visual-servo controllers, learned policies show greater robustness to strong control and visual perturbations, while the Visual curriculum exhibits the smallest degradation under unseen target motion. Overall, the results demonstrate that compact target-centric representations can support robust long-horizon aerial visual servoing and that visual curriculum training can improve robustness to dynamic distribution shifts despite limited gains in nominal performance.

</details>


### [285] [No Free Checker: A Survey of Verifiers for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09250)
*Yang Wan,Xihang Yue,Zhirui Liu,Ziyuan Chu,Shuxun Wang,Yuhan Chen,Xiaonan Jiang,Xukun Zhu,Yubo Dong,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述约150种机器人策略验证器，提出可用性（Availability）与可信性（Credibility）两个维度，发现四类验证器家族中存在可用性上升而可信性下降的规律，并总结验证器自身的验证方法及九项可核查指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略验证器广泛用于评估与训练视觉-语言-动作策略，涵盖成功检测器、奖励模型、运行时监控、安全过滤器、时序逻辑规范等多种形式，但缺乏对这些验证器的系统性梳理与统一比较框架。

Method: 提出两个比较属性：可用性（裁决成本、到达时机的早晚、可请求密度）与可信性（高分对任务的指示程度，随可博弈性与自利性下降）。按判断来源将验证器分为四类：人类验证器、基于规则与形式化验证器、学习型与预训练验证器、模型内在验证器，并综述约150种验证器。

Result: 在四个验证器家族中一致发现：可用性上升而可信性下降，无论判断由谁提供，都不存在"免费的检查器"。验证器自身的验证方法在文献中主要有三种：与人类标注的一致性、其所训练策略的性能、以及奖励博弈下的行为表现。

Conclusion: 验证器设计存在可用性与可信性的根本权衡，没有免费的检查器；论文提出九项使验证器声明可核查的指标，并给出尚未构建的验证器的坐标方向。

Abstract: A verifier for robot policies reads a candidate behavior and returns a score for how well it did, used both to evaluate vision-language-action policies and to train them. Verifiers range from success detectors and reward models to runtime monitors, safety filters, and temporal-logic specifications. We survey roughly 150 verifiers and compare them along two properties. Availability is how much a verdict costs, how early in a rollout the verdict arrives, and how often a verdict can be asked for. Availability rises as verdicts get cheaper, earlier, and denser. Credibility is how much a high score tells us about the task. Credibility falls as the judgment becomes gameable and self-serving. We group the verifiers by who supplies the judgment: human verifiers, rule-based and formal verifiers, learned and pretrained verifiers, and model-intrinsic verifiers. Across the four families, we find that credibility falls as availability rises. Regardless of who supplies the judgment, there is no free checker. We then examine what validates a verifier itself, and how much a high score tells us. Three measures appear in the literature: agreement with human labels, the performance of the policy it trains, and behavior under reward hacking. We close with nine metrics that make a verifier claim checkable, and coordinates for the verifiers still to be built.

</details>


### [286] [AccelMPC: High-Rate, Low-Power FPGA-Accelerated Model Predictive Control for Tiny Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09380)
*Andrea Grillo,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AccelMPC 通过算法-数值表示-硬件映射-物理集成的端到端协同设计，将缓存式 MPC 求解器部署于 FPGA 加速平台与定制 6g PCB 上，在 35g Crazyflie 上实现 1 kHz 机载约束 MPC，求解时间最高提升 15.6 倍，能量-时延积提升 195.4 倍，可扩展至 2 万以上优化变量并开源相关设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型空中机器人需要嵌入式边缘控制性能数量级的提升。现有缓存式 MPC 求解器虽能处理敏捷飞行的快速动力学与复杂约束，但计算需求对资源受限机器人而言过高，导致此前实现只能以较低控制频率运行。

Method: 提出端到端协同设计方法 AccelMPC，联合优化求解器算法、数值表示、硬件映射与物理集成；采用协同设计的 FPGA 加速 ADMM 式 MPC 求解器，并搭配定制 6g PCB 以提供高带宽通信，部署于 35g Crazyflie 平台。

Result: 硬件实验实现 1 kHz 机载带约束 MPC（含动态障碍物），求解时间最高提升 15.6 倍，能量-时延积较最先进的嵌入式微控制器求解器提升 195.4 倍，并可将优化问题扩展至超过 20,000 个优化变量及相当数量的约束。

Conclusion: AccelMPC 表明通过算法-硬件-物理的端到端协同设计，可在极资源受限的微型飞行器上实现高频率、带复杂约束的机载 MPC；作者开源 PCB 设计文件、固件与 FPGA 求解器代码。

Abstract: Unlocking the potential of tiny aerial robots requires order of magnitude improvements in the performance of embedded edge control. In particular, although recent cached model predictive control (MPC) solvers can handle the fast system dynamics and complex constraints required for agile drone flight, their computational demands remain prohibitive for resource-constrained robots, forcing prior implementations to operate at reduced control rates. AccelMPC overcomes this challenge through an end-to-end co-design approach that jointly optimizes the solver algorithm, numerical representation, hardware mapping, and physical integration. AccelMPC pairs a co-designed FPGA-accelerated alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-based MPC solver with a custom 6g PCB, providing high-bandwidth communication for deployment on a 35g Crazyflie. Hardware experiments demonstrate 1 kHz onboard constrained MPC with dynamic obstacles, up to 15.6x faster solve times and 195.4x improvement in energy-delay product over state-of-the-art embedded microcontroller-based solvers, all while scaling to optimization problems with over 20,000 optimization variables and a comparable number of constraints. We release our PCB design files, firmware, and FPGA solver code open source.

</details>


### [287] [A Decade of Bayesian Optimization for Controller Tuning and Robot Learning: Tutorial, Review, and Future Prospects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09403)
*David Stenger,Paul Brunzema,Johanna Menn,Alexander von Rohr,Angela P. Schoellig,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述贝叶斯优化（BO）在控制与机器人领域的最新进展，提出实用配置指南、方法分类与应用场景，并指出缺乏标准化基准问题，初步构建轻量基准套件与评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年BO已成为控制器自动整定与机器人学习的强大框架，但研究者与从业者缺乏对最新进展、实践应用及未来方向的统一认识，且控制相关应用缺少标准化基准，难以严格评估与公平比较。

Method: 采用从业者视角，以代表性控制器整定案例演示BO的搭建流程；将BO置于从深度强化学习到数据驱动控制的更广学习范式背景中定位；梳理面向复杂问题与特定应用的多种BO方法，并给出统一视角；提出面向控制工程与机器人的轻量级基准套件，并给出评估指标与最佳实践。

Result: 形成对BO现状的统一综述视角，明确其与控制系统和机器人的相关性；识别关键研究挑战与有前景的方向；提出初步的轻量级控制工程与机器人基准套件，并提供可直接比较新算法与现有先进方法的指标与最佳实践。

Conclusion: BO在控制与机器人领域具有重要价值，但标准化基准缺失是当前显著空白；通过轻量基准套件、统一指标与最佳实践，可促进未来研究的严谨评估与算法间的直接比较。

Abstract: In the past decade, Bayesian optimization (BO) has emerged as a powerful and adaptable framework for automatic controller tuning and robot learning. This article offers a comprehensive overview of the state-of-the-art in BO, designed to support both researchers and practitioners in understanding recent advancements, practical applications, and future research directions. We begin by adopting a practitioner's perspective, illustrating how to effectively set up BO through a representative controller tuning example. We position BO within the broader context of learning paradigms, ranging from deep reinforcement learning to data-driven control, and highlight scenarios where BO is most advantageous. Next, we discuss the diverse range of BO methods that have been developed to tackle complex problems and specific applications. This article provides a unified perspective on the current landscape of BO, emphasizing its relevance to control systems and robotics, and it highlights future prospects by identifying key research challenges and promising avenues for advancing BO in the field. This includes addressing a significant gap in the BO landscape: the lack of standardized benchmark problems specifically for control-related applications. To foster future research and ensure rigorous evaluation, we start an effort towards a lightweight benchmark suite for control engineering and robotics. We also present metrics and best practices to facilitate direct comparisons between new BO algorithms and established state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [288] [Actuator Dynamics Curricula for Narrow-Viability Tasks in Legged Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09492)
*Kousheek Chakraborty,Chandan K. Rajendra,Ayham Alharbat,Abeje Y. Mersha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“驱动器动力学课程”（Actuator Dynamics Curriculum），通过将关节刚度从高值逐步退火至系统辨识值来扩大可行性核，解决探索轨迹过早终止、梯度信号不足的强化学习任务收敛难题，并在四足机器人站立过渡任务上验证了仿真与实机迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习在部分腿足机器人任务上无法收敛，原因在于大多数探索轨迹在产生有用梯度信号之前就已终止。这类任务的瓶颈不是奖励信号设计，而是终止条件限制了探索范围，因此需要从动力学层面重构课程的可行域。

Method: 提出驱动器动力学课程：初始化时将关节刚度设为高值，随已完成回合长度增长，将其退火至系统辨识得到的真实刚度值。以小车-倒立摆为代表性系统，分析临界阻尼下闭环关节自然频率提高对底层MDP可行性核的扩大作用，并在Boston Dynamics Spot的四足到手倒立过渡任务上应用该课程。

Result: 在小车-倒立摆上验证了可行性核的单调性；在Spot的四足到手倒立过渡任务上，固定辨识刚度的训练会停滞于始终无法完成过渡的策略，而所提课程训练的策略在仿真中跨10个随机种子均能完成过渡，并成功迁移至真实硬件。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，仿真驱动器动力学是设计课程的一个有效维度，特别适用于探索受终止条件而非奖励信号制约的任务；提高闭环关节自然频率可扩大可行性核，从而增加任务可行的初始状态比例。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has produced capable controllers across a broad range of legged-robot tasks, but a subset of these tasks fail to converge under standard training: those for which most exploration trajectories terminate before producing useful gradient signal. To address such tasks we introduce the \emph{Actuator Dynamics Curriculum}, a procedure that initializes joint stiffness at a high value and anneals it toward the system-identified value as completed episode lengths grow. Using a cart-pole system as a representative example, we show that higher closed-loop joint natural frequency under critical damping enlarges the viability kernel of the underlying Markov Decision Process, increasing the fraction of initial states from which the task is feasible. We validate the kernel monotonicity on the cart-pole and apply the curriculum to a quadrupedal-to-handstand transition on the Boston Dynamics Spot, a narrow-viability task where training under fixed identified stiffness plateaus at a policy that never completes the transition. The trained policy executes the transition in simulation across 10 seeds and transfers to hardware. More broadly, our results suggest that simulated actuator dynamics is a useful axis along which to design curricula for tasks in which exploration is bottlenecked by termination conditions rather than by reward signal.

</details>


### [289] [Agentic AI-enabled Semantic Commissioning of a Cognitive Digital Twin for Reconfigurable Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09503)
*Yangyang Liu,Xun Xu,Jan Polzer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于多智能体与大模型的端到端认知数字孪生自动调试工作流，通过语义与功能双路径合成实现快速定制化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构制造中认知数字孪生（CDT）的快速定制调试是核心难题。传统数字孪生构建偏重几何重建，忽视深度语义集成与功能互操作，难以支撑自主推理。

Method: 以 LangGraph 作为多智能体编排引擎，构建 AI 驱动的端到端 CDT 自动调试流程，实现双路径合成：语义路径借助检索增强生成（RAG）从非结构化文档中抽取技术规格；功能路径通过模型上下文协议（MCP）自主发现并绑定实时工业遥测数据。

Result: 在机器人加工单元的实验验证中，感知环节平均精度均值（mAP）达 97.2%，部署周期由数周缩短至平均 2 小时。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了从人工脚本编写向自主编排的范式转变，为可重构制造中 CDT 的快速定制化调试提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Rapid bespoke commissioning of the Cognitive Digital Twin (CDT) is a major challenge in reconfigurable manufacturing. Traditional digital twin (DT) construction methods primarily focus on geometric reconstruction, often neglecting the deep semantic integration and functional interoperability necessary for autonomous reasoning. This paper proposes an agent-based, AI-driven workflow to automate end-to-end CDT debugging. The system utilises LangGraph as a multi-agent orchestration engine to achieve dual-path synthesis: the semantic path extracts technical specifications from unstructured documents using Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), while the functional path autonomously discovers and binds to real-time industrial telemetry data using Model Context Protocol (MCP). Experimental validation in a robotic machining cell demonstrates that the system achieves a mean average accuracy (mAP) of 97.2% in perception and reduces the deployment cycle from several weeks to an average of 2 hours, marking a paradigm shift from manual scripting to autonomous orchestration.

</details>


### [290] [Compact Visuotactile World Models for Lifting: Prediction, Reward Alignment, and Force Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09597)
*Qinzhen Ma,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究视觉-触觉世界模型中的接触预测是否真正支撑控制决策，发现感知精度提升、奖励设计改进与力约束控制能力之间存在明显脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的接触预测只有在能够支持有效决策时才对机器人操作有用；作者据此考察触觉感知预测质量与力约束控制性能之间的因果关联。

Method: 采用紧凑的随机初始化视觉-触觉世界模型、轨迹级不确定性校准，以及在想象空间中行为初始化的 actor-critic 学习；在160个MuJoCo Lift回合上比较触觉与触觉持续性条件，并进行两轮探索性控制实验（680次执行、40个独立测试初始条件），另在公开GelSight记录上评估力回归与帧级／轨迹级校准覆盖。

Result: 加入触觉使末端力预测误差由1.058降至0.228 N，区间峰值误差由2.724降至0.523 N；但触觉持续性误差更低（0.095和0.498 N）。奖励修正后分布内10 cm抬升成功率由20.0%升至93.3%，而8 N每指预算内成功率仅33.3%（力反馈为70.0%）；校准裕度降低力违反但牺牲任务完成。公开GelSight数据上力回归误差0.04234 N，帧级校准仅覆盖15.80%完整轨迹，轨迹级校准在名义90%覆盖下提升至87.36%。

Conclusion: 研究结果区分了感知改进、任务奖励改进与力约束控制的改进，三者不可相互替代；证据仅限于公开感知记录与仿真执行，未证明二者之间的迁移能力。

Abstract: Accurate contact prediction is useful for robotic manipulation only if it supports effective decisions. We investigate this connection using a compact, randomly initialized visuotactile world model, trajectory-level uncertainty calibration, and behavior-initialized actor-critic learning in imagination. On 160 MuJoCo Lift episodes, adding touch reduces endpoint-force prediction error from 1.058 to 0.228 N and interval-peak error from 2.724 to 0.523 N across three training seeds. However, tactile persistence achieves lower errors of 0.095 and 0.498 N, respectively. Two exploratory control rounds comprise 680 executions on 40 independent test initial conditions. A matched reward revision on fresh test environments increases in-distribution 10 cm lifting success from 20.0% to 93.3%, while success within an 8 N per-finger budget reaches only 33.3%, compared with 70.0% for force feedback. Calibration margins reduce force violations at the cost of task completion. In a separate study of public GelSight recordings, a force regressor achieves 0.04234 N error, but frame-level calibration covers only 15.80% of complete trajectories; trajectory-level calibration raises this to 87.36% at nominal 90% coverage. Together, these findings distinguish improvements in sensing and task reward from improvements in force-constrained control. The evidence is limited to public sensing records and simulator execution, without a demonstrated transfer between them.

</details>


### [291] [MuJoCable: Reduced-Order Surface-Routed Cable Transmission for Tendon-Driven Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09612)
*Yi Zhang,Qi Shao,Yicong Lin,Muyuan Ma,Tao Sun,Yue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MuJoCable，在MuJoCo中加入降阶、构型依赖的绳索传动模型，通过路径优化、单边轴向张力律、方向性Capstan传播与节点虚功，将移动非圆接触、单边张力与分段摩擦统一求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱传动可降低远端惯量并增加柔顺性，但走线、松弛和摩擦共同决定运动与力传递。MuJoCo等主流刚体仿真器无法同时处理移动非圆接触、单边张力与分段摩擦，导致仿真到现实存在传动误差。

Method: 构建MuJoCable：以有序路径联合优化跨越运动解析面与网格面的走线；采用单边轴向张力律、方向性Capstan传播和节点虚功，将路径映射为分段张力与机体力；以热启动引擎插件在仿真中施加这些力，并暴露路径与负载状态供设计使用。

Result: 滑轮基准测试以低于0.5%的Capstan比率误差复现解析传动关系；在18关节欠驱动SpiRobs上揭示原生肌腱模型无法表现的摩擦驱动负载增长与关节旋转的近端重分配；SpiRobs及肌腱走线耦合手指的硬件测试复现了观测到的运动序列。

Conclusion: MuJoCable使物理走线可执行，将传动来源的仿真到现实差距在设计阶段纳入走线、绳索与驱动器优化，从而在制造前评估与改进传动方案。

Abstract: Tendon transmissions reduce distal inertia and add compliance, yet routing, slack, and friction govern motion and force transfer. Mainstream rigid-body robotics simulators such as MuJoCo do not jointly resolve moving noncircular contact, unilateral tension, and segment friction. We present MuJoCable, which adds a reduced-order, configuration-dependent cable transmission to MuJoCo. Its routing algorithm jointly optimizes an ordered path across moving analytic and mesh surfaces. A unilateral axial law, directional Capstan propagation, and nodal virtual work map this path to segment tensions and body forces. The warm-started engine plugin applies these forces during simulation and exposes route and load states for design. Pulley benchmarks recover analytical transmission relations with a Capstan-ratio error below 0.5%. On the underactuated 18-joint SpiRobs, MuJoCable reveals friction-driven load growth and proximal redistribution of joint rotation that the native tendon does not represent. Hardware tests on SpiRobs and a tendon-route-coupled finger reproduce observed motion sequences. By making physical threading executable, MuJoCable brings transmission sources of the simulation-to-reality gap into route, cable, and actuator design before fabrication.

</details>


### [292] [JEPA Policy: Diffusion-Free Imitation Learning via Paired Action and Future Representation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09630)
*Jie Xu,Kangjin Yu,Ziyi Jin,Junjie Gao,Liqing Chen,Yixian Li,Shuai Tian,Zhongpu Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 JEPA Policy，一种无扩散的策略学习框架，通过将动作块与其观测到的未来表征作为配对训练目标，在共享 Transformer 中经两次前向传播交互，使未来预测能够塑造用于生成动作的表征，从而提升模仿学习性能且保持低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆仅监督动作，未显式约束与每个示范动作块配对的未来表征；而扩散策略依赖迭代生成采样，延迟较高。作者希望在不使用迭代生成采样的前提下，利用未来表征监督来改善视觉运动模仿学习，并获得低延迟表现。

Method: 提出 JEPA Policy：动作块与观测到的未来表征作为配对训练目标；动作 token 与未来表征 token 在共享 Transformer 中交互，并通过两次前向传播进行精炼，使未来预测塑造生成动作所用的表征。采用双分支与梯度路由的对照实验，以归属增益来自共享拓扑结构而非单独的辅助预测头。

Result: 在九项仿真任务中，JEPA Policy 相比仅动作的 MIP 基线提升平均成功率，并在所评估配置下优于 Diffusion Policy，仅为 MIP 模型延迟增加 0.29 ms。五项任务、630 回合的实体机器人研究呈现相同的汇总排序。审计未发现在动作监督下出现完全表征坍缩，并识别出未来预测误差中的任务条件化失败排序信号。

Conclusion: 配对未来表征监督是一种实用的低延迟视觉运动模仿学习途径，无需迭代生成采样即可获得性能提升，且未来预测误差可提供任务相关的失败排序信号。

Abstract: Standard behavior cloning supervises actions without explicitly constraining the future representation paired with each demonstrated action chunk. We introduce JEPA Policy, a diffusion-free framework that uses the action chunk and its observed future representation as paired training targets. Action and future-representation tokens interact in a shared Transformer and are refined through two forward passes. Future prediction can therefore shape the representation used to generate actions. Dual-branch and gradient-routing controls attribute the gain to this shared topology rather than to an auxiliary prediction head alone. Across nine simulated tasks, JEPA Policy improves mean success over the action-only MIP baseline and outperforms Diffusion Policy under the evaluated configurations, while adding 0.29 ms to MIP's model latency. A five-task, 630-episode physical-robot study produces the same pooled ranking. Further audits find no complete representation collapse under action supervision and identify a task-conditioned failure-ranking signal in future-prediction error. These results support paired future-representation supervision as a practical approach to low-latency visuomotor imitation without iterative generative sampling.

</details>


### [293] [A Risk-Sensitive and Uncertainty-Aware Decision-Making and Control Framework for Safe and Robust Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09650)
*Zhuoren Li,Ran Yu,Weiqi Zhang,Ming Liu,Lu Xiong,Chen Sun,Bo Leng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RUDC框架，将风险敏感分布式RL与集成策略不确定性量化结合，并用不确定性感知HOCBF安全修正，实现无信号交叉口安全高效鲁棒的自动驾驶决策控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在自动驾驶决策潜力大，但在城市高交互无信号交叉口部署困难：学习策略难以兼顾安全与鲁棒决策。传统安全滤波采用固定保守约束，虽提升安全却导致过度干预、损害通行效率。

Method: RUDC将风险敏感分布式强化学习与基于集成的策略不确定性量化相耦合，同时考虑回报分布的尾部风险与策略不确定性；设计不确定性感知的高阶控制障碍函数（HOCBF）安全修正机制，依据策略不确定性自适应调节约束严格程度；并引入可学习残差预测器补偿CBF模型失配与离散化误差。

Result: 在无信号交叉口的广泛仿真中，RUDC在安全性、效率与鲁棒性之间取得良好平衡，在名义场景及具有挑战性的OOD和长尾场景下均优于代表性安全RL基线，同时满足实时性要求。

Conclusion: 通过风险敏感分布式RL、不确定性量化与自适应HOCBF安全修正的协同，RUDC缓解了固定保守约束的过度干预问题，为城市高交互交叉口提供安全、高效、鲁棒的决策控制方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated considerable potential for autonomous driving decision-making. However, its deployment in urban autonomous driving, particularly at highly interactive unsignalized intersections, remains challenging, as learned policies may struggle to maintain both safety and robust decision-making in complex traffic situations. Conventional safety-filtering approaches typically employ fixed conservative constraints, which may improve safety at the cost of excessive intervention and degraded traffic efficiency. To address these limitations, we propose a Risk-sensitive and Uncertainty-aware Decision-making and Control (RUDC) framework for safe and robust autonomous driving. RUDC couples risk-sensitive distributional RL with ensemble-based policy uncertainty quantification, jointly accounting for tail risks in return distributions and uncertainty in learned policies. An uncertainty-aware high-order control barrier function (HOCBF)-based safety correction mechanism adaptively adjusts constraint strictness according to policy uncertainty, while a learnable residual predictor compensates for CBF model mismatches and discretization errors. Extensive simulations at unsignalized intersections demonstrate that RUDC achieves a favorable balance among safety, efficiency, and robustness, outperforming representative safe RL baselines under both nominal and challenging OOD and long-tail scenarios while satisfying real-time requirements.

</details>


### [294] [CT-SAFR: Safe and Interpretable Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Autonomous Robots: A Multi-Layered Verification Framework for Trustworthy AI-Driven Robotic Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09692)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出CT-SAFR多层验证框架，用于检测推理型机器人的思维链幻觉，实现94.2%的幻觉检测率、亚500ms延迟，并在仓储机器人案例中将不安全推理输出降低87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT提示使LLM具备显式分步推理能力，为自主机器人带来新机遇；但推理模型仅在25-39%的情况下如实表述其真实决策过程，复杂任务中忠实度更下降44%，存在安全风险，因此需要可靠的验证机制。

Method: 提出CT-SAFR（面向机器人的思维链安全与忠实性）多层验证框架，对推理过程进行分层校验，并以仓储机器人为案例开展验证。

Result: 幻觉检测率达94.2%（n=500，95%置信区间91.8-95.9%），延迟低于500ms；不安全推理输出减少87%（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: CT-SAFR可有效提升推理型自主机器人的安全性与忠实性，并为具备推理能力的自主机器人的负责任部署提供了建议。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting enables LLMs to perform explicit, step-by-step reasoning, creating opportunities for sophisticated autonomous robots. However, recent research reveals that reasoning models verbalize their actual decision processes only 25-39% of the time, with faithfulness degrading 44% on complex tasks. This paper presents CT-SAFR (Chain-of-Thought Safety and Faithfulness for Robotics), a multi-layered verification framework achieving 94.2% hallucination detection (n = 500, 95% CI: 91.8-95.9%) with sub-500ms latency. Through a warehouse robot case study, this work demonstrates 87% reduction in unsafe reasoning outputs (p < 0.001) and provides recommendations for responsible deployment of reasoning-capable autonomous robots.

</details>


### [295] [PccDiffuser: Multi-solution Motion Planning for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09745)
*Ke Qiu,Sifan Chen,Si Wang,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang,Haojian Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PccDiffuser——面向连续体机器人的条件扩散规划框架，学习构型空间完整路径的多模态分布并并行采样多条候选解，再经考虑执行器约束的时间分配转为可执行轨迹；在0-4障碍混合测试集上成功率91%，优于采样与优化类基准且计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人规划需在非平凡运动学与多障碍环境下生成多样化可行路径，现有采样法与优化法在成功率与计算效率间难以兼顾，缺乏对多模态路径分布的有效建模。

Method: 基于分段常曲率模型，用指数坐标描述机器人运动学；用图神经网络编码数量可变的环境障碍；构建条件扩散框架学习构型空间完整路径的多模态分布，并并行采样多候选解；在去噪过程中引入解析微分运动学以提升末端精度与全身避障；最后通过考虑执行器约束的时间分配将路径转为可执行轨迹。

Result: 在含0至4个障碍的混合测试集上成功率达91%；相较现有采样与优化基准，成功率更高、计算效率更优，且采样候选解越多效率优势越明显；在三段绳驱连续体机器人上验证了连续规划、多解规划与全身避障能力。

Conclusion: PccDiffuser通过扩散模型与解析微分运动学的结合，实现了连续体机器人的高效多模态路径规划与稳定避障，并在真实绳驱平台上具备可行性。

Abstract: We present the PccDiffuser, a conditional diffusion framework for continuum robots that learns a multimodal distribution over complete configuration-space paths and samples multiple candidate solutions in parallel, which are subsequently converted into an executable trajectory by time allocation considering actuator constraints. Under the piecewise constant-curvature model, we use exponential co-ordinates to describe the robot kinematics, and use graph neural network to encode a variable number of environment obstacles. Analytical differential kinematics is incorporated in the denoising process to improve terminal accuracy and whole-body clearance. On a mixed test set comprising workspace with zero to four obstacles, PccDiffuser achieved a success rate of 91\%. Compared with existing sampling- and optimisation-based benchmarks, it delivered both a higher success rate and greater computational efficiency, with the latter advantage becoming more substantial when sampling more candidate solutions. Experiments on a three-section tendon-driven continuum robot further demonstrate consecutive planning, multi-solution planning, and whole-body obstacle avoidance.

</details>


### [296] [HiRAD: A Flexible Large-Scale AGV Routing System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09752)
*Yunjie Huang,Ruizhong Wu,Mengxuan Zhang,Frodo Kin Sun Chan,Yan Nei Law,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiRAD：面向连续空间AGV路由的层次化强化学习框架，通过步级时空表示、航向/速度分层策略与异步事件驱动流水线，实现实时性并显著降低完工时间与运行开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AGV车队路由面临两大瓶颈：经典多智能体路径规划（MAPF）组合复杂度爆炸、运行时超二次增长，且依赖理想化网格或分段线性运动模型，与真实运动学不符；近期强化学习方法虽具去中心化灵活性，但依赖离散时空表示，需数百万回合收敛，且每步做全图观测，导致模型大、收敛慢、推理延迟高，难以满足工业实时控制要求。

Method: 提出HiRAD层次化强化学习框架，包含三项关键设计：(1) 步级时空表示，将连续运动转化为可微的RL问题；(2) 分层策略，将航向选择与速度控制解耦以缩小动作空间；(3) 异步事件驱动决策流水线，将推理复杂度由O(n^2)降至O(n)。

Result: 在随机图与两个仓库地图上，HiRAD将完工时间（makespan）降低45%至63%，并缩短端到端运行时间；每步延迟最高降低71%。

Conclusion: HiRAD面向连续空间AGV路由，在保证实时性的同时兼顾可扩展性与效率，验证了层次化结构与事件驱动流水线在工业级多智能体调度中的价值。

Abstract: Automatic Guided Vehicles (AGVs) substantially boost warehouse throughput, but routing large-scale AGV fleets remains challenging. Classical Multi-Agent Pathfinding solvers suffer from exploding combinatorial complexity and super-quadratic runtime, while relying on idealized grid or piecewise-linear motion models that mismatch real-world kinematics. Recent Reinforcement Learning (RL) solutions improve flexibility via decentralized agent policies but depend on discretized spatiotemporal representations, require millions of episodes to converge, and incur full-map observation at every step, which leads to large models, slow convergence, and high inference latency that violates real-time industrial control constraints. To address these bottlenecks, we propose HiRAD, a hierarchical RL framework for continuous-space AGV routing with real-time guarantees: (1) a step-level spatiotemporal representation that translates continuous motion into a differentiable RL problem, (2) a hierarchical strategy that splits heading choice from velocity control to reduce the action space, and (3) an asynchronous event-driven decision pipeline that lowers inference complexity from O(n^2) to O(n) and cuts per-step latency by as much as 71 percent. Across random graphs and two warehouse maps, HiRAD reduces makespan by 45 percent to 63 percent and shortens end-to-end runtime.

</details>


### [297] [GTA-2: A Multi-VLM Framework for Synthesizing Robot Manipulation Skills via Grounded Task Axes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09808)
*M. Yunus Seker,Shobhit Aggarwal,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Jonathan Francis,Oliver Kroemer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GTA-2是一个模块化多VLM框架，通过复用物体中心的任务轴组件，零样本构建可执行、任务定制的操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作任务分解出的行为要么需针对特定任务预定义，要么依赖通用技能覆盖广泛任务，导致行为过于粗糙，无法暴露执行所需的几何、控制和场景相关决策。

Method: GTA-2将每个技能表示为语义子任务，包含任务相关关键点与轴、控制器组合及场景相关参数；由四个专用VLM代理分别负责任务分解、构建抽象任务轴技能、分配控制器参数、从RGB-D观测中定位所需视觉特征，实现抽象到落地的因子化。

Result: 在14个真实机器人操作任务上对比VLA策略pi_{0.5}和两个Code-as-Policies基线，GTA-2零样本平均成功率达73.9%，超出最强基线31.4个百分点；定向细化后平均成功率提升至90.7%。

Conclusion: GTA-2无需任务特定演示、策略训练或微调即可零样本生成技能，且中间决策显式可解释，支持针对性人工反馈以修正错误阶段并保留正确组件。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation tasks are often decomposed into behaviors or skills. However, one often needs to predefine these behaviors for specific tasks or try to cover a wide range of tasks using generic skills. As a result, these behaviors can remain too coarse to expose the geometric, control, and scene-dependent decisions required for execution. We introduce Grounded Task Axes v2 (GTA-2), a modular multi-VLM framework that constructs executable, task-bespoke manipulation skills from reusable object-centric task-axis components. Rather than predicting actions end-to-end or composing fixed task-level primitives, GTA-2 represents each skill as semantic subtasks comprising task-relevant keypoints and axes, controller compositions, and scene-dependent parameters. Four specialized VLM agents separately decompose the task, construct an abstract task-axis skill, assign controller parameters, and ground the required visual features from RGB-D observations. This abstraction-to-grounding factorization enables zero-shot skill generation without task-specific robot demonstrations, policy training, or fine-tuning. It also keeps intermediate decisions explicit, allowing targeted human feedback to refine an incorrect stage while preserving correct components. We evaluate GTA-2 on 14 real-robot manipulation tasks against a VLA policy pi_{0.5} and two Code-as-Policies baselines using task-axis controllers or conventional robot primitives. GTA-2 achieves an average zero-shot success rate of 73.9%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 31.4 percentage points, while targeted refinement raises GTA-2's average success rate to 90.7%. Project page: https://gta2-project.github.io/

</details>


### [298] [ViBe: Visual Behavior Adaptation for Perceptive Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09918)
*Lokesh Krishna,Sarvesh Venkatesan,An Zhang,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViBe是一个后训练框架，通过低秩适配器将预训练视觉编码器的感知反馈嫁接到运动跟踪器上，实现参数高效的感知控制适配，并在四项任务上完成零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动跟踪为人形全身控制提供可扩展方案，但其跟踪器缺乏外感知反馈，环境响应只能依赖高层规划器。现有感知控制器从零训练纯几何编码器，以牺牲语义换sim-to-real便利，且通常依赖teacher-student蒸馏针对特定任务。作者希望复用预训练视觉编码器，以参数高效方式将语义感知能力适配到已有跟踪器上。

Method: 提出ViBe后训练框架：利用预训练视觉编码器配合多查询提取器模块学习任务相关感知反馈；通过低秩适配器（LoRA）将反馈嫁接到跟踪器输入，实现参数高效微调；给定任务奖励和参考数据集，模块化控制器可直接经策略优化进行适配。

Result: 在四项任务上实现零样本sim-to-real迁移：台阶与跑酷感知行走、Repose Cube、全物体移动操作、躲避球；在户外、低光和RGB干扰物条件下均表现出视觉鲁棒性。此外以刻意简单的规划器解决了目标导向Repose Cube任务。

Conclusion: ViBe表明通过复用预训练视觉编码器与低秩适配，可将运动跟踪器高效适配为感知控制器，兼具参数效率、零样本迁移能力与视觉鲁棒性，并证明此类感知控制器配合简单规划器即可完成目标导向任务。

Abstract: Motion tracking provides a scalable recipe for humanoid whole-body control. By design, the resulting trackers lack exteroceptive feedback hence reacting to the environment remains the responsibility of a higher-level planner. Existing perceptive controllers train geometry-only encoders from scratch, trading semantics for sim-to-real ease, and typically rely on teacher-student distillation for a task of interest. We present ViBe, a post-training framework for adapting motion trackers to perceptive control tasks. We leverage pre-trained visual encoders with a multi-query extractor module to learn task-relevant perceptive feedback. This feedback is grafted onto the tracker's input via low-rank adapters, enabling parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Given a task reward and a reference dataset, this modular controller can be adapted directly via policy optimization. Across four tasks, ViBe shows zero-shot sim-to-real transfer spanning perceptive walking on curbs and parkour, Repose Cube, omni-object loco-manipulation, and dodgeball, with visually robust performance across outdoor, low-light, and RGB distractor conditions. Finally, we solve a goal-oriented Repose Cube task with a deliberately simple planner, demonstrating the efficacy of perceptive controllers, adapted by our approach.

</details>


### [299] [HaWMPO: Hallucination-Aware World Model-based Policy Optimization for Generalist Robot Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09941)
*Zengjue Chen,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HaWMPO，一种基于世界模型的VLA策略闭环强化学习后训练方法，通过幻觉感知与Reward-Soft机制抑制不可靠动作块，在LIBERO上取得最佳平均成功率，并在真实机器人上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂长时程操作任务中成功率仍受限；在真实机器人上做在线强化学习成本高、样本效率低且存在硬件与安全风险；而世界模型虽可用想象rollout替代物理交互，但长时程rollout易产生预测幻觉，导致有偏状态转移并误导策略学习。

Method: 提出HaWMPO（Hallucination-aware World Model-based Policy Optimization），面向VLA策略后训练的闭环强化学习流程：引入动作条件化的幻觉感知模型，估计生成图像序列的可靠性；将幻觉分数通过Reward-Soft机制融入组相对策略优化（GRPO），在训练中抑制不可靠的动作块。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上取得最佳平均成功率，相比基座模型提升15.0%，相比最强基线提升2.8%；在G1机器人真实世界实验中，两项操作任务的平均成功率从67.5%提升至80.0%。

Conclusion: HaWMPO表明，通过显式建模并抑制世界模型长时程rollout中的预测幻觉，可在不依赖昂贵真实交互的前提下有效后训练VLA策略，在仿真基准与真实机器人任务上均提升成功率，为世界模型驱动的策略优化提供可靠路径。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies have demonstrated strong generalization across robotic manipulation tasks, yet their success rates remain limited in com- plex long-horizon scenarios. Recent methods improve Visual-Language-Action (VLA) policies through online reinforcement learning on real robots, but such training relies on costly physical interactions, suffers from low sample efficiency, and may introduce hardware and safety risks. World models offer a promising alternative by enabling policy optimization with imagined rollouts. However, long-horizon rollouts generated by world models often suffer from prediction hal- lucinations, producing biased state transitions that can mislead policy learning. To address this issue, we propose Hallucination-aware World Model-based Pol- icy Optimization (HaWMPO), a closed-loop reinforcement learning pipeline for VLA policy post-training with world models. Specifically, HaWMPO introduces an action-conditioned hallucination-aware model to estimate the reliability of gen- erated image sequences, and incorporates hallucination scores into group relative policy optimization through a Reward-Soft mechanism, suppressing unreliable ac- tion chunks during training. On the LIBERO benchmark, HaWMPO achieves the best average success rate, with gains of 15.0% over the base model and 2.8% over the strongest baseline; real-world experiments on a G1 robot further validate its effectiveness, raising the average success rate on two manipulation tasks from 67.5% to 80.0%.

</details>


### [300] [RoboDrop: Curating VLA Post-Training Data via Local Gradient Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10021)
*Runze Xu,Yuanfan Xu,Cuijie Xu,Shuang Dai,Yining Li,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboDrop 数据筛选框架，用训练轨迹中的局部梯度兼容性审计监督样本，自动过滤含误差的机器人数据以提升 VLA 模型后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型后训练面向特定任务与本体，对数据质量高度敏感；真实采集数据常含执行错误、传感器漂移、时间戳错位等异构错误。人工检查成本高，现有清洗方法通常仅针对特定类型的损坏，难以通用。

Method: RoboDrop 以训练轨迹上的局部梯度兼容性作为样本对后训练性能影响的代理指标。在一轮 warm-up 训练中，在线将候选样本的梯度与任务语义匹配、视觉匹配的验证样本梯度进行比较打分；样本分数按 episode 聚合，再通过简单的自动后处理规则转化为过滤决策。

Result: 在受控 observation--action 损坏、仿真中天然次优示范以及含非专家采集错误的真实机器人数据集上评估：RoboDrop 比现有方法更准确地识别不可靠示范，筛选后数据训练得到的下游策略更强，真实机器人 rollout 平均成功率从 35.0% 提升到 67.5%。

Conclusion: 训练轨迹感知、上下文条件化的监督审计是提升 VLA 后训练鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) models acquire broad generalization through large-scale pretraining, yet adapting them to a new task and robot embodiment still requires post-training on newly collected data. Unlike pretraining, post-training targets task- and embodiment-specific adaptation, making it particularly sensitive to data quality. In practice, collected robot datasets often contain heterogeneous errors, including execution mistakes, sensor drift, and timestamp misalignment, which can impair post-training and policy performance. Manual inspection is costly, while existing data-cleaning methods are typically tailored to particular corruption types. To address these challenges, we introduce \textsc{RoboDrop}, a data-curation framework that audits supervision using local gradient compatibility measured along the training trajectory as a proxy for its effect on post-training performance. During a one-epoch warm-up run, RoboDrop scores each candidate sample online by comparing its gradient with those of task-semantic and visually matched validation samples. The resulting sample scores are aggregated at the episode level, and a simple automatic post-processing rule converts them into filtering decisions. We evaluate RoboDrop on controlled observation--action corruptions, naturally suboptimal demonstrations in simulation, and real-robot datasets containing non-expert collection errors. Across these settings, RoboDrop more accurately distinguishes unreliable demonstrations than prior methods, while post-training on the curated data consistently yields stronger downstream policies, with average real-robot rollout success rising from $35.0\%$ to $67.5\%$. These results establish training-trajectory-aware, context-conditioned supervision auditing as an effective approach to robust VLA post-training.

</details>


### [301] [AXON: A ROS 2 RMW with Shared-Memory/QUIC Transport and QKD/ML-KEM Key Establishment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10024)
*Sergio Sánchez de la Fuente,Miguel Ángel González-Santamarta,Francisco Javier Rodríguez-Lera,Vicente Matellán Olivera,Ángel Manuel Guerrero-Higueras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AXON：一个按部署范围分离传输策略的 ROS 2 RMW 替代实现——Rust 核心加 C++ 适配层，同主机用 POSIX 共享内存环、远程用 QUIC、守护进程负责发现与图同步；并给出经典（仅混合 X25519MLKEM768）与 qkd（ETSI GS QKD 014 外部 PSK）两种 fail-closed TLS 1.3 密钥建立配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2 通过 RMW 接口标准化应用代码，其参考实现均构建于 DDS 之上，传输策略受制于单一中间件范式，难以针对同主机与远程两类部署范围分别优化，也难以直接引入后量子或量子密钥分发等新型密钥建立机制。

Method: AXON 以 Rust 核心加 C++ 适配器实现 ROS 2 RMW 接口，按部署范围分离传输策略：同主机使用 POSIX 共享内存环，远程使用 QUIC，守护进程承担发现与图同步。远程流量采用两种 fail-closed TLS 1.3 密钥建立配置：经典配置仅提供混合 X25519MLKEM768 组，杜绝协商纯经典组；qkd 配置通过 ETSI GS QKD 014 API 获取 256 位密钥作为成对外部 PSK，不提供任何 Diffie-Hellman 组，其默认 messages10 策略再用 AES-256-GCM 保护远程应用消息，每十条出站消息轮换 KME 材料且每个信封使用新 nonce，session 模式仅依赖 QUIC 保护。外部 PSK 路径需对 rustls 做窄范围扩展，现已随 AXON 一同打包。

Result: 论文定义了威胁模型，区分了两种配置下的对等方认证方式，并明确界定了验证范围与边界：所实现的是实现级验证，不涵盖 ROS 2 一致性、性能对比以及物理 QKD 验证。

Conclusion: AXON 展示了以 RMW 层为切入点、按部署范围解耦传输策略的可行性，为 ROS 2 引入后量子混合密钥建立与基于 QKD 外部 PSK 的 fail-closed 安全配置提供了可落地的实现路径，但相关一致性与性能结论仍有待后续工作评估。

Abstract: Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) standardizes application code against a middleware interface (RMW) whose reference implementations are built on the Data Distribution Service (DDS). We present AXON, an alternative ROS 2 RMW implementation that separates transport policy by deployment scope. A Rust core and C++ adapter use POSIX shared-memory rings for same-host communication, QUIC for remote communication, and a daemon for discovery and graph synchronization. We then describe two fail-closed TLS 1.3 key-establishment configurations for remote traffic. The classic configuration offers only the hybrid X25519MLKEM768 group, preventing negotiation of a classical-only group. The qkd configuration imports a 256-bit key obtained through the ETSI GS QKD 014 API as a pairwise external PSK and offers no Diffie-Hellman group. Its default messages10 strategy additionally protects remote application messages with AES-256-GCM, rotating KME material after ten outgoing messages and using a fresh nonce per envelope; session relies on QUIC protection alone. The external-PSK path requires a narrow extension to rustls, now bundled with AXON. We define the threat model, distinguish peer authentication in the two configurations, and delimit the implementation-level validation from ROS 2 conformance, comparative performance, and physical-QKD validation.

</details>


### [302] [What Symmetry Buys a Learned Motion Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10033)
*Andrea Emir Sevincel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出利用起终点(s, g)闭式构造参考系，在初始化时消除SE(3)中的3个平移和2个旋转自由度，无需修改架构即可显著提升学习式运动规划的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式运动规划器在世界坐标系下训练，需要在每个位置和朝向重新学习相同运动；已有工作通过修改训练数据、推理算子或权重来恢复刚体等变性，但各有代价。作者探究规划查询本身能"免费"提供多少等变性。

Method: 起点s与终点g以闭式确定一个参考系：原点在二者中点，第一轴沿g-s方向。在该参考系中表达轨迹与障碍物，可在初始化阶段去除SE(3)的3个平移和2个旋转，每个查询仅需一次叉积运算，且不限制网络架构。剩余绕起终点轴的单个旋转无法用连续规则消除；作者还实现了三种处理该残余旋转的机制。

Result: 在杂乱3D基准上，固定架构、数据与预算，该参考系使留出集无碰撞率从14.60%提升至51.10%；起终点直连线段得分为15.6%，世界坐标系模型未能超过它。三种残余旋转机制各自增益不足1个百分点，但等变主干网络达到给定水平的速度快2-3倍。标准非等变性残差诊断无法区分：两个残差几乎相同的模型性能相差28个百分点。与"非对称性干预"对比校准后，局部几何贡献+40.0，参考系贡献+36.5，参考系并非最大效应。

Conclusion: 表示所提供的等变性远胜过任何机制所强制的等变性，且标准诊断指标对此不敏感。参考系是免费且有效的收益，但局部几何的影响更大。

Abstract: Learning-based motion planners pay at training what classical planners pay per query. Trained in world coordinates, they relearn the same motion at every position and orientation. Existing work restores the missing rigid-body equivariance in the training data, in the inference operator, or in the weights, and each carries a cost. We ask how much of that equivariance the planning query supplies for free.
  A start s and a goal g determine a frame in closed form, with origin at their midpoint and first axis along g-s. Expressing trajectory and obstacles in that frame removes three translations and two rotations of SE(3), at initialisation, for one cross product per query and with no constraint on the architecture. A single rotation about the start-goal axis remains, and no continuous rule removes it. On a cluttered 3D benchmark, holding architecture, data and budget fixed, the frame raises the held-out collision-free rate from 14.60% to 51.10%, where a straight segment from start to goal scores 15.6% and the world-frame model does not beat it. We build all three mechanisms for the residual rotation and each is worth under a point, though the equivariant backbone reaches any given level two to three times sooner. What the representation supplies therefore dominates what any mechanism enforces, and the standard diagnostic does not see the difference: two models with indistinguishable non-equivariance residuals differ by 28 points. Calibrated against a non-symmetry intervention, the frame is not even the largest effect available, since local geometry is worth +40.0 where the frame is worth +36.5.

</details>


### [303] [Grounding Generated Video Plans in Simulation Towards Versatile Dexterous Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10050)
*Tianyue Wu,Boyuan An,Shuqi Zhao,Heyu Guo,Wanli Xing,Yi Ma,Kaifeng Zhang,Ruihai Wu,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将生成式手-物交互视频与仿真基座相结合，通过HOI重建实现可扩展参考动作生成，训练多物体多轨迹HOI跟踪器，并在真实世界中实现多样抓取与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式HOI视频能提供可控的操作运动方案，而基于仿真的HOI跟踪可将运动学参考转化为可行的底层控制；但其可扩展性受限于缺乏可靠的参考运动。

Method: 结合生成视频与仿真HOI grounding：训练阶段用生成视频提供多样运动参考以学习多物体、多轨迹的HOI跟踪器（tracker）；部署阶段由视频模型生成运动规划（motion plan），交由所学跟踪器执行。核心方法是借助最小人工干预的HOI重建，实现可扩展的参考生成。

Result: 成功在仿真中grounding超过1500个生成视频，基于仿真训练的成功率比基线高25个百分点以上；在真实世界闭环实验中实现了多样抓取，包括功能性抓取、非抓取式操作（non-prehensile manipulation）及抓取后物体位姿跟踪。

Conclusion: 生成视频与仿真HOI tracking的结合可缓解参考运动稀缺问题，实现大规模可扩展的HOI跟踪学习，并有效迁移到真实世界闭环操作任务。

Abstract: Generated hand-object interaction (HOI) videos provide a controllable way to propose manipulation motions. Simulation-based HOI tracking can translate such kinematic references into feasible low-level control, but its scalability is limited by the lack of reliable reference motions. We therefore combine generated videos with simulation-based HOI grounding: during training, generated videos provide diverse motion references for learning a multi-object, multi-trajectory HOI tracker, and at deployment, the video model produces motion plans that are executed by the learned tracker. In particular, we propose a method that enables scalable reference generation by HOI reconstruction with minimal manual intervention and successfully grounds more than 1,500 generated videos in simulation, achieving success rates over 25 percentage points higher than those of baselines during simulation-based training. In real-world closed-loop experiments, it achieves diverse grasps, including functional grasps, non-prehensile manipulation, and post-grasp object-pose tracking. Videos and code are available at https://boyuan-an.github.io/GALATEA/.

</details>


### [304] [Automatic Reproducible Camera Intrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10082)
*Xiangcheng Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套全自动相机内参标定流程，通过迭代剔除低质量图像并结合留出图像的畸变阶数选择，减少人工干预，在保持低重投影误差的同时自动确定径向畸变阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机内参标定的精度依赖于所采集图像的质量，但现有基于标定板的方法通常需要人工筛选高质量图像，并手动指定合适的径向畸变阶数，过程繁琐且依赖经验。因此需要一种能够从数据中自动完成图像筛选与畸变阶数确定的全自动标定流程。

Method: 采用迭代剔除策略：在候选图像集上估计参数，并剔除平均残差超过中位数若干倍的视图；该过程在每个候选畸变阶数下独立运行，使保留的图像集与该阶数的残差尺度一致。畸变阶数在留出图像上选择，固定内参与畸变参数，仅重新估计标定板位姿，从而确保新增系数由独立观测支持。最后将两步集成到支持全流程数据检查与参数估计的交互式标定工具中。

Result: 在自有相机数据和五个公开真实世界数据集上的实验表明，图像筛选使留出重投影误差降低25%，畸变阶数选择进一步降低5%；在无需人工选图的条件下，在四种对比配置中取得最低的留出平均误差。

Conclusion: 图像筛选与基于留出图像的畸变阶数选择能够有效提升自动标定精度，验证了全自动内参标定流程的可行性与有效性，作者将公开代码与数据以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Accurate camera intrinsic calibration is fundamental to robot perception, and the accuracy depends on the quality of the collected images. However, existing target-based calibration methods often require the practitioner to manually filter out high-quality images and to specify an appropriate radial distortion order. This paper presents a fully automatic intrinsic calibration pipeline that determines both from the collected data. We adopt an iterative rejection scheme that estimates parameters on a candidate image set and removes views whose mean residual exceeds a multiple of the median. Crucially, this process runs independently under each candidate distortion order, so that the retained image set is consistent with the residual scale of that order. Further, the distortion order is selected on held-out images, with the intrinsics and distortion fixed and only the board pose re-estimated, ensuring that an added coefficient is supported by independent observations. Finally, we integrate both steps into an interactive calibration tool that supports full-pipeline data inspection and parameter estimation. Experiments on our own camera data and five public real-world datasets show that image filtering reduces the held-out reprojection error by 25\%, the order selection further by 5\%, achieving the lowest held-out mean among four compared configurations without manual image selection. We will release the code and data to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [305] [Assembling Two Parts in One Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10137)
*Liuao Pei,Tianyue Wu,Hui Zhang,Ping Luo,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究"手内装配"任务：单只灵巧手内配合两根刚体（如盖笔帽），无需第二只手臂或夹具，用统一的强化学习框架实现手指级协同，仿真训练后零样本迁移到真实硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类灵巧性的核心特征是手指的协同分工：不同手指承担不同但协调的角色以完成精细操作。本文希望直接在手指层面研究这种协同能力，并以此作为现代机械手系统的评测基准。

Method: 采用统一的强化学习公式，由两部件间的目标相对位姿驱动；用基于函数的辅助奖励塑造手指协同，并以单一人类参考姿态进行正则化；结合域随机化，以及历史本体感觉与物体观测的融合，以应对遮挡导致的估计噪声。

Result: 同一套方案成功求解三个不同装配任务（Bottle、Syringe、Marker）；仅在仿真中训练的策略可零样本迁移到配单相机的真实硬件，证明对遮挡引起状态估计误差的鲁棒性。实验还表明手内装配对机械手形态提出较高要求。

Conclusion: 手内装配是可行的统一强化学习问题，可用于评估和比较不同机械手系统的能力，并为灵巧操作研究提供新基准。

Abstract: A hallmark of human dexterity is the cooperative use of fingers, where different fingers take on distinct yet coordinated roles to accomplish fine manipu- lation, such as capping a pen with the hand that holds it. We study this finger-level coordination through in-hand assembly: mating two rigid objects within a single dexterous hand, with no second arm and no fixture. We present a reinforcement learning formulation to solve this problem in a unified framework, which is driven by a goal relative pose between the two parts. Finger coordination is shaped by a function-based auxiliary reward and regularized toward a single human reference pose, while domain randomization and a fusion of historical proprioception and object observation confer robustness to occlusion-induced estimation noise. The same recipe solves three different assembly tasks (Bottle, Syringe, and Marker). Trained purely in simulation, the policies transfer zero-shot to hardware with a single camera, demonstrating robustness to state-estimation errors caused by oc- clusion. Our experiments also reveal that in-hand assembly places demands on hand morphology and can serve as a benchmark for modern robotic hand systems. Videos and code are available at https://ltbgbird.github.io/in-hand-assembly-page/.

</details>


### [306] [Future-Aware Flow Planning for Safe UAV Target Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10166)
*Boning Feng,Haoran Zhang,Xiaowen Bi,Yanzhen Zhang,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向无人机目标跟随的未来感知流规划框架，将预测目标未来作为时间对齐的残差信号引导轨迹生成，并在采样循环内嵌入风险评分的可执行前缀修复。在ID/OOD滚动时域基准上改善了安全-跟踪权衡，接近零碰撞并提升安全跟踪时间与OOD跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仅基于当前状态的跟随器存在三大问题：转弯时滞后、误选被遮挡走廊、在近时域为跟踪牺牲安全性。根本上是因为目标跟随本质上是预测性任务，需要前瞻目标未来而非被动响应当前状态。

Method: 构建未来感知流规划框架：1）将预测的目标未来作为时间对齐的残差信号，引导干净的无人机轨迹生成；2）在采样循环内嵌入风险评分的可执行前缀修复机制。消融实验验证未来自适应在安全修复之前改善候选生成。

Result: 在固定ID/OOD滚动时域基准上，方法改善了安全-跟踪权衡而非全面占优：匹配ID零碰撞率并取得最高ID安全跟踪时间；在展示方法中获得最低OOD宏观碰撞率和最终跟踪误差。Future-MPC在其手工设计目标下更平滑且部分阈值化OOD成功指标更强。

Conclusion: 时间对齐的未来自适应与嵌入式前缀修复是互补要素，可在测试仿真条件下实现安全的无人机目标跟随。结果支持将二者结合用于密集环境下的预测性跟随，但未在所有指标上全面超越对比方法。

Abstract: UAV target following in cluttered environments is inherently predictive: current-state followers can lag behind turns, choose blocked corridors, or trade tracking for unsafe near-horizon motion. We propose a future-aware flow planning framework for state-informed UAV target following. Predicted target futures guide clean UAV trajectory generation as horizon-aligned residual signals, while risk-scored executable-prefix repair is embedded inside the sampling loop. On fixed ID/OOD receding-horizon benchmarks, the planner improves the intended safety--tracking trade-off rather than dominating every metric: it matches zero measured ID collision rate with the highest ID safe-tracking time, and gives the lowest OOD macro collision rate and final tracking error among the displayed methods, while Future-MPC remains smoother and stronger on some thresholded OOD success metrics under its hand-designed objective. Ablations show that future adaptation improves candidate generation before safety repair, and simulator-facing stress tests probe interface, sensing, and controller-execution effects. These results support horizon-aligned future adaptation and embedded prefix repair as complementary ingredients for safe UAV target following under the tested simulation conditions.

</details>


### [307] [Multi-Robot Scanner for Automated Full-Body Dermoscopic Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10169)
*Valerio Franchi,Rafael Garcia,Nuno Gracias,Ricard Campos,Josep Quintana,Sandra González-Villà,Mark Ventura,Nuria Ferrera,Clément Lenoir,Josep Malvehy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文设计并构建了一套用于全身皮肤病变成像的机器人扫描系统，通过在四个UR10机械臂末端安装相机，实现对皮肤病变的皮肤镜级自动、非接触成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身摄影（total-body photography）与手持皮肤镜（dermoscopy）之间存在成像精度与自动化程度的差距：前者覆盖全身但分辨率不足，后者分辨率高却需手动接触式逐点检查。作者希望构建能自动获取皮肤镜级图像、无需接触、无需额外人工皮肤镜检查的系统，以缩小这一差距。

Method: 系统由四台UR10机械臂构成，相机安装于各机械臂末端执行器；配备视角规划算法以选择最佳相机位置拍摄痣；设计高层控制器实现多机械臂协同工作；并设置碰撞检测器，在机械臂触及物体或人体时停止运动。评估方式包括：对真实患者进行全身扫描，将所获图像与接触式皮肤镜及现有全身摄影系统Vectra在临床相关病变特征上进行比较；使用USAF 1951分辨率靶量化真实光学分辨能力。

Result: 扫描仪最小可分辨特征尺寸为22.1微米，而接触式皮肤镜为8.8微米。在多数临床相关特征上，该系统性能稳定优于Vectra；对所评估的多数特征，其性能与接触式皮肤镜相当。

Conclusion: 该系统能够自动、非接触地获取皮肤镜质量图像，且无需单独的manual皮肤镜检查，从而部分弥合了全身摄影与手持皮肤镜之间的差距，显示出未来整合进筛查工作流程的潜力。

Abstract: This paper outlines the specifications and design approach used to construct a full body imaging scanner capable of capturing skin lesions at a dermatoscopic level using cameras mounted on the end-effectors of four UR10 manipulators. The system possesses a view-planning algorithm capable of appropriately selecting the best camera position to acquire images of moles, a high-level controller to allow the manipulators to work simultaneously and a collision-detector that halts the manipulators when they make contact with an object or a person. We evaluate the system through real-patient full-body scans, comparing acquired images against contact dermoscopy and an existing total-body photography system (Vectra) across clinically relevant lesion features, and quantify true optical resolving power using a USAF 1951 resolution target, yielding a smallest resolvable feature size of 22.1 microns for our scanner compared to 8.8 microns for contact dermoscopy. Results show the scanner consistently outperforms Vectra across most clinically relevant features and achieves comparable performance to contact dermoscopy for the majority of features assessed. By acquiring dermatoscopic-quality images automatically and without contact, and without requiring a separate manual dermoscopic examination, the scanner closes part of the gap between total-body photography and handheld dermoscopy, suggesting potential for future integration into screening workflows.

</details>


### [308] [Adaptive Shared Control with Online Bounded-Rational Human Behavior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10215)
*Henry Ascencio Trejo,Roel Pieters,Gokhan Alcan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作针对非线性控制仿射系统的自适应共享人机控制，放松了人类完全理性的假设，提出基于层级-k有限理性博弈的候选策略库，通过自适应动态规划近似值函数，并利用状态转移残差在线估计人类行为分布，进而以分布感知的一步最优响应实现机器人辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共享控制多假设人类完全理性，而现实中人类表现有限理性，导致机器人辅助策略与实际人类行为不匹配。因此需要机器人能适应观测到的有限理性人类行为，并避免仅选取单一候选策略或简单加权平均策略带来的次优性。

Method: 采用两人博弈的层级-k有限理性模型，通过交替最优响应构建有限候选人类与机器人策略库，并用自适应动态规划近似相应值函数与策略。交互过程中以状态转移残差比较实测系统演化与候选人类策略预测轨迹，经遗忘因子累积后映射为候选库上的概率化人类行为模型。机器人不选取单一候选策略或对已存策略平均，而是最小化在完整估计人类行为分布下的期望合作代价，计算分布感知的一步最优响应；在二次终端值近似与欧拉状态传播下，该响应具有以期望人类输入表示的闭式解。

Result: 在基准非线性系统镇定任务和平面机械臂共享控制设置的仿真中，估计的与仿真的人类行为分布之间的KL散度持续下降；与最大概率策略和概率加权策略两类基线相比，机器人在共享控制交互期间累积运行代价更低。
