Conclusion: 所提方法能够在线适应有限理性人类行为，通过分布感知的一步最优响应提升共享控制性能，在非线性系统镇定与机械臂任务中验证了其有效性与优越性。

Abstract: This work considers adaptive shared human-robot control for nonlinear control-affine systems, where the assumption of a fully rational human is relaxed and the robot adapts its assistance to observed boundedly rational human behavior. We use a level-k bounded-rationality model of the two-player game to construct a finite bank of candidate human and robot policies through alternating best-response computations, with the associated value functions and policies approximated using adaptive dynamic programming. During the shared-control interaction, state-transition residuals compare the measured system evolution with the trajectories predicted by the candidate human policies. The residuals are accumulated using a forgetting factor and mapped to a probabilistic human-behavior model over the finite candidate bank. Rather than selecting a single candidate or averaging stored robot policies, the robot computes a distribution-aware one-step best response by minimizing an expected cooperative cost over the complete estimated human behavior distribution. For a quadratic terminal-value approximation and Euler state propagation, this response admits a closed-form solution expressed in terms of the expected human input. The proposed methods are evaluated in simulations of a benchmark nonlinear system stabilization task, and of a planar manipulator shared control setup. The reported results show decreasing Kullback-Leibler divergence between the estimated and simulated human behavior distributions, and a lower accumulated running cost for the robot agent over the shared control interaction period, than the maximum-probability and probability-weighted alternative policies baseline.

</details>


### [309] [CougarTail & CUB: A General-Purpose Mast and Central Utility Board for Cylindrical Underwater Enclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10230)
*Ben Washburn,Clayton Smith,Eli Gaskin,Brighton Anderson,Braden Meyers,Brady Moon,Joshua Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出CUB与CougarTail通用系统，将水下圆柱壳体内的电子堆栈由传统方形PCB水平堆叠改为同轴圆形PCB，从而释放载荷与浮力预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆柱形水密舱广泛用于UUV、ROV及传感器平台，但电子设备多采用矩形PCB水平堆叠，无法高效利用圆形截面体积，浪费了对载荷能力与浮力管理至关重要的空间，形成明显设计缺口。

Method: 设计CUB圆形PCB，适配4英寸直径舱体，集成Raspberry Pi CM5、STM32微控制器，并提供电源管理与辅助接口；配套开源CougarTail传感器桅杆，内置GPS天线与两个双频（2.4/5 GHz）全向PCB天线。二者通过台架测试并在CougUV平台上完成集成验证。

Result: 同轴安装CUB后，CougUV电子堆栈由200 mm管长、709 g缩减至25 mm、156 g，将该长度与质量预算返还给载荷与浮力配平；CougarTail与CUB均通过台架测试及CougUV平台集成验证。

Conclusion: CUB与CougarTail为圆柱形水密舱提供通用、开源的电子与传感集成方案，显著提升空间利用效率并释放载荷与浮力预算，适用于小型UUV等水下系统。

Abstract: Cylindrical watertight enclosures are widely used across various underwater systems, from unmanned underwater vehicles (UUVs), to remotely operated vehicles (ROVs), to various sensor platforms. However, electronics are typically built on rectangular PCBs arranged in horizontal stacks, which inefficiently occupy the circular cross-section volume that is critical for both payload capacity and buoyancy management. This paper presents CUB (Central Utility Board) and CougarTail, a general-purpose system designed to address this gap. CUB is a circular PCB sized for 4-inch-diameter enclosures that consolidates a Raspberry Pi Compute Module 5 (CM5) and an STM32 microcontroller, while also providing power management features and auxiliary connections. Mounted coaxially, CUB reduces the electronics stack of our CougUV from 200 mm of tube length and 709 g to 25 mm and 156 g, returning that length and mass budget to payload and buoyancy trim. CougarTail is an open-source companion sensor mast that houses a GPS antenna and two dual-band (2.4 and 5 GHz) omnidirectional PCB antennas. Both components are validated through bench testing and integration on a CougUV platform, our small open-sourced torpedo UUVs.

</details>


### [310] [FolDeX: A Physical-World Benchmark for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation of Deformable Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10243)
*Chenhuan Liu,Yi Xu,Feng Wu,Hanyang Wang,Wenxiao Kuai,Weihao Ding,Shan Wang,Yang Liu,Shuyong Gao,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FolDeX 是一个完全基于真实机器人数据的物理世界基准，以服装折叠为核心任务，围绕四条研究轴研究异构物理经验的高效复用，提供2000+小时真实机器人数据，覆盖20+任务和10+具身，并建立公平的外部策略真机评测平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能（含视觉-语言-动作与世界-动作模型）需在物理世界中可靠运行，但仿真表现良好的方法在真机上常显著退化，尤其长时程可变形物体操作中，策略需跟踪状态变化并完成可靠的多阶段双臂交互。现有真机基准多聚焦短时程刚体任务，对长时程可变形操作覆盖不足。此外，真机数据采集成本高昂，如何高效复用异构物理经验尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 以服装折叠为主要任务构建纯真实机器人数据基准，围绕四条研究轴组织：1）利用部署过程中采集的人类干预与恢复数据；2）跨任务数据迁移（跨服装类别、刚体到可变形物体）；3）跨场景复用（光照、背景、布局变化）；4）跨机器人具身迁移。提供2000+小时真机数据，涵盖20+任务与10+具身；并建立外部提交策略的真机评测平台，具备标准化任务、留出物理物体、受控初始化与统一执行协议。

Result: 提供2000+小时真实机器人数据，覆盖20+任务与10+具身；建立公开可访问的真机评测平台（https://ai.midea.com/#/fold-challenge），通过标准化任务、held-out物理物体、受控初始化与统一执行协议保障评测公平性。

Conclusion: FolDeX 有望成为异构真机数据复用与可靠长时程可变形操作的统一测试平台，推动具身智能从仿真走向真实物理世界的可靠部署。

Abstract: Embodied AI, including vision-language-action and world-action models, must operate reliably in the physical world. Yet methods that perform well in simulation can degrade substantially on real robots, especially in long-horizon deformable-object manipulation, where policies must track changing states and execute reliable multi-stage bimanual interactions. Existing real-robot benchmarks mainly focus on short-horizon rigid-object tasks and offer limited coverage of long-horizon deformable manipulation. We introduce FolDeX, a physical-world benchmark built entirely from real-robot data, with garment folding as its primary task. Since real-robot data collection is costly, FolDeX studies how heterogeneous physical experience can be reused efficiently. The benchmark is organized around four research axes: leveraging human intervention and recovery data collected during deployment; transferring data across tasks, including across garment categories and from rigid to deformable-object manipulation; reusing data across scenes with changes in lighting, background, and layout; and transferring data across robotic embodiments. FolDeX provides 2,000+ hours of real-robot data spanning 20+ tasks and 10+ embodiments. We also establish a fair real-robot evaluation platform for externally submitted policies, with standardized tasks, held-out physical objects, controlled initializations, and a unified execution protocol. The platform is publicly accessible at https://ai.midea.com/#/fold-challenge. We hope FolDeX will serve as a unified testbed for heterogeneous real-robot data reuse and reliable long-horizon deformable manipulation.

</details>


### [311] [SwingBot: Learning Whole-Body Brachiation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10283)
*Yujie Xiong,Peng Zhai,Taixian Hou,Quancheng Qian,Cunwang Liu,Kangmai Hu,Long Yang,Zhiyan Dong,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SwingBot，一个面向高自由度人形机器人连续臂跃（brachiation）的学习框架，通过仿生关键帧组织学习过程与递归特权状态估计，实现了仅凭被动腕钩、在无可靠位移与接触测量条件下的连续横杆穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 臂跃使灵长类能在空中支撑上移动，为机器人在杂乱或危险环境中提供了补充运动模式。但将这一能力赋予高自由度人形机器人存在三大困难：控制器需发现长时程的"释放—摆动—抓握"序列；需在全身动量下协调交替接触；且部署时缺乏段间相对位移与钩爪接触状态的可靠测量。

Method: SwingBot 以臂跃任务的结构来组织学习：一方面引入仿生关键帧，使早期探索阶段罕见的长时程"释放—摆动—抓握"转换变得可达，从而让任务可训练；另一方面采用递归的特权状态估计，为部署阶段提供紧凑的位置隐变量与接触隐变量，补偿真实环境中缺失的位移与接触测量。硬件端使用被动腕钩替代主动抓取机构。

Result: 硬件实验实现了连续的横杆穿越，并对负载变化、外部扰动以及不同横杆间距表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，围绕臂跃任务固有结构来组织学习（关键帧引导 + 特权状态估计隐变量）是可行的，为全身式机器人臂跃提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Brachiation enables primates to move across overhead supports when ground paths are blocked, suggesting a complementary locomotion mode for robots operating in cluttered or hazardous environments. Bringing this capabil?ity to high-DoF humanoid robots is difficult because the controller must discover a long-horizon release-swing-capture sequence, coordinate alternating contacts with whole-body momentum, and act without reliable measurements of segment?relative displacement or hook-contact state. We present SwingBot, a learning framework for continuous humanoid brachiation with passive wrist hooks. Swing?Bot makes the task trainable by organizing learning around the structure of brachi?ation: biomimetic keyframes make rare release-swing-capture transitions reach?able during early exploration, and recurrent privileged-state estimation provides compact position and contact latents for deployment. Hardware experiments demonstrate continuous bar traversal and robustness to payload, external distur?bances and different bar spacings, showing that this formulation offers a practical route to whole-body robotic brachiation.

</details>


### [312] [Learning Terrain-Adaptive Humanoid Locomotion on Granular Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10286)
*Junnosuke Kamohara,Feiyang Wu,Andy Ningan Zong,Daniel I. Goldman,Yashwanth Nakka,Seth Hutchinson,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于三维阻力理论(3D RFT)的物理颗粒接触模型，高效仿真颗粒地形用于强化学习训练，并结合教师-学生框架与VAE实现地形自适应的人形机器人运动控制，首次实现真实颗粒地形上的敏捷人形行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒地形上的人形运动因足-地交互动力学复杂、难以建模而极具挑战。现有方法要么忽略颗粒接触动力学，要么仅采用简化的法向力模型并配合启发式切向分量，导致策略难以迁移到真实颗粒地形。

Method: 1) 基于三维阻力理论(3D RFT)构建物理接地的颗粒接触模型，无需启发式即可求解颗粒侵入动力学，捕捉真实穿透与切向阻力；2) 高效仿真颗粒地形用于RL训练；3) 采用教师-学生RL训练地形自适应运动控制器，用变分自编码器(VAE)将地形信息编码为紧凑隐表示。

Result: 基于MPM与NVIDIA Newton的仿真研究表明：方法可泛化到未见过的颗粒地形，成功率显著高于基线，并实现零样本地形识别与自适应；同时通过玄武岩、干沙、海滩沙等多种真实颗粒地形的硬件实验验证了效果。

Conclusion: 该工作首次展示了真实颗粒地形上的敏捷人形运动，证明物理接地的颗粒接触模型配合地形自适应RL策略可可靠地从仿真迁移到真实颗粒环境。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion on granular terrain remains a significant challenge due to its complex foot-terrain interaction dynamics that are difficult to model. Existing approaches either ignore granular contact dynamics or incorporate simplified normal force models with heuristic tangential components. In this work, we present a physics-grounded granular contact model based on three-dimensional resistive force theory (3D RFT) and efficiently simulate granular terrain for reinforcement learning (RL) training. Unlike traditional rigid contact models and simplified granular contact models with ad-hoc heuristics, our contact solver produces physically accurate granular intrusion dynamics without resorting to heuristics. It captures realistic penetration and tangential drag during training, enabling the policy to learn behaviors that transfer reliably to real-world granular terrain where rigid contact models fail. To adapt to varying terrain conditions, we train a terrain-adaptive locomotion controller via teacher-student RL, using a variational autoencoder to encode terrain information into a compact latent representation. Simulation studies using material point method (MPM) with NVIDIA Newton demonstrate that our method generalizes to unseen granular terrains, achieves a significantly higher success rate than baselines, and demonstrates zero-shot terrain identification and adaptation. We further validate our approach through extensive hardware experiments across diverse real-world granular terrains including basalt, dry sand, and beach sand. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of agile humanoid locomotion on real-world granular terrain. Project page: https://humanoid-gm-locomotion.github.io/HUMANOID-GM/

</details>


### [313] [Deformable Object Manipulation under Partial Observability via Real-Time Full-Shape Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10308)
*Kosar Behnia,Ville Kyrki,Gokhan Alcan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出cRVAE（轻量级条件循环变分自编码器），仅利用角节点部分观测即可在推理阶段估计可变形物体的完整状态，并将其作为滚动时域最优控制的前向模型，实现避障协同操作；在绳索和布料仿真中达到参数辨识XPBD模型的精度且速度快数百倍，并在Unitree Go2机器人上完成硬件部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操作面临三大难点：高维状态空间、欠驱动动力学以及部分可观测性。传统物理仿真模型（如XPBD）需要物理参数并依赖在线参数辨识，计算开销大，难以满足实时控制预算，因而亟需一种能快速、仅凭部分传感信息估计完整状态的前向模型。

Method: 提出cRVAE，一种轻量级条件循环变分自编码器，在推理时仅从角节点观测估计DO的完整状态。将该模型作为前向模型嵌入滚动时域最优控制框架，用于避障的协同可变形物体操作。模型推理时不使用任何物理参数作为输入，也不进行在线参数辨识。

Result: 在绳索与布料的仿真中，cRVAE仅凭角节点测量即可估计完整状态，精度与参数辨识的XPBD模型相当；每次前向传播在绳索上约快350倍、布料上约快1500倍；在XPBD早已超出预算的短时域下，cRVAE仍能将基于时域的规划控制在100 ms控制预算内。并在Unitree Go2机器人上验证了硬件部署。

Conclusion: 从角节点传感实现全形状、环路速度级别的状态估计，是模型得以部署到真实硬件的关键，作者通过在Unitree Go2机器人上的实验证明了该方法的可部署性与实时性。

Abstract: Manipulating deformable objects (DOs) is challenging due to their high-dimensional state space, underactuated dynamics, and partial observability. In this paper, we propose cRVAE, a lightweight conditional recurrent variational autoencoder that estimates the full DO state from only partial corner-node observations during inference. The resulting model is used as the forward model in a receding-horizon optimal control framework for obstacle-aware collaborative DO manipulation. In simulation on rope and fabric, cRVAE estimates the full DO state from the available corner-node measurements alone, matching the accuracy of a parameter-identified XPBD model. At inference it uses no physical parameters as model inputs and performs no online parameter identification. It also runs approximately 350 times faster on the rope and over 1500 times faster on the fabric per forward pass, keeping horizon-based planning within the 100 ms control budget where XPBD exceeds it already at short horizons. Full-shape estimation from corner sensing at in-loop speed is what makes the model deployable on hardware, which we demonstrate on a Unitree Go2 robot.

</details>


### [314] [Odometer-Agnostic Drift Correction Using OpenStreetMap Lane Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10336)
*Joaquin Caballero,Emilio Garcia-Fidalgo,Alberto Ortiz,Jarno Ralli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、开源、与里程计无关的漂移校正方法，将短轨迹段对齐到OpenStreetMap（OSM）车道中心线，实现无需稠密先验的高效在线运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增量位姿积分导致的长期漂移是里程计估计的根本性限制，在大规模或无回环环境中尤为明显。现有地图辅助方法虽能减小漂移，但往往依赖稠密地图、特定传感器处理或复杂匹配流程。

Method: 将漂移校正建模为近期里程计轨迹与稀疏车道几何之间的直接对齐，轨迹段直接对齐到OSM车道中心线；方法为开源、里程计无关，无需稠密先验或昂贵预处理，可高效在线运行。

Result: 在LiDAR与视觉里程计后端上进行实验，均取得一致性改进，在严重漂移情形下增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该轻量级OSM车道中心线对齐方法可有效缓解里程计长期漂移，通用性强且适合在线部署。

Abstract: Despite significant progress in odometry estimation, long-term drift remains a fundamental limitation of incremental pose integration, especially in large-scale or loop-free environments. Existing map-assisted methods can reduce drift, but often depend on dense maps, sensor-specific processing, or complex matching pipelines. We propose a lightweight open-source, odometry-agnostic correction method that aligns short trajectory segments to OpenStreetMap (OSM) lane centerlines. By formulating drift correction as a direct alignment between recent odometry and sparse lane geometry, the method enables efficient online operation without dense priors or expensive preprocessing. Experiments with LiDAR and visual odometry backends demonstrate consistent improvements, with particularly strong gains under severe drift.

</details>


### [315] [A Confidence-Aware Multimodal Fusion Framework for Industrial Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10339)
*Xinyu Liu,Qiqi Dong,Boya Jia,Yi Zhang,Binbin Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出置信度感知多模态融合框架CAMF，实现工业人机协作中可靠的人类意图预测，识别准确率达91.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业人机协作中人类意图预测需在低光照、遮挡等干扰下保持可靠，现有多模态融合方法在模态可靠性与跨模态学习偏置方面存在不足。

Method: 融合物体6D位姿、注视、骨骼运动与IMU手部运动四种异构模态；在BiLSTM中嵌入置信度趋势驱动的动态融合机制，按实时模态可靠性自适应平衡双向时序特征；采用置信度引导的均衡学习策略结合置信度冻结机制动态调整网络梯度，抑制低质量模态噪声并缓解跨模态学习偏置；搭建基于UR3协作机器人的物理平台验证。

Result: 意图识别准确率91.86%，整体性能与稳定性优于现有多模态融合方法；在低光照与部分遮挡干扰下仍保持满意精度；在实际装配任务中实现主动、稳定的协作，环境适应性强。

Conclusion: 置信度驱动的动态融合与学习策略可有效提升多模态意图预测的可靠性与鲁棒性，满足工业人机协作的实用需求。

Abstract: A confidence-aware multimodal fusion framework (CAMF) is proposed to realize reliable human intention prediction for industrial human-robot collaboration. This framework fuses four heterogeneous modalities including object 6D pose, gaze, skeletal motion and IMU-based hand motion. It embeds a confidence-trend-driven dynamic fusion mechanism into BiLSTM to adaptively balance bidirectional temporal features according to real-time modality reliability. A confidence-guided balanced learning strategy combined with a confidence freezing mechanism is further adopted to adjust network gradients dynamically, suppress noise from low-quality modalities and mitigate cross-modal learning bias. A physical platform based on the UR3 collaborative robot is built for experimental validation. Comparative results show that the proposed method reaches an intention recognition accuracy of 91.86% and outperforms existing multimodal fusion approaches in overall performance and stability. It also maintains satisfactory accuracy under low light and partial occlusion interference. In practical assembly tasks, the framework enables proactive and stable human-robot cooperation with strong environmental adaptability.

</details>


### [316] [Data-Driven Risk Fields for Safer End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10377)
*Yuanxin Tian,Zhiyuan Liu,Jinhao Li,Zhenhua Xu,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRiF：面向端到端自动驾驶的数据驱动机器人风险场框架，通过将规则化安全先验转化为成对风险标签并采用相对风险排序监督，替代对手工绝对风险分数的回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶对安全性有根本性要求，但现有端到端驾驶模型缺乏显式的风险感知学习能力。规则化风险模型虽具可解释性，但其绝对风险分数依赖手工函数、系数与阈值；基于学习的风险表征虽减少人工设计，却常依赖占据派生标签或启发式代价，难以刻画自车条件化的规划风险。

Method: 提出数据驱动风险场框架DRiF，学习共享BEV特征，同时进行静态地图分割、动态风险预测与车辆规划。在动态风险学习中，将规则化安全先验转换为成对风险标签，训练风险场保持相对风险顺序，而非回归手工绝对风险分数。

Result: 在Bench2Drive上的实验表明，DRiF取得具有竞争力的整体性能，并在驾驶分数、成功率及碰撞相关指标上获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 相对风险监督是将显式安全结构与端到端规划有效连接的方式。数据与代码将公开。

Abstract: Safety is a fundamental requirement for autonomous driving, yet existing end-to-end driving models still lack explicit risk-aware learning capacities. Existing rule-based risk models provide interpretable safety priors, yet their absolute risk scores depend on handcrafted functions, coefficients, and thresholds. Learning-based risk representations reduce part of this manual design, but their supervision often relies on occupancy-derived labels or heuristic cost values, which may not capture ego-conditioned planning risk. In this paper, we propose DRiF, a data-driven risk-field framework for safer end-to-end autonomous driving. DRiF learns a shared BEV feature with static map segmentation, dynamic risk prediction, and vehicle planning. For dynamic risk learning, DRiF converts rule-based safety priors into pairwise risk labels, and trains the risk field to preserve relative risk ordering instead of regressing handcrafted absolute scores. Experiments on Bench2Drive show that DRiF achieves competitive overall performance, with consistent improvements in driving score, success rate, and collision-related metrics. These results establish relative risk supervision as an effective way to connect explicit safety structure with end-to-end planning. The data and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [317] [A traffic management system for large and heterogeneous vehicles in narrow industrial environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10400)
*Alessandro Bonetti,Silvia Proia,Simone Guidetti,Lorenzo Sabattini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向非标准化工业环境的AGV交通管理系统，基于NURBS路径图上的终身多智能体路径规划（L-MAPF）算法，结合改进的有界时域冲突搜索（CBS）与滚动时域冲突消解策略、自适应时域调节、死锁检测与消解机制及实车执行层，在真实工业场景中相比规则法、工业先进方法和优先级型L-MAPF变体，吞吐量提升最高达11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高密度工业环境中AGV协调是Logistics 4.0的关键难题，传统交通管理方法基于协商式优先级分配，易导致效率低下。现有先进方法多面向网格化标准环境，难以适应窄双向走廊、高流量密度、多尺寸异构AGV并存的非标准化真实工业场景。

Method: 在基于NURBS曲线生成的路径图上运行终身多智能体路径规划（L-MAPF）算法，保证局部最优协调与大型异构AGV的安全运行；在有界时域冲突搜索（CBS）改进版本基础上，将其嵌入滚动时域冲突消解框架，对每个智能体采用扩展时间时域；由拓扑地图识别走廊并进行冲突消解；引入随时（anytime）冲突消解策略与自适应时域调节、面向实车安全且符合标准的执行层，以及先进的死锁检测与消解机制。

Result: 在真实工业环境实验中，系统吞吐量较传统规则式交通管理系统、某先进工业方法以及基于优先级的L-MAPF变体均有提升，最高达11%，同时保持连续运行并提高效率。

Conclusion: 所提框架可有效应对非标准化工业场景下的高密度异构AGV交通管理，在保证安全与连续运行的前提下提升吞吐率，优于现有规则式及优先级驱动的路径规划方法。

Abstract: The coordination of Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs) in high-density industrial environments represents a critical challenge within Logistics 4.0, as traditional traffic management methods often lead to inefficiencies caused by negotiation-based priority assignment. To overcome the resulting limitations, this paper presents an innovative AGV traffic management system based on a Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (L-MAPF) algorithm operating on roadmaps generated with Non-Uniform Rational B-Splines (NURBS) curves. The approach guarantees locally optimal coordination and ensures safe operation of large and heterogeneous AGVs. Building on this concept, the proposed framework integrates a modified version of the Bounded Horizon Conflict Based Search (CBS) technique within a Rolling Horizon Conflict Resolution strategy, utilizing an extended time horizon for each agent to enable effective conflict resolution in corridors identified by a topological map. In contrast to state-of-the-art methods for AGV fleet traffic management, the proposed solution is designed for real-world, non-standardized (i.e., non-grid-like) industrial settings characterized by narrow bidirectional corridors and high-traffic density, where AGVs of various sizes and capabilities operate simultaneously. Key contributions include an anytime conflict resolution strategy with adaptive time horizon regulation, an execution layer for safe and standard-compliant interaction with real AGVs, and an advanced mechanism for deadlock detection and resolution. Experimental results obtained in realistic industrial environments demonstrate higher throughput, with improvements of up to 11% over a conventional rule-based traffic management system, a state-of-the-art industrial method, and a priority-based L-MAPF variant, while maintaining continuous operation and improved efficiency.

</details>


### [318] [Frequency-Conditioned Flow Matching for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10405)
*Haochen Niu,Shengye Dong,Hao Liu,Peiwen Lin,Wang Chuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqFM，一种频率条件化的Flow Matching框架，将动作频率从隐式轨迹属性提升为贯穿生成流程的显式条件维度，在DCT频域中构建频谱匹配源分布、自适应平衡目标并约束逐频引导残差，可无缝集成现有VLA动作专家而无需改动骨干，在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、VLA-Arena及六个真机任务上一致提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作是时间相关的轨迹，其频率分量在不同尺度上编码运动且能量分布高度非均匀；而现有基于Flow Matching的VLA模型通常直接以时间坐标生成动作，未显式建模或系统利用这种频率异质性，导致生成过程忽略频域结构信息。

Method: FreqFM在DCT频率坐标下工作：(1) 构建频谱匹配的源分布；(2) 在目标函数中跨频率自适应平衡；(3) 利用对应的参考传输尺度约束逐频率的引导残差。该方法将频率作为显式条件维度贯穿整个动作生成流程，并可插入现有Flow Matching动作专家，无需修改VLA主干。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和VLA-Arena基准上FreqFM均取得性能提升，其中LIBERO-Plus上提升9.3个百分点；并在六个真实机器人任务上进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 通过频率条件化建模，FreqFM有效利用了机器人动作的频率异质性，为VLA动作生成提供了一种无需改动骨干、易于集成且泛化性强的通用增强方案。

Abstract: Robot actions are temporally correlated trajectories whose frequency components encode motion at different scales with highly non-uniform energy distributions. Yet Flow Matching--based vision-language-action (VLA) models typically generate actions in temporal coordinates, without explicitly modeling or systematically leveraging this frequency heterogeneity. We introduce \emph{FreqFM}, a frequency-conditioned Flow Matching framework for VLA models. It raises action frequency from an implicit trajectory property to an explicit conditioning dimension that spans the entire generation pipeline. Concretely, in DCT frequency coordinates, FreqFM constructs a spectrum-matched source distribution, adaptively balances the objective across frequencies, and constrains per-frequency guidance residuals using the corresponding reference transport scales. FreqFM integrates into existing Flow Matching action experts without changing the VLA backbone. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and VLA-Arena, FreqFM consistently improves performance, including a 9.3-point gain on LIBERO-Plus, and further demonstrates its effectiveness on six real-robot tasks.

</details>


### [319] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous UAV Exploration in Wildfire Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10433)
*Caden Chandra,Jerry Ng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究构建深度强化学习框架，用于训练无人机智能体在模拟野火环境中导航与监测，结果表明智能体行为随时间趋于稳定有效，验证了DRL无人机用于自主野火监测的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火监测任务具有高风险与动态性，传统人工或规则化方式难以实现自主、持续的监测，因此需要探索能够自主学习导航与监测策略的智能体方案。

Method: 开发面向无人机智能体的深度强化学习（DRL）框架，在模拟野火环境中进行训练，通过奖励信号与环境结构的设计引导策略学习。

Result: 训练过程中损失曲线收敛，奖励信号逐步改善，导航行为（如火焰边界跟踪）更加一致，智能体表现出日益稳定且有效的策略。

Conclusion: DRL驱动的无人机系统在自主野火监测方面具有应用潜力，且环境结构与奖励设计会显著影响策略的有效性。

Abstract: This study develops a deep reinforcement learning framework for training Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) agents to navigate and monitor simulated wildfire environments. Results show that agents learn increasingly stable and effective behaviors over time, as demonstrated by converging loss trends, improved reward signals, and more consistent navigation patterns such as fire-boundary tracking. Overall, these findings highlight the potential of deep reinforcement learning (DRL) based UAV systems for autonomous wildfire monitoring and suggest that environmental structure and reward design influence policy effectiveness.

</details>


### [320] [Coastal Environment Generation with HoloOcean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10484)
*Abigail Austin,Brady Moon,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于虚幻引擎5的自动化海岸环境生成流水线，用于HoloOcean海洋机器人仿真，仅需用户提供一张俯视海岸图像即可自动生成高度图并选择、放置资产，解决仿真环境获取受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋机器人仿真（UUV/USV的自主与感知算法开发）具有安全、低成本的优势，但受限于可用的仿真环境。现有仿真套件虽支持手动编辑或创建环境，却依赖已有数据或对环境系统的专业知识，导致环境构建门槛高、效率低。

Method: 提出一种新颖的Unreal Engine 5关卡生成流水线，仅需用户提供一张海岸场景俯视图像作为输入；流水线据此生成高度图数据，并自动选择资产（asset）及其在环境中的放置位置，最终为HoloOcean生成海岸环境。

Result: 该流水线可从单张俯视图像自动生成高度图，并完成资产的自动选取与布置，实现HoloOcean海岸环境的自动创建，降低了对既有数据和环境系统专业知识的要求。

Conclusion: 通过UE5自动化关卡生成，为HoloOcean提供了一种低成本、易用的海岸仿真环境构建方案，有助于提升海洋机器人自主与感知算法在实地部署前的开发与测试效率。

Abstract: Marine robotic simulation provides a safe and inexpensive method of developing and testing algorithms for unmanned underwater vehicle (UUV) and unmanned surface vessel (USV) autonomy and perception before full field deployment. However, these simulations are often limited by the availability of simulated environments. Current marine robotics simulation suites offer manual ways to edit or create environments, but they require existing data or specialized knowledge of the environment system. To address these issues, we introduce a novel Unreal Engine 5 level generation pipeline that enables automatic creation of coastal environments for HoloOcean. Our pipeline relies on a user-provided overhead image of a coastal scene. The pipeline then uses the image to generate height map data, as well as automatically select assets and place them in the environment.

</details>


### [321] [DUET-DINO: Simultaneous Cross-View World Modeling for Latent Planning in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10506)
*Nisarga Nilavadi,Ralf Römer,Moritz Reuss,Michael Krawez,Tobias Jülg,Angela P. Schoellig,Rudolf Lioutikov,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DUET-DINO,一个跨视角同步潜在世界模型,通过侧拍与腕部相机交叉视角条件化,实现全 7-DoF 末端执行器的潜在规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件潜在世界模型在细粒度空间与旋转动作预测上不可靠,难以支撑完整 7-DoF 末端执行器控制;单视角或独立双视角方案无法获取互补信息。

Method: DUET-DINO 同时学习静态侧视角与腕部视角观测的动作条件预测,通过跨视角条件化联合建模,并比较 V-JEPA 2 与 DINOv3 在腕部视角下对细粒度动作引起视觉动态的表征能力。

Result: 在 reach、angled-reach 与 lift 任务上分别达到 92%、72.5%、60.0% 成功率,优于单视角与独立双视角基线;基于 DROID 与 RoboArena 从零训练,在视觉分布偏移下泛化稳健;DINOv3 腕部视角预测比 V-JEPA 2 更好捕捉动作条件场景变化,下游规划更优。

Conclusion: 跨视角同步潜在世界模型可有效扩展至全 7-DoF 动作空间,腕部视角表征选择对细粒度动作规划至关重要;代码与权重将开源。

Abstract: Action-conditioned latent world models predict future visual representations, enabling zero-shot goal-conditioned robot planning and control. However, their predictions for fine-grained spatial and rotational actions are unreliable for full 7-DoF end-effector control. To address this gap, we introduce DUET-DINO, a simultaneous cross-view latent world model that jointly learns action-conditioned predictions from static side- and wrist-camera observations through cross-view conditioning. By exploiting complementary global scene and gripper-centric information, DUET-DINO enables latent planning over the full 7-DoF action space. Across spatially diverse reach, orientation-intensive angled-reach, and multi-goal grasp-and-lift tasks, DUET-DINO consistently outperforms single-view and independent dual-view baselines, achieving 92% success on reach, 72.5% on angled-reach, and 60.0% on lift tasks. DUET-DINO is trained from scratch on DROID and RoboArena datasets and generalizes robustly under visual distribution shifts. We further show that while V-JEPA 2 wrist-view predictions underestimate visual dynamics induced by fine-grained actions, DINOv3 predictions better capture action-conditioned scene changes, leading to stronger downstream planning. The code and model checkpoints will be open-sourced. Project page: https://utn-air.github.io/DUET-DINO

</details>


### [322] [Show-Harness: Just a VLM Agent Can Play Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10522)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zechen Bai,Zhijun Cao,Wenzheng Zeng,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Qiming Huang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Show-Harness，一种具身"约束框架"，通过紧凑的语义动作单元接口让 VLM 直接"操控"机器人，并配合 GUMI 实现跨本体的 GUI 演示采集，无需额外模型容量或昂贵的本体专用预训练即可释放基础 VLM 的具身能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础视觉语言模型蕴含广泛的世界知识，但将其智能转化为机器人控制仍很困难。现有方法通常需要额外的模型容量或昂贵的本体专用预训练，本文旨在探索通过合适的接口设计来低成本释放 VLM 的具身能力。

Method: Show-Harness 暴露离散的语义动作单元供 VLM 推理，由本体专用解释器确定性地将其落地为本地机器人动作，使 VLM 直接负责细粒度物理决策。同一接口支持两类路径：(1) 直接解锁闭源前沿 VLM 做零样本控制；(2) 少量 GPU 小时微调小型开源 VLM 以实现低成本部署。此外提出 GUMI（GUI Manipulation Interface），将同一语义动作空间扩展到基于 GUI 的演示采集，使人类与智能体无需专用遥操作硬件即可跨本体"操控"机器人。

Result: 实验表明，配备 Show-Harness 的 VLM 智能体在任务、本体和环境上均具备稳健泛化能力，优于代表性的 agentic 与 VLA 范式。

Conclusion: 合适的接口设计即可从基础 VLM 中释放可观的具身能力，无需增加模型容量或进行昂贵的本体专用预训练。

Abstract: Foundation vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit broad intelligence about the world, yet translating this intelligence into robot control remains challenging. We present Show-Harness, an Embodied Harness that enables VLMs to "play" robots through a compact semantic interface linking intent to action. Show-Harness exposes discrete semantic action units that VLMs can naturally reason over, while embodiment-specific interpreters deterministically ground them into local robot actions, keeping the VLM directly responsible for fine-grained physical decisions. Through the same interface, Show-Harness demonstrates the feasibility of (1) directly unlocking closed-source frontier VLMs for zero-shot robot control, and (2) adapting small-scale open-source VLMs for low-cost deployment with just a few GPU-hours of fine-tuning. We further develop GUMI (GUI Manipulation Interface), which extends the same semantic action space to GUI-based demonstration collection, allowing humans and agents to "play" robots across embodiments without specialized teleoperation hardware. Extensive experiments show that Show-Harness-equipped VLM agents generalize robustly across tasks, embodiments, and environments, outperforming representative agentic and VLA paradigms. These results suggest that the right interface can unlock substantial embodied capability from foundation VLMs, without requiring additional model capacity or costly embodiment-specific pretraining.

</details>


### [323] [DCReg: Decoupled Characterization for Efficient Degenerate LiDAR Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06285)
*Xiangcheng Hu,Xieyuanli Chen,Mingkai Jia,Jin Wu,Ping Tan,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DCReg，通过"检测-刻画-缓解"范式，利用Schur补分解、基对齐特征刻画和结构化预条件子，系统解决LiDAR点云配准在退化环境中的病态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在走廊等几何退化环境中，LiDAR点云配准变得病态：某些运动方向约束较弱，导致解不稳定、精度下降。现有"先检测后缓解"方法无法可靠检测、物理解释并稳定这种病态，且可能破坏优化过程。

Method: DCReg包含三项创新：1）对Hessian矩阵进行Schur补分解，将6自由度配准解耦为3自由度纯旋转和纯平移子空间，消除全Hessian分析中掩盖退化的耦合效应；2）在子空间内通过基对齐解决特征基模糊，建立特征空间与物理运动方向的稳定映射，量化哪些运动缺乏约束及程度；3）基于谱信息设计结构化预条件子，在MAP正则化引导下仅在预条件子内进行特征值截断而非修改原问题，保持最小二乘目标与最优解不变，并用单参数预条件共轭梯度高效优化。

Result: 在多种环境下，DCReg相比退化感知基线方法实现20-50%的长时定位精度提升，以及5-30倍加速（最高116倍）。

Conclusion: DCReg建立了检测-刻画-缓解范式，系统性地解决了病态配准问题，在精度和效率上均显著优于现有方法，且保持原优化问题完整性与可解释性。

Abstract: LiDAR point cloud registration is fundamental to robotic perception and navigation. In geometrically degenerate environments (e.g., corridors), registration becomes ill-conditioned: certain motion directions are weakly constrained, causing unstable solutions and degraded accuracy. Existing detect-then-mitigate methods fail to reliably detect, physically interpret, and stabilize this ill-conditioning without corrupting the optimization. We introduce DCReg (Decoupled Characterization for Ill-conditioned Registration), establishing a detect-characterize-mitigate paradigm that systematically addresses ill-conditioned registration via three innovations. First, DCReg achieves reliable ill-conditioning detection by employing Schur complement decomposition on the Hessian matrix. This decouples the 6-DoF registration into 3-DoF clean rotational and translational subspaces, eliminating coupling effects that mask degeneracy in full-Hessian analyses. Second, within these subspaces, we develop interpretable characterization techniques resolving eigen-basis ambiguities via basis alignment. This establishes stable mappings between eigenspaces and physical motion directions, providing actionable insights on which motions lack constraints and to what extent. Third, leveraging this spectral information, we design a targeted mitigation via a structured preconditioner. Guided by MAP regularization, we implement eigenvalue clamping exclusively within the preconditioner rather than modifying the original problem. This preserves the least-squares objective and minimizer, enabling efficient optimization via Preconditioned Conjugate Gradient with a single interpretable parameter. Experiments demonstrate DCReg achieves 20-50% higher long-duration localization accuracy and 5-30x speedups (up to 116x) over degeneracy-aware baselines across diverse environments. Code: https://github.com/JokerJohn/DCReg

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [324] [OpenDiscoveryTrace: Process Traces for Evaluating AI Scientist Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09203)
*Aayam Bansal,Keertan Balaji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenDiscoveryTrace数据集，含558条AI科学智能体完整推理轨迹（9字段/步），覆盖124个科学任务和7个模型，揭示仅看最终输出无法发现的过程级行为差异，并给出5项基准任务及基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主AI科学家基准仅评估最终输出（代码、假设、论文），丢弃了获得这些输出的推理过程。这导致无法审计科学方法论、诊断失败模式，也无法区分系统性推理与侥幸猜测。因此需要面向过程级评估的数据资源。

Method: 构建并公开OpenDiscoveryTrace数据集：558条完整AI科学智能体轨迹，每条轨迹记录结构化9字段逐步迹（思维、工具调用、观察、错误、修订触发、自报置信度等），覆盖药物发现、材料科学、基因组学、科学文献分析4个领域的124个科学任务。含3个前沿模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Gemini 3.1 Pro，各124条，跨领域与难度均衡）与4个开源权重模型（Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B-v0.3、Phi-3.5-mini、Qwen2.5-1.5B，各30条），另有60条实时检索变体轨迹。对363条轨迹进行LLM评审试点分析，并定义5项基准任务，采用逻辑回归、随机森林、LSTM与Transformer作为基线。

Result: 过程迹暴露出仅输出评估无法察觉的行为差异：三个前沿模型成功率相近（84–89%），但Claude Opus 4.6的每轨迹错误数约为GPT-5.4的30倍（2.5 vs 0.08，p<0.0001，Cliff's δ=0.613），且错误剖面性质不同——Claude为66.7%工具误用，GPT-5.4为83.6%推理错误。

Conclusion: 数据集、迹模式、智能体运行框架与基准定义均以CC BY 4.0公开，支持过程级评估、科学智能体审计与AI治理研究。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for autonomous AI scientists evaluate only final outputs---generated code, hypotheses, or papers---yet discard the reasoning process by which those outputs were obtained. This makes it impossible to audit scientific methodology, diagnose failure modes, or distinguish systematic reasoning from fortunate guessing. We present \textbf{OpenDiscoveryTrace}, a public dataset of 558 complete AI scientific agent trajectories that captures how models reason, not just what they produce. Each trajectory records a structured 9-field-per-step trace---including thoughts, tool calls, observations, errors, revision triggers, and self-reported confidence---as models execute 124 scientific tasks spanning drug discovery, materials science, genomics, and scientific literature analysis. The dataset covers seven models: three frontier models (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, and Gemini 3.1 Pro; 124 trajectories each, fully balanced across domains and difficulty levels) and four open-weight models (Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, Phi-3.5-mini, and Qwen2.5-1.5B; 30 each), plus 60 live-retrieval variant trajectories. Pilot analysis on 363 LLM-judged trajectories reveals that process traces expose behavioral differences invisible to output-only evaluation: all three frontier models achieve comparable success rates (84--89%), yet Claude Opus 4.6 produces 30$\times$ more errors than GPT-5.4 (2.5 vs. 0.08 per trajectory, $p < 0.0001$, Cliff's $δ= 0.613$), with qualitatively different error profiles---66.7% tool misuse for Claude versus 83.6% reasoning errors for GPT-5.4. We define five benchmark tasks with baselines from logistic regression, random forests, LSTMs, and Transformer models. The dataset, trace schema, agent harness, and benchmark definitions are publicly available under CC BY 4.0 to support research on process-level evaluation, scientific agent auditing, and AI governance.

</details>


### [325] [Adaptive Entangled Game Modules in Artificial General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09226)
*Haochen Li,Xinshuai Guo,Jingdong Ouyang,Wei Zhang,Leilei Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出概率波框架与广义行为智能（GBI）非局域概率波方程，从中国日内股市数据中提取可检验本征模，实证支持LCA非局域纠缠神经纤维假说，并主张将自适应纠缠博弈模块引入AGI架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典金融学基于独立理性主体假设难以解释真实交易中的集体决策模式；同时，现有以人工神经网络为基础的AI依赖海量不透明参数，缺乏类脑机制。作者希望用可解析的概率波本征模刻画相互作用的自适应主体的集体行为，并为LCA非局域纠缠神经纤维假说提供间接检验途径。

Method: 构建概率波框架，提出广义行为智能（GBI）非局域概率波方程，推导可检验的本征模；利用中国股市日内数据进行实证分析，识别自适应纠缠博弈模式、对日内新闻/事件/环境的适应行为（对偶均衡态与参照点突变）以及纯粹独立模式的比例。

Result: 自适应纠缠博弈模式解释了82%–94%（整体89%）的观测决策模式，与新古典金融学基于独立理性主体的预测形成鲜明对比；2%–12%的行为表现为对日内新闻、事件和环境的适应，呈现对偶均衡态与参照点突变；纯粹独立模式占比不足5%。

Conclusion: 实证结果支持LCA假说，表明可观测交易行为反映底层脑机制与行为心理学中的内部智能决策。作者主张在AGI架构中纳入自适应纠缠博弈模块，弥补依赖万亿级不透明参数的传统ANN型AI的不足；将ANN型AI与基于概率波的大脑纠缠模拟结合，可丰富AGI基础模型并推动类脑人类处理单元（HPU）发展，从而构建更紧凑、高效、鲁棒的AGI系统，尤其适用于具身智能与机器人。

Abstract: We introduce a probability-wave framework for modeling the collective behavior of interacting adaptive agents, deriving testable eigenmodes through a generalized behavioral intelligence (GBI) nonlocal probability-wave equation. This framework captures a broad range of human intelligence behaviors with analytical mechanisms and offers an indirect method to examine the Liu-Chen-Ao (LCA) hypothesis of nonlocal entangled nerve fibers in the brain through collective trader behaviors. Our empirical analysis of Chinese intraday stock market data demonstrates that adaptive entangled game modes explain 82-94% (89% overall) of observed decision patterns, a sharp contrast to the predictions of neoclassical finance based on independent rational agents. Moreover, 2-12% of behaviors show adaption to intraday news, events, and environments, characterized by dual equilibrium states and abrupt reference point shifts, while purely independent modes occur in less than 5% of cases. These findings empirically support the LCA hypothesis, as observable trading behaviors reflect underlying brain mechanisms and internal intelligence decision-making in behavioral psychology. Our results highlight the necessity of incorporating adaptive entangled game modules into artificial general intelligence (AGI) architectures, addressing the limitations of conventional artificial neural network (ANN)-based AI, which relies on trillions of opaque parameters. By integrating ANN-based AI with probability-wave-based entangled-brain simulations, machine learning can enrich AGI foundation models (FMs) and facilitate the development of human-like processing units (HPUs) that leverage brain-inspired mechanisms. Such HPUs may ultimately create more compact, efficient, and robust AGI systems, particularly for embodied intelligence and robotics.

</details>


### [326] [Subagents vs Agent Skills: Executing Reusable Knowledge for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09233)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Rachel Lawrence,Alicia Curth,Sushrut Karmalkar,Niranjani Prasad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将"智能体技能"（技能包：含指令、脚本等多文件资源）从"加载进主上下文执行"改为"作为子智能体调用执行"，发现当技能包具备清晰输入-输出契约且指令编码了程序性知识时，子智能体执行的性能优于技能执行，代价是额外的通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务中，把技能指令加载进智能体上下文会导致上下文窗口信息不断累积、推理质量退化，使这种执行方式愈发脆弱。作者希望探索可复用知识应以何种方式组织与调用，才能更有效支撑长时程任务。

Method: 提出以子智能体方式调用技能包：不再把技能指令载入主上下文，而是为每个子任务开启独立的、全新的上下文窗口来执行该技能包，并与传统的"技能指令加载进主上下文"方式（agent-skill execution）进行对比实验。

Result: 当技能包暴露清晰的输入-输出契约、且其指令编码了完成该契约所需的程序性知识时，子智能体执行优于技能执行；其代价是需要额外 token 用于主智能体与子智能体之间的协调，即通信开销增加。

Conclusion: 可复用知识的收益不仅取决于其内容本身，还取决于它被如何组织和调用；上下文隔离式的子智能体调用是缓解长时程任务中上下文膨胀问题的一条有效路径。

Abstract: How can language model agents effectively leverage libraries of reusable knowledge to solve long-horizon tasks? Recent work has increasingly focused on agent skills: reusable capabilities represented as skill packages, i.e., multi-file bundles containing instructions, scripts, and other resources that help agents perform specific tasks. Agent skills are typically executed by loading their skill instructions into an agent's context and relying on the agent to follow them. As task horizons grow, however, this approach becomes increasingly brittle, because reasoning quality degrades as more information accumulates in the context window. We investigate an alternative approach in which skill packages are instead invoked as subagents. Rather than loading skill instructions into the main context, subagent execution spawns fresh context windows dedicated to solving individual subtasks. We show that subagent execution outperforms agent-skill execution when skill packages expose clear input-output contracts and their instructions encode the procedural knowledge needed to fulfill those contracts. The tradeoff is additional communication overhead, as extra tokens are required to coordinate between the main agent and its subagents. Our results show that the benefit of reusable knowledge depends not only on its content, but also on how it is organized and invoked.

</details>


### [327] [Gradland: On Phenomenal Experience, Differentiated Across Many Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09306)
*David Balduzzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并检验假设：物理相互作用的一阶结构（梯度/雅可比矩阵）刻画现象经验的结构，并在理想化神经网络世界 Gradland 中通过有效秩与内聚性两个度量进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究现象经验（phenomenal experience）的结构由何种物理量决定，是意识研究中的核心问题；作者假设一阶结构（梯度或雅可比矩阵）即为刻画经验结构的关键量，需要一个物理已知、函数大多可微的理想化环境来精确检验。

Method: 构建理想化世界 Gradland（由神经网络构成、物理规律已知且函数大多可微）；基于 Kirchhoff 复杂度提出两个雅可比结构度量——有效秩（effective rank）与内聚性（cohesion）；并通过一系列实例分析进行检验。

Result: 该假设可解释多种现象：1）经验可持续数百毫秒的持续时间；2）鲜明经验与模糊经验的差异；3）质感的经验；4）新生儿"繁花似锦的混沌"；5）清晰持存的观念与混乱观念的差异；6）学习过程的体验；7）丰富而稠密经验的功能。

Conclusion: 一阶物理结构（梯度/雅可比）的度量（有效秩、内聚性）能够统一解释经验的结构与功能，支持"物理相互作用的一阶结构刻画现象经验结构"这一假设。

Abstract: This paper investigates the hypothesis that the first-order structure of physical interactions, i.e. gradients or Jacobians, characterizes the structure of phenomenal experience. It does so in an idealized world inhabited by neural networks, Gradland, where the physics are known and the functions are (mostly) differentiable. The paper introduces two measures of Jacobian structure: effective rank and cohesion, based on Kirchhoff complexity. Applying the measures to a series of worked examples shows the hypothesis accounts for: (1) the duration of experience, that it can prolong over hundreds of milliseconds; (2) the difference between what is experienced vividly and obscurely; (3) the experience of texture; (4) the blooming buzzing confusion presumably experienced by newborns; (5) the difference between ideas that are held distinctly in mind and ideas that are confused; (6) what learning is like; and finally (7) the paper explains the function of rich, dense experience.

</details>


### [328] [An Autonomous GeoAI Agent for Arctic Eco-Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09374)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个人在回路的多智能体GeoAI北极生态航行系统，将运营、物理、生态与社区准则纳入统一航线规划框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海冰变化扩大北极季节性通航，同时带来运营、环境与社区风险；现有航线方法侧重时间、油耗与航行风险，常忽视生态与社区影响，而北极航线规划本质是多准则问题。

Method: 多智能体协同：地理空间数据获取与预处理、多目标航线生成、基于skyline的决策支持；以人在回路机制保留关键价值判断的人类控制权。

Result: 生态准则显式纳入对敏感区域的暴露，包括Essential Fish Habitat与海豹关键栖息地，并同时考虑潜在社区负担。

Conclusion: 该框架支持更安全、更透明、更具社会责任感的北极航行；项目页面与代码已公开。

Abstract: Arctic maritime navigation is becoming increasingly important as changing sea-ice conditions expand seasonal accessibility while simultaneously introducing substantial operational, environmental, and community risks. Arctic route planning is inherently a multi-criteria problem: routes that improve vessel safety or efficiency may increase exposure to sea ice, sensitive ecosystems, or nearby communities. Existing routing methods prioritize travel time, fuel use, and navigational risk, often overlooking ecological and community impacts. We introduce a human-in-the-loop, multi-agent GeoAI system for Arctic eco-navigation that integrates operational, physical, ecological, and community-related criteria within a unified routing framework. Multiple specialized agents coordinate geospatial data acquisition and preparation, multi-objective route generation, and skyline-based decision support. The ecological criteria explicitly account for exposure to sensitive areas, including Essential Fish Habitat and seal critical habitat. By considering these ecosystem impacts and potential community burdens while keeping consequential value judgments under human control, the framework supports safer, more transparent, and socially responsible Arctic navigation. Project page and code are publicly available. https://samiraat.github.io/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent/, https://github.com/samiraat/Arctic-Eco-Navigation-Agent

</details>


### [329] [The Menu Is an Execution Prior: State-Path Tool Menus for Online Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09395)
*Bo Yan,Weikai Lin,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"状态路径工具菜单"（State-Path Tool Menu），将面向智能体的工具选择建模为从请求状态到目标结果的执行前路径学习问题，通过在菜单中优先排列前置产出工具再排列消费工具，显著提升了多步任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实智能体面对包含数千个接口的工具库，而当前工具菜单构造方法按请求相关性排序，容易暴露最终动作却遗漏或延后较不明显的前置"生产者"工具，导致多步任务执行失败。因此需要一个能覆盖完整工具链并按可用顺序排列的菜单构造机制。

Method: 作者定义"状态路径"（state path）为从可观测请求状态到期望结果的执行前路由，并把菜单视为对这些路径的执行先验。框架包含：编码器（表示当前状态下哪些工具可运行、其输出如何满足后续输入、训练路径中哪些顺序反复出现）、检索器（覆盖可执行入口、缺失输入的产出工具及最终动作）、以及重排序器（将生产者排在消费者之前）。

Result: 在 ToolBench 上，该方法将在线成功率从 0.737 提升至 0.898，优于检索、重排序、生成与路由等基线，且无需修改智能体本身。其状态路径菜单仅用 32 个工具即可覆盖比官方 128 个工具列表更完整的工具链，且在不同模型容量的执行器家族中成功增益持续存在。

Conclusion: 将工具菜单构造为执行前的状态路径先验，能有效解决多步任务中前置工具遗漏与顺序错位问题，在更少工具数量下实现更高覆盖度与成功率，并对不同执行器具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: Language models act through tools, yet practical agents face libraries containing thousands of interfaces. We introduce the tool menu as the short, ordered subset of available tools shown to an agent before execution. The agent can call only tools in this menu. Multi-step tasks require the final action and the prerequisite tools that create its inputs in a usable order. Current constructors rank tools by request relevance, which can surface the final action while omitting or delaying less obvious producers. We introduce the state path, a pre-execution route from the observable request state to the desired outcome, and propose State-Path Tool Menu to learn it. Our framework treats the menu as an execution prior over these routes. Its encoder represents which tools can run from the current state, how their outputs satisfy later inputs, and which orders recur in training paths. A retriever covers an executable entry, the missing-input producers, and the final action. A reranker then places producers before consumers. On ToolBench, our menu raises online success from 0.737 to 0.898 and outperforms retrieval, reranking, generation, and routing baselines without changing the agent. The State-Path menu also covers more complete chains with 32 tools than the official list covers with 128, and its success gain persists across executor families with different model capacities. Our code is at https://github.com/Met2348/State-Path.

</details>


### [330] [Decision-Focused Active Learning for Scale-Aware Critical-Materials Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09413)
*Niranjan Srinivas,Debajyoti Ray,Elias Nakouzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究基于CICERO自主选择性沉淀工作流的历史记录，检验主动学习在稀土回收工艺放大的实验选择效率，并提出以“下游贝叶斯风险期望降低”为准则的批次选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工艺放大需将实验室结果与产品要求、过程成本及规模效应相衔接；同时希望减少实验次数、提高筛选效率，并建立可预注册、可验证的对比标准。

Method: 采用条件回顾性基准（conditional retrospective benchmark），使用拟合模型与回收的NdFeB磁体记录，将主动学习与自适应策略同非自适应空间填充（space filling）对比；定义富集度为相对进料的稀土/铁比。进一步提出按候选批次在当前信念下对下游贝叶斯风险的最大期望降低来选择批次，并在探索性模拟中比较“混合筛选候选”与已实现的“路线与条件联合搜索”，以及合成两阶段策略。

Result: 在条件回顾性基准中，主动学习以更少实验找到最佳记录结果；自适应策略在16–24孔（个实验）达到记录的最大富集度，而空间填充需48孔；两阶段重建与两种自适应备选并列于16孔。SmCo回收磁体条件分析显示第2轮存在纯度与名义产率的权衡（名义产率按假定起始量计算的回收分数）；NdFeB第1轮不同路线在富集度上存在差异。油气开采产出水的排序取决于相态与稀释假设，需进一步确认。探索性模拟中，带候选过滤的混合策略估计损失低于已实现的联合搜索；涉及合成两阶段策略的差异相对于估计不确定性较小。

Conclusion: 结论：以期望下游贝叶斯风险降低来选批次是有前景的决策导向准则，但现有差异常被估计不确定性掩盖。需开展共享损失与统一记录标准的预注册前瞻性试验，并明确测量与记录、定义过程决策及相关输出、引入可信经济输入，并在目标规模验证。

Abstract: Choosing a recovery process for scale-up requires connecting laboratory results with product requirements, process costs, and scale effects. We analyze records from Pacific Northwest National Laboratory's Computer Intelligence for Critical Element Recovery and Optimization (CICERO) workflow for autonomous selective precipitation. Active learning uses prior results to choose experiments. In a conditional retrospective benchmark with fitted models and recycled neodymium-iron-boron (NdFeB) magnet records, active learning finds the best recorded result with fewer experiments than nonadaptive space filling. Enrichment is the selected rare-earth-to-iron ratio relative to that in the feed. Adaptive policies reach the recorded enrichment maximum by 16 to 24 wells (individual experiments), versus 48. Our two-stage reconstruction ties two adaptive alternatives at 16 wells. Conditional analyses of recycled samarium-cobalt (SmCo) magnets show a Round 2 tradeoff between purity and nominal yield, the recovery fraction calculated from an assumed starting amount - NdFeB Round 1 routes differ in enrichment. Rankings for produced water from oil and gas extraction depend on phase and dilution assumptions requiring confirmation.
  We propose choosing batches by their expected reduction in downstream Bayes risk: the minimum expected loss among available process decisions under current beliefs. In exploratory simulations, a hybrid that filters candidates has lower estimated loss than the implemented joint search across routes and conditions. Differences involving the synthetic two-stage policy are small relative to estimation uncertainty. We outline a pre-registered prospective test under a shared loss and logging standard, requiring clarified measurements and records, a defined process decision and relevant outputs, credible economic inputs, and validation at the intended scale.

</details>


### [331] [Valerant: An Automatic Navigable Game Map Generator via Action-Conditioned World Model Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09418)
*Yiran Qiao,Feng Wang,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Valerant，一个免训练框架，将预训练的动作条件世界模型转化为世界动作模型（WAM），用于在 3D 游戏中探索并构建持久化的 3D 地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）将预测性世界建模与动作生成耦合，使智能体可依据预期未来状态行动。尽管其在具身智能中发展迅速，但通用型 WAM 在游戏领域仍鲜有探索；现有游戏方法多将动作条件世界模型与外部策略、奖励函数结合，且主要局限于 2D 视觉观测空间，无法实例化持久化的 3D 几何结构。与自动驾驶、机器人拥有独立于模型的物理环境不同，游戏缺乏外部物理基底，虚拟世界本身必须被实例化，而动作条件视频 rollout 仅提供视觉观测，不提供支撑移动与交互的空间表示，因此将 WAM 范式扩展到 3D 游戏面临独特挑战。

Method: 提出免训练框架 Valerant：以预训练的动作条件世界模型为基础，将其与基于 SLAM 的空间重建以及探索驱动的动作选择相耦合，把预测性视觉 rollout 转化为持久化的 3D 几何表示，从而将单张图像逐步扩展为可导航、可交互的 3D 游戏地图。

Result: Valerant 能够从单张图像渐进式生成持久化的 3D 游戏地图，在无需额外训练的情况下实现了 3D 空间结构的实例化与探索。

Conclusion: 该框架将基于 WAM 的交互从 2D 视觉仿真拓展至 3D 游戏地图构建，为降低 3D 游戏地图制作的人工成本提供了新思路。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple predictive world modeling with action generation, allowing anticipated future states to guide agent behavior. Although WAMs are rapidly advancing embodied AI, general-purpose counterparts remain largely unexplored in games. Existing game-oriented approaches often combine action-conditioned world models with external policies and reward functions to realize WAM-like decision-making, yet they operate mainly in 2D visual observation space and do not instantiate persistent 3D geometry. Extending this paradigm to 3D games introduces a distinct challenge. In autonomous driving and robotics, the physical environment exists independently of the model, providing a persistent 3D world in which selected actions can be executed. Games have no such external substrate; the virtual world itself must be instantiated. Most playable games require a persistent and navigable space, while 3D games additionally require explicit geometry that supports movement and interaction. Action-conditioned video rollouts provide visual observations but not this spatial representation. We present \textsc{Valerant}, a training-free framework that transforms a pretrained action-conditioned world model into a WAM for exploring and constructing 3D game maps. By coupling predictive visual rollouts with SLAM-based spatial reconstruction and exploration-driven action selection, \textsc{Valerant} progressively transforms a single image into a persistent 3D game map. This framework extends WAM-based interaction beyond 2D visual simulation and offers a new approach to reducing manual effort in 3D game-map creation.

</details>


### [332] [XAI-Arena: Can LLMs Assess the Quality of XAI Explanations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09428)
*Yanfei Hu Fleischhauer,Alona Zharova,Nadja Klein,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 XAI-Arena——一个基于大语言模型作为评判者（LLM-as-a-judge）的可扩展、可复现、多维度且面向利益相关者的 XAI 解释质量评估框架，并通过人类验证证明其与人工评分高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 XAI 解释质量评估多依赖主观人类判断，限制了可复现性、可扩展性以及研究间的可比性。作者希望探索 LLM 是否能作为一种可复现、可扩展的机制，用于对 XAI 解释质量进行比较性评估。

Method: 提出 XAI-Arena 框架，采用 LLM-as-a-judge 范式，对 XAI 解释质量进行可扩展、可复现、多维度、面向利益相关者的评估。评估维度包括：感知简洁性、清晰度、任务适切性、信任校准、可行动性、透明性、忠实性以及整体可解释性。随后在不同数据集、机器学习模型和利益相关者画像（persona）上对多种 XAI 解释方法进行基准测试。

Result: 人类验证显示 LLM 生成评分与人类评分之间存在强正相关（Spearman's rho = .693，p < .001）。

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 的评估能够捕捉 XAI 解释质量上的系统性差异，并为 XAI 解释的比较性评估提供一个可扩展且可复现的框架。

Abstract: Evaluating the quality of explanations produced by explainable AI (XAI) methods remains challenging because existing approaches often rely on subjective human judgment, limiting reproducibility, scalability, and comparability between studies. We examine whether LLMs can serve as a reproducible and scalable mechanism to make comparative assessments of the quality of XAI explanations. We introduce XAI-Arena, an LLM-as-a-judge framework for scalable, reproducible, multidimensional, and stakeholder-sensitive evaluation of XAI explanation quality. XAI-Arena then allows us to compare XAI explanations along various dimensions, namely, perceived simplicity, clarity, task adequacy, trust calibration, actionability, transparency, faithfulness, and overall interpretability. We then benchmark XAI explanation methods across various datasets, machine learning models, and stakeholder personas. Human validation shows a strong positive association between LLM-generated and human ratings (Spearman's rho=.693, p<.001). Together, LLM-based evaluations can capture systematic differences in XAI explanation quality and provide a scalable and reproducible framework for comparative assessment of XAI explanations.

</details>


### [333] [Do Agents Know When They Succeed? Calibrating Agent Confidence from Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09448)
*Priyanka Mary Mammen,Emil Joswin,Srujananjali Medicherla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究多轮智能体(multi-turn agentic)场景下的任务成功预测，提出LTD与ARP两种基于模型内部表征的方法，在多基准、多模型上一致优于表面生成与序列校准基线，实现零开销可靠性监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统正快速被安全关键应用采用，因此度量智能体动作的置信度至关重要。相比传统机器学习系统，智能体工作流具有更复杂的失效模式，涉及规划、工具调用与动态环境交互。论文关注的问题是：模型的内部表征能否为多轮智能体设置下的最终任务成功提供更强信号。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：(1) Latent Trajectory Dynamics (LTD)，通过汇总整个交互轨迹中残差流(residual-stream)表征的变化来刻画轨迹动态；(2) Action Representation Probe (ARP)，从动作决策时形成的表征预测任务成功。

Result: 在三个交互基准（Bash、SQL、Python）和三个模型家族（Qwen14B、Qwen7B、DeepSeek6.7B）上，所提方法一致优于表面层生成与基于序列的校准基线，且无需修改提示、也无需多次采样(rollout)，实现零开销的可靠性监控。

Conclusion: 模型内部表征可为多轮智能体任务成功提供强预测信号，LTD与ARP构成一种零开销的可靠性监控方案，具备跨基准与跨模型家族的泛化性。

Abstract: As agentic systems getting adopted rapidly in safety critical applications, it is vital to measure the confidence associated with the agentic actions. In comparison to the traditional machine learning systems, agentic workflows have complex failure modes with planning, tool invocation and dynamic environment interactions. In this paper, we investigate whether model's internal representations provide stronger signals of eventual task success in multi-turn agentic setups. We introduce two complementary methods: Latent Trajectory Dynamics (LTD), which summarizes changes in residual-stream representations across an an interaction trajectory, and the Action Representation Probe (ARP), which predicts success from representations formed at action decisions. Across three interactive benchmarks (Bash, SQL, Python) and three model families (Qwen14B, Qwen7B, DeepSeek6.7B), our methods consistently outperform surface level generation and sequence-based calibration baselines providing a zero-overhead reliability monitor that requires neither prompt alterations nor multi-sample rollouts.

</details>


### [334] [ContractEval: Query-Conditioned Execution Matching for Procedural Instruction Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09458)
*Praphul Singh,Shanu Kumar,Akshat Agarwal,Ganesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CONTRACTEVAL，一个将程序性指令表示为“查询激活义务”并与回答/轨迹证据匹配的诊断框架，可将遗漏、错误分支、顺序错误、额外动作、不变量违背和输出契约违规转化为不同的符合性失败；在受控审计套件上，它能检测并定位所有注入的结构性失败，优于仅看输出或轨迹的 LLM 评判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体从回答问题转向执行流程后，失败可能并非“答案明显错误”，而是“答案看似可接受但过程缺少正当性依据”。现有仅评估输出看到的是答案，轨迹感知评判看到的是活动，但二者都无法识别查询当下哪些义务应当被激活，因此无法定位被跳过的检查、分支、依赖或不变量。

Method: 将程序性指令表示为对查询激活的义务（query-active obligations），并与响应或轨迹证据进行匹配，从而显式化当前查询应当承担的义务。通过这种方式，把遗漏、错误分支、顺序错误、额外动作、不变量违背和输出契约违规区分为不同类别的符合性失败。在受控的审计程序契约套件上，对比仅输出 LLM 评判、轨迹感知 LLM 评判与 CONTRACTEVAL，并在 gold expected/observed 图以及 LLM 抽取条件下评估检测和定位能力。

Result: 在受控审计程序契约套件上，仅输出和轨迹感知的 LLM 评判会漏掉许多注入的结构性失败；在 gold expected 与 observed 图条件下，CONTRACTEVAL 能检测并定位所有失败。基于 LLM 的抽取能保留大部分信号，但仍对校准敏感。

Conclusion: CONTRACTEVAL 不是符合性保证，而是使程序符合性可审计，而非隐含在最终答案质量中。其价值在于把“答案看起来对”与“过程是否有正当依据”区分开，使程序性失败可被显式检测和定位；但 LLM 抽取的校准敏感性意味着实际部署需谨慎处理抽取误差。

Abstract: As LLM agents move from answering questions to carrying out procedures, failures can be unwarranted rather than visibly wrong: the final response looks acceptable even though the system skipped the check, branch, dependency, or invariant that made the answer justified. Output-only evaluation sees the answer, and trace-aware judging sees activity, but neither identifies which obligations were active for the query. We introduce CONTRACTEVAL, a diagnostic framework for making those active obligations explicit. It represents procedural instructions as query-active obligations and matches them against response or trace evidence, turning omissions, wrong branches, ordering errors, extra actions, invariant breaches, and output-contract violations into distinct conformance failures. On a controlled suite of audited procedural contracts, output-only and trace-aware LLM judges miss many injected structural failures; under gold expected and observed graphs, ContractEval detects and localizes all of them. LLM-backed extraction preserves much of this signal but remains calibration-sensitive. ContractEval is therefore not a compliance guarantee; it makes procedural conformance auditable rather than implicit in final-answer quality.

</details>


### [335] [Multi-Agent Agentic Graph Learning via Structural Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09565)
*Liang Qu,Jianxin Li,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体图学习框架MAAGL，通过社区划分实现区域特化，并用排列不变的固定大小结构签名与top-k语义过滤缓解顺序敏感与上下文膨胀问题，在四个基准数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体图学习（AGL）方法（单智能体或多角色智能体）本质上在整个图上共享同一推理策略，难以适配结构/语义模式异质的图区域，导致次优。虽可借鉴多智能体协作，但直接迁移面临两大挑战：一是将图结构语言化后推理对结构信息顺序敏感，破坏图的排列不变性；二是采样邻域增大使上下文迅速膨胀。

Method: MAAGL将图划分为若干社区，为每个社区分配独立智能体以实现区域特化与差异化记忆；将结构证据与语义证据分离表示：结构证据由动态更新的结构签名概括，该签名具备排列不变性且大小固定；语义证据则按相关性筛选保留top-k节点；智能体基于历史轨迹中相似签名估计自身置信度，并在需要时触发辩论式协作。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MAAGL优于当前最先进的AGL方法。

Conclusion: 通过社区级多智能体特化、排列不变且定长的结构签名以及top-k语义过滤，MAAGL在保持图排列不变性的同时控制上下文规模，并借助基于签名的置信度估计与辩论协作提升图推理性能。

Abstract: Agentic graph learning (AGL) has recently achieved promising results on graph reasoning tasks, where an agent powered by a large language model (LLM) sequentially samples the graph as evidence to support its final prediction. Existing methods either employ a single agent or orchestrate multiple role-based agents to reason and learn over the entire graph, but both essentially rely on a shared reasoning policy across different graph regions, which can be suboptimal for graphs with heterogeneous structural and semantic patterns. Inspired by the progress of multi-agent collaboration on complex reasoning tasks, a natural remedy is to let multiple agents own different memory and collaborate; however, applying this paradigm to graphs directly faces two challenges. First, existing AGL methods typically verbalize graph structures into natural-language descriptions for LLM agents, making the reasoning process sensitive to the ordering of structural information and thereby breaking the permutation-invariant nature of graphs. Second, incorporating increasingly large sampled neighborhoods leads to rapidly growing contexts. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a multi-agent agentic graph learning (i.e., MAAGL) framework. MAAGL partitions the graph into communities and assigns an independent agent to each community for region-specific specialization. MAAGL represents structural and semantic evidence separately. Structural evidence is summarized by a dynamically updated structural signature that is permutation-invariant and fixed in size, while semantic evidence is filtered to the top-k nodes ranked by relevance. Based on historical trajectories with similar signatures, agents estimate their confidence and trigger debate-style collaboration when needed. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that MAAGL outperforms SOTA AGL methods.

</details>


### [336] [CityPlanner: A Sandbox Agent for Executable Urban Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09578)
*Wentao Zhang,Jingyuan Wang,Zetong Zhou,Yifan Yang,Wenrui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CityPlanner沙盒-智能体框架，通过UrbanSandbox可执行环境与原子任务强化学习（BuildPlan与ImprovePlan），在真实城市规化基准上超越启发式、任务专用RL及通用LLM智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市规化是在成本、服务质量等实际目标下，从大规模候选空间中选取可行方案的现实空间优化问题；现有优化与强化学习方法虽对固定建模有效，但依赖任务专用表示与约束处理，通用性与可迁移性受限。

Method: 提出CityPlanner沙盒-智能体框架：构建UrbanSandbox统一文件式环境，使智能体可查看任务文件、生成方案、运行评估器并依据可执行反馈修正决策；进一步提出原子任务强化学习，将长沙盒轨迹分解为负责初始构建的BuildPlan与负责反馈式细化的ImprovePlan。

Result: 在真实世界基准上，CityPlanner持续优于启发式方法、任务专用强化学习方法与通用LLM智能体基线；消融实验验证了UrbanSandbox、原子任务强化学习以及迭代部署各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 文件式可执行沙盒环境与原子任务分解能够有效支撑城市规化中的可执行决策学习，提升跨任务泛化与性能，并已公开代码与数据集供复现研究。

Abstract: Urban planning is a real-world spatial optimization problem that requires selecting feasible actions from large candidate spaces under practical objectives such as cost and service quality. Existing optimization and reinforcement learning methods are effective for fixed formulations, but often depend on task-specific representations and constraint handling. We propose \emph{CityPlanner}, a sandbox-agent framework for executable urban planning. CityPlanner introduces \emph{UrbanSandbox}, a unified file-based environment where agents inspect task files, generate plans, run evaluators, and revise decisions based on executable feedback. To make learning tractable, we further propose atomic-task reinforcement learning, which decomposes long sandbox trajectories into \emph{BuildPlan} for initial construction and \emph{ImprovePlan} for feedback-based refinement. Experiments on a real-world benchmark show that CityPlanner consistently outperforms heuristic, task-specific RL, and general LLM-agent baselines. Ablations verify the contributions of UrbanSandbox, atomic-task RL, and iterative deployment. We release the code and dataset at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/co-agent-C1C8

</details>


### [337] [A Function-Space Approach to the Statistical Mechanics of Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09589)
*Yizhou Zhang,Weichen Wu,Lun Du,Zhengjie Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文在函数空间建立学习的统计力学描述：将参数配置视为微观实现、函数及其动力学算子视为宏观变量，导出涨落修正项 Φ_fluc(M;B)，用以刻画学习算子 M 与统计算子 B 之间的谱匹配与旋转稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络参数空间高度非线性，却表现出规则的宏观行为，亟需一个合适的宏观描述层级。作者试图直接在函数空间刻画学习动力学，以解释并预测稳定的集体组织现象。

Method: 对均方损失，精确误差动力学由学习算子 M=JJ* 支配；结合条件随机动力学的动力学玻尔兹曼权重与参数空间态密度（其局域曲率定义统计算子 B），积分掉局域涨落得到 Φ_fluc(M;B)=σ_ξ²/2·logdet(M⁻¹+B)+const。随后分析该泛函的谱与旋转性质，并在 ReLU 型函数空间、温和稳定统计条件下导出 B=σ_ξ²L*KL。

Result: 固定谱时，该涨落项在 [M,B]=0（对易）时旋转平稳；其极小化要求 M 的大特征值与 B 的小特征值配对，并对旋转失配产生局域回复作用。对 ReLU 型函数空间得到 B=σ_ξ²L*KL，其中 L 度量粗粒化二阶结构。

Conclusion: 低 B 扇区（在 K 的有界各向异性意义下）对应低结构曲率，意味着学习偏好沿光滑、数据自适应方向更快弛豫。结果表明函数空间是研究学习中稳定集体组织的自然宏观层级。

Abstract: Deep neural networks exhibit regular macroscopic behavior despite highly nonlinear dynamics in vast parameter spaces. We develop a statistical-mechanical description of learning directly in function space, treating parameter configurations as microscopic realizations and functions with their dynamical operators as macroscopic variables. For mean-squared loss, the exact error dynamics are governed by the learning operator \(M=JJ^\ast\). Combining the dynamical Boltzmann weight of the conditional stochastic dynamics with the parameter-space density of states, whose local curvature defines a statistical operator \(B\), and integrating over local fluctuations yields
  $$ Φ_{\mathrm{fluc}}(M;B)=\frac{σ_ξ^2}{2}\log\det(M^{-1}+B)+\mathrm{const}. $$
  At fixed spectrum, this term is rotationally stationary when \([M,B]=0\), is minimized by pairing large eigenvalues of \(M\) with small eigenvalues of \(B\), and generates a local restoring contribution against rotational mismatch. For ReLU-type function spaces under mild stable statistical conditions, \(B=σ_ξ^2L^\ast\mathcal K L\), where \(L\) measures coarse-grained second-order structure. Thus the low-\(B\) sector corresponds, up to bounded anisotropy of \(\mathcal K\), to low structural curvature, implying a preference for faster relaxation along smooth, data-adaptive directions. These results identify function space as a natural macroscopic level for studying stable collective organization in learning.

</details>


### [338] [From State Synchronization to Cognitive Self-Evolution: An Operational Architecture for Cognitive Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09625)
*Haoran Gao,An Li,Zhen Li,Jun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一种四层认知数字孪生（CDT）架构，包含物理层、数字孪生层、认知层与任务层，建立自演化闭环运行回路，并刻画用户请求驱动与自驱动两种认知模式，讨论了关键使能机制与部署挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDT研究多聚焦学习模块、知识图谱、大语言模型等单项使能技术，缺乏对"认知能力如何系统性融入数字孪生架构"的整体性认识，缺少结构化框架指导CDT设计与开发。

Method: 提出四层CDT架构（物理层、数字孪生层、认知层、任务层），构建跨四层的自演化闭环运行回路：物理状态同步为数字表征，认知层通过知识、记忆与注意力构建任务特定认知模型，任务层在实际约束下生成决策；运行反馈反过来精炼认知经验并更新数字表征中的关系与标注，支撑后续任务解释、发起与推理的持续演化。基于该框架刻画用户请求驱动认知与自驱动认知两种代表性运行模式，并讨论语义通信、知识查询、任务编排、闭环同步等关键使能机制与部署挑战；通过轻量级仿真进行验证。

Result: 轻量级仿真表明：在有限语义信息条件下闭环任务具有可靠可行性，并可通过积累任务经验提升运行效率。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来CDT系统的设计与开发提供了结构化基础。

Abstract: As Digital Twin (DT) systems evolve beyond state synchronization toward task-oriented and knowledge-driven operation, Cognitive Digital Twins (CDTs) have emerged as an extension that incorporates cognitive capabilities into twin operation. Existing CDT studies often focus on specific enabling techniques, such as learning modules, knowledge graphs, and large language models, while providing limited insight into how cognition can be systematically integrated into DT architectures. To address this issue, this paper proposes a four-layer CDT architecture consisting of the physical layer, digital-twin layer, cognitive layer, and task layer. The proposed architecture establishes a self-evolving closed operational loop spanning these four layers, in which physical states are synchronized into digital representations, cognition constructs task-specific cognitive models through knowledge, memory, and attention, and task-level decisions are generated under practical constraints. Operational feedback further refines cognitive experience and updates relationships and annotations in the digital representation, enabling subsequent task interpretation, initiation, and reasoning to evolve with system operation. Based on this framework, two representative operation modes are characterized: user-request-driven cognition and self-driven cognition. We further discuss key enabling mechanisms and deployment challenges associated with semantic communication, knowledge querying, task orchestration, and closed-loop synchronization. A lightweight simulation study illustrates reliable closed-loop task feasibility under limited semantic information and improved operational efficiency through accumulated task experience. The proposed framework provides a structured foundation for the design and development of future CDT systems.

</details>


### [339] [Seven Sources of Physical AI Capability Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09627)
*Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"能力形成来源"框架，将Physical AI能力归因于七类非互斥来源（RE、PM、EI、SE、MG、EC、ED），并通过重构式归纳与理论饱和的方法验证其解释力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有按形态、架构、学习算法、任务或领域划分的分类法无法直接回答"能力由何而来"，而具有相似能力表现的系统可能源自完全不同的形成历史。作者希望区分"观测能力的相似性"与"能力形成方式的相似性"，以支撑对解释、迁移、复现、依赖关系、治理证据及地缘经济基础的分析。

Method: 采用重构式归纳（reconstructive induction）与理论饱和（theoretical saturation）方法：将研究矩阵追溯至原始研究，对文献去重，设定编码规则，并开展三轮最大差异采样（maximum-difference）与负面案例采样（negative-case sampling）。覆盖的挑战主题包括课程学习与自监督学习、主动推理、开放式与发育式学习、规划与搜索、神经符号架构、数字孪生、生成式物理世界模型、形态-控制协同设计。

Result: 在截至2026年9月4日固定的范围与标准内，全部49条证据记录均可由七类来源单独或组合解释。三轮挑战（R1-R3）均未产生不可约的第八类来源，R3亦无需新增核心定义或实质性边界规则。据此作者声称在该范围内实现了理论饱和。

Conclusion: 该框架划清了能力形成来源与组件、构建步骤的界限，主张在既定范围内的理论饱和（而非逻辑完备性或穷尽未来覆盖）。其贡献在于区分能力"表现相似"与"形成路径相似"，为解释、迁移、复现、依赖分析、治理证据与地缘经济基础研究提供分析工具。

Abstract: Capabilities relevant to Physical AI can arise from materially different formation histories, yet existing taxonomies organized by morphology, architecture, learning algorithm, task, or domain do not directly answer what gives rise to a capability. We define a capability-formation source as a factor materially contributing to capability formation, distinct from components or construction steps. We identify seven non-exclusive sources: Recorded-Experience (RE), Predictive-Modeling (PM), Evaluative-Interaction (EI), Surrogate-Environment (SE), Mechanism-Grounded (MG), Embodied-Coupling (EC), and Evolution-Driven (ED) Formation. Using reconstructive induction with theoretical saturation, we traced a research matrix to primary studies, deduplicated the literature, set coding rules, and conducted three rounds of maximum-difference and negative-case sampling. Challenges included curriculum and self-supervised learning, active inference, open-ended and developmental learning, planning and search, neuro-symbolic architectures, digital twins, generative physical world models, and morphology-control co-design. Within the scope and criteria fixed as of September 4, 2026, all 49 evidence records were explainable by the seven sources individually or in combination. No R1-R3 challenge produced an irreducible eighth source, and R3 required no new core definition or substantive boundary rule. We therefore claim theoretical saturation within the stated scope, not logical completeness or exhaustive future coverage. The framework distinguishes similarity in observed capability from similarity in how it was formed, supporting analysis of explanation, transfer, replication, dependencies, governance evidence, and geoeconomic foundations.

</details>


### [340] [RobustSGPO: Search-Space Control for Agent Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09646)
*Zibo Zhao,Jijun Shi,Mo Zhou,Zhongyuan Wang,Shifu Bie,Yunfei Zhang,Xuanting Zhou,Xiangyu Wu,Bin Liu,Ruiming Tang,Wenwu Ou,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RobustSGPO，通过明确编辑请求、构造并校验补丁、以及从当前最优或保留快照继续搜索，解决语义梯度提示优化中编辑范围与操作未定问题，并在 AgentX 头脑风暴工作流上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义梯度提示优化（SGPO）虽能利用执行反馈改进智能体框架，但其局部更新规则未解决编辑范围与操作的选择问题，限制了优化效果。

Method: RobustSGPO 明确指定所请求的编辑，构造并检查补丁，随后从当前最优解或保留快照继续搜索；在 AgentX 头脑风暴流程中评测权限调度、累积控制与任务族迁移，涉及 120 个任务、95 次运行和 7,350 次候选尝试。

Result: 周期性 1→2→3 调度比固定最大权限高 0.28 测试分；RobustSGPO 在 20M token 预算下将 30 个留出任务的完成率从 60.0% 提至 80.0%，测试质量从 3.77 提至 4.14。类别保留降低迁移后源任务退化，随机保留则达到更高的目标任务终点。搜索空间控制通过可执行编辑与替代起点提升质量，但带来可测量的保留开销。

Conclusion: 显式控制编辑范围、操作与搜索起点可显著提升提示优化的稳健性与迁移表现，且权限调度与保留策略各有权衡。

Abstract: Semantic-gradient-based prompt optimization (SGPO) improves agent harnesses using execution feedback, but its local update rule leaves the choice of edit scope and operation unresolved. We introduce RobustSGPO, which specifies the requested edit, constructs and checks the patch, and continues search from either the incumbent or retained snapshots. We evaluate permission scheduling, cumulative controls, and task-family transfer in the AgentX brainstorming workflow using 120 tasks, 95 runs, and 7,350 candidate attempts. Periodic $1\to2\to3$ scheduling exceeds fixed maximum permission by 0.28 test-score points. RobustSGPO increases completion on 30 held-out tasks from 60.0% to 80.0% and improves test quality from 3.77 to 4.14 under a 20-million-token budget. Category retention reduces source-task degradation after a shift, whereas random retention reaches a higher destination endpoint. Search-space control benefits quality through executable edits and alternative starting points, with measurable retention overhead.

</details>


### [341] [Black-Box Red Teaming of Agentic AI: A Taxonomy-Driven Framework for Automated Risk Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09647)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Nitin Aravind Birur,Tanay Baswa,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出黑盒风险感知评测框架，用七域分类法、自动化红队（SAGE-RT）与LLM裁判，跨CrewAI/AutoGen与4个基座模型评测，揭示治理、隐私与行为层面的严重智能体风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统进入生产环境后可读取不可信输入、以真实权限调用工具并自主行动，安全面已超出纯对话模型；而现有评测多为单轮，无法覆盖多步智能体漏洞，且通常依赖特权访问，难以规模化。

Method: 构建仅需基础系统描述的系统性黑盒框架：1）七域风险分类法，将可观测行为映射到风险类别；2）全自动红队SAGE-RT，每域生成120个对抗场景；3）人工验证的LLM裁判评测。在两种智能体架构（CrewAI、AutoGen）与四个基座模型上开展实证验证。

Result: 结果显示显著风险暴露：平均治理风险56.25%，多智能体配置下隐私风险65%，智能体行为漏洞最高达85%。

Conclusion: 黑盒方法无需特权访问即可有效识别关键架构漏洞，为更安全的智能体部署提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Agentic systems are rapidly moving to production, where they read untrusted inputs, call tools with real permissions, and act autonomously, expanding the security surface beyond chat-only models. Yet standard evaluations remain single-turn and fail to capture multi-step agent vulnerabilities. We present a systematic black-box framework for risk-aware agent evaluation requiring only basic system descriptions. Our approach introduces: (1) a seven-domain taxonomy mapping observable behaviors to risk categories, (2) fully automated SAGE-RT red teaming producing 120 adversarial scenarios per domain, and (3) human-validated evaluation using LLM judges. Empirical validation across two agent architectures (CrewAI and AutoGen) with four base models reveals alarming patterns: 56.25\% average governance risk, 65\% privacy risk in multi-agent configurations, and agent behavior vulnerabilities reaching 85\%. Our black-box approach effectively identifies critical architectural vulnerabilities without privileged access, providing a scalable path toward safer agent deployments.

</details>


### [342] [RESCUE-BENCH: Towards Relation-Aware Multi-Party Emotional Support Conversation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09657)
*Haichuan Hu,Yang Xiao,Mingni Tang,Jiawen Duan,Quanjun Zhang,Congqing He,Hao Zhang,Jiashuo Wang,Johan F. Hoorn,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系感知情感支持对话（RESC）新任务，并构建RESCUE基准，评估LLM在多方可变关系中的理解与支持能力，发现现有模型在关系密集型任务上表现不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统主要聚焦一对一求助者-支持者互动和个体情绪状态，忽视了多方场景中的人际关系及其动态演化，导致关系敏感的情感支持建模研究不足。

Method: 从真实伴侣与家庭访谈对话中构建RESCUE基准，包含191个样本、7,079个标注对话轮次、1,064.8分钟视频，并基于社会情感与支持动态的丰富标注设计六项任务，评估关系理解与关系敏感支持两类核心能力；对十个LLM进行实验。

Result: 十个LLM在依赖局部情绪或干预线索的任务上表现相对较好，但在关系模式预测、观点预测和支持策略预测等关系密集型任务上表现困难。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在建模人际关系和做出关系敏感支持决策方面存在明显局限，RESCUE为关系感知情感支持对话的理解与评估提供了新基准。

Abstract: Existing emotional support conversation systems mainly focus on one-on-one seeker-supporter interactions and individual emotional states, leaving interpersonal relations in multi-party scenarios underexplored. In this work, we introduce relation-aware emotional support conversation, a new task that evaluates whether LLMs can capture and utilize the evolving dynamics of relationships to offer more effective emotional support. We construct RESCUE (Relation-aware Emotional Support Conversation Understanding and Evaluation Benchmark) from real couple and family interview conversations, containing 191 samples, 7,079 annotated turns, and 1,064.8 minutes of video. Based on rich annotations of socio-emotional and support-related dynamics, RESCUE defines six tasks that evaluate two core capabilities required for relation-aware emotional support: Relational Understanding and Relation-Sensitive Support. Experiments with ten LLMs show that current models perform relatively well on tasks relying on local emotional or intervention cues, but struggle with relation-intensive tasks such as relation pattern prediction, viewpoint prediction, and support strategy prediction. These findings reveal the limitations of current LLMs in modeling interpersonal relations and making relation-sensitive support decisions.

</details>


### [343] [PRAGMA: Evaluating Personalized Guidance with Memory Alignment in Lifelong Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09664)
*Hyojeong Yu,Hyukhun Koh,Minsung Kim,Yunah Jang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PRAGMA基准，用于评估长时对话中的个性化指导能力，发现现有检索、记忆系统与长上下文模型在恢复对话证据及将其用于个性化指导两方面均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM作为长期交互的个人助理，完整对话历史带来计算开销与信息利用困难，催生记忆系统。现实用户需求多为推荐、规划、决策支持等实用性指导，这类任务需跨多次对话整合信息并推理用户偏好与经验变化，但现有对话记忆评估多聚焦检索与事实回忆，缺乏对个性化指导的评估。

Method: 引入PRAGMA基准，包含经过整理的纵向对话历史、证据标注，以及基于演变的用户背景和错误用户假设所构建的指导场景；对检索系统、记忆系统和长上下文模型进行实验评估。

Result: 实验表明，现有系统在恢复合适的对话证据（检索）以及有效利用这些证据进行个性化指导（推理）两方面均存在困难。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了需要支持鲁棒对话检索与基于记忆的推理（超越单纯证据回忆）的记忆架构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as personalized assistants that interact with users over extended periods of time. As conversations grow longer, relying on full interaction histories becomes increasingly inefficient and unreliable: long contexts introduce substantial computational overhead, making it difficult for models to consistently identify and utilize the most relevant information for the current request. These challenges have motivated memory systems that structure and retrieve user-specific information. In realistic interactions, users often seek practical guidance such as recommendations, planning, and decision support. Unlike factual recall tasks, personalized guidance requires models to integrate information across multiple past conversations and reason about changing user preferences and experiences. However, existing conversational memory evaluations mainly focus on retrieval and factual recall. To study this challenge, we introduce PRAGMA, a benchmark for evaluating personalized guidance in long-term conversations. PRGAMA contains curated longitudinal conversation histories, evidence annotations, and guidance scenarios grounded in evolving user contexts and incorrect user assumptions. Experiments across retrieval systems, memory systems, and long-context models reveal that current systems struggle both to recover the appropriate conversational evidence and to effectively use it for personalized guidance. Our results highlight the need for memory architectures that support robust conversational retrieval and memory-grounded reasoning beyond evidence recall.

</details>
